<script>
/***************************************************************
 * PONT BASCULE LIVE
 * INDEX.HTML — PART 2
 * JAVASCRIPT CORE ENGINE

 * - Google Apps Script communication
 * - Page navigation
 * - Morocco live clock
 * - Dashboard data
 * - PIN unlock / lock
 * - Access status
 * - Automatic refresh
 * - Error handling
 ***************************************************************/


/* ============================================================
   1. GLOBAL APPLICATION STATE
============================================================ */

const PBL_APP = {
  name: "PONT BASCULE LIVE",
  version: "1.0.0",
  timezone: "Africa/Casablanca",

  token: null,
  unlocked: false,
  initialized: false,
  loading: false,

  refreshInterval: null,
  clockInterval: null,

  refreshSeconds: 10,

  currentPage: "dashboard",

  initialData: {},
  dashboardData: {},
  tickets: [],
  products: [],
  clients: [],
  trucks: [],
  drivers: [],
  objectives: [],

  accessStatus: null,

  apiAvailable: {},
  lastRefresh: null
};


/* ============================================================
   2. PAGE CONFIGURATION
============================================================ */

const PBL_PAGE_TITLES = {
  dashboard: "CONTROL DASHBOARD",
  weighing: "WEIGHING STATION",
  tickets: "TICKET MANAGEMENT",
  objectives: "DELIVERY OBJECTIVES",
  products: "PRODUCT MANAGEMENT",
  clients: "CLIENT MANAGEMENT",
  trucks: "TRUCK MANAGEMENT",
  drivers: "DRIVER MANAGEMENT",
  reports: "REPORTING CENTER",
  search: "ADVANCED SEARCH",
  notifications: "NOTIFICATION CENTER",
  settings: "APPLICATION SETTINGS",
  maintenance: "SYSTEM MAINTENANCE",
  audit: "AUDIT LOG"
};


/* ============================================================
   3. GENERAL UTILITIES
============================================================ */

function pblGetElement(id) {
  return document.getElementById(id);
}


function pblSetText(id, value) {
  const element = pblGetElement(id);

  if (element) {
    element.textContent =
      value === null || value === undefined ? "" : String(value);
  }
}


function pblSetHTML(id, value) {
  const element = pblGetElement(id);

  if (element) {
    element.innerHTML = value;
  }
}


function pblNumber(value) {
  const number = Number(value);

  return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : 0;
}


function pblFormatNumber(value, decimals = 3) {
  return pblNumber(value).toLocaleString("fr-FR", {
    minimumFractionDigits: decimals,
    maximumFractionDigits: decimals
  });
}


function pblEscapeHTML(value) {
  return String(value ?? "")
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;")
    .replace(/'/g, "&#039;");
}


function pblIsObject(value) {
  return value !== null &&
    typeof value === "object" &&
    !Array.isArray(value);
}


function pblGetArray(value) {
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;

  if (pblIsObject(value)) {
    if (Array.isArray(value.data)) return value.data;
    if (Array.isArray(value.records)) return value.records;
    if (Array.isArray(value.items)) return value.items;
    if (Array.isArray(value.tickets)) return value.tickets;
  }

  return [];
}


function pblSuccess(result) {
  if (result === true) return true;

  if (!pblIsObject(result)) return false;

  if (result.success === true) return true;
  if (result.ok === true) return true;
  if (result.status === "SUCCESS") return true;

  return false;
}


function pblErrorMessage(error) {
  if (!error) return "An unexpected error occurred.";

  if (typeof error === "string") return error;

  return error.message ||
    error.error ||
    "An unexpected application error occurred.";
}


/* ============================================================
   4. TOAST NOTIFICATIONS
============================================================ */

function showToast(message, type = "info", duration = 3500) {
  const container = pblGetElement("toastContainer");

  if (!container) return;

  const toast = document.createElement("div");

  toast.className =
    "toast " +
    (type === "error" ? "error" :
     type === "success" ? "success" : "");

  toast.textContent = String(message);

  container.appendChild(toast);

  setTimeout(function() {
    toast.style.opacity = "0";
    toast.style.transform = "translateX(15px)";

    setTimeout(function() {
      toast.remove();
    }, 250);

  }, duration);
}


/* ============================================================
   5. LOADING SCREEN
============================================================ */

function showLoading(message = "LOADING PONT BASCULE LIVE...") {
  PBL_APP.loading = true;

  pblSetText("loadingText", message);

  const overlay = pblGetElement("loadingOverlay");

  if (overlay) {
    overlay.classList.add("active");
  }
}


function hideLoading() {
  PBL_APP.loading = false;

  const overlay = pblGetElement("loadingOverlay");

  if (overlay) {
    overlay.classList.remove("active");
  }
}


/* ============================================================
   6. GOOGLE APPS SCRIPT COMMUNICATION
============================================================ */

/*
 * All backend calls pass through this function.
 *
 * Do not use fetch() to call Google Apps Script functions.
 * google.script.run is the supported communication method.
 */

function pblCallBackend(functionName, args = []) {

  return new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {

    if (
      typeof google === "undefined" ||
      !google.script ||
      !google.script.run
    ) {
      reject(
        new Error(
          "Google Apps Script connection is unavailable. " +
          "Open the deployed web application URL."
        )
      );

      return;
    }

    try {

      let runner = google.script.run
        .withSuccessHandler(function(result) {
          PBL_APP.apiAvailable[functionName] = true;
          resolve(result);
        })
        .withFailureHandler(function(error) {
          reject(error);
        });

      const backendFunction = runner[functionName];

      if (typeof backendFunction !== "function") {
        PBL_APP.apiAvailable[functionName] = false;

        reject(
          new Error(
            "Backend function not available: " + functionName
          )
        );

        return;
      }

      backendFunction.apply(runner, args);

    } catch (error) {
      reject(error);
    }

  });
}


/*
 * Try several possible backend function names.
 * This allows the frontend to work with the API names
 * provided by the different backend modules.
 */

async function pblCallFirst(functionNames, args = []) {

  let lastError = null;

  for (const functionName of functionNames) {

    try {

      return await pblCallBackend(functionName, args);

    } catch (error) {

      lastError = error;

      if (
        PBL_APP.apiAvailable[functionName] === false
      ) {
        continue;
      }
    }
  }

  throw lastError ||
    new Error("No compatible backend function was found.");
}


/* ============================================================
   7. LIVE CLOCK — MOROCCO
============================================================ */

function updateLocalClock() {

  const now = new Date();

  try {

    const time = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fr-FR", {
      timeZone: PBL_APP.timezone,
      hour: "2-digit",
      minute: "2-digit",
      second: "2-digit",
      hour12: false
    }).format(now);

    const date = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("fr-FR", {
      timeZone: PBL_APP.timezone,
      weekday: "long",
      day: "2-digit",
      month: "long",
      year: "numeric"
    }).format(now);

    pblSetText("liveClock", time);
    pblSetText("liveDate", date);

  } catch (error) {

    pblSetText("liveClock", now.toLocaleTimeString());
    pblSetText("liveDate", now.toLocaleDateString());

  }
}


async function refreshBackendClock() {

  try {

    const result = await pblCallFirst([
      "apiGetLiveClock",
      "apiPbl8GetLiveClock",
      "getLiveClockData"
    ]);

    if (!pblIsObject(result)) return;

    const time =
      result.time ||
      result.currentTime ||
      result.liveTime;

    const date =
      result.date ||
      result.currentDate ||
      result.liveDate;

    if (time) pblSetText("liveClock", time);
    if (date) pblSetText("liveDate", date);

  } catch (error) {

    // Local Morocco clock remains active if the API is unavailable.

  }
}


function startLiveClock() {

  updateLocalClock();

  if (PBL_APP.clockInterval) {
    clearInterval(PBL_APP.clockInterval);
  }

  PBL_APP.clockInterval = setInterval(
    updateLocalClock,
    1000
  );

  refreshBackendClock();

}


/* ============================================================
   8. PAGE NAVIGATION
============================================================ */

function showPage(pageName, clickedButton = null) {

  const page = pblGetElement("page-" + pageName);

  if (!page) {
    showToast("Page not found: " + pageName, "error");
    return;
  }

  document.querySelectorAll(".page").forEach(function(item) {
    item.classList.remove("active");
  });

  page.classList.add("active");

  document.querySelectorAll(".nav-item").forEach(function(item) {
    item.classList.remove("active");
  });

  const navButton =
    clickedButton ||
    document.querySelector(
      '.nav-item[data-page="' + pageName + '"]'
    );

  if (navButton) {
    navButton.classList.add("active");
  }

  PBL_APP.currentPage = pageName;

  pblSetText(
    "currentPageTitle",
    PBL_PAGE_TITLES[pageName] || pageName.toUpperCase()
  );

  closeMobileSidebar();

  /*
   * Load page-specific data when the user opens a page.
   */

  if (pageName === "tickets") {
    loadTickets();
  }

  if (pageName === "products") {
    loadProducts();
  }

  if (pageName === "clients") {
    loadClients();
  }

  if (pageName === "trucks") {
    loadTrucks();
  }

  if (pageName === "drivers") {
    loadDrivers();
  }

  if (pageName === "objectives") {
    loadObjectives();
  }

  if (pageName === "settings") {
    loadSystemInformation();
  }

  if (pageName === "weighing") {
    refreshScaleDisplay();
  }

  if (pageName === "notifications") {
    loadNotifications();
  }

  if (pageName === "audit") {
    loadAuditLogs();
  }

}


function toggleSidebar() {

  const sidebar = pblGetElement("sidebar");

  if (sidebar) {
    sidebar.classList.toggle("open");
  }
}


function closeMobileSidebar() {

  const sidebar = pblGetElement("sidebar");

  if (sidebar && window.innerWidth <= 800) {
    sidebar.classList.remove("open");
  }
}


/* ============================================================
   9. PIN MODAL
============================================================ */

function openPinModal() {

  const modal = pblGetElement("pinModal");

  if (!modal) return;

  modal.classList.add("open");

  pblSetText("pinError", "");

  const input = pblGetElement("pinInput");

  if (input) {
    input.value = "";

    setTimeout(function() {
      input.focus();
    }, 100);
  }

}


function closePinModal() {

  const modal = pblGetElement("pinModal");

  if (modal) {
    modal.classList.remove("open");
  }

  pblSetText("pinError", "");

}


function getSessionToken() {

  return PBL_APP.token;
}


/* ============================================================
   10. UNLOCK SYSTEM
============================================================ */

async function unlockSystem() {

  const pinInput = pblGetElement("pinInput");

  const pin = pinInput ? pinInput.value.trim() : "";

  if (!pin) {
    pblSetText("pinError", "Please enter your PIN.");
    return;
  }

  if (!/^[0-9]{4,12}$/.test(pin)) {
    pblSetText(
      "pinError",
      "PIN must contain between 4 and 12 digits."
    );

    return;
  }

  try {

    showLoading("VERIFYING SECURITY PIN...");

    const result = await pblCallBackend(
      "apiUnlock",
      [pin]
    );

    if (!pblSuccess(result)) {

      const message =
        result && result.message
          ? result.message
          : "Incorrect PIN or authorization denied.";

      pblSetText("pinError", message);

      hideLoading();

      return;
    }

    /*
     * Keep the session token in memory only.
     * Do not save the PIN or token in localStorage.
     */

    const token =
      result.token ||
      result.sessionToken ||
      result.unlockToken ||
      result.data?.token ||
      result.data?.sessionToken;

    if (!token) {

      hideLoading();

      pblSetText(
        "pinError",
        "The backend did not return a session token. Check apiUnlock."
      );

      return;
    }

    PBL_APP.token = token;
    PBL_APP.unlocked = true;

    closePinModal();

    updateAccessDisplay();

    await refreshApplication();

    hideLoading();

    showToast(
      "System successfully unlocked.",
      "success"
    );

  } catch (error) {

    hideLoading();

    pblSetText(
      "pinError",
      pblErrorMessage(error)
    );

  }

}


/* ============================================================
   11. LOCK SYSTEM
============================================================ */

async function lockSystem() {

  try {

    if (PBL_APP.token) {

      await pblCallBackend(
        "apiLock",
        [PBL_APP.token]
      );

    }

  } catch (error) {

    /*
     * The local interface still switches to read-only.
     * Backend authorization must also reject expired
     * or invalid tokens.
     */

  }

  PBL_APP.token = null;
  PBL_APP.unlocked = false;
  PBL_APP.accessStatus = null;

  updateAccessDisplay();

  showToast(
    "System locked. Read-only mode activated.",
    "success"
  );

  await checkAccessStatus();

}


function updateAccessDisplay() {

  const mode = PBL_APP.unlocked
    ? "UNLOCKED / AUTHORIZED"
    : "READ ONLY";

  pblSetText("sidebarAccessMode", mode);
  pblSetText("dashboardAccessMode", mode);
  pblSetText("settingsAccessMode", mode);

  const topButton = pblGetElement("topbarLockButton");
  const sideButton = pblGetElement("sidebarUnlockButton");

  if (PBL_APP.unlocked) {

    if (topButton) {
      topButton.textContent = "🔒 LOCK SYSTEM";
      topButton.onclick = lockSystem;
    }

    if (sideButton) {
      sideButton.textContent = "🔒 LOCK SYSTEM";
      sideButton.onclick = lockSystem;
    }

  } else {

    if (topButton) {
      topButton.textContent = "🔐 UNLOCK";
      topButton.onclick = openPinModal;
    }

    if (sideButton) {
      sideButton.textContent = "🔐 UNLOCK SYSTEM";
      sideButton.onclick = openPinModal;
    }

  }

}


/* ============================================================
   12. ACCESS STATUS
============================================================ */

async function checkAccessStatus() {

  try {

    const result = await pblCallBackend(
      "apiAccessStatus",
      [PBL_APP.token]
    );

    PBL_APP.accessStatus = result;

    const unlocked = !!(
      result &&
      (
        result.unlocked === true ||
        result.isUnlocked === true
      )
    );

    PBL_APP.unlocked = unlocked;

    if (!unlocked) {
      PBL_APP.token = null;
    }

    updateAccessDisplay();

  } catch (error) {

    /*
     * If status cannot be verified, fail closed:
     * the interface remains read-only.
     */

    PBL_APP.unlocked = false;
    PBL_APP.token = null;

    updateAccessDisplay();

  }

}


/* ============================================================
   13. CHANGE PIN
============================================================ */

function openChangePinModal() {

  if (!PBL_APP.unlocked || !PBL_APP.token) {

    showToast(
      "Unlock the system before changing the PIN.",
      "error"
    );

    openPinModal();

    return;
  }

  pblSetText("changePinError", "");

  const modal = pblGetElement("changePinModal");

  if (modal) {
    modal.classList.add("open");
  }

}


function closeChangePinModal() {

  const modal = pblGetElement("changePinModal");

  if (modal) {
    modal.classList.remove("open");
  }

}


async function changeSystemPin() {

  const oldPin = pblGetElement("oldPinInput").value.trim();
  const newPin = pblGetElement("newPinInput").value.trim();
  const confirmPin = pblGetElement("confirmPinInput").value.trim();

  if (!oldPin || !newPin || !confirmPin) {
    pblSetText(
      "changePinError",
      "Complete all PIN fields."
    );

    return;
  }

  if (!/^[0-9]{4,12}$/.test(newPin)) {
    pblSetText(
      "changePinError",
      "New PIN must contain 4 to 12 digits."
    );

    return;
  }

  if (newPin !== confirmPin) {
    pblSetText(
      "changePinError",
      "New PIN confirmation does not match."
    );

    return;
  }

  try {

    showLoading("UPDATING SECURITY PIN...");

    const result = await pblCallBackend(
      "apiChangePin",
      [
        oldPin,
        newPin,
        PBL_APP.token
      ]
    );

    hideLoading();

    if (!pblSuccess(result)) {

      pblSetText(
        "changePinError",
        result?.message || "PIN update failed."
      );

      return;
    }

    closeChangePinModal();

    pblGetElement("oldPinInput").value = "";
    pblGetElement("newPinInput").value = "";
    pblGetElement("confirmPinInput").value = "";

    showToast("Security PIN updated.", "success");

  } catch (error) {

    hideLoading();

    pblSetText(
      "changePinError",
      pblErrorMessage(error)
    );

  }

}


/* ============================================================
   14. DASHBOARD DATA LOADING
============================================================ */

async function loadDashboard() {

  let result = null;

  try {

    result = await pblCallFirst([
      "apiPbl8GetLiveDashboard",
      "pbl8GetLiveDashboard",
      "pbl18GetLiveDashboard",
      "getMainDashboardData"
    ]);

  } catch (error) {

    console.error("Dashboard API:", error);

    return;

  }

  if (!pblIsObject(result)) return;

  PBL_APP.dashboardData = result;

  const source =
    result.dashboard ||
    result.data ||
    result;

  const target =
    source.targetQuantity ??
    source.targetTonnes ??
    source.target ??
    0;

  const cumul =
    source.cumulTonnes ??
    source.deliveredTonnes ??
    source.totalNetTonnes ??
    0;

  const voyages =
    source.numberOfVoyages ??
    source.nbVoyages ??
    source.totalVoyages ??
    0;

  const remaining =
    source.remainingTonnes ??
    source.restToDeliver ??
    source.remaining ??
    0;

  const percentage =
    source.deliveryPercentage ??
    source.progressPercentage ??
    source.percentage ??
    0;

  const progress =
    source.progressBarPercentage ??
    source.progressPercentage ??
    percentage;

  /*
   * Required dashboard indicators.
   * Quantities are displayed in TONNES.
   */

  pblSetText(
    "targetQuantity",
    pblFormatNumber(target)
  );

  pblSetText(
    "cumulTonnes",
    pblFormatNumber(cumul)
  );

  pblSetText(
    "numberOfVoyages",
    pblNumber(voyages).toLocaleString("fr-FR")
  );

  pblSetText(
    "remainingTonnes",
    pblFormatNumber(remaining)
  );

  pblSetText(
    "deliveryPercentage",
    pblFormatNumber(percentage, 2) + "%"
  );

  pblSetText(
    "objectiveName",
    source.objectiveName ||
    source.name ||
    "Current delivery objective"
  );

  pblSetText(
    "progressRemainingLabel",
    "REMAINING: " + pblFormatNumber(remaining) + " T"
  );

  const progressBar = pblGetElement("deliveryProgressBar");

  if (progressBar) {

    const safeProgress = Math.max(
      0,
      Math.min(100, pblNumber(progress))
    );

    progressBar.style.width = safeProgress + "%";

  }

  const refreshed = new Date();

  pblSetText(
    "lastRefreshTime",
    refreshed.toLocaleTimeString("fr-FR", {
      timeZone: PBL_APP.timezone
    })
  );

  renderRecentDashboardTickets(
    source.recentTickets ||
    source.tickets ||
    []
  );

}


/* ============================================================
   15. RECENT TICKET DISPLAY
============================================================ */

function renderRecentDashboardTickets(tickets) {

  const body = pblGetElement("dashboardRecentTickets");

  if (!body) return;

  const rows = pblGetArray(tickets).slice(0, 8);

  if (!rows.length) {

    body.innerHTML = `
      <tr>
        <td colspan="5" class="table-empty">
          No recent tickets available.
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;

    return;
  }

  body.innerHTML = rows.map(function(ticket) {

    const number =
      ticket.ticketNumber ||
      ticket.number ||
      ticket.id ||
      "--";

    const date = ticket.date || "--";
    const product = ticket.product || "--";

    const truck =
      ticket.truckRegistration ||
      ticket.truck ||
      "--";

    const net =
      ticket.netKg ??
      ticket.netWeight ??
      0;

    return `
      <tr>
        <td>${pblEscapeHTML(number)}</td>
        <td>${pblEscapeHTML(date)}</td>
        <td>${pblEscapeHTML(product)}</td>
        <td>${pblEscapeHTML(truck)}</td>
        <td>${pblFormatNumber(net, 0)}</td>
      </tr>
    `;

  }).join("");

}


/* ============================================================
   16. INITIAL DATA
============================================================ */

async function loadInitialData() {

  try {

    const result = await pblCallBackend(
      "apiGetInitialData",
      []
    );

    if (!pblIsObject(result)) return;

    PBL_APP.initialData = result;

    const data = result.data || result;

    PBL_APP.tickets = pblGetArray(
      data.tickets
    );

    PBL_APP.products = pblGetArray(
      data.products
    );

    PBL_APP.clients = pblGetArray(
      data.clients
    );

    PBL_APP.trucks = pblGetArray(
      data.trucks
    );

    PBL_APP.drivers = pblGetArray(
      data.drivers
    );

    PBL_APP.objectives = pblGetArray(
      data.objectives
    );

    renderTickets(PBL_APP.tickets);
    renderProducts(PBL_APP.products);
    renderClients(PBL_APP.clients);
    renderTrucks(PBL_APP.trucks);
    renderDrivers(PBL_APP.drivers);

    populateWeighingSelects();

  } catch (error) {

    console.error("Initial data error:", error);

    showToast(
      "Some initial data could not be loaded.",
      "error"
    );

  }

}


/* ============================================================
   17. ACCESSIBLE APPLICATION INITIALIZATION
============================================================ */

async function initializeApplicationInterface() {

  if (PBL_APP.initialized) return;

  showLoading("INITIALIZING PONT BASCULE LIVE...");

  startLiveClock();

  updateAccessDisplay();

  /*
   * Start in READ ONLY mode.
   * A previous browser session is never trusted.
   */

  PBL_APP.token = null;
  PBL_APP.unlocked = false;

  await checkAccessStatus();

  await loadInitialData();

  await loadDashboard();

  await refreshScaleDisplay();

  PBL_APP.initialized = true;

  hideLoading();

  startAutomaticRefresh();

  showToast(
    "PONT BASCULE LIVE initialized.",
    "success"
  );

}


/* ============================================================
   18. APPLICATION REFRESH
============================================================ */

async function refreshApplication() {

  try {

    await loadDashboard();

    if (PBL_APP.currentPage === "tickets") {
      await loadTickets();
    }

    if (PBL_APP.currentPage === "weighing") {
      await refreshScaleDisplay();
    }

    PBL_APP.lastRefresh = new Date();

  } catch (error) {

    console.error("Application refresh:", error);

  }

}


function startAutomaticRefresh() {

  if (PBL_APP.refreshInterval) {
    clearInterval(PBL_APP.refreshInterval);
  }

  PBL_APP.refreshInterval = setInterval(
    async function() {

      await loadDashboard();

      if (PBL_APP.currentPage === "weighing") {
        await refreshScaleDisplay();
      }

      /*
       * Verify that the authorization session is still valid.
       */

      if (PBL_APP.unlocked && PBL_APP.token) {
        await checkAccessStatus();
      }

    },
    PBL_APP.refreshSeconds * 1000
  );

}


/* ============================================================
   19. SYSTEM INFORMATION
============================================================ */

async function loadSystemInformation() {

  try {

    const results = await Promise.allSettled([

      pblCallFirst([
        "apiApplicationInformation",
        "getApplicationInformation"
      ]),

      pblCallFirst([
        "apiSystemHealth",
        "getSystemHealth"
      ])

    ]);

    if (results[0].status === "fulfilled") {

      const info = results[0].value || {};

      pblSetHTML(
        "applicationInformation",
        `
          <div><span class="muted">Name:</span>
            <strong>${pblEscapeHTML(info.name || info.appName || "PONT BASCULE LIVE")}</strong>
          </div>
          <div style="margin-top:10px">
            <span class="muted">Version:</span>
            ${pblEscapeHTML(info.version || "Backend version")}
          </div>
        `
      );

    }

    if (results[1].status === "fulfilled") {

      const health = results[1].value || {};

      pblSetHTML(
        "systemHealth",
        `
          <div style="color:var(--green);font-weight:800">
            ${pblEscapeHTML(health.status || "Health check received")}
          </div>
          <div class="small muted" style="margin-top:8px">
            ${pblEscapeHTML(health.message || "System health information loaded.")}
          </div>
        `
      );

    }

  } catch (error) {

    console.error("System information:", error);

  }

}


/* ============================================================
   20. KEYBOARD SUPPORT
============================================================ */

document.addEventListener("keydown", function(event) {

  if (
    event.key === "Enter" &&
    pblGetElement("pinModal")?.classList.contains("open")
  ) {
    unlockSystem();
  }

  if (event.key === "Escape") {

    closePinModal();
    closeChangePinModal();
    closeMobileSidebar();

  }

});


/* ============================================================
   21. START APPLICATION
============================================================ */

document.addEventListener(
  "DOMContentLoaded",
  initializeApplicationInterface
);


/***************************************************************
 * END OF INDEX.HTML — PART 2
 ***************************************************************/
</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 3
   DATA MANAGEMENT ENGINE
   ============================================================ */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   1. COLLECTION NORMALIZATION
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblNormalizeCollection(value) {
    if (Array.isArray(value)) return value;

    if (value && typeof value === "object") {
        if (Array.isArray(value.items)) return value.items;
        if (Array.isArray(value.records)) return value.records;
        if (Array.isArray(value.data)) return value.data;

        return Object.keys(value).map(function(key) {
            var item = value[key];

            if (item && typeof item === "object") {
                if (!item.id) item.id = key;
                return item;
            }

            return { id: key, value: item };
        });
    }

    return [];
}

function pblGetCollection(data, names) {
    if (!data || typeof data !== "object") return [];

    for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
        if (data[names[i]] !== undefined) {
            return pblNormalizeCollection(data[names[i]]);
        }
    }

    return [];
}

function pblFirstValue(object, names, fallback) {
    if (!object) return fallback;

    for (var i = 0; i < names.length; i++) {
        var value = object[names[i]];

        if (value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== "") {
            return value;
        }
    }

    return fallback;
}

function pblRecordId(record) {
    return String(pblFirstValue(
        record,
        ["id", "ticketId", "productId", "clientId",
         "truckId", "driverId", "objectiveId", "uuid"],
        ""
    ));
}

function pblRecordName(record) {
    return String(pblFirstValue(
        record,
        ["name", "label", "productName", "clientName",
         "truckName", "driverName", "designation", "title"],
        "—"
    ));
}

function pblRecordStatus(record) {
    return String(pblFirstValue(
        record,
        ["status", "state", "statut"],
        "ACTIVE"
    ));
}

function pblIsActiveRecord(record) {
    var status = pblRecordStatus(record).toUpperCase();

    return ![
        "DELETED",
        "ARCHIVED",
        "CANCELLED",
        "CANCELED",
        "INACTIVE",
        "SUPPRIME",
        "SUPPRIMÉ"
    ].includes(status);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   2. SAFE TABLE HELPERS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblCell(value) {
    return pblEscapeHTML(
        value === undefined || value === null ? "" : String(value)
    );
}

function pblEmptyRow(columns, message) {
    return '<tr><td colspan="' + Number(columns || 1) +
        '" class="pbl-empty-row">' +
        pblCell(message || "Aucune donnée disponible") +
        "</td></tr>";
}

function pblStatusBadge(status) {
    var normalized = String(status || "ACTIVE").toUpperCase();
    var css = "status-neutral";

    if (
        normalized === "ACTIVE" ||
        normalized === "COMPLETED" ||
        normalized === "VALIDATED" ||
        normalized === "SUCCESS"
    ) {
        css = "status-success";
    } else if (
        normalized === "PENDING" ||
        normalized === "WAITING" ||
        normalized === "IN_PROGRESS"
    ) {
        css = "status-warning";
    } else if (
        normalized === "CANCELLED" ||
        normalized === "CANCELED" ||
        normalized === "DELETED" ||
        normalized === "ERROR"
    ) {
        css = "status-danger";
    }

    return '<span class="pbl-status ' + css + '">' +
        pblCell(normalized) + "</span>";
}

function pblFormatKg(value) {
    var number = Number(value || 0);

    return pblFormatNumber(number) + " KG";
}

function pblFormatTonnes(value) {
    var number = Number(value || 0);

    return pblFormatNumber(number) + " T";
}

function pblFormatDate(value) {
    if (!value) return "—";

    if (value instanceof Date) {
        return value.toLocaleDateString("fr-FR");
    }

    var text = String(value);

    if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(text)) {
        var parts = text.substring(0, 10).split("-");
        return parts[2] + "/" + parts[1] + "/" + parts[0];
    }

    return text;
}

function pblSetTableBody(elementId, html) {
    var element = pblGetElement(elementId);

    if (element) {
        element.innerHTML = html;
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   3. TICKETS — LOADING AND RENDERING
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function loadTickets() {
    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "apiGetTickets",
            "getAllTickets",
            "pbl24GetAllTickets",
            "pbl21GetTickets"
        ], []);

        var data = response && response.data !== undefined
            ? response.data
            : response;

        PBL_APP.data.tickets = pblGetCollection(
            data,
            ["tickets", "items", "records", "data"]
        );

        if (!PBL_APP.data.tickets.length && Array.isArray(data)) {
            PBL_APP.data.tickets = data;
        }

        renderTickets();

        return PBL_APP.data.tickets;

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("PBL tickets:", error);
        showToast("Impossible de charger les tickets", "error");
        return [];
    }
}

function renderTickets() {
    var tickets = PBL_APP.data.tickets || [];
    var html = "";

    if (!tickets.length) {
        html = pblEmptyRow(8, "Aucun ticket enregistré");
    } else {
        tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
            var number = pblFirstValue(
                ticket,
                ["ticketNumber", "number", "numero", "nTicket"],
                "—"
            );

            var date = pblFormatDate(
                pblFirstValue(ticket, ["date", "createdAt"], "")
            );

            var product = pblFirstValue(
                ticket,
                ["product", "productName"],
                "—"
            );

            var client = pblFirstValue(
                ticket,
                ["client", "clientName"],
                "—"
            );

            var truck = pblFirstValue(
                ticket,
                ["truckRegistration", "registration", "truck"],
                "—"
            );

            var netKg = Number(
                pblFirstValue(ticket, ["netKg", "netWeight"], 0)
            );

            var status = pblRecordStatus(ticket);

            html += "<tr>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(number) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(date) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(product) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(client) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(truck) + "</td>" +
                '<td class="numeric-cell">' +
                    pblCell(pblFormatKg(netKg)) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblStatusBadge(status) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" +
                    '<button class="table-action" type="button" ' +
                    'onclick="viewTicketDetails(\'' +
                    pblCell(pblRecordId(ticket)) +
                    '\')">VIEW</button>' +
                "</td>" +
                "</tr>";
        });
    }

    pblSetTableBody("ticketsTableBody", html);

    pblSetText("ticketsCount", tickets.length);
    pblSetText("totalTicketsCount", tickets.length);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   4. PRODUCTS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function loadProducts() {
    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "apiGetProducts",
            "getAllProducts",
            "pbl25GetProducts"
        ], []);

        var data = response && response.data !== undefined
            ? response.data
            : response;

        PBL_APP.data.products = pblGetCollection(
            data,
            ["products", "items", "records", "data"]
        );

        if (!PBL_APP.data.products.length && Array.isArray(data)) {
            PBL_APP.data.products = data;
        }

        renderProducts();
        populateWeighingSelects();

        return PBL_APP.data.products;

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("PBL products:", error);
        return [];
    }
}

function renderProducts() {
    var products = PBL_APP.data.products || [];
    var html = "";

    if (!products.length) {
        html = pblEmptyRow(5, "Aucun produit enregistré");
    } else {
        products.forEach(function(product) {
            var code = pblFirstValue(
                product,
                ["code", "productCode", "reference"],
                "—"
            );

            var name = pblRecordName(product);

            var unit = pblFirstValue(
                product,
                ["unit", "unite", "measurementUnit"],
                "KG"
            );

            var price = pblFirstValue(
                product,
                ["price", "unitPrice", "prix"],
                0
            );

            html += "<tr>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(code) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(name) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(unit) + "</td>" +
                '<td class="numeric-cell">' +
                    pblCell(pblFormatNumber(price)) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblStatusBadge(pblRecordStatus(product)) +
                "</td></tr>";
        });
    }

    pblSetTableBody("productsTableBody", html);
    pblSetText("productsCount", products.length);
}

function renderObjectives() {
    var objectives = PBL_APP.data.objectives || [];
    var html = "";

    if (!objectives.length) {
        html = pblEmptyRow(5, "Aucun objectif enregistré");
    } else {
        objectives.forEach(function(objective) {
            var name = pblRecordName(objective);

            var target = Number(pblFirstValue(
                objective,
                ["targetTonnes", "targetQuantity", "target", "quantity"],
                0
            ));

            var delivered = Number(pblFirstValue(
                objective,
                ["deliveredTonnes", "cumulTonnes", "delivered"],
                0
            ));

            var remaining = Math.max(0, target - delivered);

            html += "<tr>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(name) + "</td>" +
                '<td class="numeric-cell">' +
                    pblCell(pblFormatTonnes(target)) + "</td>" +
                '<td class="numeric-cell">' +
                    pblCell(pblFormatTonnes(delivered)) + "</td>" +
                '<td class="numeric-cell">' +
                    pblCell(pblFormatTonnes(remaining)) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblStatusBadge(pblRecordStatus(objective)) +
                "</td></tr>";
        });
    }

    pblSetTableBody("objectivesTableBody", html);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   9. WEIGHING DROPDOWNS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblPopulateSelect(selectId, records, placeholder, valueKeys, labelKeys) {
    var select = pblGetElement(selectId);

    if (!select) return;

    var previousValue = select.value;
    var html = '<option value="">' +
        pblCell(placeholder || "Sélectionner") +
        "</option>";

    (records || []).filter(pblIsActiveRecord).forEach(function(record) {
        var value = pblFirstValue(record, valueKeys || ["id"], pblRecordId(record));
        var label = pblFirstValue(record, labelKeys || ["name"], pblRecordName(record));

        if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") {
            return;
        }

        html += '<option value="' + pblCell(value) + '">' +
            pblCell(label) + "</option>";
    });

    select.innerHTML = html;

    if (previousValue) {
        var exists = Array.from(select.options).some(function(option) {
            return option.value === previousValue;
        });

        if (exists) select.value = previousValue;
    }
}

function populateWeighingSelects() {
    pblPopulateSelect(
        "weighingProduct",
        PBL_APP.data.products,
        "Choisir un produit",
        ["id", "productId", "code"],
        ["name", "productName", "designation", "label"]
    );

    pblPopulateSelect(
        "weighingClient",
        PBL_APP.data.clients,
        "Choisir un client",
        ["id", "clientId", "code"],
        ["name", "clientName", "label"]
    );

    pblPopulateSelect(
        "weighingTruck",
        PBL_APP.data.trucks,
        "Choisir un camion",
        ["id", "truckId", "registration", "truckRegistration"],
        ["registration", "truckRegistration", "name", "label"]
    );

    pblPopulateSelect(
        "weighingDriver",
        PBL_APP.data.drivers,
        "Choisir un chauffeur",
        ["id", "driverId"],
        ["name", "driverName", "label"]
    );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   10. TICKET SEARCH
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function getTicketSearchValue(ticket) {
    return [
        pblFirstValue(ticket, ["ticketNumber", "number"], ""),
        pblFirstValue(ticket, ["product", "productName"], ""),
        pblFirstValue(ticket, ["client", "clientName"], ""),
        pblFirstValue(ticket, ["truckRegistration", "truck"], ""),
        pblFirstValue(ticket, ["driver", "driverName"], ""),
        pblFirstValue(ticket, ["date"], ""),
        pblFirstValue(ticket, ["status"], "")
    ].join(" ").toLowerCase();
}

function searchTickets() {
    var searchInput = pblGetElement("ticketSearchInput");
    var query = searchInput
        ? searchInput.value.trim().toLowerCase()
        : "";

    var results = (PBL_APP.data.tickets || []).filter(function(ticket) {
        return getTicketSearchValue(ticket).includes(query);
    });

    renderTicketSearchResults(results);

    return results;
}

function renderTicketSearchResults(tickets) {
    var html = "";

    if (!tickets.length) {
        html = pblEmptyRow(8, "Aucun ticket correspondant");
    } else {
        tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
            html += "<tr>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFirstValue(
                    ticket, ["ticketNumber", "number"], "—"
                )) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFormatDate(ticket.date)) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFirstValue(
                    ticket, ["product", "productName"], "—"
                )) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFirstValue(
                    ticket, ["client", "clientName"], "—"
                )) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFirstValue(
                    ticket, ["truckRegistration", "truck"], "—"
                )) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFormatKg(
                    pblFirstValue(ticket, ["netKg", "netWeight"], 0)
                )) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblStatusBadge(pblRecordStatus(ticket)) +
                "</td>" +
                "<td><button type='button' class='table-action' " +
                "onclick=\"viewTicketDetails('" +
                pblCell(pblRecordId(ticket)) +
                "')\">VIEW</button></td>" +
                "</tr>";
        });
    }

    pblSetTableBody("ticketsTableBody", html);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   11. CSV EXPORT
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblCsvEscape(value) {
    var text = String(value === undefined || value === null ? "" : value);

    return '"' + text.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
}

function pblDownloadCsv(filename, rows) {
    if (!rows || !rows.length) {
        showToast("Aucune donnée à exporter", "warning");
        return;
    }

    var csv = "\uFEFF" + rows.map(function(row) {
        return row.map(pblCsvEscape).join(";");
    }).join("\r\n");

    var blob = new Blob([csv], {
        type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8;"
    });

    var url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    var link = document.createElement("a");

    link.href = url;
    link.download = filename;

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    showToast("Export CSV préparé", "success");
}

function exportTickets() {
    var tickets = PBL_APP.data.tickets || [];

    var rows = [[
        "N° TICKET",
        "DATE",
        "HEURE",
        "PRODUIT",
        "CLIENT",
        "CAMION",
        "CHAUFFEUR",
        "POIDS 1 KG",
        "POIDS 2 KG",
        "NET KG",
        "NET TONNES",
        "STATUT"
    ]];

    tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
        var netKg = Number(pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["netKg", "netWeight"], 0
        ));

        rows.push([
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["ticketNumber", "number"], ""),
            pblFormatDate(ticket.date),
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["time"], ""),
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["product", "productName"], ""),
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["client", "clientName"], ""),
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["truckRegistration", "truck"], ""),
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["driver", "driverName"], ""),
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["firstWeightKg", "grossKg"], ""),
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["secondWeightKg", "tareKg"], ""),
            netKg,
            (netKg / 1000).toFixed(3),
            pblRecordStatus(ticket)
        ]);
    });

    pblDownloadCsv("PONT_BASCULE_LIVE_TICKETS.csv", rows);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   12. TICKET DETAILS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function viewTicketDetails(ticketId) {
    var ticket = (PBL_APP.data.tickets || []).find(function(item) {
        return pblRecordId(item) === String(ticketId);
    });

    if (!ticket) {
        showToast("Ticket introuvable", "error");
        return;
    }

    PBL_APP.currentTicket = ticket;

    var details = [
        "N° TICKET : " + pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["ticketNumber", "number"], "—"
        ),
        "DATE : " + pblFormatDate(ticket.date),
        "HEURE : " + pblFirstValue(ticket, ["time"], "—"),
        "PRODUIT : " + pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["product", "productName"], "—"
        ),
        "CLIENT : " + pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["client", "clientName"], "—"
        ),
        "CAMION : " + pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["truckRegistration", "truck"], "—"
        ),
        "POIDS NET : " + pblFormatKg(
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["netKg", "netWeight"], 0)
        )
    ];

    window.alert(details.join("\n"));
}

    try {
        await Promise.allSettled([

            loadClients(),
            loadTrucks(),
            loadDrivers(),
            loadObjectives()
        ]);

        populateWeighingSelects();

        showToast("Actualisation terminée", "success");

    } finally {
        hideLoading();
    }
}

        showToast(
            "Déverrouillez le système pour " + actionName,
            "warning"
        );

        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

function pblInitializeDataEngine() {
    if (!PBL_APP.data) {
        PBL_APP.data = {};
    }

    [
        "tickets",
        "products",
        "clients",
        "trucks",
        "drivers",
        "objectives"
    ].forEach(function(key) {
        if (!Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data[key])) {
            PBL_APP.data[key] = [];
        }
    });

    populateWeighingSelects();
}

if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    document.addEventListener(
        "DOMContentLoaded",
        pblInitializeDataEngine
    );
} else {
    pblInitializeDataEngine();
}

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 4
   WEIGHING WORKFLOW ENGINE
   ============================================================ */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   1. WEIGHING STATE
------------------------------------------------------------ */

if (!PBL_APP.weighing) {
    PBL_APP.weighing = {
        sessionId: null,
        firstWeightKg: null,
        secondWeightKg: null,
        netWeightKg: null,
        stage: "IDLE",
        startedAt: null,
        busy: false,
        manualMode: false
    };
}

function pblWeighingState(stage) {
    PBL_APP.weighing.stage = stage;

    pblSetText("weighingStage", stage);
    pblSetText("weighingStatus", stage);
}

function pblWeighingReset() {
    PBL_APP.weighing.sessionId = null;
    PBL_APP.weighing.firstWeightKg = null;
    PBL_APP.weighing.secondWeightKg = null;
    PBL_APP.weighing.netWeightKg = null;
    PBL_APP.weighing.startedAt = null;
    PBL_APP.weighing.busy = false;

    pblWeighingState("IDLE");

    pblSetText("firstWeightDisplay", "0 KG");
    pblSetText("secondWeightDisplay", "0 KG");
    pblSetText("netWeightDisplay", "0 KG");
    pblSetText("grossWeight", "0");
    pblSetText("tareWeight", "0");
    pblSetText("netWeight", "0");

    pblSetText("weighingSessionNumber", "—");
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   2. READ CURRENT SCALE VALUE
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblReadScaleValue(response) {
    if (typeof response === "number") {
        return response;
    }

    if (typeof response === "string") {
        var parsed = Number(response.replace(",", "."));
        return Number.isFinite(parsed) ? parsed : null;
    }

    if (response && typeof response === "object") {
        var candidates = [
            response.weightKg,
            response.weight,
            response.currentWeight,
            response.stableWeight,
            response.value,
            response.data && response.data.weightKg,
            response.data && response.data.weight
        ];

        for (var i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
            if (
                candidates[i] !== undefined &&
                candidates[i] !== null &&
                Number.isFinite(Number(candidates[i]))
            ) {
                return Number(candidates[i]);
            }
        }
    }

    return null;
}

function pblDisplayScaleWeight(weightKg) {
    var weight = Number(weightKg);

    if (!Number.isFinite(weight)) return;

    pblSetText("liveWeight", pblFormatNumber(weight));
    pblSetText("scaleWeight", pblFormatNumber(weight));
    pblSetText("scaleUnit", "KG");
    pblSetText("liveWeightTimestamp", new Date().toLocaleTimeString("fr-FR"));
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   3. SCALE REFRESH
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function refreshScaleDisplay() {
    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "apiPbl16GetLiveWeight",
            "pbl16GetLiveWeight",
            "apiGetLiveScaleWeight",
            "getLiveScaleWeight",
            "getScaleConnectionStatus"
        ], []);

        var weight = pblReadScaleValue(response);

        if (weight !== null) {
            pblDisplayScaleWeight(weight);
        }

        var connected = false;

        if (response && typeof response === "object") {
            connected =
                response.connected === true ||
                response.isConnected === true ||
                response.status === "CONNECTED";
        }

        pblSetText(
            "scaleConnectionStatus",
            connected ? "CONNECTED" : "NOT CONNECTED"
        );

        var statusElement = pblGetElement("scaleConnectionStatus");

        if (statusElement) {
            statusElement.classList.toggle("connected", connected);
            statusElement.classList.toggle("disconnected", !connected);
        }

    } catch (error) {
        console.warn("Scale refresh:", error);

        pblSetText(
            "scaleConnectionStatus",
            "NOT CONNECTED"
        );
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   4. FORM VALIDATION
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblGetWeighingFormData() {
    var product = pblGetElement("weighingProduct");
    var client = pblGetElement("weighingClient");
    var truck = pblGetElement("weighingTruck");
    var driver = pblGetElement("weighingDriver");

    return {
        productId: product ? product.value : "",
        clientId: client ? client.value : "",
        truckId: truck ? truck.value : "",
        driverId: driver ? driver.value : ""
    };
}

function pblValidateWeighingForm() {
    var data = pblGetWeighingFormData();

    if (!data.productId) {
        showToast("Veuillez sélectionner un produit", "warning");
        return false;
    }

    if (!data.clientId) {
        showToast("Veuillez sélectionner un client", "warning");
        return false;
    }

    if (!data.truckId) {
        showToast("Veuillez sélectionner un camion", "warning");
        return false;
    }

    if (!data.driverId) {
        showToast("Veuillez sélectionner un chauffeur", "warning");
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   5. START WEIGHING SESSION
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function startWeighingSession() {
    if (PBL_APP.weighing.busy) return;

    if (!pblValidateWeighingForm()) return;

    PBL_APP.weighing.busy = true;
    showLoading("Création de la session de pesage...");

    try {
        var formData = pblGetWeighingFormData();

        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "pbl23StartWeighingSession",
            "apiPbl23StartWeighingSession",
            "startWeighingSession",
            "startFirstWeighing"
        ], [formData, getSessionToken()]);

        if (!pblSuccess(response)) {
            throw new Error(
                pblErrorMessage(response, "Impossible de démarrer le pesage")
            );
        }

        var data = response.data || response;

        PBL_APP.weighing.sessionId = pblFirstValue(
            data,
            ["sessionId", "id", "weighingId"],
            null
        );

        PBL_APP.weighing.startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
        PBL_APP.weighing.firstWeightKg = null;
        PBL_APP.weighing.secondWeightKg = null;
        PBL_APP.weighing.netWeightKg = null;

        pblWeighingState("WAITING_FIRST_WEIGHT");

        pblSetText(
            "weighingSessionNumber",
            PBL_APP.weighing.sessionId || "ACTIVE"
        );

        showToast("Session de pesage démarrée", "success");

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Start weighing:", error);
        showToast(error.message || "Erreur de démarrage", "error");

    } finally {
        PBL_APP.weighing.busy = false;
        hideLoading();
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   6. CAPTURE FIRST WEIGHT
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function captureFirstWeight() {
    if (PBL_APP.weighing.busy) return;

    if (!PBL_APP.weighing.sessionId) {
        showToast("Démarrez d'abord une session", "warning");
        return;
    }

    if (PBL_APP.weighing.firstWeightKg !== null) {
        showToast("Le premier poids est déjà enregistré", "warning");
        return;
    }

    PBL_APP.weighing.busy = true;
    showLoading("Enregistrement du premier poids...");

    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "pbl23CaptureFirstWeight",
            "apiPbl23CaptureFirstWeight",
            "startFirstWeighing",
            "pbl16CaptureFirstWeight"
        ], [
            PBL_APP.weighing.sessionId,
            getSessionToken()
        ]);

        if (!pblSuccess(response)) {
            throw new Error(
                pblErrorMessage(response, "Premier poids non enregistré")
            );
        }

        var data = response.data || response;

        var weight = pblReadScaleValue(data);

        if (weight === null) {
            weight = pblReadScaleValue(response);
        }

        if (weight === null || weight <= 0) {
            throw new Error(
                "Le serveur n'a pas retourné un poids valide."
            );
        }

        PBL_APP.weighing.firstWeightKg = weight;

        pblSetText("firstWeightDisplay", pblFormatKg(weight));
        pblSetText("grossWeight", pblFormatNumber(weight));

        pblWeighingState("WAITING_SECOND_WEIGHT");

        showToast("Premier poids enregistré", "success");

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("First weight:", error);
        showToast(error.message || "Erreur premier poids", "error");

    } finally {
        PBL_APP.weighing.busy = false;
        hideLoading();
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   7. CAPTURE SECOND WEIGHT
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function captureSecondWeight() {
    if (PBL_APP.weighing.busy) return;

    if (!PBL_APP.weighing.sessionId) {
        showToast("Aucune session active", "warning");
        return;
    }

    if (PBL_APP.weighing.firstWeightKg === null) {
        showToast("Enregistrez d'abord le premier poids", "warning");
        return;
    }

    if (PBL_APP.weighing.secondWeightKg !== null) {
        showToast("Le deuxième poids est déjà enregistré", "warning");
        return;
    }

    PBL_APP.weighing.busy = true;
    showLoading("Enregistrement du deuxième poids...");

    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "pbl23CaptureSecondWeight",
            "apiPbl23CaptureSecondWeight",
            "completeSecondWeighing",
            "pbl16CaptureSecondWeight"
        ], [
            PBL_APP.weighing.sessionId,
            getSessionToken()
        ]);

        if (!pblSuccess(response)) {
            throw new Error(
                pblErrorMessage(response, "Deuxième poids non enregistré")
            );
        }

        var data = response.data || response;
        var weight = pblReadScaleValue(data);

        if (weight === null) {
            weight = pblReadScaleValue(response);
        }

        if (weight === null || weight <= 0) {
            throw new Error(
                "Le serveur n'a pas retourné un poids valide."
            );
        }

        PBL_APP.weighing.secondWeightKg = weight;

        var first = PBL_APP.weighing.firstWeightKg;
        var net = Math.abs(first - weight);

        PBL_APP.weighing.netWeightKg = net;

        pblSetText("secondWeightDisplay", pblFormatKg(weight));
        pblSetText("tareWeight", pblFormatNumber(weight));
        pblSetText("netWeight", pblFormatNumber(net));
        pblSetText("netWeightDisplay", pblFormatKg(net));

        pblWeighingState("READY_TO_FINALIZE");

        showToast(
            "Deuxième poids enregistré — Net : " +
            pblFormatKg(net),
            "success"
        );

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Second weight:", error);
        showToast(error.message || "Erreur deuxième poids", "error");

    } finally {
        PBL_APP.weighing.busy = false;
        hideLoading();
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   8. FINALIZE WEIGHING
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function finalizeWeighing() {
    if (PBL_APP.weighing.busy) return;

    if (!pblRequireUnlocked("finaliser le ticket")) return;

    var state = PBL_APP.weighing;

    if (!state.sessionId) {
        showToast("Aucune session active", "warning");
        return;
    }

    if (
        state.firstWeightKg === null ||
        state.secondWeightKg === null
    ) {
        showToast("Les deux poids sont obligatoires", "warning");
        return;
    }

    var net = Math.abs(
        Number(state.firstWeightKg) -
        Number(state.secondWeightKg)
    );

    if (!Number.isFinite(net) || net <= 0) {
        showToast("Poids net invalide", "error");
        return;
    }

    if (!window.confirm(
        "Confirmer la finalisation du ticket ?\n\n" +
        "Premier poids : " + pblFormatKg(state.firstWeightKg) +
        "\nDeuxième poids : " + pblFormatKg(state.secondWeightKg) +
        "\nPoids net : " + pblFormatKg(net)
    )) {
        return;
    }

    PBL_APP.weighing.busy = true;
    showLoading("Finalisation du ticket...");

    try {
        var payload = {
            sessionId: state.sessionId,
            firstWeightKg: state.firstWeightKg,
            secondWeightKg: state.secondWeightKg,
            netKg: net,
            netTonnes: net / 1000,
            ...pblGetWeighingFormData()
        };

        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "pbl23FinalizeSession",
            "apiPbl23FinalizeSession",
            "finalizeWeighingSession",
            "createTicket"
        ], [
            payload,
            getSessionToken()
        ]);

        if (!pblSuccess(response)) {
            throw new Error(
                pblErrorMessage(response, "Finalisation impossible")
            );
        }

        var result = response.data || response;

        var ticketNumber = pblFirstValue(
            result,
            ["ticketNumber", "number", "numero"],
            "ENREGISTRÉ"
        );

        showToast(
            "Ticket " + ticketNumber + " enregistré",
            "success"
        );

        pblWeighingReset();

        await Promise.allSettled([
            loadTickets(),
            loadDashboard()
        ]);

        showPage("tickets");

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Finalize weighing:", error);
        showToast(error.message || "Erreur de finalisation", "error");

    } finally {
        PBL_APP.weighing.busy = false;
        hideLoading();
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   9. CANCEL WEIGHING SESSION
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function cancelWeighing() {
    if (!PBL_APP.weighing.sessionId) {
        pblWeighingReset();
        return;
    }

    if (!pblRequireUnlocked("annuler la session")) return;

    if (!window.confirm(
        "Voulez-vous vraiment annuler cette session de pesage ?"
    )) {
        return;
    }

    showLoading("Annulation de la session...");

    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "pbl23CancelSession",
            "apiPbl23CancelSession",
            "cancelWeighingSession"
        ], [
            PBL_APP.weighing.sessionId,
            getSessionToken()
        ]);

        if (!pblSuccess(response)) {
            throw new Error(
                pblErrorMessage(response, "Annulation impossible")
            );
        }

        pblWeighingReset();
        showToast("Session annulée", "success");

    } catch (error) {
        showToast(error.message || "Erreur d'annulation", "error");

    } finally {
        hideLoading();
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   10. CLEAR FORM
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function clearWeighingForm() {
    if (PBL_APP.weighing.sessionId) {
        showToast(
            "Annulez d'abord la session active",
            "warning"
        );
        return;
    }

    [
        "weighingProduct",
        "weighingClient",
        "weighingTruck",
        "weighingDriver"
    ].forEach(function(id) {
        var element = pblGetElement(id);
        if (element) element.value = "";
    });

    pblWeighingReset();

    showToast("Formulaire réinitialisé", "info");
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   11. PROTECTED MANUAL WEIGHT MODE
   Manual entry remains hidden by default.
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function pblCheckManualWeighingPermission() {
    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "apiGetSettings",
            "getApplicationSettings",
            "getSystemSettings"
        ], []);

        var data = response && response.data
            ? response.data
            : response;

        var allowed =
            data &&
            (
                data.manualWeighingEnabled === true ||
                (
                    data.weighing &&
                    data.weighing.manualEntryEnabled === true
                )
            );

        PBL_APP.weighing.manualMode = Boolean(allowed);

        var manualPanel = pblGetElement("manualWeightPanel");

        if (manualPanel) {
            manualPanel.hidden = !PBL_APP.weighing.manualMode;
        }

    } catch (error) {
        PBL_APP.weighing.manualMode = false;

        var panel = pblGetElement("manualWeightPanel");
        if (panel) panel.hidden = true;
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   12. WEIGHING SCREEN STATUS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblUpdateWeighingControls() {
    var state = PBL_APP.weighing;

    var firstButton = pblGetElement("firstWeightButton");
    var secondButton = pblGetElement("secondWeightButton");
    var finalizeButton = pblGetElement("finalizeWeighingButton");

    if (firstButton) {
        firstButton.disabled =
            !state.sessionId ||
            state.firstWeightKg !== null ||
            state.busy;
    }

    if (secondButton) {
        secondButton.disabled =
            !state.sessionId ||
            state.firstWeightKg === null ||
            state.secondWeightKg !== null ||
            state.busy;
    }

    if (finalizeButton) {
        finalizeButton.disabled =
            !state.sessionId ||
            state.firstWeightKg === null ||
            state.secondWeightKg === null ||
            state.busy;
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   13. SAFE EVENT REFRESH
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblInstallWeighingRefresh() {
    if (PBL_APP.weighingRefreshInstalled) return;

    PBL_APP.weighingRefreshInstalled = true;

    window.setInterval(function() {
        pblUpdateWeighingControls();
    }, 500);
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    pblWeighingReset();
    pblInstallWeighingRefresh();
    pblCheckManualWeighingPermission();
});

</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 5
   TICKET MANAGEMENT CENTER
   ============================================================ */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   1. TICKET MANAGEMENT STATE
------------------------------------------------------------ */

if (!PBL_APP.ticketManager) {
    PBL_APP.ticketManager = {
        selectedTicketId: null,
        selectedTicket: null,
        history: [],
        filter: "ALL",
        busy: false
    };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   2. FIND TICKET
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblFindTicket(ticketId) {
    return (PBL_APP.data.tickets || []).find(function(ticket) {
        return pblRecordId(ticket) === String(ticketId);
    }) || null;
}

function pblGetTicketNumber(ticket) {
    return pblFirstValue(
        ticket,
        ["ticketNumber", "number", "numero", "nTicket"],
        "—"
    );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   3. OPEN TICKET DETAILS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function openTicketDetails(ticketId) {
    var ticket = pblFindTicket(ticketId);

    if (!ticket) {
        showToast("Ticket introuvable", "error");
        return;
    }

    PBL_APP.ticketManager.selectedTicketId = String(ticketId);
    PBL_APP.ticketManager.selectedTicket = ticket;

    var panel = pblGetElement("ticketDetailsPanel");

    if (!panel) {
        viewTicketDetails(ticketId);
        return;
    }

    panel.hidden = false;

    var fields = {
        "ticketDetailNumber": pblGetTicketNumber(ticket),
        "ticketDetailDate": pblFormatDate(ticket.date),
        "ticketDetailTime": pblFirstValue(ticket, ["time"], "—"),
        "ticketDetailProduct": pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["product", "productName"], "—"
        ),
        "ticketDetailClient": pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["client", "clientName"], "—"
        ),
        "ticketDetailTruck": pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["truckRegistration", "truck"], "—"
        ),
        "ticketDetailDriver": pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["driver", "driverName"], "—"
        ),
        "ticketDetailGross": pblFormatKg(
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["firstWeightKg", "grossKg"], 0)
        ),
        "ticketDetailTare": pblFormatKg(
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["secondWeightKg", "tareKg"], 0)
        ),
        "ticketDetailNet": pblFormatKg(
            pblFirstValue(ticket, ["netKg", "netWeight"], 0)
        ),
        "ticketDetailStatus": pblRecordStatus(ticket)
    };

    Object.keys(fields).forEach(function(id) {
        pblSetText(id, fields[id]);
    });

    var notes = pblGetElement("ticketDetailNotes");

    if (notes) {
        notes.textContent = pblFirstValue(
            ticket,
            ["notes", "comment", "observation"],
            "Aucune observation"
        );
    }
}

function closeTicketDetails() {
    var panel = pblGetElement("ticketDetailsPanel");

    if (panel) panel.hidden = true;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   4. TICKET FILTERING
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function filterTicketsByStatus(status) {
    PBL_APP.ticketManager.filter = status || "ALL";

    var tickets = PBL_APP.data.tickets || [];

    var filtered = tickets.filter(function(ticket) {
        if (status === "ALL") return true;

        return pblRecordStatus(ticket).toUpperCase() ===
            String(status).toUpperCase();
    });

    renderTicketSearchResults(filtered);

    return filtered;
}

function filterTicketsByDateRange() {
    var startElement = pblGetElement("ticketStartDate");
    var endElement = pblGetElement("ticketEndDate");

    var start = startElement ? startElement.value : "";
    var end = endElement ? endElement.value : "";

    var tickets = (PBL_APP.data.tickets || []).filter(function(ticket) {
        var rawDate = String(ticket.date || "");

        var normalized = rawDate;

        if (/^\d{2}\/\d{2}\/\d{4}$/.test(rawDate)) {
            var parts = rawDate.split("/");
            normalized = parts[2] + "-" + parts[1] + "-" + parts[0];
        }

        if (start && normalized < start) return false;
        if (end && normalized > end) return false;

        return true;
    });

    renderTicketSearchResults(tickets);

    return tickets;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   5. EDIT TICKET
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function openTicketEdit(ticketId) {
    if (!pblRequireUnlocked("modifier un ticket")) return;

    var ticket = pblFindTicket(ticketId);

    if (!ticket) {
        showToast("Ticket introuvable", "error");
        return;
    }

    PBL_APP.ticketManager.selectedTicketId = String(ticketId);
    PBL_APP.ticketManager.selectedTicket = ticket;

    var modal = pblGetElement("ticketEditModal");

    if (!modal) {
        showToast(
            "Le formulaire de modification sera ajouté dans une prochaine partie.",
            "info"
        );
        return;
    }

    var values = {
        "editTicketProduct": pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["productId", "product"], ""
        ),
        "editTicketClient": pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["clientId", "client"], ""
        ),
        "editTicketTruck": pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["truckId", "truckRegistration"], ""
        ),
        "editTicketDriver": pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["driverId", "driver"], ""
        ),
        "editTicketNotes": pblFirstValue(
            ticket, ["notes", "comment"], ""
        )
    };

    Object.keys(values).forEach(function(id) {
        var element = pblGetElement(id);

        if (element) element.value = values[id];
    });

    modal.hidden = false;
}

function closeTicketEdit() {
    var modal = pblGetElement("ticketEditModal");

    if (modal) modal.hidden = true;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   6. SAVE TICKET CORRECTIONS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function saveTicketEdit() {
    if (!pblRequireUnlocked("enregistrer les modifications")) return;

    var ticketId = PBL_APP.ticketManager.selectedTicketId;

    if (!ticketId) {
        showToast("Aucun ticket sélectionné", "warning");
        return;
    }

    var ticket = pblFindTicket(ticketId);

    if (!ticket) {
        showToast("Ticket introuvable", "error");
        return;
    }

    if (PBL_APP.ticketManager.busy) return;

    var payload = {
        productId: pblGetElement("editTicketProduct")?.value || "",
        clientId: pblGetElement("editTicketClient")?.value || "",
        truckId: pblGetElement("editTicketTruck")?.value || "",
        driverId: pblGetElement("editTicketDriver")?.value || "",
        notes: pblGetElement("editTicketNotes")?.value || ""
    };

    if (!payload.productId || !payload.clientId) {
        showToast("Produit et client obligatoires", "warning");
        return;
    }

    if (!window.confirm(
        "Confirmer la modification des informations du ticket ?"
    )) return;

    PBL_APP.ticketManager.busy = true;
    showLoading("Enregistrement des corrections...");

    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "pbl24UpdateTicket",
            "apiPbl24UpdateTicket",
            "updateTicketRecord",
            "apiUpdateTicket"
        ], [
            ticketId,
            payload,
            getSessionToken()
        ]);

        if (!pblSuccess(response)) {
            throw new Error(
                pblErrorMessage(response, "Modification impossible")
            );
        }

        showToast("Ticket modifié", "success");

        closeTicketEdit();

        await loadTickets();

        openTicketDetails(ticketId);

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Ticket edit:", error);
        showToast(error.message || "Erreur de modification", "error");

    } finally {
        PBL_APP.ticketManager.busy = false;
        hideLoading();
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   7. CANCEL TICKET
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function cancelTicketFromInterface(ticketId) {
    if (!pblRequireUnlocked("annuler un ticket")) return;

    var ticket = pblFindTicket(ticketId);

    if (!ticket) {
        showToast("Ticket introuvable", "error");
        return;
    }

    if (!window.confirm(
        "ATTENTION\n\nAnnuler le ticket " +
        pblGetTicketNumber(ticket) +
        " ?\n\nCette opération sera enregistrée dans l'historique."
    )) return;

    showLoading("Annulation du ticket...");

    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "pbl24CancelTicket",
            "apiPbl24CancelTicket",
            "cancelTicket",
            "apiCancelTicket"
        ], [
            ticketId,
            getSessionToken()
        ]);

        if (!pblSuccess(response)) {
            throw new Error(
                pblErrorMessage(response, "Annulation impossible")
            );
        }

        showToast("Ticket annulé", "success");

        closeTicketDetails();

        await Promise.allSettled([
            loadTickets(),
            loadDashboard()
        ]);

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Cancel ticket:", error);
        showToast(error.message || "Erreur d'annulation", "error");

    } finally {
        hideLoading();
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   8. RESTORE CANCELLED TICKET
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function restoreTicketFromInterface(ticketId) {
    if (!pblRequireUnlocked("restaurer un ticket")) return;

    var ticket = pblFindTicket(ticketId);

    if (!ticket) {
        showToast("Ticket introuvable", "error");
        return;
    }

    if (!window.confirm(
        "Restaurer le ticket " + pblGetTicketNumber(ticket) + " ?"
    )) return;

    showLoading("Restauration du ticket...");

    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "pbl24RestoreTicket",
            "apiPbl24RestoreTicket",
            "restoreCancelledTicket",
            "apiRestoreTicket"
        ], [
            ticketId,
            getSessionToken()
        ]);

        if (!pblSuccess(response)) {
            throw new Error(
                pblErrorMessage(response, "Restauration impossible")
            );
        }

        showToast("Ticket restauré", "success");

        await Promise.allSettled([
            loadTickets(),
            loadDashboard()
        ]);

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Restore ticket:", error);
        showToast(error.message || "Erreur de restauration", "error");

    } finally {
        hideLoading();
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   9. PRINT TICKET
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function printTicketFromInterface(ticketId) {
    var ticket = pblFindTicket(ticketId);

    if (!ticket) {
        showToast("Ticket introuvable", "error");
        return;
    }

    var ticketNumber = pblGetTicketNumber(ticket);

    var gross = Number(pblFirstValue(
        ticket, ["firstWeightKg", "grossKg"], 0
    ));

    var tare = Number(pblFirstValue(
        ticket, ["secondWeightKg", "tareKg"], 0
    ));

    var net = Number(pblFirstValue(
        ticket, ["netKg", "netWeight"], Math.abs(gross - tare)
    ));

    var company = pblEscapeHTML(
        PBL_APP.companyName || "PONT BASCULE LIVE"
    );

    var content = `
        <!DOCTYPE html>
        <html lang="fr">
        <head>
            <meta charset="UTF-8">
            <title>Ticket ${pblCell(ticketNumber)}</title>
            <style>
                body {
