                    font-family: Arial, sans-serif;
                    margin: 25px;
                    color: #111;
                }
                .ticket {
                    max-width: 650px;
                    margin: auto;
                    border: 1px solid #222;
                    padding: 22px;
                }
                h1 {
                    text-align: center;
                    font-size: 22px;
                }
                h2 {
                    text-align: center;
                    font-size: 17px;
                }
                .line {
                    display: flex;
                    justify-content: space-between;
                    padding: 8px 0;
                    border-bottom: 1px dotted #aaa;
                }
                .net {
                    font-size: 25px;
                    font-weight: bold;
                    padding: 18px 0;
                    text-align: center;
                }
                .footer {
                    margin-top: 30px;
                    text-align: center;
                    font-size: 11px;
                }
                @media print {
                    body { margin: 0; }
                    .ticket { border: 1px solid #000; }
                }
            </style>
        </head>
        <body>
            <div class="ticket">
                <h1>${company}</h1>
                <h2>BON DE PESAGE</h2>
                <div class="line">
                    <strong>Ticket</strong>
                    <span>${pblCell(ticketNumber)}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="line">
                    <strong>Date</strong>
                    <span>${pblCell(pblFormatDate(ticket.date))}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="line">
                    <strong>Heure</strong>
                    <span>${pblCell(ticket.time || "—")}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="line">
                    <strong>Produit</strong>
                    <span>${pblCell(ticket.product || ticket.productName || "—")}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="line">
                    <strong>Client</strong>
                    <span>${pblCell(ticket.client || ticket.clientName || "—")}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="line">
                    <strong>Camion</strong>
                    <span>${pblCell(ticket.truckRegistration || ticket.truck || "—")}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="line">
                    <strong>Chauffeur</strong>
                    <span>${pblCell(ticket.driver || ticket.driverName || "—")}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="line">
                    <strong>Premier poids</strong>
                    <span>${pblCell(pblFormatKg(gross))}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="line">
                    <strong>Deuxième poids</strong>
                    <span>${pblCell(pblFormatKg(tare))}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="net">
                    POIDS NET : ${pblCell(pblFormatKg(net))}
                </div>
                <div class="footer">
                    Document imprimé depuis PONT BASCULE LIVE
                </div>
            </div>
            <script>
                window.onload = function() {
                    window.print();
                };
            <\/script>
        </body>
        </html>
    `;

    var printWindow = window.open("", "_blank");

    if (!printWindow) {
        showToast("Autorisez les fenêtres pop-up pour imprimer", "warning");
        return;
    }

    printWindow.document.open();
    printWindow.document.write(content);
    printWindow.document.close();
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   10. LOAD TICKET HISTORY
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function loadTicketHistory(ticketId) {
    if (!ticketId) {
        showToast("Ticket non sélectionné", "warning");
        return [];
    }

    try {
        var response = await pblCallFirst([
            "pbl24GetTicketHistory",
            "apiPbl24GetTicketHistory",
            "getTicketHistory",
            "apiGetTicketHistory"
        ], [
            ticketId,
            getSessionToken()
        ]);

        var data = response && response.data !== undefined
            ? response.data
            : response;

        var history = pblGetCollection(
            data,
            ["history", "logs", "events", "items", "records"]
        );

        PBL_APP.ticketManager.history = history;

        renderTicketHistory(history);

        return history;

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Ticket history:", error);
        showToast("Historique indisponible", "error");
        return [];
    }
}

function renderTicketHistory(history) {
    var container = pblGetElement("ticketHistoryContainer");

    if (!container) return;

    if (!history || !history.length) {
        container.innerHTML =
            '<div class="pbl-empty-row">Aucun historique disponible</div>';
        return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = history.map(function(item) {
        var action = pblFirstValue(
            item,
            ["action", "event", "type"],
            "ACTION"
        );

        var date = pblFirstValue(
            item,
            ["date", "timestamp", "createdAt"],
            "—"
        );

        var user = pblFirstValue(
            item,
            ["user", "username", "actor"],
            "SYSTEM"
        );

        var description = pblFirstValue(
            item,
            ["description", "message", "details"],
            ""
        );

        return `
            <div class="ticket-history-item">
                <div class="ticket-history-heading">
                    <strong>${pblCell(action)}</strong>
                    <span>${pblCell(date)}</span>
                </div>
                <div>${pblCell(description)}</div>
                <small>${pblCell(user)}</small>
            </div>
        `;
    }).join("");
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   11. REPRINT SELECTED TICKET
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function reprintSelectedTicket() {
    var ticketId = PBL_APP.ticketManager.selectedTicketId;

    if (!ticketId) {
        showToast("Sélectionnez un ticket", "warning");
        return;
    }

    printTicketFromInterface(ticketId);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   12. TICKET ACTION ROUTER
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblTicketAction(action, ticketId) {
    switch (action) {
        case "view":
            return openTicketDetails(ticketId);

        case "edit":
            return openTicketEdit(ticketId);

        case "cancel":
            return cancelTicketFromInterface(ticketId);

        case "restore":
            return restoreTicketFromInterface(ticketId);

        case "print":
            return printTicketFromInterface(ticketId);

        case "history":
            return loadTicketHistory(ticketId);

        default:
            showToast("Action inconnue", "error");
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   13. EXTEND EXISTING TICKET TABLE ACTION
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function renderTicketManagementTable(tickets) {
    var html = "";

    if (!tickets || !tickets.length) {
        html = pblEmptyRow(8, "Aucun ticket disponible");
    } else {
        tickets.forEach(function(ticket) {
            var id = pblRecordId(ticket);
            var status = pblRecordStatus(ticket).toUpperCase();

            var actions =
                '<button class="table-action" type="button" ' +
                'onclick="pblTicketAction(\'view\',\'' +
                pblCell(id) + '\')">VIEW</button> ' +
                '<button class="table-action" type="button" ' +
                'onclick="pblTicketAction(\'print\',\'' +
                pblCell(id) + '\')">PRINT</button>';

            if (PBL_APP.unlocked) {
                if (status === "CANCELLED" || status === "CANCELED") {
                    actions +=
                        '<button class="table-action" type="button" ' +
                        'onclick="pblTicketAction(\'restore\',\'' +
                        pblCell(id) + '\')">RESTORE</button>';
                } else {
                    actions +=
                        '<button class="table-action" type="button" ' +
                        'onclick="pblTicketAction(\'edit\',\'' +
                        pblCell(id) + '\')">EDIT</button> ' +
                        '<button class="table-action" type="button" ' +
                        'onclick="pblTicketAction(\'cancel\',\'' +
                        pblCell(id) + '\')">CANCEL</button>';
                }
            }

            html += "<tr>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblGetTicketNumber(ticket)) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFormatDate(ticket.date)) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFirstValue(
                    ticket, ["product", "productName"], "—"
                )) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFirstValue(
                    ticket, ["client", "clientName"], "—"
                )) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFirstValue(
                    ticket, ["truckRegistration", "truck"], "—"
                )) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblCell(pblFormatKg(
                    pblFirstValue(ticket, ["netKg", "netWeight"], 0)
                )) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + pblStatusBadge(status) + "</td>" +
                "<td>" + actions + "</td>" +
                "</tr>";
        });
    }

    pblSetTableBody("ticketsTableBody", html);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   14. INITIALIZE TICKET MANAGEMENT
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblInitializeTicketManager() {
    if (!PBL_APP.ticketManager) {
        PBL_APP.ticketManager = {
            selectedTicketId: null,
            selectedTicket: null,
            history: [],
            filter: "ALL",
            busy: false
        };
    }
}

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    pblInitializeTicketManager();
});

</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 6
   MASTER DATA MANAGEMENT
   PRODUCTS / CLIENTS / TRUCKS / DRIVERS
   ============================================================ */

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   1. MASTER DATA STATE
------------------------------------------------------------ */

if (!PBL_APP.masterData) {
    PBL_APP.masterData = {
        type: null,
        recordId: null,
        mode: "CREATE",
        busy: false
    };
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   2. MASTER DATA FIELD HELPERS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblMasterElement(id) {
    return pblGetElement(id);
}

function pblMasterValue(id) {
    var element = pblMasterElement(id);
    return element ? String(element.value || "").trim() : "";
}

function pblMasterSetValue(id, value) {
    var element = pblMasterElement(id);

    if (element) {
        element.value = value === undefined || value === null
            ? ""
            : value;
    }
}

function pblMasterClearFields(ids) {
    ids.forEach(function(id) {
        pblMasterSetValue(id, "");
    });
}

function pblMasterRequired(value, label) {
    if (!value) {
        showToast(label + " est obligatoire", "warning");
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

function pblMasterFind(collection, id) {
    return (collection || []).find(function(record) {
        return pblRecordId(record) === String(id);
    }) || null;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   3. MASTER DATA MODAL
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblOpenMasterModal(type, mode, recordId) {
    if (!pblRequireUnlocked("gérer les données")) return;

    var modal = pblGetElement("masterDataModal");

    if (!modal) {
        showToast(
            "Le conteneur du formulaire sera ajouté dans une prochaine partie.",
            "info"
        );
        return;
    }

    PBL_APP.masterData.type = type;
    PBL_APP.masterData.mode = mode || "CREATE";
    PBL_APP.masterData.recordId = recordId || null;

    var title = pblGetElement("masterDataModalTitle");

    if (title) {
        title.textContent =
            (mode === "EDIT" ? "MODIFIER " : "NOUVEAU ") +
            String(type).toUpperCase();
    }

    var form = pblGetElement("masterDataForm");

    if (form) form.reset();

    var record = null;

    if (mode === "EDIT") {
        var collection = PBL_APP.data[type] || [];
        record = pblMasterFind(collection, recordId);

        if (!record) {
            showToast("Enregistrement introuvable", "error");
            return;
        }
    }

    pblBuildMasterFields(type, record);

    modal.hidden = false;
}

function pblCloseMasterModal() {
    var modal = pblGetElement("masterDataModal");

    if (modal) modal.hidden = true;

    PBL_APP.masterData.type = null;
    PBL_APP.masterData.recordId = null;
    PBL_APP.masterData.mode = "CREATE";
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   4. DYNAMIC FORM BUILDER
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblBuildMasterFields(type, record) {
    var container = pblGetElement("masterDataFields");

    if (!container) return;

    var schemas = {
        products: [
            ["code", "CODE PRODUIT", "text"],
            ["name", "DÉSIGNATION", "text"],
            ["unit", "UNITÉ", "text"],
            ["price", "PRIX UNITAIRE", "number"]
        ],

        clients: [
            ["code", "CODE CLIENT", "text"],
            ["name", "NOM DU CLIENT", "text"],
            ["phone", "TÉLÉPHONE", "tel"],
            ["address", "ADRESSE", "text"],
            ["email", "EMAIL", "email"]
        ],

        trucks: [
            ["registration", "IMMATRICULATION", "text"],
            ["name", "NOM / IDENTIFICATION", "text"],
            ["type", "TYPE DE CAMION", "text"],
            ["capacity", "CAPACITÉ KG", "number"]
        ],

        drivers: [
            ["name", "NOM DU CHAUFFEUR", "text"],
            ["phone", "TÉLÉPHONE", "tel"],
            ["license", "NUMÉRO DE PERMIS", "text"],
            ["truckRegistration", "IMMATRICULATION CAMION", "text"]
        ]
    };

    var fields = schemas[type];

    if (!fields) {
        container.innerHTML = "";
        return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = fields.map(function(field) {
        var key = field[0];
        var label = field[1];
        var inputType = field[2];

        var value = record
            ? pblFirstValue(record, [key], "")
            : "";

        return `
            <label class="master-field">
                <span>${pblCell(label)}</span>
                <input
                    type="${pblCell(inputType)}"
                    id="master_${pblCell(key)}"
                    name="${pblCell(key)}"
                    value="${pblCell(value)}"
                    ${inputType === "number" ? 'min="0" step="any"' : ""}
                    autocomplete="off"
                >
            </label>
        `;
    }).join("");
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   5. READ FORM DATA
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblReadMasterForm(type) {
    var schemas = {
        products: ["code", "name", "unit", "price"],
        clients: ["code", "name", "phone", "address", "email"],
        trucks: ["registration", "name", "type", "capacity"],
        drivers: ["name", "phone", "license", "truckRegistration"]
    };

    var keys = schemas[type];

    if (!keys) {
        throw new Error("Type de données inconnu");
    }

    var result = {};

    keys.forEach(function(key) {
        var value = pblMasterValue("master_" + key);

        if (key === "price" || key === "capacity") {
            value = value === "" ? 0 : Number(value);

            if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 0) {
                throw new Error("Valeur numérique invalide : " + key);
            }
        }

        result[key] = value;
    });

    return result;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   6. VALIDATE MASTER DATA
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblValidateMasterData(type, data) {
    if (type === "products") {
        return pblMasterRequired(data.name, "Désignation");
    }

    if (type === "clients") {
        return pblMasterRequired(data.name, "Nom du client");
    }

    if (type === "trucks") {
        return pblMasterRequired(
            data.registration,
            "Immatriculation"
        );
    }

    if (type === "drivers") {
        return pblMasterRequired(
            data.name,
            "Nom du chauffeur"
        );
    }

    return false;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   7. SAVE MASTER DATA
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function saveMasterData() {
    if (!pblRequireUnlocked("enregistrer les données")) return;

    if (PBL_APP.masterData.busy) return;

    var type = PBL_APP.masterData.type;
    var mode = PBL_APP.masterData.mode;
    var recordId = PBL_APP.masterData.recordId;

    if (!type) {
        showToast("Aucun formulaire actif", "warning");
        return;
    }

    var payload;

    try {
        payload = pblReadMasterForm(type);
    } catch (error) {
        showToast(error.message, "warning");
        return;
    }

    if (!pblValidateMasterData(type, payload)) return;

    PBL_APP.masterData.busy = true;
    showLoading("Enregistrement...");

    var endpointMap = {
        products: {
            create: [
                "apiCreateProduct",
                "createProduct",
                "pbl25CreateProduct"
            ],
            update: [
                "apiUpdateProduct",
                "updateProduct",
                "pbl25UpdateProduct"
            ]
        },

        clients: {
            create: [
                "apiCreateClient",
                "createClient"
            ],
            update: [
                "apiUpdateClient",
                "updateClient"
            ]
        },

        trucks: {
            create: [
                "apiCreateTruck",
                "createTruck"
            ],
            update: [
                "apiUpdateTruck",
                "updateTruck"
            ]
        },

        drivers: {
            create: [
                "apiCreateDriver",
                "createDriver"
            ],
            update: [
                "apiUpdateDriver",
                "updateDriver"
            ]
        }
    };

    try {
        var endpoint = endpointMap[type][
            mode === "EDIT" ? "update" : "create"
        ];

        var args = mode === "EDIT"
            ? [recordId, payload, getSessionToken()]
            : [payload, getSessionToken()];

        var response = await pblCallFirst(endpoint, args);

        if (!pblSuccess(response)) {
            throw new Error(
                pblErrorMessage(response, "Enregistrement refusé")
            );
        }

        showToast(
            mode === "EDIT"
                ? "Modification enregistrée"
                : "Nouvel enregistrement créé",
            "success"
        );

        pblCloseMasterModal();

        await pblReloadMasterCollection(type);

    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Master data save:", error);
        showToast(error.message || "Erreur d'enregistrement", "error");

    } finally {
        PBL_APP.masterData.busy = false;
        hideLoading();
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   8. RELOAD COLLECTION
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function pblReloadMasterCollection(type) {
    var reloadMap = {
        products: loadProducts,
        clients: loadClients,
        trucks: loadTrucks,
        drivers: loadDrivers
    };

    if (reloadMap[type]) {
        await reloadMap[type]();
    }

    populateWeighingSelects();
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   9. OPEN CREATE FORMS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function openProductForm() {
    pblOpenMasterModal("products", "CREATE");
}

function openClientForm() {
    pblOpenMasterModal("clients", "CREATE");
}

function openTruckForm() {
    pblOpenMasterModal("trucks", "CREATE");
}

function openDriverForm() {
    pblOpenMasterModal("drivers", "CREATE");
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   10. OPEN EDIT FORMS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function editProduct(productId) {
    pblOpenMasterModal("products", "EDIT", productId);
}

function editClient(clientId) {
    pblOpenMasterModal("clients", "EDIT", clientId);
}

function editTruck(truckId) {
    pblOpenMasterModal("trucks", "EDIT", truckId);
}

function editDriver(driverId) {
    pblOpenMasterModal("drivers", "EDIT", driverId);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   11. SEARCH MASTER DATA
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblSearchCollection(type, query) {
    var collection = PBL_APP.data[type] || [];
    var search = String(query || "").trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!search) return collection;

    return collection.filter(function(record) {
        return Object.keys(record).some(function(key) {
            var value = record[key];

            return (
                value !== null &&
                value !== undefined &&
                String(value).toLowerCase().includes(search)
            );
        });
    });
}

function pblInstallMasterSearch(inputId, type, renderFunction) {
    var input = pblGetElement(inputId);

    if (!input || input.dataset.pblSearchInstalled) return;

    input.dataset.pblSearchInstalled = "true";

    input.addEventListener("input", function() {
        var results = pblSearchCollection(type, input.value);
        renderFunction(results);
    });
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   12. EXTENDED TABLE RENDERING
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblRenderMasterTable(type, records) {
    var definitions = {
        products: {
            target: "productsTableBody",
            columns: ["code", "name", "unit", "price"]
        },
        clients: {
            target: "clientsTableBody",
            columns: ["code", "name", "phone", "address"]
        },
        trucks: {
            target: "trucksTableBody",
            columns: ["registration", "name", "type", "capacity"]
        },
        drivers: {
            target: "driversTableBody",
            columns: ["name", "phone", "license", "truckRegistration"]
        }
    };

    var definition = definitions[type];

    if (!definition) return;

    var html = "";

    if (!records || !records.length) {
        html = pblEmptyRow(
            definition.columns.length + 2,
            "Aucune donnée disponible"
        );
    } else {
        records.forEach(function(record) {
            var id = pblRecordId(record);

            var cells = definition.columns.map(function(key) {
                var value = pblFirstValue(record, [key], "—");

                if (key === "price" || key === "capacity") {
                    value = pblFormatNumber(value);
                }

                return "<td>" + pblCell(value) + "</td>";
            }).join("");

            var editButton = "";

            if (PBL_APP.unlocked) {
                editButton =
                    '<button type="button" class="table-action" ' +
                    'onclick="pblEditMasterRecord(\'' +
                    pblCell(type) + "','" +
                    pblCell(id) +
                    '\')">EDIT</button>';
            }

            html += "<tr>" +
                cells +
                "<td>" + pblStatusBadge(pblRecordStatus(record)) +
                "</td>" +
                "<td>" + editButton + "</td>" +
                "</tr>";
        });
    }

    pblSetTableBody(definition.target, html);
}

function pblEditMasterRecord(type, id) {
    var editors = {
        products: editProduct,
        clients: editClient,
        trucks: editTruck,
        drivers: editDriver
    };

    if (editors[type]) {
        editors[type](id);
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   13. REPLACE STANDARD RENDERERS
------------------------------------------------------------ */

var pblOriginalRenderProducts = renderProducts;
var pblOriginalRenderClients = renderClients;
var pblOriginalRenderTrucks = renderTrucks;
var pblOriginalRenderDrivers = renderDrivers;

renderProducts = function(records) {
    pblRenderMasterTable(
        "products",
        records || PBL_APP.data.products || []
    );

    pblSetText(
        "productsCount",
        (PBL_APP.data.products || []).length
    );
};

renderClients = function(records) {
    pblRenderMasterTable(
        "clients",
        records || PBL_APP.data.clients || []
    );

    pblSetText(
        "clientsCount",
        (PBL_APP.data.clients || []).length
    );
};

renderTrucks = function(records) {
    pblRenderMasterTable(
        "trucks",
        records || PBL_APP.data.trucks || []
    );

    pblSetText(
        "trucksCount",
        (PBL_APP.data.trucks || []).length
    );
};

renderDrivers = function(records) {
    pblRenderMasterTable(
        "drivers",
        records || PBL_APP.data.drivers || []
    );

    pblSetText(
        "driversCount",
        (PBL_APP.data.drivers || []).length
    );
};

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   14. QUICK SEARCH INITIALIZATION
------------------------------------------------------------ */

function pblInitializeMasterSearch() {
    pblInstallMasterSearch(
        "productSearchInput",
        "products",
        renderProducts
    );

    pblInstallMasterSearch(
        "clientSearchInput",
        "clients",
        renderClients
    );

    pblInstallMasterSearch(
        "truckSearchInput",
        "trucks",
        renderTrucks
    );

    pblInstallMasterSearch(
        "driverSearchInput",
        "drivers",
        renderDrivers
    );
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   15. MASTER DATA REFRESH
------------------------------------------------------------ */

async function refreshMasterData() {
    showLoading("Actualisation des données...");

    try {
        await Promise.allSettled([
            loadProducts(),
            loadClients(),
            loadTrucks(),
            loadDrivers()
        ]);

        pblInitializeMasterSearch();
        populateWeighingSelects();

        showToast("Données actualisées", "success");

    } finally {
        hideLoading();
    }
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------
   16. INITIALIZATION
------------------------------------------------------------ */

document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", function() {
    pblInitializeMasterSearch();
});

</script>
<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 7
   OBJECTIVES & DELIVERY CONTROL CENTER
   ============================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     1. OBJECTIVE MODULE STATE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};
  PBL_APP.data = PBL_APP.data || {};
  PBL_APP.data.objectives = PBL_APP.data.objectives || [];

  PBL_APP.objectiveCenter = PBL_APP.objectiveCenter || {
    currentId: null,
    loading: false,
    lastDashboard: null,
    filter: 'ALL'
  };

  const PBL_OBJECTIVE_API = {
    list: [
      'apiGetObjectives',
      'getObjectives',
      'pbl17GetObjectives',
      'apiPbl17GetObjectives'
    ],
    create: [
      'apiCreateObjective',
      'createObjective',
      'pbl17CreateObjective',
      'apiPbl17CreateObjective'
    ],
    update: [
      'apiUpdateObjective',
      'updateObjective',
      'pbl17UpdateObjective',
      'apiPbl17UpdateObjective'
    ],
    archive: [
      'apiArchiveObjective',
      'archiveObjective',
      'pbl17ArchiveObjective'
    ],
    restore: [
      'apiRestoreObjective',
      'restoreObjective',
      'pbl17RestoreObjective'
    ]
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     2. SAFE HELPERS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function objEl(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function objText(id, value) {
    const el = objEl(id);
    if (el) el.textContent = value == null ? '' : String(value);
  }

  function objNumber(value) {
    const n = Number(String(value == null ? 0 : value).replace(',', '.'));
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  }

  function objRound(value, decimals) {
    const factor = Math.pow(10, decimals || 2);
    return Math.round((objNumber(value) + Number.EPSILON) * factor) / factor;
  }

  function objFormat(value, decimals) {
    return objRound(value, decimals == null ? 2 : decimals)
      .toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
        minimumFractionDigits: decimals == null ? 2 : decimals,
        maximumFractionDigits: decimals == null ? 2 : decimals
      });
  }

  function objEscape(value) {
    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(value == null ? '' : String(value));
    }

    return String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
  }

  function objFirst(record, keys, fallback) {
    record = record || {};

    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const value = record[keys[i]];
      if (value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== '') {
        return value;
      }
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  function objId(record) {
    return String(objFirst(record, [
      'id',
      'objectiveId',
      'objectiveID',
      'recordId',
      'uuid'
    ], ''));
  }

  function objName(record) {
    return String(objFirst(record, [
      'name',
      'objectiveName',
      'title',
      'label'
    ], 'Objectif sans nom'));
  }

  function objStatus(record) {
    return String(objFirst(record, [
      'status',
      'state',
      'objectiveStatus'
    ], 'ACTIVE')).toUpperCase();
  }

  function objIsActive(record) {
    const status = objStatus(record);
    return ![
      'ARCHIVED',
      'DELETED',
      'CANCELLED',
      'CANCELED',
      'INACTIVE'
    ].includes(status);
  }

  function objGetTargetTonnes(record) {
    record = record || {};

    /*
     * Explicit KG fields are converted to tonnes.
     * Explicit tonne fields are used directly.
     * Generic targetQuantity is treated as tonnes,
     * matching the dashboard convention in this project.
     */
    const kg = objFirst(record, [
      'targetKg',
      'targetKG',
      'targetQuantityKg',
      'target_kg'
    ], null);

    if (kg !== null) return objNumber(kg) / 1000;

    const tonnes = objFirst(record, [
      'targetTonnes',
      'targetTons',
      'target_tonnes',
      'targetQuantityTonnes'
    ], null);

    if (tonnes !== null) return objNumber(tonnes);

    return objNumber(objFirst(record, [
      'targetQuantity',
      'target',
      'quantity'
    ], 0));
  }

  function objGetCumulativeTonnes(data) {
    data = data || {};

    const explicitTonnes = objFirst(data, [
      'cumulTonnes',
      'cumulativeTonnes',
      'totalTonnes',
      'deliveredTonnes'
    ], null);

    if (explicitTonnes !== null) return objNumber(explicitTonnes);

    const kg = objFirst(data, [
      'cumulKg',
      'cumulativeKg',
      'totalKg',
      'deliveredKg'
    ], null);

    return kg === null ? 0 : objNumber(kg) / 1000;
  }

  function objGetVoyageCount(data) {
    return Math.max(0, Math.floor(objNumber(objFirst(data || {}, [
      'numberOfVoyages',
      'voyageCount',
      'totalVoyages',
      'nbVoyages',
      'numberOfTickets'
    ], 0))));
  }

  function objGetRemaining(data, target, cumulative) {
    const explicit = objFirst(data || {}, [
      'remainingTonnes',
      'restToDeliver',
      'remainingQuantity',
      'restantTonnes'
    ], null);

    if (explicit !== null) return objNumber(explicit);

    return target - cumulative;
  }

  function objNotify(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
      return;
    }

    if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast(message, type || 'info');
      return;
    }

    console.log('[PONT BASCULE LIVE]', message);
  }

  function objHasEditAccess() {
    if (typeof PBL_APP !== 'undefined' && PBL_APP.access) {
      if (
        PBL_APP.access.unlocked === true ||
        PBL_APP.access.isUnlocked === true ||
        PBL_APP.access.authorized === true
      ) {
        return true;
      }
    }

    if (typeof PBL_APP !== 'undefined' && PBL_APP.isUnlocked === true) {
      return true;
    }

    const button = objEl('unlockButton');
    if (button && button.dataset && button.dataset.unlocked === 'true') {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  }

  function objCallApi(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(new Error(
        'Le moteur de communication avec le serveur est indisponible.'
      ));
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     3. OBJECTIVE DATA LOADING
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.loadObjectives = async function () {
    if (PBL_APP.objectiveCenter.loading) return;

    PBL_APP.objectiveCenter.loading = true;

    try {
      const result = await objCallApi(PBL_OBJECTIVE_API.list, []);

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Impossible de charger les objectifs.');
      }

      let records = [];

      if (Array.isArray(result)) {
        records = result;
      } else if (result && Array.isArray(result.objectives)) {
        records = result.objectives;
      } else if (result && Array.isArray(result.data)) {
        records = result.data;
      } else if (result && result.result && Array.isArray(result.result.objectives)) {
        records = result.result.objectives;
      } else {
        records = PBL_APP.data.objectives || [];
      }

      PBL_APP.data.objectives = records;

      renderObjectives();
      objUpdateObjectiveSelector();

      return records;

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('PBL objective loading error:', error);

      if (typeof PBL_APP.data.objectives === 'undefined') {
        PBL_APP.data.objectives = [];
      }

      renderObjectives();

      objNotify(
        'Chargement des objectifs impossible : ' + error.message,
        'error'
      );

      return PBL_APP.data.objectives;

    } finally {
      PBL_APP.objectiveCenter.loading = false;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     4. OBJECTIVE TABLE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.renderObjectives = function () {
    const records = PBL_APP.data.objectives || [];

    const body =
      objEl('objectivesTableBody') ||
      objEl('objectivesBody') ||
      objEl('objectivesTable');

    if (!body) return;

    const filter = PBL_APP.objectiveCenter.filter || 'ALL';

    const visible = records.filter(function (record) {
      if (filter === 'ACTIVE') return objIsActive(record);
      if (filter === 'ARCHIVED') return !objIsActive(record);
      return true;
    });

    if (!visible.length) {
      body.innerHTML =
        '<tr><td colspan="8" class="empty-state">' +
        'Aucun objectif enregistré.' +
        '</td></tr>';
      return;
    }

    body.innerHTML = visible.map(function (record) {
      const id = objId(record);
      const target = objGetTargetTonnes(record);
      const status = objStatus(record);
      const active = objIsActive(record);

      const start = objFirst(record, [
        'startDate',
        'dateStart',
        'beginDate'
      ], '—');

      const end = objFirst(record, [
        'endDate',
        'dateEnd',
        'deadline'
      ], '—');

      const product = objFirst(record, [
        'product',
        'productName'
      ], 'Tous produits');

      const client = objFirst(record, [
        'client',
        'clientName'
      ], 'Tous clients');

      const statusClass = active ? 'status-active' : 'status-archived';

      return `
        <tr>
          <td>${objEscape(objName(record))}</td>
          <td>${objEscape(product)}</td>
          <td>${objEscape(client)}</td>
          <td class="numeric-cell">${objFormat(target)} T</td>
          <td>${objEscape(start)}</td>
          <td>${objEscape(end)}</td>
          <td>
            <span class="${statusClass}">
              ${objEscape(status)}
            </span>
          </td>
          <td class="table-actions">
            <button type="button"
              class="btn-small"
              onclick="pblEditObjective('${objEscape(id)}')">
              Modifier
            </button>
            ${
              active
                ? `<button type="button"
                    class="btn-small btn-warning"
                    onclick="pblArchiveObjective('${objEscape(id)}')">
                    Archiver
                  </button>`
                : `<button type="button"
                    class="btn-small"
                    onclick="pblRestoreObjective('${objEscape(id)}')">
                    Restaurer
                  </button>`
            }
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }).join('');
  };

  window.pblFilterObjectives = function (filter) {
    PBL_APP.objectiveCenter.filter = filter || 'ALL';
    renderObjectives();
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     5. DASHBOARD DELIVERY INDICATORS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblRenderDeliveryDashboard = function (data) {
    data = data || {};

    const target = objNumber(objFirst(data, [
      'targetQuantity',
      'targetTonnes',
      'target'
    ], 0));

    const cumulative = objGetCumulativeTonnes(data);
    const voyages = objGetVoyageCount(data);
    const remaining = objGetRemaining(data, target, cumulative);

    let percentage = objFirst(data, [
      'deliveryPercentage',
      'progressBarPercentage',
      'progressPercentage'
    ], null);

    if (percentage === null) {
      percentage = target > 0 ? (cumulative / target) * 100 : 0;
    }

    percentage = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, objNumber(percentage)));

    /*
     * These are the four primary dashboard indicators:
     * TARGET QUANTITY / CUMUL TONNES / NB VOYAGES / REST À LIVRER
     */

    objText('targetQuantity', objFormat(target) + ' T');
    objText('cumulTonnes', objFormat(cumulative) + ' T');
    objText('numberOfVoyages', String(voyages));
    objText('remainingTonnes', objFormat(remaining) + ' T');
    objText('deliveryPercentage', objFormat(percentage, 1) + '%');

    const progress = objEl('deliveryProgressBar');

    if (progress) {
      progress.style.width = percentage + '%';
      progress.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', String(percentage));
      progress.setAttribute('aria-valuemin', '0');
      progress.setAttribute('aria-valuemax', '100');
    }

    const progressText = objEl('deliveryProgressText');
    if (progressText) {
      progressText.textContent =
        objFormat(cumulative) + ' T / ' + objFormat(target) + ' T';
    }

    const remainingElement = objEl('remainingTonnes');

    if (remainingElement) {
      remainingElement.classList.toggle('delivery-complete', remaining <= 0 && target > 0);
      remainingElement.classList.toggle('delivery-pending', remaining > 0);
    }

    const objectiveName = objFirst(data, [
      'objectiveName',
      'activeObjectiveName',
      'name'
    ], '');

    if (objectiveName) {
      objText('dashboardObjectiveName', objectiveName);
    }

    PBL_APP.objectiveCenter.lastDashboard = {
      targetQuantity: target,
      cumulTonnes: cumulative,
      numberOfVoyages: voyages,
      remainingTonnes: remaining,
      deliveryPercentage: percentage
    };

    return PBL_APP.objectiveCenter.lastDashboard;
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     6. DASHBOARD REFRESH
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblRefreshDeliveryDashboard = async function () {
    try {
      let result = null;

      if (typeof loadDashboard === 'function') {
        result = await loadDashboard();
      }

      if (!result && typeof pblCallFirst === 'function') {
        result = await objCallApi([
          'apiPbl8GetLiveDashboard',
          'pbl8GetLiveDashboard',
          'getMainDashboardData'
        ], []);
      }

      if (result && result.data && !result.targetQuantity) {
        result = result.data;
      }

      if (result) {
        pblRenderDeliveryDashboard(result);
      }

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('PBL dashboard refresh error:', error);
      objNotify('Actualisation du tableau de bord impossible.', 'error');
      return null;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     7. OBJECTIVE MODAL — CREATE / EDIT
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function objEnsureModal() {
    let modal = objEl('pblObjectiveModal');

    if (modal) return modal;

    modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.id = 'pblObjectiveModal';
    modal.className = 'pbl-modal-overlay';
    modal.style.cssText =
      'display:none;position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9999;' +
      'background:rgba(0,0,0,.78);align-items:center;' +
      'justify-content:center;padding:20px;overflow:auto;';

    modal.innerHTML = `
      <div class="pbl-modal-card"
        style="width:min(680px,100%);max-height:90vh;overflow:auto;
        background:#151d2b;border:1px solid #34445d;border-radius:14px;
        padding:24px;color:#fff;box-shadow:0 20px 70px rgba(0,0,0,.5);">

        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;">
          <h2 id="pblObjectiveModalTitle" style="margin:0;">
            Nouvel objectif
          </h2>
          <button type="button"
            onclick="pblCloseObjectiveModal()"
            aria-label="Fermer">✕</button>
        </div>

        <form id="pblObjectiveForm" style="margin-top:20px;">
          <div class="pbl-objective-form-grid"
            style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:14px;">

            <label style="grid-column:1/-1;">
              Nom de l'objectif
              <input id="pblObjectiveName" name="name"
                type="text" required maxlength="120"
                placeholder="Ex. Livraison chantier A">
            </label>

            <label>
              Quantité cible (tonnes)
              <input id="pblObjectiveTarget" name="targetTonnes"
                type="number" min="0.001" step="0.001" required
                placeholder="Ex. 2500">
            </label>

            <label>
              Produit
              <input id="pblObjectiveProduct" name="product"
                type="text" placeholder="Tous produits">
            </label>

            <label>
              Client
              <input id="pblObjectiveClient" name="client"
                type="text" placeholder="Tous clients">
            </label>

            <label>
              Date de début
              <input id="pblObjectiveStart" name="startDate"
                type="date">
            </label>

            <label>
              Date de fin
              <input id="pblObjectiveEnd" name="endDate"
                type="date">
            </label>

            <label style="grid-column:1/-1;">
              Description / remarque
              <textarea id="pblObjectiveDescription" name="description"
                rows="3" placeholder="Informations complémentaires"></textarea>
            </label>
          </div>

          <div style="display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:10px;margin-top:22px;">
            <button type="button"
              onclick="pblCloseObjectiveModal()">Annuler</button>
            <button type="submit" id="pblObjectiveSaveButton">
              Enregistrer l'objectif
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    `;

    document.body.appendChild(modal);

    const form = objEl('pblObjectiveForm');

    if (form) {
      form.addEventListener('submit', function (event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        pblSaveObjective();
      });
    }

    return modal;
  }

  window.openObjectiveForm = function (objectiveId) {
    if (!objHasEditAccess()) {
      objNotify('Déverrouillez le système avec votre PIN pour modifier les objectifs.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    const modal = objEnsureModal();
    const record = objectiveId
      ? (PBL_APP.data.objectives || []).find(function (item) {
          return objId(item) === String(objectiveId);
        })
      : null;

    PBL_APP.objectiveCenter.currentId = record ? objId(record) : null;

    objText(
      'pblObjectiveModalTitle',
      record ? 'Modifier l’objectif' : 'Créer un objectif'
    );

    const fields = {
      pblObjectiveName: objName(record || { name: '' }) === 'Objectif sans nom'
        ? ''
        : objName(record || { name: '' }),

      pblObjectiveTarget: record ? objGetTargetTonnes(record) : '',
      pblObjectiveProduct: record ? objFirst(record, ['product', 'productName'], '') : '',
      pblObjectiveClient: record ? objFirst(record, ['client', 'clientName'], '') : '',
      pblObjectiveStart: record ? objFirst(record, ['startDate', 'dateStart'], '') : '',
      pblObjectiveEnd: record ? objFirst(record, ['endDate', 'dateEnd'], '') : '',
      pblObjectiveDescription: record ? objFirst(record, ['description', 'notes'], '') : ''
    };

    Object.keys(fields).forEach(function (id) {
      const el = objEl(id);
      if (el) el.value = fields[id] == null ? '' : fields[id];
    });

    modal.style.display = 'flex';
  };

  window.pblCloseObjectiveModal = function () {
    const modal = objEl('pblObjectiveModal');
    if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';

    PBL_APP.objectiveCenter.currentId = null;
  };

  window.pblEditObjective = function (id) {
    openObjectiveForm(id);
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     8. SAVE OBJECTIVE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblSaveObjective = async function () {
    if (!objHasEditAccess()) {
      objNotify('Accès verrouillé. PIN requis.', 'warning');
      return false;
    }

    const name = (objEl('pblObjectiveName') || {}).value || '';
    const targetTonnes = objNumber(
      (objEl('pblObjectiveTarget') || {}).value
    );

    if (!name.trim()) {
      objNotify('Veuillez saisir le nom de l’objectif.', 'warning');
      return false;
    }

    if (targetTonnes <= 0) {
      objNotify('La quantité cible doit être supérieure à zéro.', 'warning');
      return false;
    }

    const startDate = (objEl('pblObjectiveStart') || {}).value || '';
    const endDate = (objEl('pblObjectiveEnd') || {}).value || '';

    if (startDate && endDate && endDate < startDate) {
      objNotify('La date de fin doit être après la date de début.', 'warning');
      return false;
    }

    const payload = {
      name: name.trim(),
      objectiveName: name.trim(),
      targetTonnes: targetTonnes,
      targetQuantity: targetTonnes,
      product: ((objEl('pblObjectiveProduct') || {}).value || '').trim(),
      client: ((objEl('pblObjectiveClient') || {}).value || '').trim(),
      startDate: startDate,
      endDate: endDate,
      description: ((objEl('pblObjectiveDescription') || {}).value || '').trim(),
      status: 'ACTIVE'
    };

    const currentId = PBL_APP.objectiveCenter.currentId;
    const button = objEl('pblObjectiveSaveButton');

    if (button) {
      button.disabled = true;
      button.textContent = 'Enregistrement...';
    }

    try {
      let result;

      if (currentId) {
        payload.id = currentId;
        payload.objectiveId = currentId;

        result = await objCallApi(
          PBL_OBJECTIVE_API.update,
          [currentId, payload]
        );
      } else {
        result = await objCallApi(
          PBL_OBJECTIVE_API.create,
          [payload]
        );
      }

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Enregistrement refusé par le serveur.');
      }

      pblCloseObjectiveModal();

      await loadObjectives();
      await pblRefreshDeliveryDashboard();

      objNotify(
        currentId ? 'Objectif mis à jour.' : 'Objectif créé.',
        'success'
      );

      return true;

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('PBL objective save error:', error);

      objNotify(
        'Enregistrement impossible : ' + error.message,
        'error'
      );

      return false;

    } finally {
      if (button) {
        button.disabled = false;
        button.textContent = 'Enregistrer l’objectif';
      }
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     9. ARCHIVE / RESTORE OBJECTIVE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblArchiveObjective = async function (id) {
    if (!objHasEditAccess()) {
      objNotify('Accès verrouillé. PIN requis.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    if (!window.confirm('Archiver cet objectif ?')) return;

    try {
      const result = await objCallApi(
        PBL_OBJECTIVE_API.archive,
        [id]
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Archivage refusé.');
      }

      await loadObjectives();
      await pblRefreshDeliveryDashboard();

      objNotify('Objectif archivé.', 'success');

    } catch (error) {
      objNotify('Archivage impossible : ' + error.message, 'error');
    }
  };

  window.pblRestoreObjective = async function (id) {
    if (!objHasEditAccess()) {
      objNotify('Accès verrouillé. PIN requis.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    try {
      const result = await objCallApi(
        PBL_OBJECTIVE_API.restore,
        [id]
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Restauration refusée.');
      }

      await loadObjectives();
      await pblRefreshDeliveryDashboard();

      objNotify('Objectif restauré.', 'success');

    } catch (error) {
      objNotify('Restauration impossible : ' + error.message, 'error');
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     10. OBJECTIVE SELECTOR
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function objUpdateObjectiveSelector() {
    const selectors = [
      objEl('objectiveSelect'),
      objEl('weighingObjective'),
      objEl('ticketObjective')
    ].filter(Boolean);

    const records = (PBL_APP.data.objectives || []).filter(objIsActive);

    selectors.forEach(function (select) {
      const currentValue = select.value;

      select.innerHTML =
        '<option value="">Aucun objectif sélectionné</option>' +
        records.map(function (record) {
          const id = objId(record);

          return `<option value="${objEscape(id)}">
            ${objEscape(objName(record))}
          </option>`;
        }).join('');

      if (currentValue) select.value = currentValue;
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     11. OBJECTIVE SUMMARY
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblGetObjectiveSummary = function (objectiveId) {
    const record = (PBL_APP.data.objectives || []).find(function (item) {
      return objId(item) === String(objectiveId);
    });

    if (!record) return null;

    const target = objGetTargetTonnes(record);

    return {
      id: objId(record),
      name: objName(record),
      targetTonnes: target,
      status: objStatus(record),
      startDate: objFirst(record, ['startDate', 'dateStart'], ''),
      endDate: objFirst(record, ['endDate', 'dateEnd'], ''),
      product: objFirst(record, ['product', 'productName'], ''),
      client: objFirst(record, ['client', 'clientName'], '')
    };
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     12. DASHBOARD REFRESH HOOK
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  const previousLoadDashboard = window.loadDashboard;

  if (typeof previousLoadDashboard === 'function') {
    window.loadDashboard = async function () {
      const result = await previousLoadDashboard.apply(this, arguments);

      if (result) {
        const data = result.data || result.dashboard || result;
        pblRenderDeliveryDashboard(data);
      }

      return result;
    };
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     13. INITIALIZATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblInitializeObjectiveCenter = async function () {
    PBL_APP.data = PBL_APP.data || {};
    PBL_APP.data.objectives = PBL_APP.data.objectives || [];

    objEnsureModal();
    objUpdateObjectiveSelector();

    if (typeof renderObjectives === 'function') {
      renderObjectives();
    }

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
      pblInitializeObjectiveCenter();
    });
  } else {
    pblInitializeObjectiveCenter();
  }

  console.log('PONT BASCULE LIVE — Objective Center loaded.');

})();
</script>
<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 8
   REPORTS, ANALYTICS & DELIVERY HISTORY
   ============================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     1. REPORT CENTER STATE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};
  PBL_APP.reports = PBL_APP.reports || {
    type: 'DAILY',
    dimension: 'ALL',
    startDate: '',
    endDate: '',
    data: [],
    summary: {},
    loading: false,
    lastGenerated: null
  };

  const PBL_REPORT_APIS = {
    generate: [
      'pbl20GenerateReport',
      'apiPbl20GenerateReport',
      'apiGenerateAdvancedReport',
      'generateAdvancedReport',
      'getAdvancedReport'
    ],
    daily: [
      'pbl20GetDailyReport',
      'apiPbl20GetDailyReport',
      'getDailyReport'
    ],
    monthly: [
      'pbl20GetMonthlyReport',
      'apiPbl20GetMonthlyReport',
      'getMonthlyReport'
    ],
    yearly: [
      'pbl20GetYearlyReport',
      'apiPbl20GetYearlyReport',
      'getYearlyReport'
    ],
    product: [
      'pbl20GetProductReport',
      'apiPbl20GetProductReport',
      'getProductReport'
    ],
    client: [
      'pbl20GetClientReport',
      'apiPbl20GetClientReport',
      'getClientReport'
    ],
    truck: [
      'pbl20GetTruckReport',
      'apiPbl20GetTruckReport',
      'getTruckReport'
    ],
    driver: [
      'pbl20GetDriverReport',
      'apiPbl20GetDriverReport',
      'getDriverReport'
    ],
    history: [
      'pbl20GetReportHistory',
      'apiPbl20GetReportHistory',
      'getReportHistory'
    ]
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     2. GENERAL HELPERS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function rptEl(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function rptText(id, value) {
    const el = rptEl(id);
    if (el) el.textContent = value == null ? '' : String(value);
  }

  function rptNumber(value) {
    const n = Number(String(value == null ? 0 : value).replace(',', '.'));
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  }

  function rptRound(value, decimals) {
    const factor = Math.pow(10, decimals == null ? 2 : decimals);
    return Math.round((rptNumber(value) + Number.EPSILON) * factor) / factor;
  }

  function rptFormat(value, decimals) {
    return rptRound(value, decimals == null ? 2 : decimals)
      .toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
        minimumFractionDigits: decimals == null ? 2 : decimals,
        maximumFractionDigits: decimals == null ? 2 : decimals
      });
  }

  function rptEscape(value) {
    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(value == null ? '' : String(value));
    }

    return String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
  }

  function rptFirst(record, keys, fallback) {
    record = record || {};

    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const value = record[keys[i]];
      if (value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== '') {
        return value;
      }
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  function rptNotify(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL REPORTS]', message);
    }
  }

  function rptCall(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Connexion au serveur indisponible.')
      );
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function rptDateInput(date) {
    const d = date || new Date();
    const local = new Date(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
    return local.toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  }

  function rptDateLabel(value) {
    if (!value) return '—';

    const str = String(value);

    if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(str)) {
      const parts = str.slice(0, 10).split('-');
      return parts[2] + '/' + parts[1] + '/' + parts[0];
    }

    return str;
  }

  function rptGetKg(record) {
    const explicitKg = rptFirst(record, [
      'netKg',
      'netWeightKg',
      'netWeight',
      'weightNet',
      'net_kg',
      'quantityKg'
    ], null);

    if (explicitKg !== null) return rptNumber(explicitKg);

    const tonnes = rptFirst(record, [
      'netTonnes',
      'netTons',
      'quantityTonnes'
    ], null);

    return tonnes === null ? 0 : rptNumber(tonnes) * 1000;
  }

  function rptGetTonnes(record) {
    const explicitTonnes = rptFirst(record, [
      'netTonnes',
      'netTons',
      'quantityTonnes'
    ], null);

    if (explicitTonnes !== null) return rptNumber(explicitTonnes);

    return rptGetKg(record) / 1000;
  }

  function rptGetVoyageNumber(record) {
    return rptNumber(rptFirst(record, [
      'numberOfVoyages',
      'voyages',
      'tripCount',
      'count'
    ], 1));
  }

  function rptGetRows(result) {
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result;
    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return [];

    const candidates = [
      result.rows,
      result.records,
      result.tickets,
      result.results,
      result.data,
      result.items,
      result.report
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
      if (Array.isArray(candidates[i])) return candidates[i];
    }

    if (result.data && Array.isArray(result.data.rows)) {
      return result.data.rows;
    }

    return [];
  }

  function rptGetSummary(result, rows) {
    const source = result && typeof result === 'object' ? result : {};

    const summary = source.summary ||
      source.totals ||
      source.statistics ||
      source.kpis ||
      {};

    const totalKg = rptNumber(rptFirst(summary, [
      'totalKg',
      'cumulKg',
      'netKg',
      'totalWeightKg'
    ], rows.reduce(function (sum, row) {
      return sum + rptGetKg(row);
    }, 0)));

    const totalTonnes = rptFirst(summary, [
      'totalTonnes',
      'cumulTonnes',
      'netTonnes',
      'deliveredTonnes'
    ], null);

    const voyages = rptNumber(rptFirst(summary, [
      'numberOfVoyages',
      'voyageCount',
      'totalVoyages',
      'count'
    ], rows.length));

    return {
      totalKg: totalKg,
      totalTonnes: totalTonnes === null
        ? totalKg / 1000
        : rptNumber(totalTonnes),
      voyages: voyages,
      averageTonnes: voyages > 0
        ? (totalTonnes === null ? totalKg / 1000 : rptNumber(totalTonnes)) / voyages
        : 0
    };
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     3. REPORT CENTER INTERFACE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function rptFindReportsPage() {
    return rptEl('reportsPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="reports"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-reports');
  }

  function rptEnsureInterface() {
    let root = rptEl('pblReportCenter');
    if (root) return root;

    const page = rptFindReportsPage();

    if (!page) {
      console.warn('PBL reports page container was not found.');
      return null;
    }

    root = document.createElement('div');
    root.id = 'pblReportCenter';
    root.className = 'pbl-report-center';

    root.innerHTML = `
      <style>
        #pblReportCenter {
          color: inherit;
          width: 100%;
        }
        #pblReportCenter .rpt-toolbar {
          display: flex;
          flex-wrap: wrap;
          gap: 12px;
          align-items: end;
          padding: 16px;
          margin-bottom: 18px;
          border: 1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.22);
          border-radius: 12px;
          background: rgba(20,30,46,.55);
        }
        #pblReportCenter .rpt-field {
          display: flex;
          flex-direction: column;
          gap: 6px;
          min-width: 145px;
          flex: 1;
        }
        #pblReportCenter .rpt-field label {
          font-size: 12px;
          opacity: .75;
        }
        #pblReportCenter input,
        #pblReportCenter select {
          width: 100%;
          min-height: 39px;
          padding: 8px 10px;
          color: inherit;
          background: rgba(8,15,27,.8);
          border: 1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.3);
          border-radius: 7px;
        }
        #pblReportCenter button {
          cursor: pointer;
          border-radius: 7px;
          padding: 10px 14px;
          border: 1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.35);
        }
        #pblReportCenter .rpt-primary {
          background: #2563eb;
          color: white;
          border-color: #2563eb;
        }
        #pblReportCenter .rpt-kpis {
          display: grid;
          grid-template-columns: repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));
          gap: 12px;
          margin-bottom: 18px;

      <div class="rpt-toolbar">

            <option value="CLIENT">Par client</option>

          </select>
        </div>

        <div class="rpt-field">
          <label for="pblReportStart">DATE DE DÉBUT</label>
          <input type="date" id="pblReportStart">
        </div>

        <div class="rpt-field">
          <label for="pblReportEnd">DATE DE FIN</label>
          <input type="date" id="pblReportEnd">
        </div>

        <div class="rpt-field">
          <label for="pblReportDimension">REGROUPEMENT</label>
          <select id="pblReportDimension">
            <option value="ALL">Tous</option>
            <option value="PRODUCT">Produit</option>
            <option value="CLIENT">Client</option>
            <option value="TRUCK">Camion</option>
            <option value="DRIVER">Chauffeur</option>
          </select>

        <button type="button" class="rpt-primary"
          id="pblGenerateReportButton"
          onclick="pblGenerateReport()">

      <div class="rpt-kpis">
        <div class="rpt-kpi">

        </div>
        <div class="rpt-kpi">
          <div class="rpt-kpi-label">NOMBRE DE VOYAGES</div>
          <div class="rpt-kpi-value" id="pblReportVoyages">0</div>
        </div>
        <div class="rpt-kpi">
          <div class="rpt-kpi-label">MOYENNE PAR VOYAGE</div>
          <div class="rpt-kpi-value" id="pblReportAverage">0,00 T</div>
        </div>
        <div class="rpt-kpi">

      rptUpdateTypeDefaults();
