  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     4. REPORT TYPE DEFAULTS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function rptUpdateTypeDefaults() {
    const type = rptEl('pblReportType');
    const start = rptEl('pblReportStart');
    const end = rptEl('pblReportEnd');

    if (!type || !start || !end) return;

    const now = new Date();
    let from = new Date(now);

    if (type.value === 'DAILY') {
      from = new Date(now);
    } else if (type.value === 'MONTHLY') {
      from = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), 1);
    } else if (type.value === 'YEARLY') {
      from = new Date(now.getFullYear(), 0, 1);
    } else if (type.value === 'PRODUCT' ||
               type.value === 'CLIENT' ||
               type.value === 'TRUCK' ||
               type.value === 'DRIVER' ||
               type.value === 'CUSTOM') {
      return;
    }

    start.value = rptDateInput(from);
    end.value = rptDateInput(now);
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     5. BUILD REPORT REQUEST
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function rptBuildRequest() {
    const type = (rptEl('pblReportType') || {}).value || 'DAILY';
    const startDate = (rptEl('pblReportStart') || {}).value || '';
    const endDate = (rptEl('pblReportEnd') || {}).value || '';
    const dimension = (rptEl('pblReportDimension') || {}).value || 'ALL';

    if (!startDate || !endDate) {
      throw new Error('Veuillez choisir les deux dates.');
    }

    if (endDate < startDate) {
      throw new Error('La date de fin doit être après la date de début.');
    }

    return {
      type: type,
      reportType: type,
      startDate: startDate,
      endDate: endDate,
      dateFrom: startDate,
      dateTo: endDate,
      dimension: dimension,
      groupBy: dimension,
      timezone: 'Africa/Casablanca'
    };
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     6. GENERATE REPORT
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblGenerateReport = async function () {
    if (PBL_APP.reports.loading) return;

    let request;

    try {
      request = rptBuildRequest();
    } catch (error) {
      rptNotify(error.message, 'warning');
      return;
    }

    PBL_APP.reports.loading = true;

    const button = rptEl('pblGenerateReportButton');

    if (button) {
      button.disabled = true;
      button.textContent = 'Génération...';
    }

    try {
      const result = await rptCall(
        PBL_REPORT_APIS.generate,
        [request]
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Le serveur a refusé le rapport.');
      }

      const rows = rptGetRows(result);
      const summary = rptGetSummary(result, rows);

      PBL_APP.reports.type = request.type;
      PBL_APP.reports.dimension = request.dimension;
      PBL_APP.reports.startDate = request.startDate;
      PBL_APP.reports.endDate = request.endDate;
      PBL_APP.reports.data = rows;
      PBL_APP.reports.summary = summary;
      PBL_APP.reports.lastGenerated = new Date().toISOString();

      rptRenderSummary(summary);
      rptRenderRows(rows);

      rptText(
        'pblReportPeriodLabel',
        'Période : ' + rptDateLabel(request.startDate) +
        ' au ' + rptDateLabel(request.endDate) +
        ' — ' + request.type
      );

      await pblLoadReportHistory();

      rptNotify('Rapport généré.', 'success');

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('PBL report generation error:', error);

      rptNotify(
        'Impossible de générer le rapport : ' + error.message,
        'error'
      );

      return null;

    } finally {
      PBL_APP.reports.loading = false;

      if (button) {
        button.disabled = false;
        button.textContent = 'Générer le rapport';
      }
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     7. REPORT SUMMARY DISPLAY
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function rptRenderSummary(summary) {
    summary = summary || {};

    rptText(
      'pblReportTotalTonnes',
      rptFormat(summary.totalTonnes) + ' T'
    );

    rptText(
      'pblReportVoyages',
      String(summary.voyages || 0)
    );

    rptText(
      'pblReportAverage',
      rptFormat(summary.averageTonnes) + ' T'
    );

    rptText(
      'pblReportTotalKg',
      Math.round(summary.totalKg || 0).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' KG'
    );
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     8. REPORT TABLE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function rptRenderRows(rows) {
    const body = rptEl('pblReportTableBody');
    if (!body) return;

    if (!rows || !rows.length) {
      body.innerHTML =
        '<tr><td colspan="8" class="rpt-empty">' +
        'Aucun résultat pour cette période.' +
        '</td></tr>';
      return;
    }

    body.innerHTML = rows.map(function (record) {
      const date = rptFirst(record, [
        'date',
        'weighingDate',
        'createdAt',
        'timestamp'
      ], '—');

      const ticket = rptFirst(record, [
        'ticketNumber',
        'ticketNo',
        'number',
        'nBon',
        'id'
      ], '—');

      const product = rptFirst(record, [
        'product',
        'productName'
      ], '—');

      const client = rptFirst(record, [
        'client',
        'clientName'
      ], '—');

      const truck = rptFirst(record, [
        'truckRegistration',
        'registration',
        'truck',
        'vehicle'
      ], '—');

      const driver = rptFirst(record, [
        'driver',
        'driverName'
      ], '—');

      const kg = rptGetKg(record);
      const tonnes = rptGetTonnes(record);

      return `
        <tr>
          <td>${rptEscape(rptDateLabel(date))}</td>
          <td>${rptEscape(ticket)}</td>
          <td>${rptEscape(product)}</td>
          <td>${rptEscape(client)}</td>
          <td>${rptEscape(truck)}</td>
          <td>${rptEscape(driver)}</td>
          <td>${Math.round(kg).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}</td>
          <td>${rptFormat(tonnes)}</td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     9. REPORT HISTORY
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblLoadReportHistory = async function () {
    const body = rptEl('pblReportHistoryBody');
    if (!body) return [];

    try {
      const result = await rptCall(PBL_REPORT_APIS.history, []);
      const records = rptGetRows(result);

      if (!records.length) {
        body.innerHTML =
          '<tr><td colspan="5" class="rpt-empty">' +
          'Aucun historique disponible.' +
          '</td></tr>';
        return [];
      }

      body.innerHTML = records.map(function (record) {
        const date = rptFirst(record, [
          'generatedAt',
          'createdAt',
          'date',
          'timestamp'
        ], '—');

        const type = rptFirst(record, [
          'type',
          'reportType',
          'name'
        ], '—');

        const period = rptFirst(record, [
          'period',
          'dateRange',
          'startDate'
        ], '—');

        const user = rptFirst(record, [
          'user',
          'username',
          'createdBy'
        ], '—');

        const status = rptFirst(record, [
          'status',
          'result'
        ], 'GÉNÉRÉ');

        return `
          <tr>
            <td>${rptEscape(date)}</td>
            <td>${rptEscape(type)}</td>
            <td>${rptEscape(period)}</td>
            <td>${rptEscape(user)}</td>
            <td>${rptEscape(status)}</td>
          </tr>
        `;
      }).join('');

      return records;

    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('PBL report history unavailable:', error);

      body.innerHTML =
        '<tr><td colspan="5" class="rpt-empty">' +
        'Historique indisponible ou API non configurée.' +
        '</td></tr>';

      return [];
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     10. CSV EXPORT
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function rptCsvCell(value) {
    const text = String(value == null ? '' : value);
    return '"' + text.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
  }

  window.pblExportReportCSV = function () {
    const rows = PBL_APP.reports.data || [];

    if (!rows.length) {
      rptNotify('Aucune donnée à exporter.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    const headers = [
      'Date',
      'Numero ticket',
      'Produit',
      'Client',
      'Camion',
      'Chauffeur',
      'Poids net KG',
      'Tonnes'
    ];

    const lines = [headers.map(rptCsvCell).join(';')];

    rows.forEach(function (record) {
      lines.push([
        rptFirst(record, ['date', 'weighingDate', 'createdAt'], ''),
        rptFirst(record, ['ticketNumber', 'ticketNo', 'number', 'nBon'], ''),
        rptFirst(record, ['product', 'productName'], ''),
        rptFirst(record, ['client', 'clientName'], ''),
        rptFirst(record, ['truckRegistration', 'registration', 'truck'], ''),
        rptFirst(record, ['driver', 'driverName'], ''),
        Math.round(rptGetKg(record)),
        rptGetTonnes(record).toFixed(3)
      ].map(rptCsvCell).join(';'));
    });

    const csv = '\uFEFF' + lines.join('\r\n');
    const blob = new Blob([csv], {
      type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;'
    });

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');

    link.href = url;
    link.download =
      'PONT_BASCULE_LIVE_RAPPORT_' +
      (PBL_APP.reports.type || 'EXPORT') + '_' +
      rptDateInput(new Date()) + '.csv';

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    rptNotify('Export CSV préparé.', 'success');
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     11. PRINT REPORT
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblPrintReport = function () {
    if (!(PBL_APP.reports.data || []).length) {
      rptNotify('Générez d’abord un rapport.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    const area = rptEl('pblReportPrintArea');

    if (!area) {
      rptNotify('Zone d’impression introuvable.', 'error');
      return;
    }

    const title = document.createElement('h2');
    title.textContent = 'PONT BASCULE LIVE — RAPPORT';

    const subtitle = document.createElement('p');
    subtitle.textContent =
      'Période : ' +
      rptDateLabel(PBL_APP.reports.startDate) +
      ' au ' +
      rptDateLabel(PBL_APP.reports.endDate);

    const metrics = document.createElement('p');
    metrics.textContent =
      'Tonnage total : ' +
      rptFormat(PBL_APP.reports.summary.totalTonnes) +
      ' T | Voyages : ' +
      String(PBL_APP.reports.summary.voyages || 0);

    const header = document.createElement('div');
    header.id = 'pblReportPrintHeading';
    header.appendChild(title);
    header.appendChild(subtitle);
    header.appendChild(metrics);

    area.insertBefore(header, area.firstChild);

    window.print();

    setTimeout(function () {
      if (header.parentNode) header.parentNode.removeChild(header);
    }, 1000);
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     12. LOCAL REPORT FROM LOADED TICKETS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblBuildLocalReport = function (tickets, request) {
    tickets = Array.isArray(tickets) ? tickets : [];
    request = request || {};

    const start = request.startDate || '';
    const end = request.endDate || '';

    function normalizeDate(value) {
      if (!value) return '';

      const str = String(value);

      if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(str)) {
        return str.slice(0, 10);
      }

      const match = str.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);

      if (match) {
        return match[3] + '-' + match[2] + '-' + match[1];
      }

      return '';
    }

    const filtered = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      const status = String(rptFirst(ticket, [
        'status',
        'state'
      ], 'ACTIVE')).toUpperCase();

      if ([
        'CANCELLED',
        'CANCELED',
        'DELETED',
        'VOID'
      ].includes(status)) {
        return false;
      }

      const date = normalizeDate(rptFirst(ticket, [
        'date',
        'weighingDate',
        'createdAt'
      ], ''));

      if (start && date && date < start) return false;
      if (end && date && date > end) return false;

      return true;
    });

    const summary = rptGetSummary(filtered, filtered);

    return {
      rows: filtered,
      summary: summary
    };
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     13. QUICK REPORT FUNCTIONS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblGenerateDailyReport = function () {
    const type = rptEl('pblReportType');
    if (type) type.value = 'DAILY';

    rptUpdateTypeDefaults();
    return pblGenerateReport();
  };

  window.pblGenerateMonthlyReport = function () {
    const type = rptEl('pblReportType');
    if (type) type.value = 'MONTHLY';

    rptUpdateTypeDefaults();
    return pblGenerateReport();
  };

  window.pblGenerateYearlyReport = function () {
    const type = rptEl('pblReportType');
    if (type) type.value = 'YEARLY';

    rptUpdateTypeDefaults();
    return pblGenerateReport();
  };

  window.pblGenerateProductReport = function () {
    const type = rptEl('pblReportType');
    if (type) type.value = 'PRODUCT';

    return pblGenerateReport();
  };

  window.pblGenerateClientReport = function () {
    const type = rptEl('pblReportType');
    if (type) type.value = 'CLIENT';

    return pblGenerateReport();
  };

  window.pblGenerateTruckReport = function () {
    const type = rptEl('pblReportType');
    if (type) type.value = 'TRUCK';

    return pblGenerateReport();
  };

  window.pblGenerateDriverReport = function () {
    const type = rptEl('pblReportType');
    if (type) type.value = 'DRIVER';

    return pblGenerateReport();
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     14. INITIALIZATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblInitializeReportCenter = function () {
    rptEnsureInterface();
    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
      pblInitializeReportCenter();
    });
  } else {
    pblInitializeReportCenter();
  }

  console.log('PONT BASCULE LIVE — Report Center loaded.');

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 9
   ADVANCED SEARCH, FILTERS & TICKET FINDER
   ============================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     1. SEARCH ENGINE STATE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};
  PBL_APP.data = PBL_APP.data || {};

  PBL_APP.advancedSearch = PBL_APP.advancedSearch || {
    query: '',
    filters: {},
    results: [],
    page: 1,
    pageSize: 25,
    total: 0,
    loading: false,
    lastSearch: null,
    savedSearches: []
  };

  const PBL_SEARCH_APIS = {
    search: [
      'pbl21AdvancedSearch',
      'apiPbl21AdvancedSearch',
      'pbl21SearchTickets',
      'apiPbl21SearchTickets',
      'apiAdvancedSearchTickets',
      'advancedSearchTickets'
    ],
    saved: [
      'pbl21GetSavedSearches',
      'apiPbl21GetSavedSearches',
      'getSavedSearches'
    ],
    save: [
      'pbl21SaveSearch',
      'apiPbl21SaveSearch',
      'saveSearch'
    ],
    deleteSaved: [
      'pbl21DeleteSavedSearch',
      'apiPbl21DeleteSavedSearch',
      'deleteSavedSearch'
    ],
    history: [
      'pbl21GetSearchHistory',
      'apiPbl21GetSearchHistory',
      'getSearchHistory'
    ]
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     2. HELPERS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function srEl(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function srNumber(value) {
    const n = Number(String(value == null ? 0 : value).replace(',', '.'));
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  }

  function srEscape(value) {
    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(value == null ? '' : String(value));
    }

    return String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
  }

  function srFirst(record, keys, fallback) {
    record = record || {};

    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const value = record[keys[i]];

      if (value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== '') {
        return value;
      }
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  function srNotify(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL SEARCH]', message);
    }
  }

  function srCall(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Le moteur de communication serveur est indisponible.')
      );
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function srGetRows(result) {
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result;
    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return [];

    const candidates = [
      result.results,
      result.rows,
      result.records,
      result.tickets,
      result.items,
      result.data
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
      if (Array.isArray(candidates[i])) return candidates[i];
    }

    if (result.data && Array.isArray(result.data.results)) {
      return result.data.results;
    }

    return [];
  }

  function srTicketId(ticket) {
    return String(srFirst(ticket, [
      'id',
      'ticketId',
      'recordId',
      'uuid'
    ], ''));
  }

  function srTicketNumber(ticket) {
    return String(srFirst(ticket, [
      'ticketNumber',
      'ticketNo',
      'number',
      'nBon',
      'bonNumber'
    ], '—'));
  }

  function srTicketDate(ticket) {
    return String(srFirst(ticket, [
      'date',
      'weighingDate',
      'createdAt',
      'timestamp'
    ], '—'));
  }

  function srTicketProduct(ticket) {
    return String(srFirst(ticket, [
      'product',
      'productName'
    ], '—'));
  }

  function srTicketClient(ticket) {
    return String(srFirst(ticket, [
      'client',
      'clientName'
    ], '—'));
  }

  function srTicketTruck(ticket) {
    return String(srFirst(ticket, [
      'truckRegistration',
      'registration',
      'truck',
      'vehicle'
    ], '—'));
  }

  function srTicketDriver(ticket) {
    return String(srFirst(ticket, [
      'driver',
      'driverName'
    ], '—'));
  }

  function srTicketKg(ticket) {
    const kg = srFirst(ticket, [
      'netKg',
      'netWeightKg',
      'netWeight',
      'weightNet',
      'net_kg'
    ], null);

    if (kg !== null) return srNumber(kg);

    const tonnes = srFirst(ticket, [
      'netTonnes',
      'netTons'
    ], 0);

    return srNumber(tonnes) * 1000;
  }

  function srTicketStatus(ticket) {
    return String(srFirst(ticket, [
      'status',
      'state'
    ], 'ACTIVE')).toUpperCase();
  }

  function srNormalizeDate(value) {
    if (!value) return '';

    const str = String(value);

    if (/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/.test(str)) {
      return str.slice(0, 10);
    }

    const match = str.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})$/);

    if (match) {
      return match[3] + '-' + match[2] + '-' + match[1];
    }

    return '';
  }

  function srDateDisplay(value) {
    const normalized = srNormalizeDate(value);

    if (!normalized) return String(value || '—');

    const parts = normalized.split('-');
    return parts[2] + '/' + parts[1] + '/' + parts[0];
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------

     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function srFindSearchPage() {
    return srEl('searchPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="search"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-search');
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     4. READ FILTERS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function srReadFilters() {
    return {
      query: (srEl('pblSearchQuery') || {}).value || '',
      ticketNumber: (srEl('pblSearchTicket') || {}).value || '',
      product: (srEl('pblSearchProduct') || {}).value || '',
      client: (srEl('pblSearchClient') || {}).value || '',
      truckRegistration: (srEl('pblSearchTruck') || {}).value || '',
      driver: (srEl('pblSearchDriver') || {}).value || '',
      startDate: (srEl('pblSearchStart') || {}).value || '',
      endDate: (srEl('pblSearchEnd') || {}).value || '',
      status: (srEl('pblSearchStatus') || {}).value || 'ALL',
      minKg: (srEl('pblSearchMinKg') || {}).value || '',
      maxKg: (srEl('pblSearchMaxKg') || {}).value || '',
      page: PBL_APP.advancedSearch.page || 1,
      pageSize: Number((srEl('pblSearchPageSize') || {}).value || 25)
    };
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     5. LOCAL SEARCH FALLBACK
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function srMatchesText(value, term) {
    if (!term) return true;

    return String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .toLowerCase()
      .includes(String(term).toLowerCase());
  }

  function srLocalSearch(filters) {
    const tickets = Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data.tickets)
      ? PBL_APP.data.tickets
      : [];

    const filtered = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      const globalText = [
        srTicketNumber(ticket),
        srTicketProduct(ticket),
        srTicketClient(ticket),
        srTicketTruck(ticket),
        srTicketDriver(ticket)
      ].join(' ');

      if (!srMatchesText(globalText, filters.query)) return false;
      if (!srMatchesText(srTicketNumber(ticket), filters.ticketNumber)) return false;
      if (!srMatchesText(srTicketProduct(ticket), filters.product)) return false;
      if (!srMatchesText(srTicketClient(ticket), filters.client)) return false;
      if (!srMatchesText(srTicketTruck(ticket), filters.truckRegistration)) return false;
      if (!srMatchesText(srTicketDriver(ticket), filters.driver)) return false;

      const date = srNormalizeDate(srTicketDate(ticket));

      if (filters.startDate && date && date < filters.startDate) return false;
      if (filters.endDate && date && date > filters.endDate) return false;

      const status = srTicketStatus(ticket);

      if (filters.status !== 'ALL') {
        if (filters.status === 'ACTIVE' &&
            ['CANCELLED', 'CANCELED', 'ARCHIVED', 'DELETED'].includes(status)) {
          return false;
        }

        if (filters.status === 'CANCELLED' &&
            !['CANCELLED', 'CANCELED'].includes(status)) {
          return false;
        }

        if (filters.status === 'ARCHIVED' && status !== 'ARCHIVED') {
          return false;
        }
      }

      const kg = srTicketKg(ticket);

      if (filters.minKg !== '' && kg < srNumber(filters.minKg)) return false;
      if (filters.maxKg !== '' && kg > srNumber(filters.maxKg)) return false;

      return true;
    });

    const pageSize = Math.max(1, filters.pageSize);
    const total = filtered.length;
    const start = (filters.page - 1) * pageSize;

    return {
      results: filtered.slice(start, start + pageSize),
      total: total,
      page: filters.page,
      pageSize: pageSize,
      totalPages: Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / pageSize)),
      source: 'LOCAL'
    };
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     6. RUN ADVANCED SEARCH
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblRunAdvancedSearch = async function () {
    const state = PBL_APP.advancedSearch;

    if (state.loading) return;

    const filters = srReadFilters();

    if (filters.startDate && filters.endDate &&
        filters.endDate < filters.startDate) {
      srNotify('La date de fin doit être après la date de début.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    if (filters.minKg !== '' && filters.maxKg !== '' &&
        srNumber(filters.minKg) > srNumber(filters.maxKg)) {
      srNotify('Le poids minimum dépasse le poids maximum.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    state.loading = true;
    state.filters = filters;
    state.query = filters.query;

    const button = document.querySelector(
      '#pblAdvancedSearchCenter .sr-primary'
    );

    if (button) {
      button.disabled = true;
      button.textContent = 'Recherche...';
    }

    try {
      let response;

      try {
        response = await srCall(
          PBL_SEARCH_APIS.search,
          [filters]
        );
      } catch (serverError) {
        console.warn(
          'PBL server search unavailable; using local records.',
          serverError
        );

        response = srLocalSearch(filters);
      }

      if (response && response.success === false) {
        throw new Error(response.message || 'La recherche a échoué.');
      }

      let results = srGetRows(response);

      const total = srFirst(response || {}, [
        'total',
        'totalCount',
        'totalResults',
        'count'
      ], results.length);

      const page = srNumber(srFirst(response || {}, [
        'page',
        'currentPage'
      ], filters.page));

      const pageSize = srNumber(srFirst(response || {}, [
        'pageSize',
        'limit'
      ], filters.pageSize));

      const totalPages = srNumber(srFirst(response || {}, [
        'totalPages',
        'pages'
      ], Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / Math.max(1, pageSize)))));

      state.results = results;
      state.total = srNumber(total);
      state.page = page || filters.page;
      state.pageSize = pageSize || filters.pageSize;
      state.lastSearch = {
        filters: filters,
        createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
      };

      srRenderResults(results);
      srUpdatePagination({
        total: state.total,
        page: state.page,
        pageSize: state.pageSize,
        totalPages: totalPages
      });

      srNotify(
        state.total + ' résultat(s) trouvé(s).',
        'success'
      );

      return response;

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('PBL advanced search error:', error);

      srNotify(
        'Recherche impossible : ' + error.message,
        'error'
      );

      return null;

    } finally {
      state.loading = false;

      if (button) {
        button.disabled = false;
        button.textContent = 'Rechercher';
      }
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     7. RENDER SEARCH RESULTS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function srRenderResults(results) {
    const body = srEl('pblAdvancedSearchResults');
    if (!body) return;

    if (!results || !results.length) {
      body.innerHTML =
        '<tr><td colspan="10" class="sr-empty">' +
        'Aucun ticket correspondant aux critères.' +
        '</td></tr>';

      srEl('pblSearchTotalCount').textContent = '0';
      srEl('pblSearchResultDescription').textContent =
        'Aucun résultat trouvé.';

      return;
    }

    body.innerHTML = results.map(function (ticket) {
      const id = srTicketId(ticket);
      const kg = srTicketKg(ticket);
      const tonnes = kg / 1000;
      const status = srTicketStatus(ticket);

      const safeId = encodeURIComponent(id);

      return `
        <tr>
          <td>${srEscape(srDateDisplay(srTicketDate(ticket)))}</td>
          <td><strong>${srEscape(srTicketNumber(ticket))}</strong></td>
          <td>${srEscape(srTicketProduct(ticket))}</td>
          <td>${srEscape(srTicketClient(ticket))}</td>
          <td>${srEscape(srTicketTruck(ticket))}</td>
          <td>${srEscape(srTicketDriver(ticket))}</td>
          <td>${Math.round(kg).toLocaleString('fr-FR')}</td>
          <td>${tonnes.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
            minimumFractionDigits: 3,
            maximumFractionDigits: 3
          })}</td>
          <td>${srEscape(status)}</td>
          <td>
            <button type="button"
              onclick="pblSearchOpenTicket('${safeId}')">
              Voir
            </button>
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }).join('');

    srEl('pblSearchTotalCount').textContent =
      String(PBL_APP.advancedSearch.total);

    srEl('pblSearchResultDescription').textContent =
      'Recherche terminée — ' +
      results.length + ' ligne(s) affichée(s).';
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     8. PAGINATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function srUpdatePagination(data) {
    const state = PBL_APP.advancedSearch;

    const totalPages = Math.max(1, srNumber(data.totalPages));
    const page = Math.max(1, srNumber(data.page));

    state.page = page;

    srEl('pblSearchPageDescription').textContent =
      'Page ' + page + ' / ' + totalPages +
      ' — ' + srNumber(data.total) + ' résultat(s)';

    const previous = document.querySelector(
      '#pblAdvancedSearchCenter button[onclick="pblSearchPreviousPage()"]'
    );

    const next = document.querySelector(
      '#pblAdvancedSearchCenter button[onclick="pblSearchNextPage()"]'
    );

    if (previous) previous.disabled = page <= 1;
    if (next) next.disabled = page >= totalPages;
  }

  window.pblSearchPreviousPage = function () {
    const state = PBL_APP.advancedSearch;

    if (state.page <= 1) return;

    state.page--;
    pblRunAdvancedSearch();
  };

  window.pblSearchNextPage = function () {
    const state = PBL_APP.advancedSearch;
    const totalPages = Math.max(
      1,
      Math.ceil(state.total / Math.max(1, state.pageSize))
    );

    if (state.page >= totalPages) return;

    state.page++;
    pblRunAdvancedSearch();
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     9. RESET FILTERS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblResetAdvancedSearch = function () {
    [
      'pblSearchQuery',
      'pblSearchTicket',
      'pblSearchProduct',
      'pblSearchClient',
      'pblSearchTruck',
      'pblSearchDriver',
      'pblSearchStart',
      'pblSearchEnd',
      'pblSearchMinKg',
      'pblSearchMaxKg'
    ].forEach(function (id) {
      const el = srEl(id);
      if (el) el.value = '';
    });

    if (srEl('pblSearchStatus')) {
      srEl('pblSearchStatus').value = 'ALL';
    }

    if (srEl('pblSearchPageSize')) {
      srEl('pblSearchPageSize').value = '25';
    }

    PBL_APP.advancedSearch.page = 1;
    PBL_APP.advancedSearch.results = [];
    PBL_APP.advancedSearch.total = 0;

    const body = srEl('pblAdvancedSearchResults');

    if (body) {
      body.innerHTML =
        '<tr><td colspan="10" class="sr-empty">' +
        'Saisissez un critère et lancez une recherche.' +
        '</td></tr>';
    }

    srEl('pblSearchTotalCount').textContent = '0';
    srEl('pblSearchResultDescription').textContent =
      'Filtres réinitialisés.';

    srEl('pblSearchPageDescription').textContent = 'Page 1';
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     10. OPEN TICKET FROM SEARCH
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblSearchOpenTicket = function (encodedId) {
    const id = decodeURIComponent(String(encodedId || ''));

    const ticket = (PBL_APP.advancedSearch.results || []).find(
      function (record) {
        return srTicketId(record) === id;
      }
    );

    if (!ticket) {
      srNotify('Ticket introuvable dans les résultats actuels.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    if (typeof openTicketDetails === 'function') {
      openTicketDetails(id);
      return;
    }

    if (typeof viewTicketDetails === 'function') {
      viewTicketDetails(id);
      return;
    }

    if (typeof pblViewTicket === 'function') {
      pblViewTicket(id);
      return;
    }

    window.alert(
      'Ticket : ' + srTicketNumber(ticket) +
      '\nDate : ' + srTicketDate(ticket) +
      '\nProduit : ' + srTicketProduct(ticket) +
      '\nClient : ' + srTicketClient(ticket) +
      '\nCamion : ' + srTicketTruck(ticket) +
      '\nChauffeur : ' + srTicketDriver(ticket) +
      '\nPoids net : ' + srTicketKg(ticket) + ' KG'
    );
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     11. EXPORT SEARCH RESULTS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function srCsvCell(value) {
    return '"' + String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
  }

  window.pblExportSearchCSV = function () {
    const rows = PBL_APP.advancedSearch.results || [];

    if (!rows.length) {
      srNotify('Aucun résultat à exporter.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    const headers = [
      'Date',
      'Numero bon',
      'Produit',
      'Client',
      'Camion',
      'Chauffeur',
      'Poids net KG',
      'Tonnes',
      'Statut'
    ];

    const lines = [
      headers.map(srCsvCell).join(';')
    ];

    rows.forEach(function (ticket) {
      const kg = srTicketKg(ticket);

      lines.push([
        srTicketDate(ticket),
        srTicketNumber(ticket),
        srTicketProduct(ticket),
        srTicketClient(ticket),
        srTicketTruck(ticket),
        srTicketDriver(ticket),
        Math.round(kg),
        (kg / 1000).toFixed(3),
        srTicketStatus(ticket)
      ].map(srCsvCell).join(';'));
    });

    const blob = new Blob(
      ['\uFEFF' + lines.join('\r\n')],
      { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
    );

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');

    link.href = url;
    link.download =
      'PBL_RECHERCHE_' +
      new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) +
      '.csv';

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    srNotify('Export CSV préparé.', 'success');
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     12. SAVE SEARCH
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblSaveCurrentSearch = async function () {
    const filters = srReadFilters();

    const name = window.prompt(
      'Donnez un nom à cette recherche :',
      'Ma recherche'
    );

    if (name === null) return;

    if (!name.trim()) {
      srNotify('Le nom de la recherche est obligatoire.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    const payload = {
      name: name.trim(),
      filters: filters,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    try {
      const result = await srCall(
        PBL_SEARCH_APIS.save,
        [payload]
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Sauvegarde refusée.');
      }

      srNotify('Recherche sauvegardée.', 'success');
      await pblLoadSavedSearches();

    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('PBL saved-search API unavailable:', error);

      const saved = PBL_APP.advancedSearch.savedSearches || [];

      saved.push({
        id: 'local-' + Date.now(),
        name: payload.name,
        filters: filters,
        localOnly: true
      });

      PBL_APP.advancedSearch.savedSearches = saved;

      srRenderSavedSearches(saved);

      srNotify(
        'Recherche sauvegardée localement dans cette session uniquement.',
        'warning'
      );
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     13. LOAD SAVED SEARCHES
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblLoadSavedSearches = async function () {
    try {
      const result = await srCall(
        PBL_SEARCH_APIS.saved,
        []
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Chargement refusé.');
      }

      const records = srGetRows(result);

      PBL_APP.advancedSearch.savedSearches = records;
      srRenderSavedSearches(records);

      return records;

    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('PBL saved searches unavailable:', error);

      const records =
        PBL_APP.advancedSearch.savedSearches || [];

      srRenderSavedSearches(records);
      return records;
    }
  };

  function srRenderSavedSearches(records) {
    const container = srEl('pblSavedSearchesList');
    if (!container) return;

    if (!records || !records.length) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div class="sr-empty">Aucune recherche sauvegardée.</div>';
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = records.map(function (record, index) {
      const id = String(srFirst(record, ['id', 'searchId'], index));
      const name = srFirst(record, ['name', 'title'], 'Recherche sans nom');

      return `
        <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;
          justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;padding:12px;
          border-bottom:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.16);">

          <div>
            <strong>${srEscape(name)}</strong>
            <div class="sr-muted">
              ${record.localOnly ? 'Sauvegarde locale' : 'Sauvegarde serveur'}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="sr-actions">
            <button type="button"
              onclick="pblApplySavedSearch(${index})">
              Charger
            </button>
            <button type="button"
              onclick="pblDeleteSavedSearch('${encodeURIComponent(id)}')">
              Supprimer
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     14. APPLY SAVED SEARCH
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblApplySavedSearch = function (index) {
    const record =
      (PBL_APP.advancedSearch.savedSearches || [])[index];

    if (!record) {
      srNotify('Recherche sauvegardée introuvable.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    const filters = record.filters || record.criteria || {};

    const mapping = {
      pblSearchQuery: ['query'],
      pblSearchTicket: ['ticketNumber', 'ticketNo'],
      pblSearchProduct: ['product'],
      pblSearchClient: ['client'],
      pblSearchTruck: ['truckRegistration', 'truck'],
      pblSearchDriver: ['driver'],
      pblSearchStart: ['startDate', 'dateFrom'],
      pblSearchEnd: ['endDate', 'dateTo'],
      pblSearchStatus: ['status'],
      pblSearchMinKg: ['minKg'],
      pblSearchMaxKg: ['maxKg'],
      pblSearchPageSize: ['pageSize']
    };

    Object.keys(mapping).forEach(function (elementId) {
      const el = srEl(elementId);
      if (!el) return;

      const value = srFirst(filters, mapping[elementId], '');

      if (value !== undefined && value !== null) {
        el.value = value;
      }
    });

    PBL_APP.advancedSearch.page = 1;

    pblRunAdvancedSearch();
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     15. DELETE SAVED SEARCH
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblDeleteSavedSearch = async function (encodedId) {
    const id = decodeURIComponent(String(encodedId || ''));

    if (!window.confirm('Supprimer cette recherche sauvegardée ?')) {
      return;
    }

    const records = PBL_APP.advancedSearch.savedSearches || [];

    const record = records.find(function (item) {
      return String(srFirst(item, ['id', 'searchId'], '')) === id;
    });

    if (record && record.localOnly) {
      PBL_APP.advancedSearch.savedSearches =
        records.filter(function (item) {
          return item !== record;
        });

      srRenderSavedSearches(PBL_APP.advancedSearch.savedSearches);
      srNotify('Recherche locale supprimée.', 'success');
      return;
    }

    try {
      const result = await srCall(
        PBL_SEARCH_APIS.deleteSaved,
        [id]
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Suppression refusée.');
      }

      await pblLoadSavedSearches();
      srNotify('Recherche supprimée.', 'success');

    } catch (error) {
      srNotify('Suppression impossible : ' + error.message, 'error');
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     16. QUICK SEARCH FROM OTHER PAGES
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblSearchTicketNumber = function (ticketNumber) {
    const page = srFindSearchPage();

    if (typeof showPage === 'function') {
      showPage('search');
    }

    if (!page) return;

    const input = srEl('pblSearchTicket');

    if (input) {
      input.value = String(ticketNumber || '');
    }

    PBL_APP.advancedSearch.page = 1;

    setTimeout(function () {
      pblRunAdvancedSearch();
    }, 100);
  };

  window.pblSearchByProduct = function (product) {
    if (typeof showPage === 'function') {
      showPage('search');
    }

    const input = srEl('pblSearchProduct');

    if (input) input.value = String(product || '');

    PBL_APP.advancedSearch.page = 1;

    setTimeout(pblRunAdvancedSearch, 100);
  };

  window.pblSearchByClient = function (client) {
    if (typeof showPage === 'function') {
      showPage('search');
    }

    const input = srEl('pblSearchClient');

    if (input) input.value = String(client || '');

    PBL_APP.advancedSearch.page = 1;

    setTimeout(pblRunAdvancedSearch, 100);
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     17. INITIALIZATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblInitializeAdvancedSearch = function () {
    PBL_APP.data = PBL_APP.data || {};
    PBL_APP.advancedSearch.results =
      PBL_APP.advancedSearch.results || [];

    srEnsureInterface();

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
      pblInitializeAdvancedSearch();
    });
  } else {
    pblInitializeAdvancedSearch();
  }

  console.log('PONT BASCULE LIVE — Advanced Search loaded.');

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 10
   NOTIFICATIONS, ALERTS & LIVE ACTIVITY CENTER
   ============================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     1. NOTIFICATION STATE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  PBL_APP.notifications = PBL_APP.notifications || {
    items: [],
    activities: [],
    alerts: [],
    unreadCount: 0,
    filter: 'ALL',
    lastRefresh: null,
    loading: false,
    refreshInterval: 30000
  };

  const PBL_NOTIFICATION_APIS = {
    notifications: [
      'apiPbl8GetNotifications',
      'pbl8GetNotifications',
      'apiGetNotifications',
      'getNotifications'
    ],
    activities: [
      'apiPbl8GetRecentActivity',
      'pbl8GetRecentActivity',
      'apiGetRecentActivity',
      'getRecentActivity'
    ],
    alerts: [
      'apiPbl8GetAlerts',
      'pbl8GetAlerts',
      'apiGetAlerts',
      'getAlerts'
    ],
    read: [
      'apiMarkNotificationRead',
      'markNotificationRead',
      'pbl8MarkNotificationRead'
    ],
    readAll: [
      'apiMarkAllNotificationsRead',
      'markAllNotificationsRead',
      'pbl8MarkAllNotificationsRead'
    ],
    delete: [
      'apiDeleteNotification',
      'deleteNotification',
      'pbl8DeleteNotification'
    ]
  };

  let pblNotificationTimer = null;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     2. HELPERS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function ntEl(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function ntFirst(record, keys, fallback) {
    record = record || {};

    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const value = record[keys[i]];

      if (value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== '') {
        return value;
      }
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  function ntEscape(value) {
    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(value == null ? '' : String(value));
    }

    return String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
  }

  function ntCall(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Connexion au serveur indisponible.')
      );
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function ntNotify(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else if (typeof showToast === 'function') {
      showToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL NOTIFICATION]', message);
    }
  }

  function ntGetRows(result) {
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result;
    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return [];

    const candidates = [
      result.notifications,
      result.activities,
      result.alerts,
      result.items,
      result.records,
      result.results,
      result.data
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
      if (Array.isArray(candidates[i])) return candidates[i];
    }

    return [];
  }

  function ntId(record) {
    return String(ntFirst(record, [
      'id',
      'notificationId',
      'activityId',
      'alertId',
      'uuid'
    ], ''));
  }

  function ntTitle(record) {
    return String(ntFirst(record, [
      'title',
      'name',
      'subject',
      'event'
    ], 'Notification'));
  }

  function ntMessage(record) {
    return String(ntFirst(record, [
      'message',
      'description',
      'details',
      'text'
    ], ''));
  }

  function ntType(record) {
    return String(ntFirst(record, [
      'type',
      'category',
      'level'
    ], 'INFO')).toUpperCase();
  }

  function ntIsRead(record) {
    const value = ntFirst(record, [
      'read',
      'isRead',
      'seen',
      'isSeen'
    ], false);

    return value === true ||
      String(value).toLowerCase() === 'true' ||
      String(value).toUpperCase() === 'READ';
  }

  function ntDate(record) {
    return String(ntFirst(record, [
      'createdAt',
      'date',
      'timestamp',
      'time'
    ], ''));
  }

  function ntDateLabel(value) {
    if (!value) return 'Date inconnue';

    const date = new Date(value);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
      return String(value);
    }

    return date.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
      timeZone: 'Africa/Casablanca',
      year: 'numeric',
      month: '2-digit',
      day: '2-digit',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit'
    });
  }

  function ntSeverity(record) {
    const type = ntType(record);

    if (
      type.includes('CRITICAL') ||
      type.includes('ERROR') ||
      type.includes('DANGER')
    ) return 'critical';

    if (
      type.includes('WARNING') ||
      type.includes('WARN')
    ) return 'warning';

    if (
      type.includes('SUCCESS') ||
      type.includes('COMPLETE')
    ) return 'success';

    return 'info';
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     3. NOTIFICATION CENTER INTERFACE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function ntFindPage() {
    return ntEl('notificationsPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="notifications"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-notifications');
  }

  function ntEnsureInterface() {
    let root = ntEl('pblNotificationCenter');

    if (root) return root;

    const page = ntFindPage();

    if (!page) {
      console.warn('PBL notifications page container was not found.');
      return null;
    }

    root = document.createElement('div');
    root.id = 'pblNotificationCenter';

    root.innerHTML = `
      <style>
        #pblNotificationCenter {
          width: 100%;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-panel {
          padding: 16px;
          margin-bottom: 16px;
          border: 1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.2);
          border-radius: 12px;
          background: rgba(20,30,46,.5);
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-header {
          display: flex;
          flex-wrap: wrap;
          justify-content: space-between;
          align-items: center;
          gap: 12px;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-actions {
          display: flex;
          flex-wrap: wrap;
          gap: 8px;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter button,
        #pblNotificationCenter select {
          padding: 9px 12px;
          border-radius: 7px;
          border: 1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.3);
          background: rgba(8,15,27,.7);
          color: inherit;
          cursor: pointer;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-counts {
          display: grid;
          grid-template-columns: repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));
          gap: 12px;
          margin-top: 15px;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-count {
          padding: 15px;
          border-radius: 10px;
          border: 1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.2);
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-count-label {
          opacity: .7;
          font-size: 12px;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-count-value {
          font-size: 25px;
          font-weight: 700;
          margin-top: 5px;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-toolbar {
          display: flex;
          flex-wrap: wrap;
          gap: 10px;
          align-items: center;
          margin-bottom: 13px;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-item {
          display: flex;
          gap: 13px;
          padding: 14px 10px;
          border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.15);
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-item.unread {
          background: rgba(37,99,235,.08);
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-indicator {
          width: 9px;
          height: 9px;
          border-radius: 50%;
          flex: 0 0 9px;
          margin-top: 6px;
          background: #64748b;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-indicator.critical {
          background: #ef4444;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-indicator.warning {
          background: #f59e0b;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-indicator.success {
          background: #22c55e;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-content {
          flex: 1;
          min-width: 0;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-title {
          font-weight: 700;
          margin-bottom: 5px;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-message {
          opacity: .8;
          overflow-wrap: anywhere;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-meta {
          font-size: 11px;
          opacity: .6;
          margin-top: 7px;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-item-actions {
          display: flex;
          flex-direction: column;
          gap: 5px;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-empty {
          padding: 28px;
          text-align: center;
          opacity: .65;
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-critical-panel {
          border-color: rgba(239,68,68,.45);
        }

        #pblNotificationCenter .nt-alert {
          padding: 11px;
          margin: 8px 0;
          border-radius: 8px;
          background: rgba(239,68,68,.08);
          border: 1px solid rgba(239,68,68,.2);
        }

        @media(max-width:600px) {
          #pblNotificationCenter .nt-counts {
            grid-template-columns: 1fr;
          }

          #pblNotificationCenter .nt-item {
            flex-wrap: wrap;
          }
        }
      </style>

      <div class="nt-panel">
        <div class="nt-header">
          <div>
            <h3 style="margin:0;">Centre de notifications</h3>
            <div style="opacity:.65;font-size:12px;margin-top:5px;"
              id="pblNotificationLastRefresh">
              En attente de synchronisation
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="nt-actions">
            <button type="button" onclick="pblRefreshNotifications()">
              Actualiser
            </button>

            <button type="button" onclick="pblMarkAllNotificationsRead()">
              Tout marquer comme lu
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="nt-counts">
          <div class="nt-count">
            <div class="nt-count-label">NON LUES</div>
            <div class="nt-count-value" id="pblUnreadNotificationCount">0</div>
          </div>

          <div class="nt-count">
            <div class="nt-count-label">ALERTES ACTIVES</div>
            <div class="nt-count-value" id="pblActiveAlertCount">0</div>
          </div>

          <div class="nt-count">
            <div class="nt-count-label">ACTIVITÉS RÉCENTES</div>
            <div class="nt-count-value" id="pblActivityCount">0</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="nt-panel nt-critical-panel">
        <div class="nt-header">
          <h3 style="margin:0;">Alertes système</h3>
          <span id="pblAlertStatus" style="font-size:12px;opacity:.7;">
            Surveillance
          </span>
        </div>

        <div id="pblAlertList">
          <div class="nt-empty">Aucune alerte chargée.</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="nt-panel">
        <div class="nt-toolbar">
          <h3 style="margin:0;">Notifications</h3>

          <select id="pblNotificationFilter">
            <option value="ALL">Toutes</option>
            <option value="UNREAD">Non lues</option>
            <option value="READ">Lues</option>
            <option value="CRITICAL">Critiques</option>
            <option value="WARNING">Avertissements</option>
            <option value="SUCCESS">Succès</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        <div id="pblNotificationList">
          <div class="nt-empty">Aucune notification chargée.</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="nt-panel">
        <div class="nt-header">
          <h3 style="margin:0;">Journal d'activité récent</h3>
          <button type="button" onclick="pblRefreshNotifications()">
            Actualiser
          </button>
        </div>

        <div id="pblRecentActivityList">
          <div class="nt-empty">Aucune activité chargée.</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    page.appendChild(root);

    const filter = ntEl('pblNotificationFilter');

    if (filter) {
      filter.addEventListener('change', function () {
        PBL_APP.notifications.filter = this.value;
        ntRenderNotifications();
      });
    }

    return root;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     4. NORMALIZE NOTIFICATION COLLECTIONS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function ntNormalize(record, index, category) {
    record = record || {};

    return Object.assign({}, record, {
      id: ntId(record) || category + '-' + index,
      title: ntTitle(record),
      message: ntMessage(record),
      type: ntType(record),
      createdAt: ntDate(record),
      read: ntIsRead(record),
      category: category
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     5. LOAD NOTIFICATIONS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  async function ntLoadNotifications() {
    try {
      const result = await ntCall(
        PBL_NOTIFICATION_APIS.notifications,
        []
      );

      const rows = ntGetRows(result);

      PBL_APP.notifications.items = rows.map(function (record, index) {
        return ntNormalize(record, index, 'NOTIFICATION');
      });

      return PBL_APP.notifications.items;

    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Notification API unavailable:', error);
      return PBL_APP.notifications.items || [];
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     6. LOAD ACTIVITIES
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  async function ntLoadActivities() {
    try {
      const result = await ntCall(
        PBL_NOTIFICATION_APIS.activities,
        []
      );

      const rows = ntGetRows(result);

      PBL_APP.notifications.activities =
        rows.map(function (record, index) {
          return ntNormalize(record, index, 'ACTIVITY');
        });

      return PBL_APP.notifications.activities;

    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Activity API unavailable:', error);
      return PBL_APP.notifications.activities || [];
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     7. LOAD ALERTS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  async function ntLoadAlerts() {
    try {
      const result = await ntCall(
        PBL_NOTIFICATION_APIS.alerts,
        []
      );

      const rows = ntGetRows(result);

      PBL_APP.notifications.alerts =
        rows.map(function (record, index) {
          return ntNormalize(record, index, 'ALERT');
        });

      return PBL_APP.notifications.alerts;

    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Alert API unavailable:', error);
      return PBL_APP.notifications.alerts || [];
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     8. REFRESH ALL NOTIFICATION DATA
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblRefreshNotifications = async function () {
    if (PBL_APP.notifications.loading) return;

    PBL_APP.notifications.loading = true;

    try {
      await Promise.all([
        ntLoadNotifications(),
        ntLoadActivities(),
        ntLoadAlerts()
      ]);

      ntRenderNotifications();
      ntRenderActivities();
      ntRenderAlerts();
      ntUpdateCounters();

      PBL_APP.notifications.lastRefresh = new Date().toISOString();

      const refresh = ntEl('pblNotificationLastRefresh');

      if (refresh) {
        refresh.textContent =
          'Dernière synchronisation : ' +
          ntDateLabel(PBL_APP.notifications.lastRefresh);
      }

      return true;

    } finally {
      PBL_APP.notifications.loading = false;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     9. FILTER NOTIFICATIONS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function ntFilteredItems() {
    const items = PBL_APP.notifications.items || [];
    const filter = PBL_APP.notifications.filter || 'ALL';

    return items.filter(function (item) {
      const severity = ntSeverity(item);

      if (filter === 'UNREAD') return !item.read;
      if (filter === 'READ') return item.read;
      if (filter === 'CRITICAL') return severity === 'critical';
      if (filter === 'WARNING') return severity === 'warning';
      if (filter === 'SUCCESS') return severity === 'success';

      return true;
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     10. RENDER NOTIFICATIONS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function ntRenderNotifications() {
    const container = ntEl('pblNotificationList');
    if (!container) return;

    const items = ntFilteredItems();

    if (!items.length) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div class="nt-empty">Aucune notification pour ce filtre.</div>';
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = items.map(function (item) {
      const severity = ntSeverity(item);
      const id = encodeURIComponent(item.id);

      return `
        <div class="nt-item ${item.read ? '' : 'unread'}">
          <div class="nt-indicator ${severity}"></div>

          <div class="nt-content">
            <div class="nt-title">${ntEscape(item.title)}</div>
            <div class="nt-message">${ntEscape(item.message)}</div>
            <div class="nt-meta">
              ${ntEscape(ntDateLabel(item.createdAt))}
              · ${ntEscape(item.type)}
              · ${item.read ? 'Lue' : 'Non lue'}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="nt-item-actions">
            ${
              item.read
                ? ''
                : `<button type="button"
                    onclick="pblMarkNotificationRead('${id}')">
                    Marquer lue
                  </button>`
            }

            <button type="button"
              onclick="pblDeleteNotification('${id}')">
              Supprimer
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     11. RENDER ACTIVITIES
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function ntRenderActivities() {
    const container = ntEl('pblRecentActivityList');
    if (!container) return;

    const activities = PBL_APP.notifications.activities || [];

    if (!activities.length) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div class="nt-empty">Aucune activité récente disponible.</div>';
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = activities.map(function (item) {
      return `
        <div class="nt-item">
          <div class="nt-indicator ${ntSeverity(item)}"></div>

          <div class="nt-content">
            <div class="nt-title">${ntEscape(item.title)}</div>
            <div class="nt-message">${ntEscape(item.message)}</div>
            <div class="nt-meta">
              ${ntEscape(ntDateLabel(item.createdAt))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     12. RENDER ALERTS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function ntRenderAlerts() {
    const container = ntEl('pblAlertList');
    if (!container) return;

    const alerts = PBL_APP.notifications.alerts || [];

    const active = alerts.filter(function (alert) {
      const status = String(ntFirst(alert, [
        'status',
        'state'
      ], 'ACTIVE')).toUpperCase();

      return ![
        'RESOLVED',
        'CLOSED',
        'ARCHIVED',
        'INACTIVE'
      ].includes(status);
    });

    if (!active.length) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div class="nt-empty">Aucune alerte active.</div>';
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = active.map(function (alert) {
      return `
        <div class="nt-alert">
          <strong>${ntEscape(alert.title)}</strong>
          <div>${ntEscape(alert.message)}</div>
          <div class="nt-meta">
            ${ntEscape(ntDateLabel(alert.createdAt))}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     13. UPDATE COUNTERS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function ntUpdateCounters() {
    const items = PBL_APP.notifications.items || [];
    const alerts = PBL_APP.notifications.alerts || [];
    const activities = PBL_APP.notifications.activities || [];

    const unread = items.filter(function (item) {
      return !item.read;
    }).length;

    const activeAlerts = alerts.filter(function (alert) {
      const status = String(ntFirst(alert, [
        'status',
        'state'
      ], 'ACTIVE')).toUpperCase();

      return ![
        'RESOLVED',
        'CLOSED',
        'ARCHIVED',
        'INACTIVE'
      ].includes(status);
    }).length;

    PBL_APP.notifications.unreadCount = unread;

    const unreadEl = ntEl('pblUnreadNotificationCount');
    const alertEl = ntEl('pblActiveAlertCount');
    const activityEl = ntEl('pblActivityCount');

    if (unreadEl) unreadEl.textContent = String(unread);
    if (alertEl) alertEl.textContent = String(activeAlerts);
    if (activityEl) activityEl.textContent = String(activities.length);

    /* Update common header/sidebar counters if present. */
    [
      'notificationBadge',
      'notificationCount',
      'sidebarNotificationCount',
      'headerNotificationCount'
    ].forEach(function (id) {
      const badge = ntEl(id);

      if (badge) {
        badge.textContent = String(unread);
        badge.style.display = unread > 0 ? '' : 'none';
      }
    });

    const alertStatus = ntEl('pblAlertStatus');

    if (alertStatus) {
      alertStatus.textContent =
        activeAlerts > 0
          ? activeAlerts + ' alerte(s) active(s)'
          : 'Aucun incident actif';
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     14. MARK ONE NOTIFICATION AS READ
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblMarkNotificationRead = async function (encodedId) {
    const id = decodeURIComponent(String(encodedId || ''));

    const item = PBL_APP.notifications.items.find(function (record) {
      return String(record.id) === id;
    });

    if (!item) return;

    try {
      const result = await ntCall(
        PBL_NOTIFICATION_APIS.read,
        [id]
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Action refusée.');
      }

      item.read = true;

      ntRenderNotifications();
      ntUpdateCounters();

      ntNotify('Notification marquée comme lue.', 'success');

    } catch (error) {
      ntNotify('Impossible de marquer cette notification : ' + error.message, 'error');
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     15. MARK ALL AS READ
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblMarkAllNotificationsRead = async function () {
    try {
      const result = await ntCall(
        PBL_NOTIFICATION_APIS.readAll,
        []
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Action refusée.');
      }

      PBL_APP.notifications.items.forEach(function (item) {
        item.read = true;
      });

      ntRenderNotifications();
      ntUpdateCounters();

      ntNotify('Toutes les notifications sont marquées comme lues.', 'success');

    } catch (error) {
      ntNotify('Action impossible : ' + error.message, 'error');
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     16. DELETE NOTIFICATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblDeleteNotification = async function (encodedId) {
    const id = decodeURIComponent(String(encodedId || ''));

    if (!window.confirm('Supprimer cette notification ?')) return;

    try {
      const result = await ntCall(
        PBL_NOTIFICATION_APIS.delete,
        [id]
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Suppression refusée.');
      }

      PBL_APP.notifications.items =
        PBL_APP.notifications.items.filter(function (item) {
          return String(item.id) !== id;
        });

      ntRenderNotifications();
      ntUpdateCounters();

      ntNotify('Notification supprimée.', 'success');

    } catch (error) {
      ntNotify('Suppression impossible : ' + error.message, 'error');
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     17. LOCAL NOTIFICATION CREATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblAddLocalNotification = function (title, message, type) {
    const record = {
      id: 'local-' + Date.now() + '-' + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10000),
      title: String(title || 'Notification'),
      message: String(message || ''),
      type: String(type || 'INFO').toUpperCase(),
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      read: false,
      localOnly: true
    };

    PBL_APP.notifications.items.unshift(record);

    ntRenderNotifications();
    ntUpdateCounters();

    return record;
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     18. WEIGHING EVENT NOTIFICATIONS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblNotifyWeighingCompleted = function (ticket) {
    ticket = ticket || {};

    const number = ntFirst(ticket, [
      'ticketNumber',
      'ticketNo',
      'number'
    ], 'Nouveau ticket');

    const tonnes = Number(
      ntFirst(ticket, ['netTonnes'], 0)
    );

    pblAddLocalNotification(
      'Pesée terminée',
      'Ticket ' + number +
      ' — poids net : ' + tonnes.toFixed(3) + ' tonnes.',
      'SUCCESS'
    );
  };

  window.pblNotifyTicketCancelled = function (ticketNumber) {
    pblAddLocalNotification(
      'Ticket annulé',
      'Le ticket ' + ticketNumber + ' a été annulé.',
      'WARNING'
    );
  };

  window.pblNotifySystemWarning = function (message) {
    pblAddLocalNotification(
      'Avertissement système',
      message,
      'WARNING'
    );
  };

  window.pblNotifySystemCritical = function (message) {
    pblAddLocalNotification(
      'Alerte critique',
      message,
      'CRITICAL'
    );
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     19. AUTOMATIC REFRESH
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblStartNotificationRefresh = function () {
    if (pblNotificationTimer) {
      clearInterval(pblNotificationTimer);
    }

    pblNotificationTimer = setInterval(function () {
      if (document.hidden) return;

      pblRefreshNotifications().catch(function (error) {
        console.warn('Notification refresh error:', error);
      });

    }, PBL_APP.notifications.refreshInterval);
  };

  window.pblStopNotificationRefresh = function () {
    if (pblNotificationTimer) {
      clearInterval(pblNotificationTimer);
      pblNotificationTimer = null;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     20. INITIALIZATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblInitializeNotificationCenter = function () {
    ntEnsureInterface();

    ntRenderNotifications();
    ntRenderActivities();
    ntRenderAlerts();
    ntUpdateCounters();

    pblRefreshNotifications().catch(function (error) {
      console.warn('PBL notification initialization:', error);
    });

    pblStartNotificationRefresh();

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
      pblInitializeNotificationCenter();
    });
  } else {
    pblInitializeNotificationCenter();
  }

  window.addEventListener('beforeunload', function () {
    pblStopNotificationRefresh();
  });

  console.log('PONT BASCULE LIVE — Notification Center loaded.');

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 11
   SETTINGS & SYSTEM CONFIGURATION CENTER
   ============================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};
  PBL_APP.settings = PBL_APP.settings || {
    values: {},
    loaded: false,
    saving: false,
    modified: false
  };

  const SETTINGS_APIS = {
    get: [
      'apiGetSettings',
      'apiPbl12GetSettings',
      'pbl12GetSettings',
      'getSettings'
    ],
    save: [
      'apiSaveSettings',
      'apiPbl12SaveSettings',
      'pbl12SaveSettings',
      'saveSettings'
    ],
    manualPermission: [
      'apiSetManualWeighingPermission',
      'pbl12SetManualWeighingPermission',
      'setManualWeighingPermission'
    ],
    information: [
      'apiApplicationInformation',
      'apiGetApplicationInformation',
      'getApplicationInformation'
    ]
  };

  let settingsRoot = null;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     1. HELPERS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function stEl(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function stEscape(value) {
    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(value == null ? '' : String(value));
    }

    return String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
  }

  function stCall(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(new Error('Backend indisponible.'));
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function stToast(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL SETTINGS]', message);
    }
  }

  function stObject(value) {
    if (value && typeof value === 'object') return value;
    return {};
  }

  function stFindSettingsPage() {
    return stEl('settingsPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="settings"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-settings');
  }

  function stRead(id) {
    const element = stEl(id);
    if (!element) return '';

    if (element.type === 'checkbox') {
      return element.checked;
    }

    return element.value;
  }

  function stSet(id, value) {
    const element = stEl(id);
    if (!element || value === undefined || value === null) return;

    if (element.type === 'checkbox') {
      element.checked =
        value === true ||
        String(value).toLowerCase() === 'true';
    } else {
      element.value = String(value);
    }
  }

  function stSetStatus(message, type) {
    const status = stEl('pblSettingsStatus');
    if (!status) return;

    status.textContent = message;
    status.dataset.status = type || 'info';
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     2. SETTINGS INTERFACE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function stEnsureInterface() {
    settingsRoot = stEl('pblSettingsCenter');

    if (settingsRoot) return settingsRoot;

    const page = stFindSettingsPage();

    if (!page) {
      console.warn('Settings page container not found.');
      return null;
    }

    settingsRoot = document.createElement('div');
    settingsRoot.id = 'pblSettingsCenter';

    settingsRoot.innerHTML = `
      <style>
        #pblSettingsCenter {
          width:100%;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter .st-panel {
          padding:17px;
          margin-bottom:15px;
          border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.2);
          border-radius:12px;
          background:rgba(20,30,46,.5);
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter .st-panel h3 {
          margin:0 0 15px 0;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter .st-grid {
          display:grid;
          grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));
          gap:13px;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter .st-field {
          display:flex;
          flex-direction:column;
          gap:6px;
          min-width:0;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter .st-field label {
          font-size:12px;
          opacity:.75;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter input,
        #pblSettingsCenter select,
        #pblSettingsCenter textarea {
          width:100%;
          box-sizing:border-box;
          padding:10px;
          border-radius:7px;
          border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.3);
          background:rgba(8,15,27,.75);
          color:inherit;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter textarea {
          min-height:75px;
          resize:vertical;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter .st-full {
          grid-column:1/-1;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter .st-toggle {
          display:flex;
          align-items:center;
          justify-content:space-between;
          gap:15px;
          padding:12px 0;
          border-bottom:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.12);
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter .st-toggle input {
          width:20px;
          height:20px;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter .st-actions {
          display:flex;
          flex-wrap:wrap;
          gap:9px;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter button {
          padding:10px 14px;
          border-radius:7px;
          border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.3);
          background:rgba(8,15,27,.8);
          color:inherit;
          cursor:pointer;
        }

        #pblSettingsCenter .st-primary {
          background:#2563eb;
          border-color:#2563eb;
          color:white;

        }
