          font-size:11px;
          opacity:.6;
          line-height:1.5;
          margin-top:8px;

          opacity:.8;
        }

        @media(max-width:650px) {
          #pblSettingsCenter .st-grid {
            grid-template-columns:1fr;
          }

          #pblSettingsCenter .st-full {
            grid-column:auto;
          }
        }
      </style>

      <div class="st-panel">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <div>
            <h3 style="margin:0;">Configuration générale</h3>
            <div class="st-help">
              Paramètres de fonctionnement de PONT BASCULE LIVE.
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="st-actions">
            <button type="button" onclick="pblLoadSystemSettings()">
              Recharger
            </button>
            <button type="button" class="st-primary"
              onclick="pblSaveSystemSettings()">
              Enregistrer les paramètres
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div id="pblSettingsStatus" class="st-status">
          Chargement des paramètres...
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="st-panel">
        <h3>Identité de l'entreprise</h3>

        <div class="st-grid">
          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Nom de l'entreprise</label>
            <input id="stCompanyName" maxlength="120">
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">

            <input id="stStationName" maxlength="120">

          <div class="st-field">

      <div class="st-panel">

          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Capacité maximale (KG)</label>
            <input id="stScaleCapacity" type="number" min="0">
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Précision d'affichage</label>
            <select id="stScalePrecision">
              <option value="1">1 KG</option>
              <option value="2">2 KG</option>
              <option value="5">5 KG</option>
              <option value="10">10 KG</option>
              <option value="20">20 KG</option>
              <option value="50">50 KG</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Unité de pesée</label>
            <select id="stWeightUnit">
              <option value="KG">Kilogramme (KG)</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Mode de pesée</label>
            <select id="stWeighingMode">
              <option value="TWO_PASS">Deux passages</option>
              <option value="DIRECT">Poids direct</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Fuseau horaire</label>

              <option value="Africa/Casablanca">Africa/Casablanca</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="st-help">
          La configuration ne connecte pas physiquement un indicateur de pesage.
          L'intégration matérielle doit être réalisée et testée séparément.
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="st-panel">
        <h3>Tickets et numérotation</h3>

        <div class="st-grid">
          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Préfixe des tickets</label>
            <input id="stTicketPrefix" maxlength="15">
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Prochain numéro</label>
            <input id="stNextTicketNumber" type="number" min="1">
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Nombre de décimales en tonnes</label>
            <select id="stTonnesDecimals">
              <option value="0">0</option>
              <option value="1">1</option>
              <option value="2">2</option>
              <option value="3">3</option>
              <option value="4">4</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Langue des documents</label>
            <select id="stDocumentLanguage">
              <option value="FR">Français</option>
              <option value="AR">العربية</option>
              <option value="EN">English</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="st-panel">
        <h3>Interface et affichage</h3>

        <div class="st-toggle">
          <div>
            <strong>Afficher l'horloge en direct</strong>
            <div class="st-help">Date et heure de Casablanca.</div>
          </div>
          <input type="checkbox" id="stShowClock" checked>
        </div>

        <div class="st-toggle">
          <div>
            <strong>Actualisation automatique du tableau de bord</strong>
            <div class="st-help">Rafraîchissement périodique des indicateurs.</div>
          </div>
          <input type="checkbox" id="stAutoDashboard" checked>
        </div>

        <div class="st-toggle">
          <div>
            <strong>Notifications sonores</strong>
            <div class="st-help">Activer les sons d'événements, si disponibles.</div>
          </div>
          <input type="checkbox" id="stSoundNotifications">
        </div>

        <div class="st-toggle">
          <div>
            <strong>Afficher les confirmations avant annulation</strong>
            <div class="st-help">Évite les annulations accidentelles.</div>
          </div>
          <input type="checkbox" id="stConfirmCancellation" checked>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="st-panel">
        <h3>Sécurité et autorisations</h3>

        <div class="st-toggle">
          <div>
            <strong>Saisie manuelle du poids</strong>
            <div class="st-help">
              Autorisation sensible. Désactivée par défaut.
              Elle ne doit jamais être exposée dans l'écran normal de pesée.
            </div>
          </div>
          <input type="checkbox" id="stManualWeighing">
        </div>

        <div class="st-toggle">
          <div>
            <strong>Verrouillage automatique</strong>
            <div class="st-help">Verrouiller après une période d'inactivité.</div>
          </div>
          <input type="checkbox" id="stAutoLock" checked>
        </div>

        <div class="st-field" style="margin-top:12px;max-width:300px;">
          <label>Durée avant verrouillage (minutes)</label>
          <input id="stAutoLockMinutes" type="number" min="1" max="240" value="30">
        </div>

        <div class="st-actions" style="margin-top:15px;">
          <button type="button" onclick="pblOpenPinChange()">
            Modifier le PIN
          </button>

          <button type="button" class="st-danger"
            onclick="pblLockFromSettings()">
            Verrouiller maintenant
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="st-help">
          Les changements d'autorisation doivent être vérifiés côté serveur.
          Une case cochée dans le navigateur ne constitue pas une autorisation.
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="st-panel">
        <h3>Informations système</h3>

        <div class="st-grid">
          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Application</label>
            <input value="PONT BASCULE LIVE" readonly>
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Fuseau horaire actif</label>
            <input value="Africa/Casablanca" readonly>
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>Version interface</label>
            <input value="11.0" readonly>
          </div>

          <div class="st-field">
            <label>État du système</label>
            <input id="stSystemStatus" value="Vérification..." readonly>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="st-actions" style="margin-top:15px;">
          <button type="button" onclick="pblLoadSystemInformation()">
            Vérifier le système
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="st-panel">
        <h3>Enregistrement</h3>

        <div class="st-actions">
          <button type="button" class="st-primary"
            onclick="pblSaveSystemSettings()">
            Enregistrer les paramètres
          </button>

          <button type="button"
            onclick="pblResetSettingsForm()">
            Annuler les modifications locales
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="st-help">
          Les modifications ne sont appliquées qu'après confirmation du serveur.
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    page.appendChild(settingsRoot);

    settingsRoot.querySelectorAll('input,select,textarea')
      .forEach(function (field) {
        field.addEventListener('change', function () {
          PBL_APP.settings.modified = true;
          stSetStatus('Modifications non enregistrées.', 'warning');
        });
      });

    return settingsRoot;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     3. READ SERVER SETTINGS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function stExtractSettings(result) {
    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return {};

    if (result.settings && typeof result.settings === 'object') {
      return result.settings;
    }

    if (result.data && typeof result.data === 'object') {
      return result.data;
    }

    return result;
  }

  function stMapServerSettings(settings) {
    settings = stObject(settings);

    const nestedCompany = stObject(settings.company);
    const nestedScale = stObject(settings.scale);
    const nestedTickets = stObject(settings.tickets);
    const nestedSecurity = stObject(settings.security);
    const nestedInterface = stObject(settings.interface);

    return {
      companyName:
        settings.companyName || nestedCompany.name || '',
      stationName:
        settings.stationName || nestedScale.name || 'PONT BASCULE LIVE',
      companyAddress:
        settings.companyAddress || nestedCompany.address || '',
      companyPhone:
        settings.companyPhone || nestedCompany.phone || '',
      companyEmail:
        settings.companyEmail || nestedCompany.email || '',
      companyICE:
        settings.companyICE || nestedCompany.ice || '',
      companyDescription:
        settings.companyDescription || nestedCompany.description || '',

      scaleName:
        settings.scaleName || nestedScale.name || 'PONT BASCULE LIVE',
      scaleCapacity:
        settings.scaleCapacity || nestedScale.capacityKg || '',
      scalePrecision:
        settings.scalePrecision || nestedScale.precision || 1,
      weightUnit:
        settings.weightUnit || nestedScale.unit || 'KG',
      weighingMode:
        settings.weighingMode || nestedScale.mode || 'TWO_PASS',
      timezone:
        settings.timezone || 'Africa/Casablanca',

      ticketPrefix:
        settings.ticketPrefix || nestedTickets.prefix || '',
      nextTicketNumber:
        settings.nextTicketNumber || nestedTickets.nextNumber || '',
      tonnesDecimals:
        settings.tonnesDecimals !== undefined
          ? settings.tonnesDecimals : 3,
      documentLanguage:
        settings.documentLanguage || 'FR',

      showClock:
        settings.showClock !== undefined
          ? settings.showClock : true,
      autoDashboard:
        settings.autoDashboard !== undefined
          ? settings.autoDashboard : true,
      soundNotifications:
        settings.soundNotifications || false,
      confirmCancellation:
        settings.confirmCancellation !== undefined
          ? settings.confirmCancellation : true,

      manualWeighing:
        settings.manualWeighing !== undefined
          ? settings.manualWeighing
          : nestedSecurity.manualWeighing || false,
      autoLock:
        settings.autoLock !== undefined
          ? settings.autoLock : true,
      autoLockMinutes:
        settings.autoLockMinutes || nestedSecurity.autoLockMinutes || 30
    };
  }

  function stApplySettings(settings) {
    settings = stMapServerSettings(settings);

    const mapping = {
      companyName: 'stCompanyName',
      stationName: 'stStationName',
      companyAddress: 'stCompanyAddress',
      companyPhone: 'stCompanyPhone',
      companyEmail: 'stCompanyEmail',
      companyICE: 'stCompanyICE',
      companyDescription: 'stCompanyDescription',
      scaleName: 'stScaleName',
      scaleCapacity: 'stScaleCapacity',
      scalePrecision: 'stScalePrecision',
      weightUnit: 'stWeightUnit',
      weighingMode: 'stWeighingMode',
      timezone: 'stTimezone',
      ticketPrefix: 'stTicketPrefix',
      nextTicketNumber: 'stNextTicketNumber',
      tonnesDecimals: 'stTonnesDecimals',
      documentLanguage: 'stDocumentLanguage',
      showClock: 'stShowClock',
      autoDashboard: 'stAutoDashboard',
      soundNotifications: 'stSoundNotifications',
      confirmCancellation: 'stConfirmCancellation',
      manualWeighing: 'stManualWeighing',
      autoLock: 'stAutoLock',
      autoLockMinutes: 'stAutoLockMinutes'
    };

    Object.keys(mapping).forEach(function (key) {
      stSet(mapping[key], settings[key]);
    });

    PBL_APP.settings.values = settings;
    PBL_APP.settings.loaded = true;
    PBL_APP.settings.modified = false;

    stSetStatus('Paramètres chargés.', 'success');
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     4. LOAD SETTINGS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblLoadSystemSettings = async function () {
    stEnsureInterface();
    stSetStatus('Chargement des paramètres...', 'info');

    try {
      const result = await stCall(SETTINGS_APIS.get, []);

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Chargement refusé.');
      }

      stApplySettings(stExtractSettings(result));

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      stSetStatus(
        'Chargement serveur indisponible : ' + error.message,
        'warning'
      );

      console.warn('PBL settings load:', error);
      return null;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     5. COLLECT SETTINGS FROM FORM
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function stCollectSettings() {
    return {
      companyName: stRead('stCompanyName'),
      stationName: stRead('stStationName'),
      companyAddress: stRead('stCompanyAddress'),
      companyPhone: stRead('stCompanyPhone'),
      companyEmail: stRead('stCompanyEmail'),
      companyICE: stRead('stCompanyICE'),
      companyDescription: stRead('stCompanyDescription'),

      scaleName: stRead('stScaleName'),
      scaleCapacity: Number(stRead('stScaleCapacity') || 0),
      scalePrecision: Number(stRead('stScalePrecision') || 1),
      weightUnit: 'KG',
      weighingMode: stRead('stWeighingMode'),
      timezone: 'Africa/Casablanca',

      ticketPrefix: stRead('stTicketPrefix'),
      nextTicketNumber: Number(stRead('stNextTicketNumber') || 0),
      tonnesDecimals: Number(stRead('stTonnesDecimals') || 3),
      documentLanguage: stRead('stDocumentLanguage'),

      showClock: stRead('stShowClock'),
      autoDashboard: stRead('stAutoDashboard'),
      soundNotifications: stRead('stSoundNotifications'),
      confirmCancellation: stRead('stConfirmCancellation'),

      manualWeighing: stRead('stManualWeighing'),
      autoLock: stRead('stAutoLock'),
      autoLockMinutes: Number(stRead('stAutoLockMinutes') || 30)
    };
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     6. VALIDATE SETTINGS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function stValidateSettings(settings) {
    if (
      settings.companyEmail &&
      !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(settings.companyEmail)
    ) {
      return 'Adresse email invalide.';
    }

    if (settings.scaleCapacity < 0) {
      return 'La capacité du pont-bascule doit être positive.';
    }

    if (
      settings.autoLockMinutes < 1 ||
      settings.autoLockMinutes > 240
    ) {
      return 'La durée de verrouillage doit être comprise entre 1 et 240 minutes.';
    }

    if (
      settings.tonnesDecimals < 0 ||
      settings.tonnesDecimals > 4
    ) {
      return 'Le nombre de décimales doit être compris entre 0 et 4.';
    }

    return '';
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     7. SAVE SETTINGS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblSaveSystemSettings = async function () {
    if (PBL_APP.settings.saving) return;

    const settings = stCollectSettings();
    const validation = stValidateSettings(settings);

    if (validation) {
      stToast(validation, 'error');
      return;
    }

    const access = PBL_APP.access || {};

    if (
      typeof pblHasEditAccess === 'function' &&
      !pblHasEditAccess()
    ) {
      stToast(
        'Déverrouillez le système avec le PIN avant de modifier les paramètres.',
        'warning'
      );
      return;
    }

    if (
      typeof pblHasEditAccess !== 'function' &&
      access.unlocked === false
    ) {
      stToast('Accès verrouillé.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    PBL_APP.settings.saving = true;
    stSetStatus('Enregistrement en cours...', 'info');

    try {
      const result = await stCall(
        SETTINGS_APIS.save,
        [settings]
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Enregistrement refusé.');
      }

      PBL_APP.settings.values = settings;
      PBL_APP.settings.modified = false;

      stSetStatus('Paramètres enregistrés.', 'success');
      stToast('Configuration enregistrée.', 'success');

      if (settings.manualWeighing) {
        stToast(
          'La saisie manuelle reste soumise à la validation du serveur.',
          'warning'
        );
      }

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      stSetStatus('Échec de l’enregistrement : ' + error.message, 'error');
      stToast('Impossible d’enregistrer les paramètres.', 'error');
      console.error('PBL settings save:', error);
      return null;

    } finally {
      PBL_APP.settings.saving = false;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     8. RESET LOCAL FORM
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblResetSettingsForm = function () {
    if (PBL_APP.settings.modified) {
      if (!window.confirm('Annuler toutes les modifications non enregistrées ?')) {
        return;
      }
    }

    stApplySettings(PBL_APP.settings.values || {});
    stToast('Modifications locales annulées.', 'info');
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     9. MANUAL WEIGHING PERMISSION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblUpdateManualWeighingPermission = async function (enabled) {
    if (typeof pblHasEditAccess === 'function' && !pblHasEditAccess()) {
      stSet('stManualWeighing', false);

      stToast(
        'Accès PIN requis pour modifier cette autorisation.',
        'warning'
      );

      return false;
    }

    try {
      const result = await stCall(
        SETTINGS_APIS.manualPermission,
        [Boolean(enabled)]
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Autorisation refusée.');
      }

      stToast(
        enabled
          ? 'Demande d’activation transmise au serveur.'
          : 'Demande de désactivation transmise au serveur.',
        'success'
      );

      return true;

    } catch (error) {
      stSet('stManualWeighing', false);
      stToast('Modification refusée : ' + error.message, 'error');
      return false;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     10. PIN CHANGE SHORTCUT
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblOpenPinChange = function () {
    if (typeof openChangePinModal === 'function') {
      openChangePinModal();
      return;
    }

    if (typeof pblOpenChangePinModal === 'function') {
      pblOpenChangePinModal();
      return;
    }

    const modal = stEl('changePinModal');

    if (modal) {
      modal.style.display = 'flex';
      return;
    }

    stToast('Fenêtre de modification du PIN indisponible.', 'warning');
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     11. LOCK SHORTCUT
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblLockFromSettings = async function () {
    if (!window.confirm('Verrouiller maintenant PONT BASCULE LIVE ?')) {
      return;
    }

    if (typeof apiLock === 'function') {
      try {
        await apiLock();
        stToast('Système verrouillé.', 'success');
      } catch (error) {
        stToast('Verrouillage impossible.', 'error');
      }

      return;
    }

    if (typeof pblLockApplication === 'function') {
      await pblLockApplication();
      return;
    }

    stToast('Fonction de verrouillage indisponible.', 'warning');
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     12. SYSTEM INFORMATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblLoadSystemInformation = async function () {
    const status = stEl('stSystemStatus');

    if (status) status.value = 'Vérification...';

    try {
      const result = await stCall(
        SETTINGS_APIS.information,
        []
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Vérification refusée.');
      }

      const systemStatus = result &&
        (
          result.status ||
          result.systemStatus ||
          result.health ||
          'Disponible'
        );

      if (status) status.value = String(systemStatus);

      stToast('Informations système actualisées.', 'success');

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      if (status) status.value = 'Non vérifié';

      console.warn('System information:', error);
      stToast('Informations système indisponibles.', 'warning');

      return null;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     13. HANDLE MANUAL WEIGHING CHECKBOX
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function stBindSecurityControls() {
    const manual = stEl('stManualWeighing');

    if (!manual || manual.dataset.bound === 'true') return;

    manual.dataset.bound = 'true';

    manual.addEventListener('change', async function () {
      const enabled = this.checked;

      const accepted = window.confirm(
        enabled
          ? 'Demander l’activation de la saisie manuelle des poids ? Cette opération est sensible.'
          : 'Désactiver la saisie manuelle des poids ?'
      );

      if (!accepted) {
        this.checked = !enabled;
        return;
      }

      const success = await pblUpdateManualWeighingPermission(enabled);

      if (!success) {
        this.checked = !enabled;
      }
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     14. INITIALIZATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblInitializeSettingsCenter = function () {
    stEnsureInterface();
    stBindSecurityControls();

    pblLoadSystemSettings();
    pblLoadSystemInformation();

    console.log('PONT BASCULE LIVE — Settings Center loaded.');

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
      pblInitializeSettingsCenter();
    });
  } else {
    pblInitializeSettingsCenter();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 12
   MAINTENANCE, BACKUP, RESTORE & DATA INTEGRITY
   ============================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  PBL_APP.maintenance = PBL_APP.maintenance || {
    backups: [],
    logs: [],
    diagnostics: {},
    integrity: {},
    loading: false,
    lastCheck: null
  };

  const MAINTENANCE_APIS = {
    backup: [
      'apiCreateBackup',
      'apiPbl9CreateBackup',
      'pbl9CreateBackup',
      'createBackup'
    ],
    restore: [
      'apiRestoreBackup',
      'apiPbl9RestoreBackup',
      'pbl9RestoreBackup',
      'restoreBackup'
    ],
    diagnostics: [
      'apiSystemDiagnostics',
      'apiPbl9SystemDiagnostics',
      'pbl9SystemDiagnostics',
      'apiSystemHealth'
    ],
    integrity: [
      'apiCheckDataIntegrity',
      'apiPbl9CheckDataIntegrity',
      'pbl9CheckDataIntegrity',
      'checkDataIntegrity'
    ],
    logs: [
      'apiGetMaintenanceLogs',
      'apiPbl9GetMaintenanceLogs',
      'pbl9GetMaintenanceLogs',
      'getMaintenanceLogs'
    ],
    cleanup: [
      'apiRunMaintenance',
      'apiPbl9RunMaintenance',
      'pbl9RunMaintenance',
      'runMaintenance'
    ]
  };

  let maintenanceRoot = null;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     1. HELPERS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function mtEl(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function mtEscape(value) {
    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(value == null ? '' : String(value));
    }

    return String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
  }

  function mtCall(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(new Error('Connexion serveur indisponible.'));
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function mtToast(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL MAINTENANCE]', message);
    }
  }

  function mtFindPage() {
    return mtEl('maintenancePage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="maintenance"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-maintenance');
  }

  function mtRows(result) {
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result;
    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return [];

    const possible = [
      result.items,
      result.records,
      result.logs,
      result.backups,
      result.results,
      result.data
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < possible.length; i++) {
      if (Array.isArray(possible[i])) return possible[i];
    }

    return [];
  }

  function mtValue(object, keys, fallback) {
    object = object || {};

    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const value = object[keys[i]];

      if (value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== '') {
        return value;
      }
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  function mtDate(value) {
    if (!value) return 'Date inconnue';

    const date = new Date(value);

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return String(value);

    return date.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
      timeZone: 'Africa/Casablanca',
      year: 'numeric',
      month: '2-digit',
      day: '2-digit',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit'
    });
  }

  function mtSetStatus(id, message) {
    const element = mtEl(id);
    if (element) element.textContent = message;
  }

  function mtIsSuccess(result) {
    if (!result) return false;
    if (result.success === true || result.ok === true) return true;

    return String(result.status || '').toUpperCase() === 'SUCCESS';
  }

  function mtHasEditAccess() {
    if (typeof pblHasEditAccess === 'function') {
      return pblHasEditAccess();
    }

    return Boolean(
      PBL_APP.access &&
      PBL_APP.access.unlocked
    );
  }

  function mtRequireAccess() {
    if (mtHasEditAccess()) return true;

    mtToast(
      'Déverrouillez PONT BASCULE LIVE avec le PIN pour effectuer cette opération.',
      'warning'
    );

    return false;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     2. MAINTENANCE INTERFACE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function mtEnsureInterface() {
    maintenanceRoot = mtEl('pblMaintenanceCenter');

    if (maintenanceRoot) return maintenanceRoot;

    const page = mtFindPage();

    if (!page) {
      console.warn('Maintenance page container not found.');
      return null;
    }

    maintenanceRoot = document.createElement('div');
    maintenanceRoot.id = 'pblMaintenanceCenter';

    maintenanceRoot.innerHTML = `
      <style>
        #pblMaintenanceCenter {
          width:100%;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-panel {
          padding:17px;
          margin-bottom:15px;
          border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.2);
          border-radius:12px;
          background:rgba(20,30,46,.5);
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-grid {
          display:grid;
          grid-template-columns:repeat(3,minmax(0,1fr));
          gap:12px;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-stat {
          padding:15px;
          border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.2);
          border-radius:9px;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-label {
          font-size:11px;
          opacity:.65;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-number {
          font-size:24px;
          font-weight:700;
          margin-top:7px;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-actions {
          display:flex;
          flex-wrap:wrap;
          gap:9px;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter button {
          padding:10px 14px;
          border-radius:7px;
          border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.3);
          background:rgba(8,15,27,.8);
          color:inherit;
          cursor:pointer;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter button.primary {
          background:#2563eb;
          border-color:#2563eb;
          color:white;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter button.danger {
          border-color:rgba(239,68,68,.5);
          color:#fca5a5;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-row {
          display:flex;
          justify-content:space-between;
          align-items:center;
          gap:12px;
          flex-wrap:wrap;
          padding:12px 0;
          border-bottom:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.12);
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-table-wrap {
          overflow-x:auto;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter table {
          width:100%;
          border-collapse:collapse;
          font-size:12px;
          min-width:550px;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter th,
        #pblMaintenanceCenter td {
          padding:11px;
          border-bottom:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.15);
          text-align:left;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-empty {
          padding:25px;
          text-align:center;
          opacity:.6;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-help {
          font-size:11px;
          opacity:.65;
          line-height:1.6;
          margin-top:9px;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter input[type=file] {
          max-width:100%;
          padding:10px;
        }

        #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-status {
          margin-top:10px;
          font-size:12px;
          opacity:.8;
        }

        @media(max-width:700px) {
          #pblMaintenanceCenter .mt-grid {
            grid-template-columns:1fr;
          }
        }
      </style>

      <div class="mt-panel">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <div>
            <h3 style="margin:0;">Centre de maintenance</h3>
            <div class="mt-help">
              Sauvegarde, restauration, diagnostic et contrôle des données.
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="mt-actions">
            <button type="button" onclick="pblRefreshMaintenance()">
              Actualiser
            </button>
            <button type="button" class="primary"
              onclick="pblRunFullMaintenanceCheck()">
              Lancer les vérifications
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div id="pblMaintenanceStatus" class="mt-status">
          Initialisation...
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-panel">
        <h3>État général</h3>

        <div class="mt-grid">
          <div class="mt-stat">
            <div class="mt-label">DIAGNOSTIC SYSTÈME</div>
            <div class="mt-number" id="mtDiagnosticStatus">--</div>
          </div>

          <div class="mt-stat">
            <div class="mt-label">INTÉGRITÉ DES DONNÉES</div>
            <div class="mt-number" id="mtIntegrityStatus">--</div>
          </div>

          <div class="mt-stat">
            <div class="mt-label">DERNIÈRE VÉRIFICATION</div>
            <div class="mt-number" style="font-size:14px;"
              id="mtLastCheck">--</div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-panel">
        <h3>Sauvegarde des données</h3>

        <div class="mt-actions">
          <button type="button" class="primary"
            onclick="pblCreateSystemBackup()">
            Créer une sauvegarde serveur
          </button>

          <button type="button"
            onclick="pblDownloadLocalDataBackup()">
            Télécharger les données disponibles
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="mt-help">
          La sauvegarde serveur doit être réalisée par le backend.
          Le téléchargement local ne contient que les données actuellement
          disponibles dans le navigateur.
        </div>

        <div id="mtBackupStatus" class="mt-status">
          Aucune sauvegarde demandée.
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-panel">
        <h3>Restauration</h3>

        <div class="mt-row">
          <div>
            <strong>Restaurer une sauvegarde JSON</strong>
            <div class="mt-help">
              Cette opération peut remplacer les données actuelles.
            </div>
          </div>

          <input type="file" id="mtRestoreFile" accept=".json,application/json">
        </div>

        <div class="mt-actions">
          <button type="button" class="danger"
            onclick="pblRestoreSelectedBackup()">
            Restaurer la sauvegarde sélectionnée
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="mt-help">
          N'effectuez jamais une restauration sans conserver une sauvegarde
          récente des données actuelles. Le serveur doit valider le fichier.
        </div>

        <div id="mtRestoreStatus" class="mt-status">
          Aucun fichier sélectionné.
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-panel">
        <h3>Contrôle d'intégrité</h3>

        <div class="mt-actions">
          <button type="button" class="primary"
            onclick="pblCheckDataIntegrity()">
            Vérifier les données
          </button>
        </div>

        <div id="mtIntegrityDetails" style="margin-top:12px;">
          <div class="mt-empty">Aucun contrôle effectué.</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-panel">
        <h3>Diagnostics système</h3>

        <div class="mt-actions">
          <button type="button"
            onclick="pblRunSystemDiagnostics()">
            Exécuter le diagnostic
          </button>
        </div>

        <div id="mtDiagnosticDetails" style="margin-top:12px;">
          <div class="mt-empty">Diagnostic non effectué.</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-panel">
        <h3>Historique de maintenance</h3>

        <div id="mtMaintenanceLogs">
          <div class="mt-empty">Aucun historique chargé.</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="mt-panel">
        <h3>Maintenance préventive</h3>

        <div class="mt-row">
          <div>
            <strong>Vérifier les données et les tickets</strong>
            <div class="mt-help">
              Recherche des incohérences sans modification automatique.
            </div>
          </div>

          <button type="button"
            onclick="pblRunFullMaintenanceCheck()">
            Vérifier
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="mt-row">
          <div>
            <strong>Nettoyage contrôlé</strong>
            <div class="mt-help">
              Fonction serveur facultative. Ne supprimez pas les historiques
              de pesée ou les journaux d'audit.
            </div>
          </div>

          <button type="button" class="danger"
            onclick="pblRunMaintenanceCleanup()">
            Lancer le nettoyage
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    page.appendChild(maintenanceRoot);

    const file = mtEl('mtRestoreFile');

    if (file) {
      file.addEventListener('change', function () {
        const selected = this.files && this.files[0];

        mtSetStatus(
          'mtRestoreStatus',
          selected
            ? 'Fichier sélectionné : ' + selected.name
            : 'Aucun fichier sélectionné.'
        );
      });
    }

    return maintenanceRoot;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     3. DIAGNOSTICS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblRunSystemDiagnostics = async function () {
    if (!maintenanceRoot) mtEnsureInterface();

    mtSetStatus('pblMaintenanceStatus', 'Diagnostic en cours...');

    try {
      const result = await mtCall(
        MAINTENANCE_APIS.diagnostics,
        []
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Diagnostic refusé.');
      }

      PBL_APP.maintenance.diagnostics = result || {};

      mtRenderDiagnostics(result);

      mtToast('Diagnostic système terminé.', 'success');

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      mtSetStatus(
        'pblMaintenanceStatus',
        'Diagnostic indisponible : ' + error.message
      );

      mtToast('Impossible de terminer le diagnostic.', 'warning');

      return null;
    }
  };

  function mtRenderDiagnostics(result) {
    const container = mtEl('mtDiagnosticDetails');
    const status = mtEl('mtDiagnosticStatus');

    if (!container) return;

    const data = result && result.diagnostics
      ? result.diagnostics
      : result || {};

    const rows = [];

    Object.keys(data).forEach(function (key) {
      if (
        key === 'success' ||
        key === 'message' ||
        key === 'diagnostics'
      ) return;

      const value = data[key];

      if (
        value === null ||
        typeof value === 'object'
      ) return;

      rows.push({
        name: key,
        value: String(value)
      });
    });

    if (status) {
      status.textContent =
        mtValue(data, ['status', 'health', 'systemStatus'], 'Vérifié');
    }

    if (!rows.length) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div class="mt-empty">Diagnostic reçu. Aucun détail structuré disponible.</div>';
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = rows.map(function (row) {
      return `
        <div class="mt-row">
          <span>${mtEscape(row.name)}</span>
          <strong>${mtEscape(row.value)}</strong>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     4. DATA INTEGRITY
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblCheckDataIntegrity = async function () {
    mtSetStatus('pblMaintenanceStatus', 'Vérification des données...');

    try {
      const result = await mtCall(
        MAINTENANCE_APIS.integrity,
        []
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Contrôle refusé.');
      }

      PBL_APP.maintenance.integrity = result || {};

      mtRenderIntegrity(result);

      mtToast('Contrôle d’intégrité terminé.', 'success');

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      mtSetStatus(
        'pblMaintenanceStatus',
        'Contrôle indisponible : ' + error.message
      );

      mtToast('Impossible de vérifier les données.', 'warning');

      return null;
    }
  };

  function mtRenderIntegrity(result) {
    const container = mtEl('mtIntegrityDetails');
    const status = mtEl('mtIntegrityStatus');

    if (!container) return;

    const data = result && result.integrity
      ? result.integrity
      : result || {};

    const checks = [
      ['Total tickets', ['totalTickets', 'ticketCount']],
      ['Tickets valides', ['validTickets', 'validCount']],
      ['Tickets invalides', ['invalidTickets', 'invalidCount']],
      ['Doublons détectés', ['duplicates', 'duplicateCount']],
      ['Poids manquants', ['missingWeights', 'missingWeightCount']],
      ['Produits manquants', ['missingProducts', 'missingProductCount']],
      ['Clients manquants', ['missingClients', 'missingClientCount']]
    ];

    const found = [];

    checks.forEach(function (check) {
      const value = mtValue(data, check[1], undefined);

      if (value !== undefined) {
        found.push({
          name: check[0],
          value: value
        });
      }
    });

    const integrityStatus = mtValue(
      data,
      ['status', 'integrityStatus', 'result'],
      'Vérifié'
    );

    if (status) status.textContent = String(integrityStatus);

    if (!found.length) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div class="mt-empty">Résultat reçu. Aucun indicateur détaillé fourni par le serveur.</div>';
    } else {
      container.innerHTML = found.map(function (item) {
        return `
          <div class="mt-row">
            <span>${mtEscape(item.name)}</span>
            <strong>${mtEscape(item.value)}</strong>
          </div>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    const issues = mtRows(data.issues || data.errors || []);

    if (issues.length) {
      container.innerHTML += `
        <h4>Anomalies détectées</h4>
        ${issues.map(function (issue) {
          const message = typeof issue === 'string'
            ? issue
            : mtValue(issue, ['message', 'description', 'issue'], 'Anomalie');

          return `
            <div class="mt-row">
              <span>${mtEscape(message)}</span>
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('')}
      `;
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     5. CREATE SERVER BACKUP
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblCreateSystemBackup = async function () {
    if (!mtRequireAccess()) return;

    if (!window.confirm(
      'Créer une sauvegarde serveur des données de PONT BASCULE LIVE ?'
    )) return;

    mtSetStatus('mtBackupStatus', 'Création de la sauvegarde...');

    try {
      const result = await mtCall(
        MAINTENANCE_APIS.backup,
        []
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Sauvegarde refusée.');
      }

      const backupId = mtValue(
        result,
        ['backupId', 'id', 'fileId', 'filename'],
        'Sauvegarde créée'
      );

      mtSetStatus(
        'mtBackupStatus',
        'Résultat serveur : ' + String(backupId)
      );

      mtToast('Demande de sauvegarde terminée.', 'success');

      await pblLoadMaintenanceLogs();

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      mtSetStatus(
        'mtBackupStatus',
        'Échec : ' + error.message
      );

      mtToast('Échec de la sauvegarde serveur.', 'error');

      return null;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     6. LOCAL DATA BACKUP
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblDownloadLocalDataBackup = function () {
    const data = {
      application: 'PONT BASCULE LIVE',
      exportType: 'BROWSER_AVAILABLE_DATA',
      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      timezone: 'Africa/Casablanca',
      warning: 'Export des seules données actuellement présentes dans le navigateur.',
      collections: {}
    };

    const source = PBL_APP.data || {};

    [
      'tickets',
      'products',
      'clients',
      'trucks',
      'drivers',
      'objectives',
      'notifications',
      'alerts',
      'activity'
    ].forEach(function (key) {
      data.collections[key] = Array.isArray(source[key])
        ? source[key]
        : [];
    });

    const blob = new Blob(
      [JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)],
      { type: 'application/json;charset=utf-8' }
    );

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');

    const stamp = new Date()
      .toISOString()
      .replace(/[:.]/g, '-');

    link.href = url;
    link.download = 'PONT_BASCULE_LIVE_LOCAL_BACKUP_' + stamp + '.json';

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    mtToast('Export local téléchargé.', 'success');
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     7. RESTORE SERVER BACKUP
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblRestoreSelectedBackup = async function () {
    if (!mtRequireAccess()) return;

    const input = mtEl('mtRestoreFile');

    if (!input || !input.files || !input.files[0]) {
      mtToast('Sélectionnez un fichier JSON.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    const file = input.files[0];

    if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) {
      mtToast('Fichier trop volumineux : limite locale de 10 Mo.', 'error');
      return;
    }

    let parsed;

    try {
      const text = await file.text();
      parsed = JSON.parse(text);
    } catch (error) {
      mtToast('Fichier JSON invalide.', 'error');
      return;
    }

    if (
      !parsed ||
      typeof parsed !== 'object' ||
      Array.isArray(parsed)
    ) {
      mtToast('Format de sauvegarde incorrect.', 'error');
      return;
    }

    if (!window.confirm(
      'ATTENTION : la restauration peut remplacer les données actuelles. Continuer ?'
    )) return;

    mtSetStatus('mtRestoreStatus', 'Transmission au serveur...');

    try {
      const result = await mtCall(
        MAINTENANCE_APIS.restore,
        [parsed]
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Restauration refusée.');
      }

      mtSetStatus(
        'mtRestoreStatus',
        'Résultat de restauration reçu du serveur.'
      );

      mtToast('Demande de restauration traitée.', 'success');

      if (typeof loadInitialData === 'function') {
        await loadInitialData();
      }

      if (typeof refreshAllBusinessData === 'function') {
        await refreshAllBusinessData();
      }

      await pblRefreshMaintenance();

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      mtSetStatus(
        'mtRestoreStatus',
        'Échec : ' + error.message
      );

      mtToast('Restauration impossible.', 'error');

      return null;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     8. LOAD MAINTENANCE LOGS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblLoadMaintenanceLogs = async function () {
    try {
      const result = await mtCall(
        MAINTENANCE_APIS.logs,
        []
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Lecture refusée.');
      }

      PBL_APP.maintenance.logs = mtRows(result);

      mtRenderMaintenanceLogs();

      return PBL_APP.maintenance.logs;

    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Maintenance logs unavailable:', error);
      return [];
    }
  };

  function mtRenderMaintenanceLogs() {
    const container = mtEl('mtMaintenanceLogs');
    if (!container) return;

    const logs = PBL_APP.maintenance.logs || [];

    if (!logs.length) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div class="mt-empty">Aucun journal de maintenance disponible.</div>';
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="mt-table-wrap">
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Date</th>
              <th>Opération</th>
              <th>Utilisateur</th>
              <th>Statut</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            ${logs.map(function (log) {
              return `
                <tr>
                  <td>${mtEscape(mtDate(mtValue(log, [
                    'createdAt',
                    'date',
                    'timestamp'
                  ], '')))}</td>

                  <td>${mtEscape(mtValue(log, [
                    'action',
                    'operation',
                    'type',
                    'message'
                  ], 'Maintenance'))}</td>

                  <td>${mtEscape(mtValue(log, [
                    'user',
                    'username',
                    'actor'
                  ], 'Système'))}</td>

                  <td>${mtEscape(mtValue(log, [
                    'status',
                    'result'
                  ], '—'))}</td>
                </tr>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     9. PREVENTIVE MAINTENANCE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblRunFullMaintenanceCheck = async function () {
    if (PBL_APP.maintenance.loading) return;

    PBL_APP.maintenance.loading = true;

    mtSetStatus(
      'pblMaintenanceStatus',
      'Exécution des contrôles...'
    );

    try {
      const results = await Promise.all([
        pblRunSystemDiagnostics(),
        pblCheckDataIntegrity()
      ]);

      PBL_APP.maintenance.lastCheck = new Date().toISOString();

      mtSetStatus(
        'pblMaintenanceStatus',
        'Vérifications terminées : ' +
        mtDate(PBL_APP.maintenance.lastCheck)
      );

      const lastCheck = mtEl('mtLastCheck');

      if (lastCheck) {
        lastCheck.textContent = mtDate(
          PBL_APP.maintenance.lastCheck
        );
      }

      return results;

    } finally {
      PBL_APP.maintenance.loading = false;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     10. CLEANUP
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblRunMaintenanceCleanup = async function () {
    if (!mtRequireAccess()) return;

    if (!window.confirm(
      'Lancer le nettoyage contrôlé du système ?'
    )) return;

    try {
      const result = await mtCall(
        MAINTENANCE_APIS.cleanup,
        []
      );

      if (result && result.success === false) {
        throw new Error(result.message || 'Nettoyage refusé.');
      }

      mtToast('Opération de maintenance transmise.', 'success');

      await pblLoadMaintenanceLogs();

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      mtToast('Nettoyage impossible : ' + error.message, 'error');
      return null;
    }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     11. REFRESH MAINTENANCE CENTER
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblRefreshMaintenance = async function () {
    if (!maintenanceRoot) mtEnsureInterface();

    mtSetStatus(
      'pblMaintenanceStatus',
      'Actualisation des informations...'
    );

    await Promise.all([
      pblLoadMaintenanceLogs(),
      pblRunSystemDiagnostics(),
      pblCheckDataIntegrity()
    ]);

    mtSetStatus(
      'pblMaintenanceStatus',
      'Actualisation terminée.'
    );

    return true;
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     12. INITIALIZATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblInitializeMaintenanceCenter = function () {
    mtEnsureInterface();

    pblLoadMaintenanceLogs();

    console.log(
      'PONT BASCULE LIVE — Maintenance Center loaded.'
    );

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
      pblInitializeMaintenanceCenter();
    });
  } else {
    pblInitializeMaintenanceCenter();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 14
   TRUCK MOVEMENTS & FLEET CONTROL CENTER
   ============================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  PBL_APP.fleet = PBL_APP.fleet || {
    trucks: [],
    tickets: [],
    filtered: [],
    selectedTruck: null,
    search: '',
    status: 'ALL',
    startDate: '',
    endDate: '',
    page: 1,
    pageSize: 20
  };

  const FLEET_APIS = {
    trucks: [
      'apiGetTrucks',
      'apiPbl25GetTrucks',
      'getAllTrucks',
      'getTrucks'
    ],
    tickets: [
      'apiGetTickets',
      'apiGetAllTickets',
      'getAllTickets',
      'apiPbl18GetActiveTickets'
    ],
    history: [
      'apiGetTruckHistory',
      'apiPbl14GetTruckHistory',
      'pbl14GetTruckHistory',
      'getTruckHistory'
    ]
  };

  let fleetRoot = null;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     1. HELPERS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function flEl(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function flEscape(value) {
    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(value == null ? '' : String(value));
    }

    return String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
      .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
      .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
      .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
      .replace(/'/g, '&#039;');
  }

  function flCall(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(new Error('Backend indisponible.'));
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function flToast(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL FLEET]', message);
    }
  }

  function flRows(result) {
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result;
    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return [];

    const candidates = [
      result.trucks,
      result.vehicles,
      result.tickets,
      result.records,
      result.items,
      result.results,
      result.data
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
      if (Array.isArray(candidates[i])) return candidates[i];
    }

    return [];
  }

  function flValue(record, keys, fallback) {
    record = record || {};

    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const value = record[keys[i]];

      if (value !== undefined && value !== null && value !== '') {
        return value;
      }
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  function flKg(ticket) {
    const direct = Number(flValue(ticket, [
      'netKg',
      'netWeightKg',
      'netWeight',
      'net'
    ], 0));

    if (Number.isFinite(direct) && direct > 0) return direct;

    const tonnes = Number(flValue(ticket, [
      'netTonnes',
      'netTons'
    ], 0));

    return Number.isFinite(tonnes) ? tonnes * 1000 : 0;
  }

  function flTonnes(kg) {
    return (Number(kg || 0) / 1000).toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
      minimumFractionDigits: 3,
      maximumFractionDigits: 3
    });
  }

  function flNumber(value) {
    const n = Number(value);
    return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
  }

  function flTruckRegistration(record) {
    return String(flValue(record, [
      'truckRegistration',
      'registration',
      'registrationNumber',
      'plate',
      'vehicleRegistration',
      'truck'
    ], '')).trim();
  }

  function flTicketDate(ticket) {
    return flValue(ticket, [
      'timestamp',
      'createdAt',
      'date',
      'weighingDate'
    ], '');
  }

  function flDateObject(value) {
    if (!value) return null;

    if (typeof value === 'number') {
      const d = new Date(
        value < 1000000000000 ? value * 1000 : value
      );

      return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
    }

    const raw = String(value);

    // Support dd/MM/yyyy ticket dates.
    const match = raw.match(/^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})/);

    if (match) {
      const d = new Date(
        Number(match[3]),
        Number(match[2]) - 1,
        Number(match[1])
      );

      return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
    }

    const d = new Date(raw);

    return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
  }

  function flFormatDate(value) {
    const date = flDateObject(value);

    if (!date) return String(value || 'Date inconnue');

    return date.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
      timeZone: 'Africa/Casablanca',
      year: 'numeric',
      month: '2-digit',
      day: '2-digit',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit'
    });
  }

  function flFindPage() {
    return flEl('fleetPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="fleet"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-fleet') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="trucks"]');
  }

  function flIsCancelled(ticket) {
    const status = String(flValue(ticket, [
      'status',
      'ticketStatus'
    ], '')).toUpperCase();

    return (
      status.includes('CANCEL') ||
      status.includes('DELETED') ||
      status.includes('ANNUL')
    );
  }

  function flIsCompleted(ticket) {
    const status = String(flValue(ticket, [
      'status',
      'ticketStatus'
    ], '')).toUpperCase();

    return (
      !flIsCancelled(ticket) &&
      (
        status.includes('COMPLETE') ||
        status.includes('FINAL') ||
        status.includes('VALID') ||
        status.includes('CLOSED') ||
        flKg(ticket) > 0
      )
    );
  }

  function flActiveTrucks() {
    return PBL_APP.fleet.trucks.filter(function (truck) {
      const status = String(flValue(truck, [
        'status',
        'state'
      ], 'ACTIVE')).toUpperCase();

      return !(
        status.includes('INACTIVE') ||
        status.includes('ARCHIVED') ||
        status.includes('DISABLED')
      );
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     2. INTERFACE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function flEnsureInterface() {
    fleetRoot = flEl('pblFleetCenter');

    if (fleetRoot) return fleetRoot;

      console.warn('Fleet page container not found.');
      return null;

    fleetRoot = document.createElement('div');
    fleetRoot.id = 'pblFleetCenter';
