  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     3. LOAD DATA
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblRefreshFleetCenter = async function () {
    flSetStatus('Chargement des données véhicules...');

    const results = await Promise.allSettled([
      flCall(FLEET_APIS.trucks, []),
      flCall(FLEET_APIS.tickets, [])
    ]);

    if (results[0].status === 'fulfilled') {
      PBL_APP.fleet.trucks = flRows(results[0].value);
    } else {
      PBL_APP.fleet.trucks =
        Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data?.trucks)
          ? PBL_APP.data.trucks
          : [];
    }

    if (results[1].status === 'fulfilled') {
      PBL_APP.fleet.tickets = flRows(results[1].value);
    } else {
      PBL_APP.fleet.tickets =
        Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data?.tickets)
          ? PBL_APP.data.tickets
          : [];
    }

    flUpdateSummary();
    pblApplyFleetFilters();
    flRenderActivitySummary();

    flSetStatus(
      'Données chargées : ' +
      PBL_APP.fleet.trucks.length + ' camion(s), ' +
      PBL_APP.fleet.tickets.length + ' ticket(s).'
    );

    return {
      trucks: PBL_APP.fleet.trucks,
      tickets: PBL_APP.fleet.tickets
    };
  };

  function flSetStatus(message) {
    const element = flEl('flStatus');
    if (element) element.textContent = message;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     4. SUMMARY
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function flUpdateSummary() {
    const trucks = PBL_APP.fleet.trucks;
    const tickets = PBL_APP.fleet.tickets;

    const active = flActiveTrucks();

    const validTickets = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return !flIsCancelled(ticket);
    });

    const totalKg = validTickets.reduce(function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + flKg(ticket);
    }, 0);

    flSetText('flTotalTrucks', trucks.length);
    flSetText('flActiveTrucks', active.length);
    flSetText('flTotalTrips', validTickets.length);
    flSetText('flTotalTonnes', flTonnes(totalKg));
  }

  function flSetText(id, value) {
    const element = flEl(id);
    if (element) element.textContent = String(value);
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     5. FILTER MOVEMENTS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblApplyFleetFilters = function () {
    const search = String(
      flEl('flSearch')?.value || ''
    ).trim().toLowerCase();

    const status = flEl('flStatusFilter')?.value || 'ALL';
    const startValue = flEl('flStartDate')?.value || '';
    const endValue = flEl('flEndDate')?.value || '';

    PBL_APP.fleet.pageSize = Number(
      flEl('flPageSize')?.value || 20
    );

    const start = startValue
      ? new Date(startValue + 'T00:00:00').getTime()
      : null;

    const end = endValue
      ? new Date(endValue + 'T23:59:59').getTime()
      : null;

    PBL_APP.fleet.filtered = PBL_APP.fleet.tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      const registration = flTruckRegistration(ticket).toLowerCase();

      const ticketNumber = String(flValue(ticket, [
        'ticketNumber',
        'number',
        'bonNumber',
        'numeroBon',
        'nBon'
      ], '')).toLowerCase();

      const product = String(flValue(ticket, [
        'product',
        'productName'
      ], '')).toLowerCase();

      const client = String(flValue(ticket, [
        'client',
        'clientName'
      ], '')).toLowerCase();

      const driver = String(flValue(ticket, [
        'driver',
        'driverName'
      ], '')).toLowerCase();

      const searchable = [
        registration,
        ticketNumber,
        product,
        client,
        driver
      ].join(' ');

      if (search && !searchable.includes(search)) return false;

      if (status === 'CANCELLED' && !flIsCancelled(ticket)) {
        return false;
      }

      if (status === 'COMPLETED' && !flIsCompleted(ticket)) {
        return false;
      }

      if (
        status === 'PENDING' &&
        (flIsCompleted(ticket) || flIsCancelled(ticket))
      ) return false;

      const date = flDateObject(flTicketDate(ticket));
      const timestamp = date ? date.getTime() : 0;

      if (start !== null && timestamp && timestamp < start) {
        return false;
      }

      if (end !== null && timestamp && timestamp > end) {
        return false;
      }

      return true;
    });

    PBL_APP.fleet.filtered.sort(function (a, b) {
      const dateA = flDateObject(flTicketDate(a));
      const dateB = flDateObject(flTicketDate(b));

      return (dateB ? dateB.getTime() : 0) -
        (dateA ? dateA.getTime() : 0);
    });

    PBL_APP.fleet.page = 1;

    flRenderMovements();

    return PBL_APP.fleet.filtered;
  };

  window.pblResetFleetFilters = function () {
    [
      'flSearch',
      'flStartDate',
      'flEndDate'
    ].forEach(function (id) {
      const element = flEl(id);
      if (element) element.value = '';
    });

    if (flEl('flStatusFilter')) {
      flEl('flStatusFilter').value = 'ALL';
    }

    if (flEl('flPageSize')) {
      flEl('flPageSize').value = '20';
    }

    pblApplyFleetFilters();
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     6. MOVEMENT TABLE
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function flRenderMovements() {
    const body = flEl('flMovementBody');
    if (!body) return;

    const rows = PBL_APP.fleet.filtered;
    const pageSize = PBL_APP.fleet.pageSize;

    const totalPages = Math.max(
      1,
      Math.ceil(rows.length / pageSize)
    );

    PBL_APP.fleet.page = Math.min(
      Math.max(1, PBL_APP.fleet.page),
      totalPages
    );

    const start = (PBL_APP.fleet.page - 1) * pageSize;
    const pageRows = rows.slice(start, start + pageSize);

    if (!pageRows.length) {
      body.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="10" class="fl-empty">
            Aucun mouvement trouvé.
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    } else {
      body.innerHTML = pageRows.map(function (ticket, index) {
        const registration = flTruckRegistration(ticket);

        const number = flValue(ticket, [
          'ticketNumber',
          'number',
          'bonNumber',
          'numeroBon',
          'nBon'
        ], '—');

        const kg = flKg(ticket);

        const status = flIsCancelled(ticket)
          ? 'ANNULÉ'
          : flIsCompleted(ticket)
            ? 'TERMINÉ'
            : 'EN ATTENTE';

        const originalIndex = start + index;

        return `
          <tr>
            <td>${flEscape(flFormatDate(flTicketDate(ticket)))}</td>

            <td>${flEscape(number)}</td>

            <td>
              <strong>${flEscape(registration || 'Non renseigné')}</strong>
            </td>

            <td>${flEscape(flValue(ticket, [
              'product',
              'productName'
            ], '—'))}</td>

            <td>${flEscape(flValue(ticket, [
              'client',
              'clientName'
            ], '—'))}</td>

            <td>${flEscape(flValue(ticket, [
              'driver',
              'driverName'
            ], '—'))}</td>

            <td>${flEscape(kg.toLocaleString('fr-FR'))}</td>

            <td>${flEscape(flTonnes(kg))}</td>

            <td>
              <span class="fl-tag">${flEscape(status)}</span>
            </td>

            <td>
              <button type="button"
                onclick="pblViewFleetTicket(${originalIndex})">
                Consulter
              </button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    flSetText(
      'flPageInfo',
      'Page ' + PBL_APP.fleet.page +
      ' / ' + totalPages +
      ' — ' + rows.length + ' mouvement(s)'
    );
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     7. PAGINATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblFleetPreviousPage = function () {
    if (PBL_APP.fleet.page <= 1) return;

    PBL_APP.fleet.page--;
    flRenderMovements();
  };

  window.pblFleetNextPage = function () {
    const totalPages = Math.max(
      1,
      Math.ceil(
        PBL_APP.fleet.filtered.length /
        PBL_APP.fleet.pageSize
      )
    );

    if (PBL_APP.fleet.page >= totalPages) return;

    PBL_APP.fleet.page++;
    flRenderMovements();
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     8. VIEW TRUCK / TICKET DETAILS
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblViewFleetTicket = function (index) {
    const ticket = PBL_APP.fleet.filtered[Number(index)];

    if (!ticket) {
      flToast('Mouvement introuvable.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    const registration = flTruckRegistration(ticket);

    const truck = PBL_APP.fleet.trucks.find(function (item) {
      return flTruckRegistration(item).toLowerCase() ===
        registration.toLowerCase();
    });

    PBL_APP.fleet.selectedTruck = truck || {
      registration: registration
    };

    flRenderTruckDetails(
      PBL_APP.fleet.selectedTruck,
      ticket
    );

    const container = flEl('flTruckDetails');

    if (container) {
      container.scrollIntoView({
        behavior: 'smooth',
        block: 'nearest'
      });
    }
  };

  function flRenderTruckDetails(truck, selectedTicket) {
    const container = flEl('flTruckDetails');
    if (!container) return;

    const registration = flTruckRegistration(truck) ||
      flTruckRegistration(selectedTicket);

    const history = PBL_APP.fleet.tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return flTruckRegistration(ticket).toLowerCase() ===
        registration.toLowerCase();
    });

    const valid = history.filter(function (ticket) {
      return !flIsCancelled(ticket);
    });

    const totalKg = valid.reduce(function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + flKg(ticket);
    }, 0);

    const capacity = flNumber(flValue(truck, [
      'capacity',
      'capacityKg',
      'maxCapacityKg'
    ], 0));

    const averageKg = valid.length
      ? totalKg / valid.length
      : 0;

    const latest = history.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
      const dateA = flDateObject(flTicketDate(a));
      const dateB = flDateObject(flTicketDate(b));

      return (dateB ? dateB.getTime() : 0) -
        (dateA ? dateA.getTime() : 0);
    })[0];

    container.innerHTML = `
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;">
        <div>
          <h3 style="margin:0;">${flEscape(registration || 'Véhicule inconnu')}</h3>
          <div class="fl-help">
            ${flEscape(flValue(truck, ['name', 'truckName'], 'Fiche véhicule'))}
          </div>
        </div>

        <span class="fl-tag">
          ${flEscape(flValue(truck, ['status'], 'STATUT NON RENSEIGNÉ'))}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div class="fl-detail-grid" style="margin-top:15px;">
        <div class="fl-detail-card">
          <div class="fl-label">VOYAGES VALIDES</div>
          <div class="fl-number">${valid.length}</div>
        </div>

        <div class="fl-detail-card">
          <div class="fl-label">TONNES TRANSPORTÉES</div>
          <div class="fl-number">${flTonnes(totalKg)}</div>
        </div>

        <div class="fl-detail-card">
          <div class="fl-label">MOYENNE PAR VOYAGE</div>
          <div class="fl-number">${flTonnes(averageKg)}</div>
        </div>

        <div class="fl-detail-card">
          <div class="fl-label">CAPACITÉ ENREGISTRÉE</div>
          <div class="fl-number" style="font-size:18px;">
            ${capacity ? flEscape(capacity.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' KG') : 'Non renseignée'}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="fl-detail-card">
          <div class="fl-label">DERNIER PASSAGE</div>
          <div style="font-weight:700;margin-top:7px;">
            ${latest ? flEscape(flFormatDate(flTicketDate(latest))) : 'Aucun passage'}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="fl-detail-card">
          <div class="fl-label">TYPE DE VÉHICULE</div>
          <div style="font-weight:700;margin-top:7px;">
            ${flEscape(flValue(truck, ['type', 'vehicleType'], 'Non renseigné'))}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h4 style="margin-top:18px;">Historique de ce camion</h4>

      <div class="fl-table-wrap">
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Date</th>
              <th>N° Bon</th>
              <th>Produit</th>
              <th>Client</th>
              <th>Net KG</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            ${
              history.length
                ? history.map(function (ticket) {
                    return `
                      <tr>
                        <td>${flEscape(flFormatDate(flTicketDate(ticket)))}</td>
                        <td>${flEscape(flValue(ticket, [
                          'ticketNumber',
                          'number',
                          'bonNumber',
                          'nBon'
                        ], '—'))}</td>
                        <td>${flEscape(flValue(ticket, [
                          'product',
                          'productName'
                        ], '—'))}</td>
                        <td>${flEscape(flValue(ticket, [
                          'client',
                          'clientName'
                        ], '—'))}</td>
                        <td>${flEscape(flKg(ticket).toLocaleString('fr-FR'))}</td>
                      </tr>
                    `;
                  }).join('')
                : '<tr><td colspan="5" class="fl-empty">Aucun passage enregistré.</td></tr>'
            }
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <div class="fl-help">
        Les statistiques sont calculées à partir des tickets actuellement
        chargés dans l'application. Elles ne constituent pas un compteur
        indépendant des données serveur.
      </div>
    `;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     9. ACTIVITY SUMMARY
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  function flRenderActivitySummary() {
    const container = flEl('flActivitySummary');
    if (!container) return;

    const tickets = PBL_APP.fleet.tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return !flIsCancelled(ticket);
    });

    const map = {};

    tickets.forEach(function (ticket) {
      const registration = flTruckRegistration(ticket);

      if (!registration) return;

      if (!map[registration]) {
        map[registration] = {
          registration: registration,
          trips: 0,
          kg: 0
        };
      }

      map[registration].trips++;
      map[registration].kg += flKg(ticket);
    });

    const ranked = Object.values(map).sort(function (a, b) {
      return b.kg - a.kg;
    });

    if (!ranked.length) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div class="fl-empty">Aucune activité véhicule disponible.</div>';
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="fl-table-wrap">
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Classement</th>
              <th>Immatriculation</th>
              <th>Nombre de voyages</th>
              <th>Tonnes transportées</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            ${ranked.map(function (item, index) {
              return `
                <tr>
                  <td>${index + 1}</td>
                  <td><strong>${flEscape(item.registration)}</strong></td>
                  <td>${item.trips}</td>
                  <td>${flEscape(flTonnes(item.kg))}</td>
                </tr>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     10. CSV EXPORT
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblExportFleetCSV = function () {
    const rows = PBL_APP.fleet.filtered || [];

    if (!rows.length) {
      flToast('Aucun mouvement à exporter.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    function cell(value) {
      return '"' + String(value == null ? '' : value)
        .replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
    }

    const headers = [
      'Date',
      'Numero bon',
      'Immatriculation',
      'Produit',
      'Client',
      'Chauffeur',
      'Net KG',
      'Net Tonnes',
      'Statut'
    ];

    const csvRows = rows.map(function (ticket) {
      const kg = flKg(ticket);

      return [
        flFormatDate(flTicketDate(ticket)),
        flValue(ticket, [
          'ticketNumber',
          'number',
          'bonNumber',
          'numeroBon',
          'nBon'
        ], ''),
        flTruckRegistration(ticket),
        flValue(ticket, ['product', 'productName'], ''),
        flValue(ticket, ['client', 'clientName'], ''),
        flValue(ticket, ['driver', 'driverName'], ''),
        kg,
        (kg / 1000).toFixed(3),
        flIsCancelled(ticket)
          ? 'CANCELLED'
          : flIsCompleted(ticket)
            ? 'COMPLETED'
            : 'PENDING'
      ].map(cell).join(';');
    });

    const csv = '\uFEFF' +
      headers.map(cell).join(';') +
      '\r\n' +
      csvRows.join('\r\n');

    const blob = new Blob(
      [csv],
      { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
    );

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');

    link.href = url;
    link.download =
      'PONT_BASCULE_LIVE_FLEET_' +
      new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) +
      '.csv';

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    flToast('Export des mouvements terminé.', 'success');
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------
     11. INITIALIZATION
     ------------------------------------------------------------ */

  window.pblInitializeFleetCenter = function () {
    flEnsureInterface();

    pblRefreshFleetCenter();

    console.log(
      'PONT BASCULE LIVE — Fleet Control Center loaded.'
    );

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
      pblInitializeFleetCenter();
    });
  } else {
    pblInitializeFleetCenter();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 15
   DRIVER MANAGEMENT & TRANSPORT OPERATIONS
   ============================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  PBL_APP.driverCenter = {
    drivers: [],
    tickets: [],
    filtered: [],
    selectedDriver: null,
    page: 1,
    pageSize: 20,
    search: '',
    status: 'ALL'
  };

  const DR15_APIS = {
    drivers: [
      'apiGetDrivers',
      'getAllDrivers',
      'getDrivers'
    ],
    tickets: [
      'apiGetTickets',
      'apiGetAllTickets',
      'getAllTickets'
    ],
    history: [
      'apiGetDriverHistory',
      'apiPbl15GetDriverHistory',
      'pbl15GetDriverHistory',
      'getDriverHistory'
    ]
  };

  let dr15Root = null;

  /* ============================================================
     1. GENERAL HELPERS
     ============================================================ */

  function dr15El(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function dr15Escape(value) {
    const v = String(value == null ? '' : value);

    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(v);
    }

    return v.replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (char) {
      return {
        '&': '&amp;',
        '<': '&lt;',
        '>': '&gt;',
        '"': '&quot;',
        "'": '&#039;'
      }[char];
    });
  }

  function dr15Call(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Communication serveur indisponible.')
      );
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function dr15Toast(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL DRIVER]', message);
    }
  }

  function dr15Rows(result) {
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result;

    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return [];

    const candidates = [
      result.drivers,
      result.records,
      result.tickets,
      result.items,
      result.results,
      result.data
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
      if (Array.isArray(candidates[i])) {
        return candidates[i];
      }
    }

    return [];
  }

  function dr15Value(record, keys, fallback) {
    record = record || {};

    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const value = record[keys[i]];

      if (
        value !== undefined &&
        value !== null &&
        value !== ''
      ) {
        return value;
      }
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  function dr15DriverName(record) {
    return String(dr15Value(record, [
      'driver',
      'driverName',
      'name',
      'fullName',
      'chauffeur'
    ], '')).trim();
  }

  function dr15Truck(ticket) {
    return String(dr15Value(ticket, [
      'truckRegistration',
      'registration',
      'registrationNumber',
      'plate',
      'vehicleRegistration'
    ], '')).trim();
  }

  function dr15Kg(ticket) {
    const kg = Number(dr15Value(ticket, [
      'netKg',
      'netWeightKg',
      'netWeight',
      'net'
    ], 0));

    if (Number.isFinite(kg) && kg > 0) return kg;

    const tonnes = Number(dr15Value(ticket, [
      'netTonnes',
      'netTons'
    ], 0));

    return Number.isFinite(tonnes) ? tonnes * 1000 : 0;
  }

  function dr15Tonnes(kg) {
    return (Number(kg || 0) / 1000).toLocaleString(
      'fr-FR',
      {
        minimumFractionDigits: 3,
        maximumFractionDigits: 3
      }
    );
  }

  function dr15Date(value) {
    if (!value) return null;

    if (typeof value === 'number') {
      const d = new Date(
        value < 1000000000000 ? value * 1000 : value
      );

      return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
    }

    const raw = String(value);
    const match = raw.match(
      /^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})/
    );

    if (match) {
      const d = new Date(
        Number(match[3]),
        Number(match[2]) - 1,
        Number(match[1])
      );

      return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
    }

    const d = new Date(raw);

    return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
  }

  function dr15FormatDate(value) {
    const d = dr15Date(value);

    if (!d) return String(value || 'Date inconnue');

    return d.toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
      timeZone: 'Africa/Casablanca',
      year: 'numeric',
      month: '2-digit',
      day: '2-digit',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit'
    });
  }

  function dr15TicketDate(ticket) {
    return dr15Value(ticket, [
      'timestamp',
      'createdAt',
      'date',
      'weighingDate'
    ], '');
  }

  function dr15Cancelled(ticket) {
    const status = String(dr15Value(ticket, [
      'status',
      'ticketStatus'
    ], '')).toUpperCase();

    return (
      status.includes('CANCEL') ||
      status.includes('ANNUL') ||
      status.includes('DELETE')
    );
  }

  function dr15Completed(ticket) {
    if (dr15Cancelled(ticket)) return false;

    const status = String(dr15Value(ticket, [
      'status',
      'ticketStatus'
    ], '')).toUpperCase();

    return (
      status.includes('COMPLETE') ||
      status.includes('FINAL') ||
      status.includes('VALID') ||
      status.includes('CLOSED') ||
      dr15Kg(ticket) > 0
    );
  }

  function dr15SetText(id, value) {
    const el = dr15El(id);

    if (el) {
      el.textContent = String(value);
    }
  }

  function dr15FindPage() {
    return dr15El('driverPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="drivers"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-drivers');
  }

  /* ============================================================
     2. DRIVER OPERATIONS INTERFACE
     ============================================================ */

  /* ============================================================
     3. LOAD DRIVERS AND TICKETS
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblRefreshDriverOperations = async function () {
    dr15SetStatus('Chargement des chauffeurs et des voyages...');

    const results = await Promise.allSettled([
      dr15Call(DR15_APIS.drivers, []),
      dr15Call(DR15_APIS.tickets, [])
    ]);

    if (results[0].status === 'fulfilled') {
      PBL_APP.driverCenter.drivers =
        dr15Rows(results[0].value);
    } else {
      PBL_APP.driverCenter.drivers =
        Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data?.drivers)
          ? PBL_APP.data.drivers
          : [];
    }

    if (results[1].status === 'fulfilled') {
      PBL_APP.driverCenter.tickets =
        dr15Rows(results[1].value);
    } else {
      PBL_APP.driverCenter.tickets =
        Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data?.tickets)
          ? PBL_APP.data.tickets
          : [];
    }

    dr15UpdateSummary();
    pblApplyDriverFilters();
    dr15RenderRanking();

    dr15SetStatus(
      'Chargement terminé : ' +
      PBL_APP.driverCenter.drivers.length +
      ' chauffeur(s), ' +
      PBL_APP.driverCenter.tickets.length +
      ' ticket(s).'
    );

    return {
      drivers: PBL_APP.driverCenter.drivers,
      tickets: PBL_APP.driverCenter.tickets
    };
  };

  function dr15SetStatus(message) {
    const el = dr15El('dr15Status');

    if (el) el.textContent = message;
  }

  /* ============================================================
     4. DRIVER STATISTICS
     ============================================================ */

  function dr15DriverTrips(name) {
    const normalized = String(name || '').trim().toLowerCase();

    return PBL_APP.driverCenter.tickets.filter(
      function (ticket) {
        return dr15DriverName(ticket).toLowerCase() === normalized;
      }
    );
  }

  function dr15DriverStats(name) {
    const history = dr15DriverTrips(name);

    const valid = history.filter(function (ticket) {
      return !dr15Cancelled(ticket);
    });

    const completed = valid.filter(dr15Completed);

    const totalKg = valid.reduce(function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + dr15Kg(ticket);
    }, 0);

    const trucks = Array.from(new Set(
      valid.map(dr15Truck).filter(Boolean)
    ));

    const clients = Array.from(new Set(
      valid.map(function (ticket) {
        return String(dr15Value(ticket, [
          'client',
          'clientName'
        ], '')).trim();
      }).filter(Boolean)
    ));

    const products = Array.from(new Set(
      valid.map(function (ticket) {
        return String(dr15Value(ticket, [
          'product',
          'productName'
        ], '')).trim();
      }).filter(Boolean)
    ));

    return {
      history: history,
      valid: valid,
      completed: completed,
      trips: valid.length,
      totalKg: totalKg,
      averageKg: valid.length ? totalKg / valid.length : 0,
      trucks: trucks,
      clients: clients,
      products: products,
      cancelled: history.filter(dr15Cancelled).length
    };
  }

  function dr15UpdateSummary() {
    const drivers = PBL_APP.driverCenter.drivers;
    const tickets = PBL_APP.driverCenter.tickets;

    const activeDrivers = drivers.filter(function (driver) {
      const status = String(dr15Value(driver, [
        'status',
        'state'
      ], 'ACTIVE')).toUpperCase();

      return !(
        status.includes('INACTIVE') ||
        status.includes('ARCHIVED') ||
        status.includes('DISABLED')
      );
    });

    const valid = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return !dr15Cancelled(ticket);
    });

    const totalKg = valid.reduce(function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + dr15Kg(ticket);
    }, 0);

    dr15SetText('dr15TotalDrivers', drivers.length);
    dr15SetText('dr15ActiveDrivers', activeDrivers.length);
    dr15SetText('dr15TotalTrips', valid.length);
    dr15SetText('dr15TotalTonnes', dr15Tonnes(totalKg));
  }

  /* ============================================================
     5. DRIVER FILTERING
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblApplyDriverFilters = function () {
    const search = String(
      dr15El('dr15Search')?.value || ''
    ).trim().toLowerCase();

    const status = dr15El('dr15StatusFilter')?.value || 'ALL';

    PBL_APP.driverCenter.pageSize = Number(
      dr15El('dr15PageSize')?.value || 20
    );

    let drivers = PBL_APP.driverCenter.drivers.slice();

    if (search) {
      drivers = drivers.filter(function (driver) {
        const name = dr15DriverName(driver).toLowerCase();

        const phone = String(dr15Value(driver, [
          'phone',
          'telephone',
          'mobile'
        ], '')).toLowerCase();

        const license = String(dr15Value(driver, [
          'license',
          'licenseNumber',
          'permis'
        ], '')).toLowerCase();

        return (
          name.includes(search) ||
          phone.includes(search) ||
          license.includes(search)
        );
      });
    }

    if (status !== 'ALL') {
      drivers = drivers.filter(function (driver) {
        const name = dr15DriverName(driver);
        const trips = dr15DriverTrips(name);

        if (status === 'COMPLETED') {
          return trips.some(dr15Completed);
        }

        if (status === 'CANCELLED') {
          return trips.some(dr15Cancelled);
        }

        if (status === 'PENDING') {
          return trips.some(function (ticket) {
            return !dr15Completed(ticket) &&
              !dr15Cancelled(ticket);
          });
        }

        return true;
      });
    }

    PBL_APP.driverCenter.filtered = drivers;

    PBL_APP.driverCenter.page = 1;

    dr15RenderDrivers();

    return drivers;
  };

  window.pblResetDriverFilters = function () {
    if (dr15El('dr15Search')) {
      dr15El('dr15Search').value = '';
    }

    if (dr15El('dr15StatusFilter')) {
      dr15El('dr15StatusFilter').value = 'ALL';
    }

    if (dr15El('dr15PageSize')) {
      dr15El('dr15PageSize').value = '20';
    }

    pblApplyDriverFilters();
  };

  /* ============================================================
     6. DRIVER TABLE
     ============================================================ */

  function dr15RenderDrivers() {
    const body = dr15El('dr15DriverBody');

    if (!body) return;

    const drivers = PBL_APP.driverCenter.filtered;
    const pageSize = PBL_APP.driverCenter.pageSize;

    const totalPages = Math.max(
      1,
      Math.ceil(drivers.length / pageSize)
    );

    PBL_APP.driverCenter.page = Math.min(
      Math.max(1, PBL_APP.driverCenter.page),
      totalPages
    );

    const start =
      (PBL_APP.driverCenter.page - 1) * pageSize;

    const pageRows = drivers.slice(
      start,
      start + pageSize
    );

    if (!pageRows.length) {
      body.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="8" class="dr15-empty">
            Aucun chauffeur trouvé.
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    } else {
      body.innerHTML = pageRows.map(function (driver, index) {
        const name = dr15DriverName(driver);
        const stats = dr15DriverStats(name);

        const phone = dr15Value(driver, [
          'phone',
          'telephone',
          'mobile'
        ], '—');

        const truck = dr15Value(driver, [
          'truckRegistration',
          'registration',
          'vehicle'
        ], '—');

        const license = dr15Value(driver, [
          'license',
          'licenseNumber',
          'permis'
        ], '—');

        const status = dr15Value(driver, [
          'status',
          'state'
        ], 'ACTIVE');

        return `
          <tr>
            <td><strong>${dr15Escape(name || 'Sans nom')}</strong></td>
            <td>${dr15Escape(phone)}</td>
            <td>${dr15Escape(truck)}</td>
            <td>${dr15Escape(license)}</td>
            <td>${stats.trips}</td>
            <td>${dr15Escape(dr15Tonnes(stats.totalKg))}</td>
            <td><span class="dr15-tag">${dr15Escape(status)}</span></td>
            <td>
              <button type="button"
                onclick="pblViewDriverProfile(${start + index})">
                Profil
              </button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    dr15SetText(
      'dr15PageInfo',
      'Page ' + PBL_APP.driverCenter.page +
      ' / ' + totalPages +
      ' — ' + drivers.length + ' chauffeur(s)'
    );
  }

  /* ============================================================
     7. PAGINATION
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblDriverPreviousPage = function () {
    if (PBL_APP.driverCenter.page <= 1) return;

    PBL_APP.driverCenter.page--;
    dr15RenderDrivers();
  };

  window.pblDriverNextPage = function () {
    const totalPages = Math.max(
      1,
      Math.ceil(
        PBL_APP.driverCenter.filtered.length /
        PBL_APP.driverCenter.pageSize
      )
    );

    if (PBL_APP.driverCenter.page >= totalPages) return;

    PBL_APP.driverCenter.page++;
    dr15RenderDrivers();
  };

  /* ============================================================
     8. DRIVER PROFILE
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblViewDriverProfile = function (index) {
    const driver = PBL_APP.driverCenter.filtered[
      Number(index)
    ];

    if (!driver) {
      dr15Toast('Chauffeur introuvable.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    PBL_APP.driverCenter.selectedDriver = driver;

    dr15RenderDriverProfile(driver);

    const container = dr15El('dr15DriverProfile');

    if (container) {
      container.scrollIntoView({
        behavior: 'smooth',
        block: 'nearest'
      });
    }
  };

  function dr15RenderDriverProfile(driver) {
    const container = dr15El('dr15DriverProfile');

    if (!container) return;

    const name = dr15DriverName(driver);
    const stats = dr15DriverStats(name);

    const latest = stats.history.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
      const da = dr15Date(dr15TicketDate(a));
      const db = dr15Date(dr15TicketDate(b));

      return (db ? db.getTime() : 0) -
        (da ? da.getTime() : 0);
    })[0];

    const phone = dr15Value(driver, [
      'phone',
      'telephone',
      'mobile'
    ], 'Non renseigné');

    const license = dr15Value(driver, [
      'license',
      'licenseNumber',
      'permis'
    ], 'Non renseigné');

    const email = dr15Value(driver, [
      'email',
      'mail'
    ], 'Non renseigné');

    const address = dr15Value(driver, [
      'address',
      'adresse'
    ], 'Non renseignée');

    container.innerHTML = `
      <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
        <div>
          <h3 style="margin:0;">${dr15Escape(name || 'Chauffeur')}</h3>
          <div class="dr15-help">
            Fiche individuelle et performance opérationnelle.
          </div>
        </div>

        <span class="dr15-tag">
          ${dr15Escape(dr15Value(driver, ['status'], 'ACTIVE'))}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div class="dr15-profile-grid" style="margin-top:15px;">
        <div class="dr15-profile-card">
          <div class="dr15-label">TÉLÉPHONE</div>
          <strong>${dr15Escape(phone)}</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="dr15-profile-card">
          <div class="dr15-label">PERMIS</div>
          <strong>${dr15Escape(license)}</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="dr15-profile-card">
          <div class="dr15-label">EMAIL</div>
          <strong>${dr15Escape(email)}</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="dr15-profile-card">
          <div class="dr15-label">ADRESSE</div>
          <strong>${dr15Escape(address)}</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="dr15-profile-card">
          <div class="dr15-label">VOYAGES VALIDES</div>
          <div class="dr15-number">${stats.trips}</div>
        </div>

        <div class="dr15-profile-card">
          <div class="dr15-label">TONNES TRANSPORTÉES</div>
          <div class="dr15-number">${dr15Tonnes(stats.totalKg)}</div>
        </div>

        <div class="dr15-profile-card">
          <div class="dr15-label">MOYENNE PAR VOYAGE</div>
          <div class="dr15-number">${dr15Tonnes(stats.averageKg)}</div>
        </div>

        <div class="dr15-profile-card">
          <div class="dr15-label">ANNULATIONS</div>
          <div class="dr15-number">${stats.cancelled}</div>
        </div>

        <div class="dr15-profile-card">
          <div class="dr15-label">DERNIER VOYAGE</div>
          <strong>
            ${latest
              ? dr15Escape(dr15FormatDate(dr15TicketDate(latest)))
              : 'Aucun voyage'}
          </strong>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h4 style="margin-top:18px;">Véhicules utilisés</h4>

      <div class="dr15-actions">
        ${
          stats.trucks.length
            ? stats.trucks.map(function (truck) {
                return '<span class="dr15-tag">' +
                  dr15Escape(truck) +
                  '</span>';
              }).join('')
            : '<span class="dr15-help">Aucun véhicule renseigné.</span>'
        }
      </div>

      <h4 style="margin-top:18px;">Clients desservis</h4>

      <div class="dr15-actions">
        ${
          stats.clients.length
            ? stats.clients.map(function (client) {
                return '<span class="dr15-tag">' +
                  dr15Escape(client) +
                  '</span>';
              }).join('')
            : '<span class="dr15-help">Aucun client renseigné.</span>'
        }
      </div>

      <h4 style="margin-top:18px;">Produits transportés</h4>

      <div class="dr15-actions">
        ${
          stats.products.length
            ? stats.products.map(function (product) {
                return '<span class="dr15-tag">' +
                  dr15Escape(product) +
                  '</span>';
              }).join('')
            : '<span class="dr15-help">Aucun produit renseigné.</span>'
        }
      </div>

      <h4 style="margin-top:18px;">Historique des voyages</h4>

      <div class="dr15-table-wrap">
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Date</th>
              <th>N° Bon</th>
              <th>Véhicule</th>
              <th>Produit</th>
              <th>Client</th>
              <th>Net KG</th>
              <th>Statut</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            ${
              stats.history.length
                ? stats.history.map(function (ticket) {
                    const status = dr15Cancelled(ticket)
                      ? 'ANNULÉ'
                      : dr15Completed(ticket)
                        ? 'TERMINÉ'
                        : 'EN ATTENTE';

                    return `
                      <tr>
                        <td>${dr15Escape(dr15FormatDate(dr15TicketDate(ticket)))}</td>
                        <td>${dr15Escape(dr15Value(ticket, [
                          'ticketNumber',
                          'number',
                          'bonNumber',
                          'nBon'
                        ], '—'))}</td>
                        <td>${dr15Escape(dr15Truck(ticket) || '—')}</td>
                        <td>${dr15Escape(dr15Value(ticket, [
                          'product',
                          'productName'
                        ], '—'))}</td>
                        <td>${dr15Escape(dr15Value(ticket, [
                          'client',
                          'clientName'
                        ], '—'))}</td>
                        <td>${dr15Escape(dr15Kg(ticket).toLocaleString('fr-FR'))}</td>
                        <td><span class="dr15-tag">${status}</span></td>
                      </tr>
                    `;
                  }).join('')
                : '<tr><td colspan="7" class="dr15-empty">Aucun voyage enregistré.</td></tr>'
            }
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <div class="dr15-help">
        Les données de performance sont calculées à partir des tickets
        disponibles dans cette session. Elles ne remplacent pas un registre
        de présence ou une feuille de temps du chauffeur.
      </div>
    `;
  }

  /* ============================================================
     9. DRIVER RANKING
     ============================================================ */

  function dr15RenderRanking() {
    const container = dr15El('dr15Ranking');

    if (!container) return;

    const ranking = PBL_APP.driverCenter.drivers.map(
      function (driver) {
        const name = dr15DriverName(driver);
        const stats = dr15DriverStats(name);

        return {
          name: name,
          trips: stats.trips,
          kg: stats.totalKg
        };
      }
    ).sort(function (a, b) {
      return b.kg - a.kg;
    });

    const useful = ranking.filter(function (item) {
      return item.trips > 0;
    });

    if (!useful.length) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div class="dr15-empty">Aucune performance enregistrée.</div>';
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = `
      <div class="dr15-table-wrap">
        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Rang</th>
              <th>Chauffeur</th>
              <th>Voyages</th>
              <th>Tonnes transportées</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            ${useful.map(function (item, index) {
              return `
                <tr>
                  <td><strong>${index + 1}</strong></td>
                  <td>${dr15Escape(item.name)}</td>
                  <td>${item.trips}</td>
                  <td>${dr15Escape(dr15Tonnes(item.kg))}</td>
                </tr>
              `;
            }).join('')}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  /* ============================================================
     10. CSV EXPORT
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblExportDriverOperationsCSV = function () {
    const drivers = PBL_APP.driverCenter.filtered || [];

    if (!drivers.length) {
      dr15Toast('Aucun chauffeur à exporter.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    function csvCell(value) {
      return '"' + String(value == null ? '' : value)
        .replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
    }

    const headers = [
      'Chauffeur',
      'Telephone',
      'Permis',
      'Vehicule',
      'Voyages',
      'Tonnes',
      'Annulations',
      'Statut'
    ];

    const rows = drivers.map(function (driver) {
      const name = dr15DriverName(driver);
      const stats = dr15DriverStats(name);

      return [
        name,
        dr15Value(driver, ['phone', 'telephone'], ''),
        dr15Value(driver, ['license', 'licenseNumber'], ''),
        dr15Value(driver, [
          'truckRegistration',
          'registration'
        ], ''),
        stats.trips,
        (stats.totalKg / 1000).toFixed(3),
        stats.cancelled,
        dr15Value(driver, ['status'], 'ACTIVE')
      ].map(csvCell).join(';');
    });

    const csv = '\uFEFF' +
      headers.map(csvCell).join(';') +
      '\r\n' +
      rows.join('\r\n');

    const blob = new Blob(
      [csv],
      { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
    );

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');

    link.href = url;
    link.download =
      'PONT_BASCULE_LIVE_DRIVERS_' +
      new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) +
      '.csv';

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    dr15Toast('Export des chauffeurs terminé.', 'success');
  };

  /* ============================================================
     11. INITIALIZATION
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblInitializeDriverOperations = function () {
    dr15EnsureInterface();

    pblRefreshDriverOperations();

    console.log(
      'PONT BASCULE LIVE — Driver Operations loaded.'
    );

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      pblInitializeDriverOperations
    );
  } else {
    pblInitializeDriverOperations();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 16
   LIVE SCALE MONITOR & WEIGHBRIDGE DIAGNOSTICS
   ============================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  PBL_APP.scaleMonitor = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor || {
    connected: false,
    hardwareDetected: false,
    readingAvailable: false,
    currentWeight: null,
    previousWeight: null,
    readings: [],
    stable: false,
    lastUpdate: null,
    status: 'UNKNOWN',
    source: 'UNAVAILABLE',
    errors: [],
    pollInterval: 2500,
    timer: null,
    polling: false,
    initialized: false
  };

  const SM16_APIS = {
    status: [
      'apiPbl16GetScaleStatus',
      'pbl16GetScaleStatus',
      'getScaleConnectionStatus',
      'apiGetScaleStatus'
    ],

    weight: [
      'apiPbl16GetLiveWeight',
      'pbl16GetLiveWeight',
      'apiGetLiveWeight',
      'getLiveWeight',
      'apiReadScale'
    ],

    diagnostics: [
      'apiPbl16ScaleDiagnostics',
      'pbl16ScaleDiagnostics',
      'apiScaleDiagnostics'
    ]
  };

  let sm16Root = null;

  /* ============================================================
     1. GENERAL UTILITIES
     ============================================================ */

  function sm16El(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function sm16Escape(value) {
    const str = String(value == null ? '' : value);

    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(str);
    }

    return str.replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (char) {
      return {
        '&': '&amp;',
        '<': '&lt;',
        '>': '&gt;',
        '"': '&quot;',
        "'": '&#039;'
      }[char];
    });
  }

  function sm16Call(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Communication serveur indisponible.')
      );
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function sm16Toast(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL SCALE]', message);
    }
  }

  function sm16SetText(id, value) {
    const element = sm16El(id);

    if (element) {
      element.textContent = String(value);
    }
  }

  function sm16Number(value) {
    if (typeof value === 'string') {
      value = value.replace(/\s/g, '').replace(',', '.');
    }

    const number = Number(value);

    return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : null;
  }

  function sm16FormatWeight(value) {
    if (value === null || value === undefined) {
      return '---';
    }

    return Number(value).toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
      minimumFractionDigits: 0,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2
    });
  }

  function sm16Time(value) {
    const date = value ? new Date(value) : new Date();

    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
      return '--:--:--';
    }

    return date.toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR', {
      timeZone: 'Africa/Casablanca',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
      second: '2-digit'
    });
  }

  function sm16FindPage() {
    return sm16El('weighingPage') ||
      sm16El('page-weighing') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="weighing"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-weighing');
  }

  /* ============================================================
     2. INTERFACE CREATION
     ============================================================ */

  function sm16EnsureInterface() {
    sm16Root = sm16El('pblScaleMonitor');

    if (sm16Root) return sm16Root;

    const page = sm16FindPage();

    if (!page) {
      console.warn(
        'PBL weighing page not found. Scale monitor not inserted.'
      );
      return null;
    }

    sm16Root = document.createElement('section');
    sm16Root.id = 'pblScaleMonitor';

    sm16Root.innerHTML = `
      <style>
        #pblScaleMonitor {
          width:100%;
          margin-top:18px;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-panel {
          padding:17px;
          margin-bottom:15px;
          border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.2);
          border-radius:12px;
          background:rgba(20,30,46,.5);
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-main-display {
          padding:24px 15px;
          text-align:center;
          border:1px solid rgba(70,130,220,.3);
          border-radius:12px;
          background:rgba(7,15,30,.8);
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-weight {
          font-size:clamp(38px,7vw,76px);
          font-weight:800;
          letter-spacing:2px;
          font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;
          overflow-wrap:anywhere;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-unit {
          font-size:17px;
          opacity:.7;
          margin-left:7px;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-caption {
          font-size:11px;
          opacity:.65;
          letter-spacing:1.4px;
          margin-bottom:13px;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-grid {
          display:grid;
          grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));
          gap:10px;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-stat {
          padding:13px;
          border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.2);
          border-radius:9px;
          min-width:0;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-label {
          font-size:10px;
          opacity:.65;
          letter-spacing:.5px;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-value {
          font-size:18px;
          font-weight:700;
          margin-top:7px;
          overflow-wrap:anywhere;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-status {
          display:inline-flex;
          align-items:center;
          gap:7px;
          padding:7px 11px;
          border-radius:20px;
          border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.3);
          font-size:11px;
          font-weight:700;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-dot {
          width:8px;
          height:8px;
          display:inline-block;
          border-radius:50%;
          background:#94a3b8;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-status.online .sm16-dot {
          background:#22c55e;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-status.offline .sm16-dot {
          background:#ef4444;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-status.warning .sm16-dot {
          background:#f59e0b;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-actions {
          display:flex;
          flex-wrap:wrap;
          gap:8px;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor button {
          padding:9px 13px;
          border-radius:7px;
          border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.3);
          background:rgba(8,15,27,.8);
          color:inherit;
          cursor:pointer;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor button.primary {
          background:#2563eb;
          border-color:#2563eb;
          color:white;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor table {
          width:100%;
          border-collapse:collapse;
          min-width:450px;
          font-size:12px;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor th,
        #pblScaleMonitor td {
          padding:10px;
          text-align:left;
          border-bottom:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.15);
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-table-wrap {
          overflow-x:auto;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-empty {
          padding:20px;
          text-align:center;
          opacity:.65;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-help {
          font-size:11px;
          line-height:1.6;
          opacity:.65;
          margin-top:8px;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-progress {
          width:100%;
          height:7px;
          overflow:hidden;
          border-radius:20px;
          background:rgba(130,150,180,.18);
          margin-top:12px;
        }

        #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-progress-inner {
          width:0%;
          height:100%;
          border-radius:20px;
          background:#3b82f6;
          transition:width .2s ease;
        }

        @media(max-width:800px) {
          #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-grid {
            grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));
          }
        }

        @media(max-width:480px) {
          #pblScaleMonitor .sm16-grid {
            grid-template-columns:1fr;
          }
        }
      </style>

      <div class="sm16-panel">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;">
          <div>
            <h3 style="margin:0;">LIVE WEIGHBRIDGE MONITOR</h3>
            <div class="sm16-help">
              Surveillance du signal de pesage et état de connexion.
            </div>
          </div>

          <div id="sm16ConnectionBadge"
            class="sm16-status warning">
            <span class="sm16-dot"></span>
            <span id="sm16ConnectionText">VÉRIFICATION</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="sm16-panel">
        <div class="sm16-main-display">
          <div class="sm16-caption">
            POIDS ACTUEL DU PONT BASCULE
          </div>

          <div>
            <span id="sm16LiveWeight" class="sm16-weight">
              ---
            </span>

            <span class="sm16-unit">KG</span>
          </div>

          <div class="sm16-help">
            Dernière actualisation :
            <strong id="sm16LastUpdate">--:--:--</strong>
          </div>

          <div class="sm16-progress">
            <div id="sm16SignalBar"
              class="sm16-progress-inner"></div>
          </div>

          <div class="sm16-help" id="sm16SignalText">
            En attente de données du système de pesage.
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="sm16-panel">
        <h3>État technique</h3>

        <div class="sm16-grid">
          <div class="sm16-stat">
            <div class="sm16-label">CONNEXION</div>
            <div class="sm16-value" id="sm16ConnectionState">
              Inconnue
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="sm16-stat">
            <div class="sm16-label">STABILITÉ</div>
            <div class="sm16-value" id="sm16Stability">
              Inconnue
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="sm16-stat">
            <div class="sm16-label">SOURCE DE LECTURE</div>
            <div class="sm16-value" id="sm16Source">
              Inconnue
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="sm16-stat">
            <div class="sm16-label">VARIATION</div>
            <div class="sm16-value" id="sm16Variation">
              ---
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="sm16-stat">
            <div class="sm16-label">DERNIER POIDS</div>
            <div class="sm16-value" id="sm16Previous">
              ---
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="sm16-stat">
            <div class="sm16-label">LECTURES REÇUES</div>
            <div class="sm16-value" id="sm16ReadingCount">
              0
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="sm16-stat">
            <div class="sm16-label">ZÉRO DÉTECTÉ</div>
            <div class="sm16-value" id="sm16Zero">
              Inconnu
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="sm16-stat">
            <div class="sm16-label">DERNIÈRE SOURCE</div>
            <div class="sm16-value" id="sm16Hardware">
              Non vérifiée
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="sm16-panel">
        <h3>Commandes de surveillance</h3>

        <div class="sm16-actions">
          <button type="button" class="primary"
            onclick="pblScaleMonitorRefresh()">
            Actualiser maintenant
          </button>

          <button type="button"
            onclick="pblScaleMonitorStart()">
            Démarrer surveillance
          </button>

          <button type="button"
            onclick="pblScaleMonitorStop()">
            Arrêter surveillance
          </button>

          <button type="button"
            onclick="pblScaleMonitorDiagnostics()">
            Diagnostic technique
          </button>
        </div>

        <div class="sm16-help">
          Ces commandes surveillent uniquement les données retournées
          par le serveur. Elles ne commandent pas le pont bascule,
          ne mettent pas la balance à zéro et ne modifient pas son étalonnage.
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="sm16-panel">
        <h3>Historique des dernières lectures</h3>

        <div class="sm16-table-wrap">
          <table>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Heure</th>
                <th>Poids KG</th>
                <th>Variation KG</th>
                <th>Stabilité</th>
                <th>Source</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody id="sm16History">
              <tr>
                <td colspan="5" class="sm16-empty">
                  Aucune lecture reçue.
                </td>
              </tr>
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="sm16-panel">
        <h3>Diagnostic et alertes</h3>

        <div id="sm16Diagnostics">
          <div class="sm16-empty">
            En attente du diagnostic.
