        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="sm16-panel">
        <h3>Sécurité de pesage</h3>

        <div class="sm16-help">
          <strong>Règles de fonctionnement :</strong>
          <ul>
            <li>Une lecture indisponible ne doit jamais être considérée comme zéro.</li>
            <li>Une lecture instable ne doit pas être utilisée comme poids final.</li>
            <li>Une pesée doit conserver sa première et sa deuxième mesure.</li>
            <li>Une saisie manuelle doit rester protégée par l'autorisation PIN.</li>
            <li>Une valeur simulée ne doit jamais être présentée comme une mesure physique.</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    page.appendChild(sm16Root);

    return sm16Root;
  }

  /* ============================================================
     3. NORMALIZE SERVER RESPONSE
     ============================================================ */

  function sm16ExtractWeight(result) {
    if (typeof result === 'number') {
      return {
        weight: Number.isFinite(result) ? result : null,
        stable: null,
        source: 'SERVER_RESPONSE',
        available: Number.isFinite(result)
      };
    }

    if (typeof result === 'string') {
      const number = sm16Number(result);

      return {
        weight: number,
        stable: null,
        source: 'SERVER_RESPONSE',
        available: number !== null
      };
    }

    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') {
      return {
        weight: null,
        stable: null,
        source: 'UNAVAILABLE',
        available: false
      };
    }

    const weightValue =
      result.weight ??
      result.weightKg ??
      result.currentWeight ??
      result.liveWeight ??
      result.netWeight ??
      result.value ??
      result.kg;

    const weight = sm16Number(weightValue);

    const explicitAvailable =
      result.available ??
      result.readingAvailable ??
      result.success;

    const source = String(
      result.source ||
      result.mode ||
      result.device ||
      'SERVER_RESPONSE'
    ).toUpperCase();

    const stable =
      typeof result.stable === 'boolean'
        ? result.stable
        : typeof result.isStable === 'boolean'
          ? result.isStable
          : typeof result.stability === 'boolean'
            ? result.stability
            : null;

    return {
      weight: weight,
      stable: stable,
      source: source,
      available:
        explicitAvailable === false
          ? false
          : weight !== null,
      raw: result
    };
  }

  function sm16IsSimulation(source, raw) {
    const value = String(source || '').toUpperCase();

    if (
      value.includes('SIMUL') ||
      value.includes('DEMO') ||
      value.includes('TEST') ||
      value.includes('MOCK')
    ) {
      return true;
    }

    if (raw && (
      raw.simulated === true ||
      raw.isSimulation === true ||
      raw.demo === true
    )) {
      return true;
    }

    return false;
  }

  function sm16IsConnected(result) {
    if (typeof result === 'boolean') return result;

    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') {
      return false;
    }

    if (result.connected === true) return true;
    if (result.isConnected === true) return true;
    if (result.hardwareConnected === true) return true;

    const status = String(
      result.status || result.connectionStatus || ''
    ).toUpperCase();

    return (
      status === 'CONNECTED' ||
      status === 'ONLINE' ||
      status === 'READY'
    );
  }

  /* ============================================================
     4. READING HISTORY
     ============================================================ */

  function sm16RecordReading(data) {
    if (!data || data.weight === null) return;

    const state = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor;

    const previous = state.currentWeight;

    state.previousWeight = previous;
    state.currentWeight = data.weight;
    state.readingAvailable = true;
    state.lastUpdate = new Date().toISOString();
    state.source = data.source;
    state.stable = data.stable;
    state.errors = [];

    const variation = previous === null
      ? 0
      : data.weight - previous;

    state.readings.unshift({
      time: state.lastUpdate,
      weight: data.weight,
      variation: variation,
      stable: data.stable,
      source: data.source
    });

    if (state.readings.length > 30) {
      state.readings.length = 30;
    }

    sm16RenderReading();
  }

  /* ============================================================
     5. CONNECTION STATUS
     ============================================================ */

  async function sm16RefreshConnection() {
    try {
      const result = await sm16Call(
        SM16_APIS.status,
        []
      );

      const state = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor;

      state.connected = sm16IsConnected(result);
      state.hardwareDetected =
        result && typeof result === 'object'
          ? (
              result.hardwareDetected === true ||
              result.deviceDetected === true ||
              result.hardwareConnected === true
            )
          : state.connected;

      state.status = state.connected
        ? 'CONNECTED'
        : 'DISCONNECTED';

      sm16RenderConnection();

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      PBL_APP.scaleMonitor.connected = false;
      PBL_APP.scaleMonitor.hardwareDetected = false;
      PBL_APP.scaleMonitor.status = 'UNAVAILABLE';

      sm16RenderConnection();

      return null;
    }
  }

  function sm16RenderConnection() {
    const state = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor;

    const badge = sm16El('sm16ConnectionBadge');

    if (badge) {
      badge.className =
        'sm16-status ' +
        (state.connected ? 'online' : 'offline');
    }

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16ConnectionText',
      state.connected ? 'CONNECTÉ' : 'NON CONNECTÉ'
    );

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16ConnectionState',
      state.connected ? 'EN LIGNE' : 'HORS LIGNE'
    );

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16Hardware',
      state.hardwareDetected
        ? 'Détecté'
        : 'Non détecté'
    );
  }

  /* ============================================================
     6. LIVE WEIGHT READING
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblScaleMonitorRefresh = async function () {
    const state = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor;

    if (state.polling) return;

    state.polling = true;

    try {
      await sm16RefreshConnection();

      const result = await sm16Call(
        SM16_APIS.weight,
        []
      );

      const data = sm16ExtractWeight(result);

      if (!data.available || data.weight === null) {
        state.readingAvailable = false;
        state.errors.unshift(
          'Aucune valeur de poids valide reçue.'
        );

        state.errors = state.errors.slice(0, 10);

        sm16RenderUnavailable();
        return;
      }

      if (!Number.isFinite(data.weight) || data.weight < 0) {
        throw new Error('Valeur de poids invalide.');
      }

      state.source = data.source;

      const simulated = sm16IsSimulation(
        data.source,
        data.raw
      );

      if (simulated) {
        state.connected = false;
        state.hardwareDetected = false;
        state.status = 'SIMULATION';
      }

      sm16RecordReading(data);

      if (!simulated) {
        state.readingAvailable = true;
      }

      sm16SetText(
        'sm16LastUpdate',
        sm16Time(state.lastUpdate)
      );

      sm16RenderConnection();

      sm16ToastIfNeeded(data);

    } catch (error) {
      state.readingAvailable = false;

      state.errors.unshift(
        error && error.message
          ? error.message
          : 'Erreur de lecture.'
      );

      state.errors = state.errors.slice(0, 10);

      sm16RenderUnavailable();

    } finally {
      state.polling = false;
    }
  };

  function sm16ToastIfNeeded(data) {
    if (data.stable === false) {
      sm16SetText(
        'sm16SignalText',
        'Attention : le poids reçu est instable.'
      );
    }
  }

  function sm16RenderUnavailable() {
    sm16SetText('sm16LiveWeight', '---');
    sm16SetText('sm16Stability', 'INCONNUE');
    sm16SetText('sm16Source', 'INDISPONIBLE');
    sm16SetText('sm16Zero', 'INCONNU');
    sm16SetText('sm16SignalText', 'Aucune lecture valide disponible.');

    const bar = sm16El('sm16SignalBar');

    if (bar) {
      bar.style.width = '0%';
    }

    sm16RenderDiagnostics();
  }

  /* ============================================================
     7. RENDER LIVE READING
     ============================================================ */

  function sm16RenderReading() {
    const state = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor;

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16LiveWeight',
      sm16FormatWeight(state.currentWeight)
    );

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16LastUpdate',
      sm16Time(state.lastUpdate)
    );

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16Source',
      state.source
    );

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16Previous',
      sm16FormatWeight(state.previousWeight)
    );

    const variation =
      state.previousWeight === null
        ? null
        : state.currentWeight - state.previousWeight;

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16Variation',
      variation === null
        ? '---'
        : (
            variation > 0 ? '+' : ''
          ) + sm16FormatWeight(variation) + ' KG'
    );

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16Stability',
      state.stable === true
        ? 'STABLE'
        : state.stable === false
          ? 'INSTABLE'
          : 'NON FOURNIE'
    );

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16Zero',
      state.currentWeight === 0
        ? 'OUI'
        : state.currentWeight === null
          ? 'INCONNU'
          : 'NON'
    );

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16ReadingCount',
      state.readings.length
    );

    const bar = sm16El('sm16SignalBar');

    if (bar) {
      bar.style.width =
        state.stable === true
          ? '100%'
          : state.stable === false
            ? '35%'
            : '60%';
    }

    sm16SetText(
      'sm16SignalText',
      state.stable === true
        ? 'Le serveur signale une lecture stable.'
        : state.stable === false
          ? 'Lecture instable : ne pas valider cette mesure.'
          : 'Lecture reçue ; stabilité non communiquée par le serveur.'
    );

    sm16RenderHistory();
    sm16RenderDiagnostics();
  }

  /* ============================================================
     8. HISTORY TABLE
     ============================================================ */

  function sm16RenderHistory() {
    const body = sm16El('sm16History');

    if (!body) return;

    const readings = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor.readings;

    if (!readings.length) {
      body.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="5" class="sm16-empty">
            Aucune lecture reçue.
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
      return;
    }

    body.innerHTML = readings.map(function (reading) {
      const stable =
        reading.stable === true
          ? 'STABLE'
          : reading.stable === false
            ? 'INSTABLE'
            : 'NON FOURNIE';

      return `
        <tr>
          <td>${sm16Escape(sm16Time(reading.time))}</td>
          <td><strong>${sm16Escape(sm16FormatWeight(reading.weight))}</strong></td>
          <td>${sm16Escape(sm16FormatWeight(reading.variation))}</td>
          <td>${stable}</td>
          <td>${sm16Escape(reading.source)}</td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ============================================================
     9. DIAGNOSTICS
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblScaleMonitorDiagnostics = async function () {
    const container = sm16El('sm16Diagnostics');

    if (container) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div class="sm16-empty">Diagnostic en cours...</div>';
    }

    let serverResult = null;

    try {
      serverResult = await sm16Call(
        SM16_APIS.diagnostics,
        []
      );
    } catch (error) {
      serverResult = {
        available: false,
        message: error.message || 'Diagnostic serveur indisponible.'
      };
    }

    const state = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor;

    const checks = [
      {
        name: 'Communication serveur',
        ok: !!serverResult &&
          serverResult.available !== false
      },
      {
        name: 'Connexion matériel',
        ok: state.connected
      },
      {
        name: 'Valeur de poids reçue',
        ok: state.readingAvailable
      },
      {
        name: 'Stabilité confirmée',
        ok: state.stable === true
      },
      {
        name: 'Source non simulée',
        ok: !sm16IsSimulation(state.source)
      }
    ];

    const serverMessage =
      serverResult && typeof serverResult === 'object'
        ? (
            serverResult.message ||
            serverResult.details ||
            ''
          )
        : '';

    if (container) {
      container.innerHTML = `
        <div style="display:grid;gap:9px;">
          ${checks.map(function (check) {
            return `
              <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;padding:9px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.15);">
                <span>${sm16Escape(check.name)}</span>
                <strong>${check.ok ? 'OK' : 'NON CONFIRMÉ'}</strong>
              </div>
            `;
          }).join('')}
        </div>

        <div class="sm16-help">
          ${sm16Escape(serverMessage)}
        </div>

        <div class="sm16-help">
          Un diagnostic positif ne remplace pas un contrôle métrologique,
          une vérification de l'étalonnage ou une inspection physique
          de l'installation.
        </div>
      `;
    }

    return {
      checks: checks,
      server: serverResult
    };
  };

  function sm16RenderDiagnostics() {
    const state = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor;

    if (!state.errors.length) return;

    const container = sm16El('sm16Diagnostics');

    if (!container) return;

    container.innerHTML = `
      <strong>Derniers problèmes signalés</strong>
      <ul>
        ${state.errors.slice(0, 5).map(function (error) {
          return '<li>' + sm16Escape(error) + '</li>';
        }).join('')}
      </ul>
    `;
  }

  /* ============================================================
     10. AUTOMATIC MONITORING
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblScaleMonitorStart = function () {
    const state = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor;

    if (state.timer) {
      sm16Toast('La surveillance est déjà active.', 'info');
      return;
    }

    pblScaleMonitorRefresh();

    state.timer = window.setInterval(function () {
      pblScaleMonitorRefresh();
    }, state.pollInterval);

    sm16Toast(
      'Surveillance du pont bascule démarrée.',
      'success'
    );

    return true;
  };

  window.pblScaleMonitorStop = function () {
    const state = PBL_APP.scaleMonitor;

    if (state.timer) {
      window.clearInterval(state.timer);
      state.timer = null;
    }

    sm16Toast(
      'Surveillance automatique arrêtée.',
      'info'
    );

    return true;
  };

  /* ============================================================
     11. INITIALIZATION
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblInitializeScaleMonitor = function () {
    if (PBL_APP.scaleMonitor.initialized) {
      return true;
    }

    const root = sm16EnsureInterface();

    if (!root) return false;

    PBL_APP.scaleMonitor.initialized = true;

    pblScaleMonitorRefresh();

    console.log(
      'PONT BASCULE LIVE — Scale Monitor initialized.'
    );

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      pblInitializeScaleMonitor
    );
  } else {
    pblInitializeScaleMonitor();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ============================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 17
   PROFESSIONAL TICKET PRINTING & PRINT MANAGEMENT
   ============================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  PBL_APP.printCenter = PBL_APP.printCenter || {
    selectedTicket: null,
    history: [],
    layout: 'A4',
    includeSignature: true,
    includeCompany: true,
    includeQrPayload: true,
    printing: false
  };

  const PT17_APIS = {
    ticket: [
      'apiGetTicketById',
      'getTicketById',
      'apiFindTicket',
      'findTicketByNumber'
    ],

    history: [
      'apiGetPrintHistory',
      'apiPbl17GetPrintHistory',
      'pbl17GetPrintHistory',
      'getPrintHistory'
    ],

    record: [
      'apiRecordPrintHistory',
      'apiPbl17RecordPrintHistory',
      'pbl17RecordPrintHistory',
      'recordPrintHistory'
    ],

    printable: [
      'apiPreparePrintableTicket',
      'apiPbl17PreparePrintableTicket',
      'pbl17PreparePrintableTicket',
      'preparePrintableTicket'
    ]
  };

  let pt17Root = null;

  /* ============================================================
     1. GENERAL UTILITIES
     ============================================================ */

  function pt17El(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function pt17Escape(value) {
    const str = String(value == null ? '' : value);

    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(str);
    }

    return str.replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (char) {
      return {
        '&': '&amp;',
        '<': '&lt;',
        '>': '&gt;',
        '"': '&quot;',
        "'": '&#039;'
      }[char];
    });
  }

  function pt17Call(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Communication serveur indisponible.')
      );
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function pt17Toast(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL PRINT]', message);
    }
  }

  function pt17Value(record, keys, fallback) {
    record = record || {};

    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const value = record[keys[i]];

      if (
        value !== undefined &&
        value !== null &&
        value !== ''
      ) {
        return value;
      }
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  function pt17Kg(value) {
    const number = Number(value);

    return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : 0;
  }

  function pt17FormatKg(value) {
    return pt17Kg(value).toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
      minimumFractionDigits: 0,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2
    });
  }

  function pt17FormatTonnes(value) {
    return (pt17Kg(value) / 1000).toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
      minimumFractionDigits: 3,
      maximumFractionDigits: 3
    });
  }

  function pt17Date(value) {
    if (!value) return null;

    if (typeof value === 'number') {
      const date = new Date(
        value < 1000000000000 ? value * 1000 : value
      );

      return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
    }

    const raw = String(value);
    const match = raw.match(
      /^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})/
    );

    if (match) {
      const date = new Date(
        Number(match[3]),
        Number(match[2]) - 1,
        Number(match[1])
      );

      return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
    }

    const date = new Date(raw);

    return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
  }

  function pt17FormatDate(value) {
    const date = pt17Date(value);

    if (!date) return String(value || '—');

    return date.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', {
      timeZone: 'Africa/Casablanca',
      day: '2-digit',
      month: '2-digit',
      year: 'numeric'
    });
  }

  function pt17FormatTime(value) {
    const date = pt17Date(value);

    if (!date) return '—';

    return date.toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR', {
      timeZone: 'Africa/Casablanca',
      hour: '2-digit',
      minute: '2-digit',
      second: '2-digit'
    });
  }

  function pt17FindPage() {
    return pt17El('ticketsPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="tickets"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-tickets');
  }

  function pt17Rows(result) {
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result;

    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return [];

    const candidates = [
      result.history,
      result.records,
      result.items,
      result.tickets,
      result.data
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
      if (Array.isArray(candidates[i])) {
        return candidates[i];
      }
    }

    return [];
  }

  function pt17TicketNumber(ticket) {
    return pt17Value(ticket, [
      'ticketNumber',
      'number',
      'bonNumber',
      'numeroBon',
      'nBon'
    ], 'SANS NUMÉRO');
  }

  function pt17Gross(ticket) {
    return pt17Kg(pt17Value(ticket, [
      'grossKg',
      'grossWeightKg',
      'grossWeight',
      'firstWeight',
      'weight1'
    ], 0));
  }

  function pt17Tare(ticket) {
    return pt17Kg(pt17Value(ticket, [
      'tareKg',
      'tareWeightKg',
      'tareWeight',
      'secondWeight',
      'weight2'
    ], 0));
  }

  function pt17Net(ticket) {
    const net = pt17Value(ticket, [
      'netKg',
      'netWeightKg',
      'netWeight',
      'net'
    ], null);

    if (net !== null) return pt17Kg(net);

    return Math.abs(pt17Gross(ticket) - pt17Tare(ticket));
  }

  function pt17TicketDate(ticket) {
    return pt17Value(ticket, [
      'timestamp',
      'createdAt',
      'date',
      'weighingDate'
    ], '');
  }

  /* ============================================================
     3. TICKET COLLECTION
     ============================================================ */

  function pt17Tickets() {
    return Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data?.tickets)
      ? PBL_APP.data.tickets
      : [];
  }

  window.pblPrintLoadTicketSelect = function () {
    const select = pt17El('pt17TicketSelect');

    if (!select) return;

    const tickets = pt17Tickets();

    select.innerHTML =
      '<option value="">Sélectionner un ticket chargé</option>';

    tickets.forEach(function (ticket, index) {
      const option = document.createElement('option');

      option.value = String(index);
      option.textContent =
        String(pt17TicketNumber(ticket)) +
        ' — ' +
        String(pt17Value(ticket, [
          'product',
          'productName'
        ], 'Produit non renseigné'));

      select.appendChild(option);
    });

    pt17Toast(
      tickets.length + ' ticket(s) chargé(s).',
      'success'
    );
  };

  /* ============================================================
     4. FIND TICKET
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblPrintFindTicket = async function () {
    const number = String(
      pt17El('pt17TicketNumber')?.value || ''
    ).trim();

    if (!number) {
      pt17Toast('Entrez un numéro de bon.', 'warning');
      return null;
    }

    const local = pt17Tickets().find(function (ticket) {
      return String(pt17TicketNumber(ticket)) === number;
    });

    if (local) {
      PBL_APP.printCenter.selectedTicket = local;

      pblPrintPreviewSelected();

      pt17Toast('Ticket trouvé.', 'success');

      return local;
    }

    try {
      const result = await pt17Call(
        PT17_APIS.ticket,
        [number]
      );

      let ticket = result && result.ticket
        ? result.ticket
        : result;

      if (
        !ticket ||
        typeof ticket !== 'object' ||
        Array.isArray(ticket)
      ) {
        throw new Error('Ticket introuvable.');
      }

      PBL_APP.printCenter.selectedTicket = ticket;

      pblPrintPreviewSelected();

      pt17Toast('Ticket récupéré depuis le serveur.', 'success');

      return ticket;

    } catch (error) {
      pt17Toast(
        'Ticket introuvable dans les données disponibles.',
        'error'
      );

      return null;
    }
  };

  /* ============================================================
     5. SELECT TICKET
     ============================================================ */

  function pt17GetSelectedTicket() {
    const select = pt17El('pt17TicketSelect');

    if (!select || select.value === '') {
      return PBL_APP.printCenter.selectedTicket;
    }

    const index = Number(select.value);

    return pt17Tickets()[index] || null;
  }

  window.pblPrintPreviewSelected = function () {
    const ticket = pt17GetSelectedTicket();

    if (!ticket) {
      pt17Toast('Aucun ticket sélectionné.', 'warning');
      return null;
    }

    PBL_APP.printCenter.selectedTicket = ticket;

    const layout = pt17El('pt17Layout')?.value || 'A4';

    PBL_APP.printCenter.layout = layout;

    pt17RenderPreview(ticket);

    return ticket;
  };

  /* ============================================================
     6. COMPANY INFORMATION
     ============================================================ */

  function pt17Company() {
    const settings = PBL_APP.settings || {};

    return {
      name: pt17Value(settings, [
        'companyName',
        'company',
        'businessName'
      ], 'PONT BASCULE LIVE'),

      address: pt17Value(settings, [
        'companyAddress',
        'address'
      ], ''),

      phone: pt17Value(settings, [
        'companyPhone',
        'phone'
      ], ''),

      ice: pt17Value(settings, [
        'ice',
        'companyIce'
      ], ''),

      logo: pt17Value(settings, [
        'logoUrl',
        'companyLogo'
      ], '')
    };
  }

  /* ============================================================
     7. BUILD PRINTABLE TICKET
     ============================================================ */

  function pt17BuildTicketHTML(ticket, layout) {
    const company = pt17Company();

    const number = pt17TicketNumber(ticket);

    const date = pt17TicketDate(ticket);

    const gross = pt17Gross(ticket);
    const tare = pt17Tare(ticket);
    const net = pt17Net(ticket);

    const firstDate = pt17Value(ticket, [
      'firstWeightTime',
      'firstWeighingTime',
      'grossTime'
    ], '');

    const secondDate = pt17Value(ticket, [
      'secondWeightTime',
      'secondWeighingTime',
      'tareTime'
    ], '');

    const vehicle = pt17Value(ticket, [
      'truckRegistration',
      'registration',
      'vehicle'
    ], '—');

    const driver = pt17Value(ticket, [
      'driver',
      'driverName'
    ], '—');

    const product = pt17Value(ticket, [
      'product',
      'productName'
    ], '—');

    const client = pt17Value(ticket, [
      'client',
      'clientName'
    ], '—');

    const observations = pt17Value(ticket, [
      'notes',
      'observation',
      'remarks'
    ], '');

    const companyIncluded =
      pt17El('pt17IncludeCompany')?.checked !== false;

    const signaturesIncluded =
      pt17El('pt17IncludeSignature')?.checked !== false;

    const qrIncluded =
      pt17El('pt17IncludeQR')?.checked !== false;

    const companyHTML = companyIncluded
      ? `
        <div style="text-align:center;margin-bottom:14px;">
          <h2 style="margin:0;">${pt17Escape(company.name)}</h2>
          ${company.address
            ? '<div>' + pt17Escape(company.address) + '</div>'
            : ''}
          ${company.phone
            ? '<div>Tél : ' + pt17Escape(company.phone) + '</div>'
            : ''}
          ${company.ice
            ? '<div>ICE : ' + pt17Escape(company.ice) + '</div>'
            : ''}
        </div>
      `
      : '';

    const signaturesHTML = signaturesIncluded
      ? `
        <div class="pt17-signatures">
          <div class="pt17-signature">
            Signature du chauffeur
          </div>

          <div class="pt17-signature">
            Cachet et signature
          </div>
        </div>
      `
      : '';

    const reference = String(
      pt17Value(ticket, [
        'id',
        'ticketId',
        'recordId'
      ], number)
    );

    const referenceHTML = qrIncluded
      ? `
        <div style="font-size:10px;margin-top:14px;">
          Référence de vérification : ${pt17Escape(reference)}
        </div>
      `
      : '';

    const heading = `
      <div class="pt17-heading">
        ${companyHTML}
        <h2 style="margin:10px 0;">BON DE PESÉE</h2>
        <div style="font-size:13px;">
          <strong>N° ${pt17Escape(number)}</strong>
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    const information = `
      <table>
        <tbody>
          <tr>
            <th>Date</th>
            <td>${pt17Escape(pt17FormatDate(date))}</td>
            <th>Heure</th>
            <td>${pt17Escape(pt17FormatTime(date))}</td>
          </tr>

          <tr>
            <th>Client</th>
            <td colspan="3">${pt17Escape(client)}</td>
          </tr>

          <tr>
            <th>Produit</th>
            <td colspan="3">${pt17Escape(product)}</td>
          </tr>

          <tr>
            <th>Véhicule</th>
            <td>${pt17Escape(vehicle)}</td>
            <th>Chauffeur</th>
            <td>${pt17Escape(driver)}</td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>
    `;

    const weights = `
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>DÉSIGNATION</th>
            <th>POIDS KG</th>
            <th>HEURE</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>

        <tbody>
          <tr>
            <td>Poids brut</td>
            <td>${pt17Escape(pt17FormatKg(gross))}</td>
            <td>${pt17Escape(pt17FormatTime(firstDate))}</td>
          </tr>

          <tr>
            <td>Tare</td>
            <td>${pt17Escape(pt17FormatKg(tare))}</td>
            <td>${pt17Escape(pt17FormatTime(secondDate))}</td>
          </tr>

          <tr>
            <td><strong>POIDS NET</strong></td>
            <td class="pt17-total">
              ${pt17Escape(pt17FormatKg(net))}
            </td>
            <td>KG</td>
          </tr>
        </tbody>
      </table>

      <div style="text-align:right;margin-top:10px;">
        <strong>Poids net en tonnes : ${pt17Escape(pt17FormatTonnes(net))} T</strong>
      </div>
    `;

    const notes = observations
      ? `
        <div style="margin-top:15px;">
          <strong>Observations :</strong>
          ${pt17Escape(observations)}
        </div>
      `
      : '';

    if (layout === 'THERMAL') {
      return `
        <div style="font-family:Arial,sans-serif;max-width:420px;margin:auto;">
          ${heading}

          <div style="font-size:12px;margin-top:13px;line-height:1.8;">
            <div>Date : ${pt17Escape(pt17FormatDate(date))}</div>
            <div>Heure : ${pt17Escape(pt17FormatTime(date))}</div>
            <div>Client : ${pt17Escape(client)}</div>
            <div>Produit : ${pt17Escape(product)}</div>
            <div>Camion : ${pt17Escape(vehicle)}</div>
            <div>Chauffeur : ${pt17Escape(driver)}</div>
          </div>

          <hr style="border:0;border-top:1px dashed #555;margin:12px 0;">

          <div style="line-height:2;">
            <div>BRUT : ${pt17Escape(pt17FormatKg(gross))} KG</div>
            <div>TARE : ${pt17Escape(pt17FormatKg(tare))} KG</div>
            <div style="font-size:21px;font-weight:bold;">
              NET : ${pt17Escape(pt17FormatKg(net))} KG
            </div>
            <div>NET : ${pt17Escape(pt17FormatTonnes(net))} T</div>
          </div>

          ${notes}
          ${signaturesHTML}
          ${referenceHTML}

          <div style="text-align:center;margin-top:18px;font-size:10px;">
            Merci de votre confiance.
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }

    return `
      <div style="font-family:Arial,sans-serif;">
        ${heading}
        ${information}
        ${weights}
        ${notes}
        ${signaturesHTML}
        ${referenceHTML}

        <div style="text-align:center;margin-top:25px;font-size:10px;">
          Document de pesée — PONT BASCULE LIVE
        </div>
      </div>
    `;
  }

  /* ============================================================
     8. PREVIEW
     ============================================================ */

  function pt17RenderPreview(ticket) {
    const container = pt17El('pblPrintDocument');

    if (!container) return;

    const layout = pt17El('pt17Layout')?.value || 'A4';

    container.innerHTML = pt17BuildTicketHTML(
      ticket,
      layout
    );

    container.style.maxWidth =
      layout === 'THERMAL' ? '420px' : '100%';

    container.style.margin = '15px auto';
  }

  /* ============================================================
     9. PRINT WINDOW
     ============================================================ */

  function pt17Print(layout) {
    const ticket = PBL_APP.printCenter.selectedTicket;

    if (!ticket) {
      pt17Toast('Sélectionnez un ticket avant impression.', 'warning');
      return;
    }

    if (PBL_APP.printCenter.printing) {
      return;
    }

    PBL_APP.printCenter.printing = true;

    const content = pt17BuildTicketHTML(ticket, layout);

    const printWindow = window.open(
      '',
      '_blank',
      'width=950,height=750'
    );

    if (!printWindow) {
      PBL_APP.printCenter.printing = false;

      pt17Toast(
        'Autorisez les fenêtres pop-up pour imprimer.',
        'warning'
      );

      return;
    }

    const pageSize = layout === 'THERMAL'
      ? '80mm auto'
      : 'A4';

    printWindow.document.open();

    printWindow.document.write(`
      <!DOCTYPE html>
      <html lang="fr">
      <head>
        <meta charset="UTF-8">
        <title>Bon de pesée ${pt17Escape(pt17TicketNumber(ticket))}</title>

        <style>
          @page {
            size:${pageSize};
            margin:${layout === 'THERMAL' ? '4mm' : '12mm'};
          }

          * {
            box-sizing:border-box;
          }

          body {
            margin:0;
            padding:0;
            background:white;
            color:#111827;
            font-family:Arial,sans-serif;
            font-size:${layout === 'THERMAL' ? '11px' : '13px'};
          }

          table {
            width:100%;
            border-collapse:collapse;
            margin-top:15px;
          }

          th,td {
            border:1px solid #cbd5e1;
            padding:${layout === 'THERMAL' ? '5px' : '9px'};
            text-align:left;
          }

          th {
            background:#f1f5f9;
          }

          .pt17-heading {
            text-align:center;
            border-bottom:2px solid #111827;
            padding-bottom:13px;
          }

          .pt17-total {
            font-size:${layout === 'THERMAL' ? '17px' : '22px'};
            font-weight:800;
          }

          .pt17-signatures {
            display:grid;
            grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;
            gap:20px;
            margin-top:40px;
          }

          .pt17-signature {
            border-top:1px solid #555;
            padding-top:8px;
            min-height:40px;
            text-align:center;
          }

          @media print {
            body {
              -webkit-print-color-adjust:exact;
              print-color-adjust:exact;
            }
          }
        </style>
      </head>

      <body>
        ${content}
      </body>
      </html>
    `);

    printWindow.document.close();

    const finish = function () {
      PBL_APP.printCenter.printing = false;
    };

    printWindow.onload = function () {
      printWindow.focus();

      window.setTimeout(function () {
        try {
          printWindow.print();
        } catch (error) {
          pt17Toast('Erreur pendant l’impression.', 'error');
        }

        finish();
      }, 400);
    };

    window.setTimeout(finish, 10000);

    pt17RecordPrint(ticket, layout);
  }

  window.pblPrintSelectedTicket = function () {
    pt17Print('A4');
  };

  window.pblPrintSelectedTicketThermal = function () {
    pt17Print('THERMAL');
  };

  /* ============================================================
     10. PRINT HISTORY
     ============================================================ */

  async function pt17RecordPrint(ticket, layout) {
    const record = {
      ticketNumber: pt17TicketNumber(ticket),
      ticketId: pt17Value(ticket, [
        'id',
        'ticketId'
      ], ''),
      layout: layout,
      operation: 'PRINT_REQUESTED',
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    try {
      await pt17Call(
        PT17_APIS.record,
        [record]
      );
    } catch (error) {
      PBL_APP.printCenter.history.unshift(record);

      PBL_APP.printCenter.history =
        PBL_APP.printCenter.history.slice(0, 100);
    }

    pt17RenderHistory();
  }

  window.pblPrintRefreshHistory = async function () {
    const body = pt17El('pt17HistoryBody');

    if (body) {
      body.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="4" class="pt17-empty">
            Chargement...
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }

    try {
      const result = await pt17Call(
        PT17_APIS.history,
        []
      );

      PBL_APP.printCenter.history = pt17Rows(result);

    } catch (error) {
      if (!PBL_APP.printCenter.history.length) {
        PBL_APP.printCenter.history = [];
      }
    }

    pt17RenderHistory();

    return PBL_APP.printCenter.history;
  };

  function pt17RenderHistory() {
    const body = pt17El('pt17HistoryBody');

    if (!body) return;

    const history = PBL_APP.printCenter.history;

    if (!history.length) {
      body.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="4" class="pt17-empty">
            Aucun historique disponible.
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
      return;
    }

    body.innerHTML = history.map(function (item) {
      return `
        <tr>
          <td>
            ${pt17Escape(pt17FormatDate(
              pt17Value(item, ['timestamp', 'date', 'createdAt'], '')
            ))}
          </td>

          <td>
            ${pt17Escape(pt17Value(item, [
              'ticketNumber',
              'number'
            ], '—'))}
          </td>

          <td>
            ${pt17Escape(pt17Value(item, [
              'layout',
              'format'
            ], '—'))}
          </td>

          <td>
            ${pt17Escape(pt17Value(item, [
              'operation',
              'action'
            ], 'IMPRESSION'))}
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ============================================================
     11. INITIALIZATION
     ============================================================ */

  window.pblInitializePrintCenter = function () {
    const root = pt17EnsureInterface();

    if (!root) return false;

    pblPrintLoadTicketSelect();
    pblPrintRefreshHistory();

    console.log(
      'PONT BASCULE LIVE — Print Center initialized.'
    );

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      pblInitializePrintCenter
    );
  } else {
    pblInitializePrintCenter();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ================================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  PBL_APP.transactionControl = PBL_APP.transactionControl || {
    active: false,
    busy: false,
    validated: false,
    firstWeight: null,
    secondWeight: null,
    netWeight: null,
    firstTimestamp: null,
    secondTimestamp: null,
    transactionId: null,
    validationErrors: [],
    validationWarnings: [],
    lastSubmission: null,
    duplicateGuard: false
  };

  const TC18 = PBL_APP.transactionControl;

  const TC18_CONFIG = {
    minimumWeightKg: 1,
    maximumWeightKg: 1000000,
    maximumDifferenceMinutes: 1440,
    duplicateProtectionMs: 8000,
    allowedZeroWeight: false,
    requirePositiveNet: true,
    requireVehicle: true,
    requireProduct: true,
    requireClient: true
  };

  let tc18Root = null;

  /* ================================================================
     1. UTILITIES
     ================================================================ */

  function tc18El(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function tc18Escape(value) {
    const text = String(value == null ? '' : value);

    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(text);
    }

    return text.replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return {
        '&': '&amp;',
        '<': '&lt;',
        '>': '&gt;',
        '"': '&quot;',
        "'": '&#039;'
      }[c];
    });
  }

  function tc18Number(value) {
    if (typeof value === 'string') {
      value = value.trim().replace(/\s/g, '').replace(',', '.');
    }

    const number = Number(value);

    return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : null;
  }

  function tc18Round(value) {
    return Math.round((Number(value) + Number.EPSILON) * 100) / 100;
  }

  function tc18Format(value) {
    return Number(value || 0).toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
      minimumFractionDigits: 0,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2
    });
  }

  function tc18Now() {
    return new Date().toISOString();
  }

  function tc18Toast(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL TRANSACTION]', message);
    }
  }

  function tc18HasAccess() {
    if (typeof pblHasEditAccess === 'function') {
      return !!pblHasEditAccess();
    }

    return !!(
      PBL_APP.access &&
      PBL_APP.access.unlocked
    );
  }

  function tc18Call(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Connexion au serveur indisponible.')
      );
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function tc18SetText(id, value) {
    const element = tc18El(id);
    if (element) element.textContent = String(value ?? '');
  }

  function tc18ReadValue(ids) {
    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
      const element = tc18El(ids[i]);

      if (element && element.value !== undefined) {
        const value = String(element.value).trim();

        if (value) return value;
      }
    }

    return '';
  }

  function tc18ReadWeight(ids) {
    for (let i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
      const element = tc18El(ids[i]);

      if (!element) continue;

      const value = tc18Number(element.value);

      if (value !== null) return value;
    }

    return null;
  }

  function tc18GetCurrentWeight() {
    const candidates = [
      'scaleDisplay',
      'liveScaleWeight',
      'currentScaleWeight',
      'scaleWeight',
      'liveWeight',
      'pt17LiveWeight'
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
      const element = tc18El(candidates[i]);

      if (!element) continue;

      const raw = element.value !== undefined
        ? element.value
        : element.textContent;

      const match = String(raw || '').replace(/\s/g, '')
        .replace(',', '.')
        .match(/-?\d+(\.\d+)?/);

      if (match) {
        const number = Number(match[0]);

        if (Number.isFinite(number)) return number;
      }
    }

    if (
      PBL_APP.scale &&
      PBL_APP.scale.weight !== undefined
    ) {
      return tc18Number(PBL_APP.scale.weight);
    }

    if (
      PBL_APP.weighing &&
      PBL_APP.weighing.currentWeight !== undefined
    ) {
      return tc18Number(PBL_APP.weighing.currentWeight);
    }

    return null;
  }

  function tc18UniqueId() {
    return 'PBL-TX-' +
      Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase() +
      '-' +
      Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8).toUpperCase();
  }

  function tc18DifferenceMinutes(a, b) {
    const first = new Date(a).getTime();
    const second = new Date(b).getTime();

    if (!Number.isFinite(first) || !Number.isFinite(second)) {
      return null;
    }

    return Math.abs(second - first) / 60000;
  }

    return tc18El('weighingPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="weighing"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-weighing');
  }

    `;

    page.appendChild(tc18Root);

    return tc18Root;

  /* ================================================================
     3. READ WEIGHING DATA
     ================================================================ */

  function tc18ReadWeighingState() {
    const weighing = PBL_APP.weighing || {};

    const first = tc18Number(
      weighing.firstWeight ??
      weighing.grossWeight ??
      weighing.grossKg
    );

    const second = tc18Number(
      weighing.secondWeight ??
      weighing.tareWeight ??
      weighing.tareKg
    );

    const gross = first !== null
      ? first
      : tc18ReadWeight([
          'grossWeight',
          'grossKg',
          'firstWeight',
          'weight1'
        ]);

    const tare = second !== null
      ? second
      : tc18ReadWeight([
          'tareWeight',
          'tareKg',
          'secondWeight',
          'weight2'
        ]);

    const firstTimestamp =
      weighing.firstWeightTime ||
      weighing.firstTimestamp ||
      weighing.firstWeighingTime ||
      TC18.firstTimestamp;

    const secondTimestamp =
      weighing.secondWeightTime ||
      weighing.secondTimestamp ||
      weighing.secondWeighingTime ||
      TC18.secondTimestamp;

    return {
      gross: gross,
      tare: tare,
      firstTimestamp: firstTimestamp || null,
      secondTimestamp: secondTimestamp || null
    };
  }

  function tc18ReadTransactionDetails() {
    return {
      vehicle: tc18ReadValue([
        'truckRegistration',
        'vehicleRegistration',
        'truckSelect',
        'truck'
      ]),

      client: tc18ReadValue([
        'clientSelect',
        'client',
        'clientName'
      ]),

      product: tc18ReadValue([
        'productSelect',
        'product',
        'productName'
      ]),

      driver: tc18ReadValue([
        'driverSelect',
        'driver',
        'driverName'
      ])
    };
  }

  /* ================================================================
     4. VALIDATION ENGINE
     ================================================================ */

  function tc18Validate() {
    const errors = [];
    const warnings = [];

    const weights = tc18ReadWeighingState();
    const details = tc18ReadTransactionDetails();

    if (weights.gross === null) {
      errors.push('La première pesée est manquante.');
    }

    if (weights.tare === null) {
      errors.push('La deuxième pesée est manquante.');
    }

    if (weights.gross !== null) {
      if (weights.gross < 0) {
        errors.push('Le poids brut ne peut pas être négatif.');
      }

      if (weights.gross > TC18_CONFIG.maximumWeightKg) {
        errors.push('Le poids brut dépasse la limite configurée.');
      }

      if (
        !TC18_CONFIG.allowedZeroWeight &&
        weights.gross === 0
      ) {
        errors.push('Le poids brut est égal à zéro.');
      }
    }

    if (weights.tare !== null) {
      if (weights.tare < 0) {
        errors.push('La tare ne peut pas être négative.');
      }

      if (weights.tare > TC18_CONFIG.maximumWeightKg) {
        errors.push('La tare dépasse la limite configurée.');
      }

      if (
        !TC18_CONFIG.allowedZeroWeight &&
        weights.tare === 0
      ) {
        errors.push('La tare est égale à zéro.');
      }
    }

    let net = null;

    if (
      weights.gross !== null &&
      weights.tare !== null
    ) {
      net = tc18Round(
        Math.abs(weights.gross - weights.tare)
      );

      if (
        TC18_CONFIG.requirePositiveNet &&
        net <= 0
      ) {
        errors.push('Le poids net doit être supérieur à zéro.');
      }

      if (
        net > TC18_CONFIG.maximumWeightKg
      ) {
        errors.push('Le poids net dépasse la limite configurée.');
      }

      if (weights.tare > weights.gross) {
        warnings.push(
          'La tare est supérieure au poids brut. Vérifiez l’ordre des pesées.'
        );
      }

      if (net < TC18_CONFIG.minimumWeightKg) {
        warnings.push(
          'Le poids net est inférieur au seuil minimal configuré.'
        );
      }
    }

    if (
      TC18_CONFIG.requireVehicle &&
      !details.vehicle
    ) {
      errors.push('Le véhicule n’est pas renseigné.');
    }

    if (
      TC18_CONFIG.requireClient &&
      !details.client
    ) {
      errors.push('Le client n’est pas renseigné.');
    }

    if (
      TC18_CONFIG.requireProduct &&
      !details.product
    ) {
      errors.push('Le produit n’est pas renseigné.');
    }

    if (!details.driver) {
      warnings.push('Le chauffeur n’est pas renseigné.');
    }

    if (
      weights.firstTimestamp &&
      weights.secondTimestamp
    ) {
      const elapsed = tc18DifferenceMinutes(
        weights.firstTimestamp,
        weights.secondTimestamp
      );

      if (
        elapsed !== null &&
        elapsed > TC18_CONFIG.maximumDifferenceMinutes
      ) {
        warnings.push(
          'Un délai important sépare les deux pesées.'
        );
      }
    }

    if (
      weights.firstTimestamp &&
      weights.secondTimestamp &&
      new Date(weights.secondTimestamp).getTime() <
      new Date(weights.firstTimestamp).getTime()
    ) {
      errors.push(
        'La date de la deuxième pesée précède celle de la première.'
      );
    }

    return {
      valid: errors.length === 0,
      errors: errors,
      warnings: warnings,
      gross: weights.gross,
      tare: weights.tare,
      net: net,
      details: details,
      firstTimestamp: weights.firstTimestamp,
      secondTimestamp: weights.secondTimestamp
    };
  }

  /* ================================================================
     5. DISPLAY VALIDATION
     ================================================================ */

  function tc18RenderValidation(result) {
    TC18.validationErrors = result.errors.slice();
    TC18.validationWarnings = result.warnings.slice();
    TC18.validated = result.valid;

    const status = tc18El('tc18OverallStatus');

    if (status) {
      status.textContent = result.valid
        ? 'CONTRÔLE RÉUSSI'
        : 'ANOMALIES DÉTECTÉES';

      status.className =
        'tc18-status ' +
        (result.valid ? 'good' : 'bad');
    }

    const validation = tc18El('tc18ValidationStatus');

    if (validation) {
      validation.innerHTML = result.valid
        ? '<span class="tc18-status good">Les contrôles obligatoires sont satisfaits.</span>'
        : '<span class="tc18-status bad">La transaction doit être corrigée.</span>';
    }

    const errors = tc18El('tc18Errors');

    if (errors) {
      errors.innerHTML = result.errors.length
        ? `
          <div class="tc18-errors">
            <strong>Erreurs à corriger</strong>
            <ul class="tc18-list">
              ${result.errors.map(function (item) {
                return '<li>' + tc18Escape(item) + '</li>';
              }).join('')}
            </ul>
          </div>
        `
        : '';
    }

    const warnings = tc18El('tc18Warnings');

    if (warnings) {
      warnings.innerHTML = result.warnings.length
        ? `
          <div class="tc18-warnings">
            <strong>Points à vérifier</strong>
            <ul class="tc18-list">
              ${result.warnings.map(function (item) {
                return '<li>' + tc18Escape(item) + '</li>';
              }).join('')}
            </ul>
          </div>
        `
        : '';
    }

    const button = tc18El('tc18FinalizeButton');

    if (button) {
      button.disabled = !result.valid || TC18.busy;
    }

    tc18SetText(
      'tc18FirstWeight',
      result.gross === null
        ? '— KG'
        : tc18Format(result.gross) + ' KG'
    );

    tc18SetText(
      'tc18SecondWeight',
      result.tare === null
        ? '— KG'
        : tc18Format(result.tare) + ' KG'
    );

    tc18SetText(
      'tc18NetWeight',
      result.net === null
        ? '— KG'
        : tc18Format(result.net) + ' KG'
    );

    tc18SetText(
      'tc18NetTonnes',
      result.net === null
        ? '— T'
        : tc18Format(result.net / 1000) + ' T'
    );

    tc18SetText(
      'tc18Vehicle',
      result.details.vehicle || '—'
    );

    tc18SetText(
      'tc18Client',
      result.details.client || '—'
    );

    tc18SetText(
      'tc18Product',
      result.details.product || '—'
    );

    tc18SetText(
      'tc18Driver',
      result.details.driver || '—'
    );

    tc18SetText(
      'tc18FirstTime',
      result.firstTimestamp
        ? new Date(result.firstTimestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR')
        : 'Heure non disponible'
    );

    tc18SetText(
      'tc18SecondTime',
      result.secondTimestamp
        ? new Date(result.secondTimestamp).toLocaleString('fr-FR')
        : 'Heure non disponible'
    );
  }

  /* ================================================================
     6. PUBLIC VALIDATION ACTIONS
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblTC18ValidateTransaction = function () {
    const result = tc18Validate();

    TC18.firstWeight = result.gross;
    TC18.secondWeight = result.tare;
    TC18.netWeight = result.net;

    tc18RenderValidation(result);

    if (result.valid) {
      tc18Toast(
        'Contrôle effectué. Vérifiez les avertissements avant de continuer.',
        'success'
      );
    } else {
      tc18Toast(
        result.errors.length + ' anomalie(s) détectée(s).',
        'error'
      );
    }

    return result;
  };

  window.pblTC18RefreshSummary = function () {
    const result = tc18Validate();

    tc18RenderValidation(result);

    return result;
  };

  window.pblTC18ResetValidation = function () {
    TC18.validated = false;
    TC18.validationErrors = [];
    TC18.validationWarnings = [];

    const errors = tc18El('tc18Errors');
    const warnings = tc18El('tc18Warnings');

    if (errors) errors.innerHTML = '';
    if (warnings) warnings.innerHTML = '';

    const status = tc18El('tc18OverallStatus');

    if (status) {
      status.textContent = 'EN ATTENTE';
      status.className = 'tc18-status';
    }

    const validation = tc18El('tc18ValidationStatus');

    if (validation) {
      validation.textContent =
        'Contrôle réinitialisé. Relancez la vérification.';
    }

    const button = tc18El('tc18FinalizeButton');

    if (button) button.disabled = true;

    tc18Toast('Contrôle réinitialisé.', 'info');
  };

  /* ================================================================
     7. TRANSACTION SNAPSHOT
     ================================================================ */

  function tc18BuildSnapshot(result) {
    return {
      transactionId: TC18.transactionId || tc18UniqueId(),

      grossKg: tc18Round(result.gross),
      tareKg: tc18Round(result.tare),
      netKg: tc18Round(result.net),

      netTonnes: tc18Round(result.net / 1000),

      vehicle: result.details.vehicle,
      client: result.details.client,
      product: result.details.product,
      driver: result.details.driver,

      firstWeightTime: result.firstTimestamp,
      secondWeightTime: result.secondTimestamp,

      createdAt: tc18Now(),

      validation: {
        passed: true,
        errors: [],
        warnings: result.warnings.slice()
      },

      source: 'PBL_BROWSER_TRANSACTION_CONTROL'
    };
  }

  /* ================================================================
     8. DUPLICATE SUBMISSION PROTECTION
     ================================================================ */

  function tc18CreateFingerprint(snapshot) {
    return [
      snapshot.vehicle,
      snapshot.client,
      snapshot.product,
      snapshot.grossKg,
      snapshot.tareKg,
      snapshot.netKg
    ].join('|').toLowerCase();
  }

  function tc18CheckDuplicate(snapshot) {
    const fingerprint = tc18CreateFingerprint(snapshot);

    const previous = TC18.lastSubmission;

    if (!previous) return false;

    const elapsed = Date.now() - previous.time;

    return (
      previous.fingerprint === fingerprint &&
      elapsed < TC18_CONFIG.duplicateProtectionMs
    );
  }

  /* ================================================================
     9. FINALIZATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblTC18FinalizeTransaction = async function () {
    if (TC18.busy) {
      tc18Toast('Une opération est déjà en cours.', 'warning');
      return false;
    }

    if (!tc18HasAccess()) {
      tc18Toast(
        'Déverrouillez le système avec votre PIN pour enregistrer.',
        'warning'
      );

      if (typeof pblOpenUnlockModal === 'function') {
        pblOpenUnlockModal();
      } else if (typeof openUnlockModal === 'function') {
        openUnlockModal();
      }

      return false;
    }

    const result = tc18Validate();

    tc18RenderValidation(result);

    if (!result.valid) {
      tc18Toast(
        'Impossible de valider : corrigez les erreurs.',
        'error'
      );

      return false;
    }

    const snapshot = tc18BuildSnapshot(result);

    if (tc18CheckDuplicate(snapshot)) {
      tc18Toast(
        'Une demande identique vient déjà d’être envoyée.',
        'warning'
      );

      return false;
    }

    TC18.busy = true;

    const button = tc18El('tc18FinalizeButton');

    if (button) {
      button.disabled = true;
      button.textContent = 'ENREGISTREMENT...';
    }

    TC18.lastSubmission = {
      fingerprint: tc18CreateFingerprint(snapshot),
      time: Date.now()
    };

    try {
      /*
       * The actual server endpoint must validate:
       * - current PIN session
       * - ticket state
       * - gross and tare
       * - net = absolute gross - tare
       * - duplicate transaction ID
       * - business rules
       *
       * Candidate endpoints are attempted for compatibility.
       */

      const response = await tc18Call([
        'apiPbl18FinalizeTransaction',
        'pbl18FinalizeTransaction',
        'apiFinalizeWeighingTransaction',
        'apiCreateTicket',
        'createTicket'
      ], [snapshot]);

      if (
        response &&
        response.success === false
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          response.message || 'Le serveur a refusé la transaction.'
        );
      }

      if (
        response &&
        response.ok === false
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          response.message || 'Échec de l’enregistrement.'
        );
      }

      if (
        response &&
        response.status === 'ERROR'
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          response.message || 'Erreur serveur.'
        );
      }

      TC18.lastSubmission = {
        fingerprint: tc18CreateFingerprint(snapshot),
        time: Date.now(),
        completed: true
      };

      TC18.active = false;
      TC18.validated = false;

      tc18Toast(
        'Demande d’enregistrement transmise au serveur.',
        'success'
      );

      if (typeof refreshAllBusinessData === 'function') {
        await refreshAllBusinessData();
      } else if (typeof loadDashboard === 'function') {
        await loadDashboard();
      }

      return response;

    } catch (error) {
      /*
       * Do not automatically retry a failed request.
       * A timeout may occur after the server has committed.
       * Verify the ticket list before submitting again.
       */
      tc18Toast(
        'Résultat de l’enregistrement à vérifier : ' +
        (error.message || 'erreur inconnue'),
        'error'
      );

      return false;

    } finally {
      TC18.busy = false;

      if (button) {
        button.textContent = 'Valider et enregistrer le ticket';

        const current = tc18Validate();

        button.disabled = !current.valid;
      }
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     10. CONNECT TO EXISTING WEIGHING WORKFLOW
     ================================================================ */

  function tc18WrapFunction(functionName, onComplete) {
    const original = window[functionName];

    if (
      typeof original !== 'function' ||
      original.__tc18Wrapped
    ) {
      return;
    }

    const wrapped = function () {
      const result = original.apply(this, arguments);

      if (result && typeof result.then === 'function') {
        return result.then(function (value) {
          onComplete();
          return value;
        });
      }

      onComplete();

      return result;
    };

    wrapped.__tc18Wrapped = true;
    wrapped.__tc18Original = original;

    window[functionName] = wrapped;
  }

  function tc18AfterWeightCapture() {
    TC18.validated = false;

    const weights = tc18ReadWeighingState();

    if (weights.gross !== null) {
      TC18.firstWeight = weights.gross;
      TC18.firstTimestamp =
        weights.firstTimestamp || TC18.firstTimestamp;
    }

    if (weights.tare !== null) {
      TC18.secondWeight = weights.tare;
      TC18.secondTimestamp =
        weights.secondTimestamp || TC18.secondTimestamp;
    }

    TC18.netWeight =
      weights.gross !== null && weights.tare !== null
        ? tc18Round(Math.abs(weights.gross - weights.tare))
        : null;

    tc18RenderValidation(tc18Validate());
  }

  function tc18InstallWorkflowHooks() {
    [
      'captureFirstWeight',
      'captureSecondWeight',
      'finalizeWeighing',
      'startWeighingSession',
      'clearWeighingForm'
    ].forEach(function (name) {
      tc18WrapFunction(name, tc18AfterWeightCapture);
    });
  }

  /* ================================================================
     11. MANUAL REFRESH AND OBSERVATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblTC18ReadLiveScale = function () {
    const weight = tc18GetCurrentWeight();

    if (weight === null) {
      tc18Toast(
        'Aucune lecture de poids disponible.',
        'warning'
      );

      return null;
    }

    tc18Toast(
      'Lecture actuelle : ' + tc18Format(weight) + ' KG',
      'info'
    );

    return weight;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     12. INITIALIZATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblInitializeTransactionControl = function () {
    const root = tc18EnsureInterface();

    if (!root) return false;

    tc18InstallWorkflowHooks();

    const button = tc18El('tc18FinalizeButton');

    if (button) button.disabled = true;

    tc18AfterWeightCapture();

    console.log(
      'PONT BASCULE LIVE — Transaction Control initialized.'
    );

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      pblInitializeTransactionControl
    );
  } else {
    pblInitializeTransactionControl();
  }

<script>
/* ================================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE

   ADVANCED DASHBOARD & OPERATIONS CONTROL CENTER
   ================================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  PBL_APP.operationsCenter = PBL_APP.operationsCenter || {
    initialized: false,
    refreshing: false,
    autoRefresh: true,
    refreshSeconds: 15,
    lastRefresh: null,
    refreshTimer: null,
    dashboard: {},
    tickets: [],
    objectives: [],
    alerts: [],
    selectedPeriod: 'ALL',
    selectedMetric: 'TONNES'
  };

  const OC19 = PBL_APP.operationsCenter;

  const OC19_API = {
    dashboard: [
      'apiPbl8GetLiveDashboard',
      'pbl8GetLiveDashboard',
      'pbl18GetLiveDashboard',
      'getMainDashboardData'
    ],

    tickets: [
      'apiGetTickets',
      'apiGetAllTickets',
      'getAllTickets',
      'getTickets'
    ],

    objectives: [
      'apiGetObjectives',
      'apiGetAllObjectives',
      'getAllObjectives',
      'getObjectives'
    ],

    alerts: [
      'apiGetAlerts',
      'apiPbl8GetAlerts',
      'pbl8GetAlerts',
      'getSystemAlerts'
    ],

    activity: [
      'apiGetRecentActivity',
      'apiPbl8GetRecentActivity',
      'pbl8GetRecentActivity',
      'getRecentActivity'
    ]
  };

  let oc19Root = null;

  /* ================================================================
     1. UTILITIES
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19El(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function oc19Escape(value) {
    const text = String(value == null ? '' : value);

    if (typeof pblEscapeHTML === 'function') {
      return pblEscapeHTML(text);
    }

    return text.replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return {
        '&': '&amp;',
        '<': '&lt;',
        '>': '&gt;',
        '"': '&quot;',
        "'": '&#039;'
      }[c];
    });
  }

  function oc19Number(value) {
    if (typeof value === 'string') {
      value = value.trim().replace(/\s/g, '').replace(',', '.');
    }

    const number = Number(value);

    return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : 0;
  }

  function oc19Format(value, decimals) {
    return Number(value || 0).toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
      minimumFractionDigits: decimals || 0,
      maximumFractionDigits: decimals === undefined ? 2 : decimals
    });
  }

  function oc19Call(names, args) {
    if (typeof pblCallFirst !== 'function') {
      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Communication serveur indisponible.')
      );
    }

    return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
  }

  function oc19Toast(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL OPERATIONS]', message);
    }
  }

  function oc19SetText(id, value) {
    const element = oc19El(id);

    if (element) {
      element.textContent = String(value ?? '');
    }
  }

  function oc19Rows(result, keys) {
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result;

    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return [];

    const candidates = keys || [
      'records',
      'items',
      'tickets',
      'objectives',
      'alerts',
      'activity',
      'history',
      'data'
    ];

    for (let i = 0; i < candidates.length; i++) {
      if (Array.isArray(result[candidates[i]])) {
        return result[candidates[i]];
      }
    }

    return [];
  }

  function oc19First(record, keys, fallback) {
    record = record || {};

    for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
      const value = record[keys[i]];

      if (
        value !== undefined &&
        value !== null &&
        value !== ''
      ) {
        return value;
      }
    }

    return fallback;
  }

  function oc19Date(value) {
    if (!value) return null;

    if (value instanceof Date) return value;

    if (typeof value === 'number') {
      const date = new Date(
        value < 1000000000000 ? value * 1000 : value
      );

      return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
    }

    const raw = String(value);

    const french = raw.match(
      /^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})/
    );

    if (french) {
      return new Date(
        Number(french[3]),
        Number(french[2]) - 1,
        Number(french[1])
      );
    }

    const date = new Date(raw);

    return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
  }

  function oc19DateKey(value) {
    const date = oc19Date(value);

    if (!date) return '';

    return [
      date.getFullYear(),
      String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0'),
      String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')
    ].join('-');
  }

  function oc19IsActive(ticket) {
    const status = String(
      oc19First(ticket, ['status', 'state'], 'ACTIVE')
    ).toUpperCase();

    return ![
      'CANCELLED',
      'CANCELED',
      'DELETED',
      'ARCHIVED',
      'ANNULÉ',
      'ANNULE'
    ].includes(status);
  }

  function oc19TicketKg(ticket) {
    const kg = oc19First(ticket, [
      'netKg',
      'netWeightKg',
      'netWeight',
      'net'
    ], null);

    if (kg !== null) return oc19Number(kg);

    return oc19Number(
      oc19First(ticket, ['netTonnes'], 0)
    ) * 1000;
  }

  function oc19TicketDate(ticket) {
    return oc19First(ticket, [
      'timestamp',
      'createdAt',
      'date',
      'weighingDate'
    ], '');
  }

  function oc19TicketNumber(ticket) {
    return oc19First(ticket, [
      'ticketNumber',
      'number',
      'bonNumber',
      'numeroBon'
    ], '—');
  }

  function oc19TicketProduct(ticket) {
    return oc19First(ticket, [
      'product',
      'productName'
    ], 'Non renseigné');
  }

  function oc19TicketClient(ticket) {
    return oc19First(ticket, [
      'client',
      'clientName'
    ], 'Non renseigné');
  }

  function oc19TicketTruck(ticket) {
    return oc19First(ticket, [
      'truckRegistration',
      'registration',
      'vehicle'
    ], '—');
  }

  function oc19TicketDriver(ticket) {
    return oc19First(ticket, [
      'driver',
      'driverName'
    ], '—');
  }

  }

            <span class="oc19-label">MOYENNE / VOYAGE</span>
            <div id="oc19Average" class="oc19-value">0 T</div>

          <div class="oc19-grid" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr));">
            <div class="oc19-card">
              <span class="oc19-label">TICKETS ACTIFS</span>
              <div id="oc19ActiveTickets" class="oc19-value">0</div>
            </div>

            <div class="oc19-card">

      </div>

      <div class="oc19-two" style="margin-top:0;">
        <div class="oc19-panel">
          <h3 style="margin-top:0;">Derniers tickets enregistrés</h3>

          <div class="oc19-table-wrap">
            <table>

                  <td colspan="5" class="oc19-empty">
                    Chargement...
