        <div class="oc19-panel">
          <h3 style="margin-top:0;">Alertes et surveillance</h3>

          <div id="oc19Alerts">
            <div class="oc19-empty">
              Aucune alerte chargée.
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div class="oc19-panel">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;">
          <div>
            <h3 style="margin:0 0 5px;">Actualisation automatique</h3>

            <div class="oc19-muted">
              Les données sont rechargées depuis les services disponibles.
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="oc19-actions">
            <select id="oc19RefreshRate"
              onchange="pblOC19SetRefreshRate(this.value)">
              <option value="5">5 secondes</option>
              <option value="15" selected>15 secondes</option>
              <option value="30">30 secondes</option>
              <option value="60">60 secondes</option>
            </select>

            <button type="button"
              onclick="pblOC19ToggleAutoRefresh()"
              id="oc19AutoButton">
              Désactiver
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="oc19-muted" style="margin-top:12px;">
          L'actualisation dépend de la disponibilité du serveur.
          Elle ne constitue pas une connexion directe au pont-bascule.
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    page.appendChild(oc19Root);

    return oc19Root;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     4. LOCAL DATA
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19LocalTickets() {
    return Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data?.tickets)
      ? PBL_APP.data.tickets
      : [];
  }

  function oc19LocalObjectives() {
    return Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data?.objectives)
      ? PBL_APP.data.objectives
      : [];
  }

  function oc19LocalAlerts() {
    return Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data?.alerts)
      ? PBL_APP.data.alerts
      : [];
  }

  /* ================================================================
     5. FETCH DASHBOARD DATA
     ================================================================ */

  async function oc19FetchDashboard() {
    try {
      const result = await oc19Call(
        OC19_API.dashboard,
        []
      );

      OC19.dashboard = result || {};

      return result;

    } catch (error) {
      console.warn('Dashboard server data unavailable.', error);
      return null;
    }
  }

  async function oc19FetchTickets() {
    try {
      const result = await oc19Call(
        OC19_API.tickets,
        []
      );

      const rows = oc19Rows(result, [
        'tickets',
        'records',
        'items',
        'data'
      ]);

      if (rows.length) {
        OC19.tickets = rows;
        PBL_APP.data = PBL_APP.data || {};
        PBL_APP.data.tickets = rows;
      } else {
        OC19.tickets = oc19LocalTickets();
      }

    } catch (error) {
      OC19.tickets = oc19LocalTickets();
    }

    return OC19.tickets;
  }

  async function oc19FetchObjectives() {
    try {
      const result = await oc19Call(
        OC19_API.objectives,
        []
      );

      const rows = oc19Rows(result, [
        'objectives',
        'records',
        'items',
        'data'
      ]);

      OC19.objectives = rows.length
        ? rows
        : oc19LocalObjectives();

    } catch (error) {
      OC19.objectives = oc19LocalObjectives();
    }

    return OC19.objectives;
  }

  async function oc19FetchAlerts() {
    try {
      const result = await oc19Call(
        OC19_API.alerts,
        []
      );

      OC19.alerts = oc19Rows(result, [
        'alerts',
        'records',
        'items',
        'data'
      ]);

    } catch (error) {
      OC19.alerts = oc19LocalAlerts();
    }

    return OC19.alerts;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     6. OBJECTIVE CALCULATIONS
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19ActiveObjective() {
    const active = OC19.objectives.filter(function (objective) {
      const status = String(
        oc19First(objective, ['status', 'state'], 'ACTIVE')
      ).toUpperCase();

      return ![
        'ARCHIVED',
        'CANCELLED',
        'CANCELED',
        'COMPLETED'
      ].includes(status);
    });

    return active.length ? active[0] : null;
  }

  function oc19DashboardMetric(keys, fallback) {
    return oc19Number(
      oc19First(OC19.dashboard, keys, fallback)
    );
  }

  function oc19TargetTonnes() {
    const dashboard = OC19.dashboard || {};

    const directTonnes = oc19First(dashboard, [
      'targetTonnes',
      'targetQuantityTonnes'
    ], null);

    if (directTonnes !== null) {
      return oc19Number(directTonnes);
    }

    const target = oc19First(dashboard, [
      'targetQuantity',
      'target',
      'objectiveQuantity'
    ], null);

    if (target !== null) {
      return oc19Number(target);
    }

    const objective = oc19ActiveObjective();

    if (!objective) return 0;

    const tonnes = oc19First(objective, [
      'targetTonnes',
      'quantityTonnes'
    ], null);

    if (tonnes !== null) return oc19Number(tonnes);

    return oc19Number(oc19First(objective, [
      'targetQuantity',
      'targetKg',
      'quantityKg'
    ], 0)) / 1000;
  }

  function oc19CumulTonnes() {
    const dashboard = OC19.dashboard || {};

    const direct = oc19First(dashboard, [
      'cumulTonnes',
      'totalTonnes',
      'deliveredTonnes'
    ], null);

    if (direct !== null) {
      return oc19Number(direct);
    }

    const kg = oc19First(dashboard, [
      'totalKg',
      'cumulKg'
    ], null);

    if (kg !== null) {
      return oc19Number(kg) / 1000;
    }

    return OC19.tickets
      .filter(oc19IsActive)
      .reduce(function (sum, ticket) {
        return sum + oc19TicketKg(ticket);
      }, 0) / 1000;
  }

  function oc19VoyageCount() {
    const dashboard = OC19.dashboard || {};

    const direct = oc19First(dashboard, [
      'numberOfVoyages',
      'totalTrips',
      'voyages'
    ], null);

    if (direct !== null) {
      return oc19Number(direct);
    }

    return OC19.tickets.filter(oc19IsActive).length;
  }

  function oc19RenderMainMetrics() {
    const target = oc19TargetTonnes();
    const cumul = oc19CumulTonnes();
    const trips = oc19VoyageCount();

    const remaining = Math.max(0, target - cumul);

    const percent = target > 0
      ? Math.min(100, cumul / target * 100)
      : 0;

    oc19SetText('oc19Target', oc19Format(target, 3) + ' T');
    oc19SetText('oc19Cumul', oc19Format(cumul, 3) + ' T');
    oc19SetText('oc19Trips', oc19Format(trips));
    oc19SetText('oc19Remaining', oc19Format(remaining, 3) + ' T');

    oc19SetText(
      'oc19ProgressText',
      oc19Format(percent, 1) + '%'
    );

    const bar = oc19El('oc19ProgressBar');

    if (bar) {
      bar.style.width = percent + '%';
    }

    const status = oc19El('oc19ObjectiveStatus');

    if (status) {
      status.textContent = target > 0
        ? 'OBJECTIF ACTIF'
        : 'OBJECTIF NON DÉFINI';

      status.className =
        'oc19-status ' +
        (target > 0 ? 'good' : 'warn');
    }
  }

  /* ================================================================
     7. PERIOD FILTERING
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19FilterTickets(period) {
    const now = new Date();

    const today = oc19DateKey(now);

    const weekStart = new Date(now);
    weekStart.setDate(now.getDate() - 6);
    weekStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

    const month = now.getMonth();
    const year = now.getFullYear();

    return OC19.tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      if (!oc19IsActive(ticket)) return false;

      const date = oc19Date(oc19TicketDate(ticket));

      if (!date) return false;

      if (period === 'TODAY') {
        return oc19DateKey(date) === today;
      }

      if (period === 'WEEK') {
        return date >= weekStart && date <= now;
      }

      if (period === 'MONTH') {
        return (
          date.getMonth() === month &&
          date.getFullYear() === year
        );
      }

      return true;
    });
  }

  function oc19RenderPeriod() {
    const period = OC19.selectedPeriod;

    const rows = oc19FilterTickets(period);

    const kg = rows.reduce(function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + oc19TicketKg(ticket);
    }, 0);

    const tonnes = kg / 1000;

    const average = rows.length
      ? tonnes / rows.length
      : 0;

    oc19SetText(
      'oc19PeriodTonnes',
      oc19Format(tonnes, 3) + ' T'
    );

    oc19SetText(
      'oc19PeriodTrips',
      oc19Format(rows.length)
    );

    oc19SetText(
      'oc19Average',
      oc19Format(average, 3) + ' T'
    );

    const todayRows = oc19FilterTickets('TODAY');

    const todayKg = todayRows.reduce(function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + oc19TicketKg(ticket);
    }, 0);

    oc19SetText(
      'oc19TodayTonnes',
      oc19Format(todayKg / 1000, 3) + ' T'
    );
  }

  window.pblOC19ChangePeriod = function (period) {
    OC19.selectedPeriod = period || 'ALL';

    oc19RenderPeriod();
    oc19RenderChart();
  };

  /* ================================================================
     8. OPERATION COUNTERS
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19RenderOperationCounters() {
    const tickets = OC19.tickets;

    const active = tickets.filter(oc19IsActive);

    const cancelled = tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return !oc19IsActive(ticket);
    });

    const trucks = new Set();
    const products = new Set();

    active.forEach(function (ticket) {
      trucks.add(String(oc19TicketTruck(ticket)));
      products.add(String(oc19TicketProduct(ticket)));
    });

    oc19SetText('oc19ActiveTickets', active.length);
    oc19SetText('oc19CancelledTickets', cancelled.length);

    oc19SetText(
      'oc19Trucks',
      trucks.has('—') ? trucks.size - 1 : trucks.size
    );

    oc19SetText(
      'oc19Products',
      products.has('Non renseigné')
        ? products.size - 1
        : products.size
    );
  }

  /* ================================================================
     9. RECENT TICKETS
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19RenderRecentTickets() {
    const body = oc19El('oc19RecentTickets');

    if (!body) return;

    const tickets = OC19.tickets.slice().sort(function (a, b) {
      const dateA = oc19Date(oc19TicketDate(a));
      const dateB = oc19Date(oc19TicketDate(b));

      return (dateB ? dateB.getTime() : 0) -
        (dateA ? dateA.getTime() : 0);
    }).slice(0, 10);

    if (!tickets.length) {
      body.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="5" class="oc19-empty">
            Aucun ticket disponible.
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
      return;
    }

    body.innerHTML = tickets.map(function (ticket) {
      const date = oc19Date(oc19TicketDate(ticket));

      return `
        <tr>
          <td>${oc19Escape(oc19TicketNumber(ticket))}</td>

          <td>
            ${date
              ? oc19Escape(date.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR'))
              : '—'}
          </td>

          <td>${oc19Escape(oc19TicketProduct(ticket))}</td>

          <td>${oc19Escape(oc19TicketTruck(ticket))}</td>

          <td>${oc19Format(oc19TicketKg(ticket))}</td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ================================================================
     10. DELIVERY CHART
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19RenderChart() {
    const container = oc19El('oc19Chart');

    if (!container) return;

    const now = new Date();

    const days = [];

    for (let i = 6; i >= 0; i--) {
      const date = new Date(now);

      date.setDate(now.getDate() - i);

      days.push({
        date: date,
        key: oc19DateKey(date),
        kg: 0
      });
    }

    const dayMap = {};

    days.forEach(function (day) {
      dayMap[day.key] = day;
    });

    OC19.tickets.filter(oc19IsActive).forEach(function (ticket) {
      const key = oc19DateKey(oc19TicketDate(ticket));

      if (dayMap[key]) {
        dayMap[key].kg += oc19TicketKg(ticket);
      }
    });

    const max = Math.max(
      1,
      ...days.map(function (day) {
        return day.kg;
      })
    );

    container.innerHTML = days.map(function (day) {
      const percent = Math.max(
        3,
        day.kg / max * 100
      );

      return `
        <div
          class="oc19-bar"
          title="${oc19Escape(oc19Format(day.kg / 1000, 3))} tonnes"
          style="height:${percent}%;">
          <span>
            ${day.date.toLocaleDateString('fr-FR', {
              day:'2-digit',
              month:'2-digit'
            })}
          </span>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ================================================================
     11. ALERTS
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19RenderAlerts() {
    const container = oc19El('oc19Alerts');

    if (!container) return;

    const alerts = OC19.alerts.slice(0, 12);

    const localAlerts = [];

    const target = oc19TargetTonnes();
    const cumul = oc19CumulTonnes();

    if (target <= 0) {
      localAlerts.push({
        title: 'Aucun objectif de livraison actif',
        severity: 'warning'
      });
    }

    if (target > 0 && cumul >= target) {
      localAlerts.push({
        title: 'Objectif de livraison atteint ou dépassé',
        severity: 'normal'
      });
    }

    const incomplete = OC19.tickets.filter(function (ticket) {
      return oc19IsActive(ticket) &&
        oc19TicketKg(ticket) <= 0;
    });

    if (incomplete.length) {
      localAlerts.push({
        title: incomplete.length +
          ' ticket(s) avec poids net nul ou absent',
        severity: 'critical'
      });
    }

    const combined = localAlerts.concat(alerts);

    if (!combined.length) {
      container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="oc19-alert normal">
          Aucun problème signalé dans les données chargées.
        </div>
      `;
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = combined.slice(0, 12).map(function (alert) {
      const severity = String(
        oc19First(alert, [
          'severity',
          'level',
          'type'
        ], 'warning')
      ).toLowerCase();

      const critical = [
        'critical',
        'error',
        'high',
        'danger'
      ].includes(severity);

      const normal = [
        'success',
        'normal',
        'info'
      ].includes(severity);

      const title = oc19First(alert, [
        'title',
        'message',
        'description',
        'name'
      ], 'Alerte système');

      return `
        <div class="oc19-alert ${
          critical ? 'critical' : normal ? 'normal' : ''
        }">
          <strong>${oc19Escape(title)}</strong>

          <div class="oc19-muted">
            ${oc19Escape(
              oc19First(alert, [
                'createdAt',
                'date',
                'timestamp'
              ], '')
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ================================================================
     12. CONNECTION STATUS
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19SetConnection(success) {
    const status = oc19El('oc19ConnectionStatus');

    if (!status) return;

    status.textContent = success
      ? 'DONNÉES ACTUALISÉES'
      : 'DONNÉES LOCALES / SERVEUR INDISPONIBLE';

    status.className =
      'oc19-status ' +
      (success ? 'good' : 'warn');
  }

  /* ================================================================
     13. RENDER ALL
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19RenderAll() {
    oc19RenderMainMetrics();
    oc19RenderPeriod();
    oc19RenderOperationCounters();
    oc19RenderRecentTickets();
    oc19RenderChart();
    oc19RenderAlerts();

    OC19.lastRefresh = new Date();

    oc19SetText(
      'oc19LastRefresh',
      OC19.lastRefresh.toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR', {
        timeZone: 'Africa/Casablanca'
      })
    );
  }

  /* ================================================================
     14. REFRESH
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblOC19Refresh = async function () {
    if (OC19.refreshing) return false;

    OC19.refreshing = true;

    try {
      const results = await Promise.allSettled([
        oc19FetchDashboard(),
        oc19FetchTickets(),
        oc19FetchObjectives(),
        oc19FetchAlerts()
      ]);

      const serverDashboardAvailable =
        results[0].status === 'fulfilled' &&
        results[0].value !== null;

      oc19SetConnection(serverDashboardAvailable);

      oc19RenderAll();

      return true;

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Operations refresh failed.', error);

      oc19SetConnection(false);
      oc19RenderAll();

      return false;

    } finally {
      OC19.refreshing = false;
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     15. AUTOMATIC REFRESH
     ================================================================ */

  function oc19StopTimer() {
    if (OC19.refreshTimer) {
      clearInterval(OC19.refreshTimer);
      OC19.refreshTimer = null;
    }
  }

  function oc19StartTimer() {
    oc19StopTimer();

    if (!OC19.autoRefresh) return;

    OC19.refreshTimer = setInterval(function () {
      const page = oc19FindDashboard();

      if (!page) return;

      const visible =
        !page.hidden &&
        page.offsetParent !== null;

      if (visible) {
        pblOC19Refresh();
      }
    }, OC19.refreshSeconds * 1000);
  }

  window.pblOC19SetRefreshRate = function (seconds) {
    const value = Number(seconds);

    if (![5, 15, 30, 60].includes(value)) {
      return;
    }

    OC19.refreshSeconds = value;

    oc19StartTimer();

    oc19Toast(
      'Actualisation réglée sur ' + value + ' secondes.',
      'success'
    );
  };

  window.pblOC19ToggleAutoRefresh = function () {
    OC19.autoRefresh = !OC19.autoRefresh;

    const button = oc19El('oc19AutoButton');

    if (button) {
      button.textContent = OC19.autoRefresh
        ? 'Désactiver'
        : 'Activer';
    }

    if (OC19.autoRefresh) {
      oc19StartTimer();
      pblOC19Refresh();
    } else {
      oc19StopTimer();
    }

    oc19Toast(
      OC19.autoRefresh
        ? 'Actualisation automatique activée.'
        : 'Actualisation automatique désactivée.',
      'info'
    );
  };

  /* ================================================================
     16. EXPORT OPERATIONS CSV
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblOC19ExportCSV = function () {
    const rows = OC19.tickets;

    if (!rows.length) {
      oc19Toast('Aucun ticket à exporter.', 'warning');
      return false;
    }

    const header = [
      'Numero bon',
      'Date',
      'Produit',
      'Client',
      'Camion',
      'Chauffeur',
      'Net KG',
      'Net Tonnes',
      'Statut'
    ];

    const lines = [header];

    rows.forEach(function (ticket) {
      const kg = oc19TicketKg(ticket);

      lines.push([
        oc19TicketNumber(ticket),
        oc19TicketDate(ticket),
        oc19TicketProduct(ticket),
        oc19TicketClient(ticket),
        oc19TicketTruck(ticket),
        oc19TicketDriver(ticket),
        kg,
        kg / 1000,
        oc19First(ticket, ['status'], 'ACTIVE')
      ]);
    });

    const csv = '\uFEFF' + lines.map(function (row) {
      return row.map(function (value) {
        return '"' +
          String(value == null ? '' : value)
            .replace(/"/g, '""') +
          '"';
      }).join(';');
    }).join('\r\n');

    const blob = new Blob(
      [csv],
      { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
    );

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

    const link = document.createElement('a');

    link.href = url;
    link.download = 'PONT_BASCULE_LIVE_OPERATIONS.csv';

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    oc19Toast('Export CSV préparé.', 'success');

    return true;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     17. INITIALIZATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblInitializeOperationsCenter = function () {
    if (OC19.initialized) {
      return true;
    }

    const root = oc19EnsureInterface();

    if (!root) return false;

    OC19.initialized = true;

    pblOC19Refresh();

    oc19StartTimer();

    console.log(
      'PONT BASCULE LIVE — Operations Center initialized.'
    );

    return true;
  };

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      pblInitializeOperationsCenter
    );
  } else {
    pblInitializeOperationsCenter();
  }

})();
</script>

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ================================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 20
   FINAL FRONTEND INTEGRATION & INITIALIZATION
   ================================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  PBL_APP.integration = {
    initialized: false,
    startedAt: new Date(),
    modules: {},
    errors: [],
    lastCheck: null
  };

  const INT20 = PBL_APP.integration;

  /* ================================================================
     1. GLOBAL UTILITIES
     ================================================================ */

  function int20Element(id) {
    return document.getElementById(id);
  }

  function int20Escape(value) {
    const text = String(value == null ? '' : value);

    return text.replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return {
        '&': '&amp;',
        '<': '&lt;',
        '>': '&gt;',
        '"': '&quot;',
        "'": '&#039;'
      }[c];
    });
  }

  function int20Toast(message, type) {
    if (typeof pblToast === 'function') {
      pblToast(message, type || 'info');
    } else {
      console.log('[PBL]', message);
    }
  }

  function int20RecordError(module, error) {
    INT20.errors.push({
      module: module,
      message: String(error && error.message || error),
      time: new Date().toISOString()
    });

    if (INT20.errors.length > 100) {
      INT20.errors.shift();
    }

    console.error('[PBL INTEGRATION]', module, error);
  }

  function int20RegisterModule(name, available) {
    INT20.modules[name] = {
      available: Boolean(available),
      checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    };
  }

  /* ================================================================
     2. MISSING MASTER DATA MODAL
     ================================================================ */

  function int20EnsureMasterModal() {
    if (int20Element('masterDataModal')) {
      return true;
    }

    const modal = document.createElement('div');

    modal.id = 'masterDataModal';

    modal.style.cssText = [
      'display:none',
      'position:fixed',
      'inset:0',
      'z-index:9999',
      'background:rgba(0,0,0,.72)',
      'align-items:center',
      'justify-content:center',
      'padding:15px'
    ].join(';');

    modal.innerHTML = `
      <div style="
        width:min(650px,100%);
        max-height:90vh;
        overflow:auto;
        background:#111827;
        color:#f9fafb;
        border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.25);
        border-radius:14px;
        padding:22px;
        box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,.45);
      ">
        <div style="
          display:flex;
          justify-content:space-between;
          align-items:center;
          gap:10px;
          margin-bottom:18px;
        ">
          <h3 id="masterDataModalTitle"
            style="margin:0;">
            Gestion des données
          </h3>

          <button type="button"
            onclick="pblCloseMasterModal()"
            style="
              border:0;
              background:transparent;
              color:inherit;
              font-size:23px;
              cursor:pointer;
            ">
            ×
          </button>
        </div>

        <form id="masterDataForm"
          onsubmit="pblSaveMasterData(event)">

          <div id="masterDataFields"></div>

          <div style="
            display:flex;
            justify-content:flex-end;
            gap:10px;
            margin-top:20px;
          ">
            <button type="button"
              onclick="pblCloseMasterModal()">
              Annuler
            </button>

            <button type="submit"
              class="primary">
              Enregistrer
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    `;

    document.body.appendChild(modal);

    modal.addEventListener('click', function (event) {
      if (event.target === modal) {
        if (typeof pblCloseMasterModal === 'function') {
          pblCloseMasterModal();
        } else {
          modal.style.display = 'none';
        }
      }
    });

    return true;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     3. NAVIGATION COMPATIBILITY
     ================================================================ */

  function int20FindPage(name) {
    const candidates = [
      name + 'Page',
      'page-' + name,
      name
    ];

    for (const id of candidates) {
      const element = int20Element(id);

      if (element) return element;
    }

    return document.querySelector(
      '[data-page="' + name + '"]'
    );
  }

  function int20RefreshPage(name) {
    const refreshers = {
      dashboard: [
        'loadDashboard',
        'pblOC19Refresh'
      ],

      weighing: [
        'refreshScaleDisplay',
        'sm16RefreshConnection'
      ],

      tickets: [
        'loadTickets',
        'loadTicketHistory'
      ],

      objectives: [
        'loadObjectives'
      ],

      products: [
        'loadProducts'
      ],

      clients: [
        'loadClients'
      ],

      trucks: [
        'loadTrucks'
      ],

      drivers: [
        'loadDrivers'
      ],

      reports: [
        'pblInitializeReports'
      ],

      search: [
        'pblInitializeAdvancedSearch'
      ],

      notifications: [
        'loadNotifications'
      ],

      maintenance: [
        'pblInitializeMaintenance'
      ],

      audit: [
        'pblInitializeAuditCenter'
      ]
    };

    const functions = refreshers[name] || [];

    functions.forEach(function (functionName) {
      try {
        const fn = window[functionName];

        if (typeof fn === 'function') {
          const result = fn();

          if (result && typeof result.catch === 'function') {
            result.catch(function (error) {
              int20RecordError(functionName, error);
            });
          }
        }
      } catch (error) {
        int20RecordError(functionName, error);
      }
    });
  }

  function int20NormalizePage(value) {
    return String(value || '')
      .replace(/^page-/, '')
      .replace(/Page$/, '')
      .toLowerCase();
  }

  function int20FindNavigationLinks() {
    return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(
      '[data-page], [data-target], [data-navigation], ' +
      '.sidebar a, .sidebar button, nav a, nav button'
    ));
  }

  function int20ConnectNavigation() {
    const links = int20FindNavigationLinks();

    links.forEach(function (element) {
      if (element.dataset.pblInt20Bound === '1') {
        return;
      }

      element.dataset.pblInt20Bound = '1';

      element.addEventListener('click', function () {
        const raw =
          element.dataset.page ||
          element.dataset.target ||
          element.dataset.navigation ||
          element.getAttribute('href') ||
          '';

        const page = int20NormalizePage(
          raw.replace(/^#/, '')
        );

        if (!page) return;

        window.setTimeout(function () {
          int20RefreshPage(page);
        }, 100);
      });
    });
  }

  /* ================================================================
     4. WRAP EXISTING NAVIGATION FUNCTION
     ================================================================ */

  function int20WrapNavigation() {
    if (typeof window.showPage !== 'function') {
      return false;
    }

    if (window.showPage.__pblInt20Wrapped) {
      return true;
    }

    const original = window.showPage;

    function wrappedShowPage(pageName) {
      const result = original.apply(this, arguments);

      const page = int20NormalizePage(pageName);

      window.setTimeout(function () {
        int20RefreshPage(page);
        int20ConnectNavigation();
      }, 100);

      return result;
    }

    wrappedShowPage.__pblInt20Wrapped = true;
    wrappedShowPage.__original = original;

    window.showPage = wrappedShowPage;

    return true;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     5. GLOBAL BUTTONS AND KEYBOARD SHORTCUTS
     ================================================================ */

  function int20ConnectKeyboard() {
    if (document.body.dataset.pblInt20Keyboard === '1') {
      return;
    }

    document.body.dataset.pblInt20Keyboard = '1';

    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (event) {
      const target = event.target;

      const isTyping = target &&
        (
          target.tagName === 'INPUT' ||
          target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA' ||
          target.tagName === 'SELECT' ||
          target.isContentEditable
        );

      if (isTyping) return;

      if (
        event.ctrlKey &&
        event.shiftKey &&
        event.key.toLowerCase() === 'r'
      ) {
        event.preventDefault();

        if (typeof pblOC19Refresh === 'function') {
          pblOC19Refresh();
        }

        if (typeof refreshAllBusinessData === 'function') {
          refreshAllBusinessData();
        }
      }

      if (
        event.ctrlKey &&
        event.shiftKey &&
        event.key.toLowerCase() === 'l'
      ) {
        event.preventDefault();

        if (typeof apiLock === 'function') {
          apiLock();
        }
      }
    });
  }

  /* ================================================================
     6. APPLICATION MODULE CHECK
     ================================================================ */

  function int20CheckModules() {
    const modules = {
      dashboard: typeof loadDashboard === 'function',
      weighing: typeof startWeighingSession === 'function',
      tickets: typeof loadTickets === 'function',
      products: typeof loadProducts === 'function',
      clients: typeof loadClients === 'function',
      trucks: typeof loadTrucks === 'function',
      drivers: typeof loadDrivers === 'function',
      objectives: typeof loadObjectives === 'function',
      reporting: typeof pblInitializeReports === 'function',
      search: typeof pblInitializeAdvancedSearch === 'function',
      maintenance: typeof pblInitializeMaintenance === 'function',
      audit: typeof pblInitializeAuditCenter === 'function',
      scaleMonitor: typeof pblInitializeScaleMonitor === 'function',
      operationsCenter: typeof pblInitializeOperationsCenter === 'function'
    };

    Object.keys(modules).forEach(function (name) {
      int20RegisterModule(name, modules[name]);
    });

    return modules;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     7. GLOBAL INITIAL DATA
     ================================================================ */

  async function int20LoadInitialData() {
    const functions = [
      'loadTickets',
      'loadProducts',
      'loadClients',
      'loadTrucks',
      'loadDrivers',
      'loadObjectives'
    ];

    const jobs = functions.map(function (name) {
      if (typeof window[name] !== 'function') {
        return Promise.resolve(null);
      }

      try {
        return Promise.resolve(window[name]()).catch(
          function (error) {
            int20RecordError(name, error);
            return null;
          }
        );
      } catch (error) {
        int20RecordError(name, error);
        return Promise.resolve(null);
      }
    });

    return Promise.allSettled(jobs);
  }

  /* ================================================================
     8. SYSTEM HEALTH DISPLAY
     ================================================================ */

  function int20EnsureHealthPanel() {
    if (int20Element('pblIntegrationHealth')) {
      return true;
    }

    const dashboard = document.getElementById('dashboardPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="dashboard"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-dashboard');

    if (!dashboard) return false;

    const panel = document.createElement('div');

    panel.id = 'pblIntegrationHealth';

    panel.style.cssText = [
      'margin-top:14px',
      'padding:12px 15px',
      'border:1px solid rgba(130,150,180,.18)',
      'border-radius:9px',
      'font-size:11px',
      'opacity:.85'
    ].join(';');

    panel.innerHTML = `
      <div style="
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        align-items:center;
        gap:10px;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
      ">
        <strong>ÉTAT DES MODULES PONT BASCULE LIVE</strong>

        <span id="pblIntegrationHealthStatus">
          Vérification...
        </span>
      </div>

      <div id="pblIntegrationHealthDetails"
        style="
          display:flex;
          flex-wrap:wrap;
          gap:8px;
          margin-top:10px;
        ">
      </div>
    `;

    dashboard.appendChild(panel);

    return true;
  }

  function int20RenderHealth() {
    int20EnsureHealthPanel();

    const details = int20Element(
      'pblIntegrationHealthDetails'
    );

    const status = int20Element(
      'pblIntegrationHealthStatus'
    );

    if (!details || !status) return;

    const modules = INT20.modules;

    const names = Object.keys(modules);

    const available = names.filter(function (name) {
      return modules[name].available;
    }).length;

    details.innerHTML = names.map(function (name) {
      const active = modules[name].available;

      return `
        <span style="
          border-radius:20px;
          padding:5px 8px;
          background:${active
            ? 'rgba(34,197,94,.12)'
            : 'rgba(245,158,11,.12)'};
          color:${active ? '#4ade80' : '#fbbf24'};
        ">
          ${int20Escape(name)}:
          ${active ? 'DISPONIBLE' : 'À VÉRIFIER'}
        </span>
      `;
    }).join('');

    status.textContent =
      available + ' / ' + names.length +
      ' fonctions détectées';

    status.style.color =
      available === names.length
        ? '#4ade80'
        : '#fbbf24';
  }

  /* ================================================================
     9. SYSTEM DIAGNOSTIC
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblIntegrationDiagnostics = function () {
    const modules = int20CheckModules();

    const result = {
      application: 'PONT BASCULE LIVE',
      version: 'Frontend Integration 20',
      initialized: INT20.initialized,
      startedAt: INT20.startedAt.toISOString(),
      checkedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      modules: modules,
      errors: INT20.errors.slice(-20),
      backendAvailable:
        typeof google !== 'undefined' &&
        Boolean(google.script) &&
        Boolean(google.script.run)
    };

    INT20.lastCheck = result;

    int20RenderHealth();

    return result;
  };

  window.pblIntegrationExportDiagnostics = function () {
    const result = pblIntegrationDiagnostics();

    const blob = new Blob(
      [JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)],
      { type: 'application/json' }
    );

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

    const link = document.createElement('a');

    link.href = url;
    link.download = 'PBL_INTEGRATION_DIAGNOSTICS.json';

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

    int20Toast('Diagnostic exporté.', 'success');
  };

  /* ================================================================
     10. GLOBAL REFRESH
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblRefreshCompleteApplication = async function () {
    const jobs = [];

    [
      'loadDashboard',
      'loadTickets',
      'loadProducts',
      'loadClients',
      'loadTrucks',
      'loadDrivers',
      'loadObjectives',
      'pblOC19Refresh'
    ].forEach(function (name) {
      if (typeof window[name] !== 'function') return;

      try {
        jobs.push(
          Promise.resolve(window[name]()).catch(
            function (error) {
              int20RecordError(name, error);
            }
          )
        );
      } catch (error) {
        int20RecordError(name, error);
      }
    });

    const result = await Promise.allSettled(jobs);

    int20Toast(
      'Actualisation terminée. ' +
      result.length + ' opérations exécutées.',
      'success'
    );

    return result;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     11. REINITIALIZE AFTER PAGE CHANGES
     ================================================================ */

  function int20InitializePageModules() {
    const initializers = [
      'pblInitializeOperationsCenter',
      'pblInitializeScaleMonitor'
    ];

    initializers.forEach(function (name) {
      try {
        if (typeof window[name] === 'function') {
          window[name]();
        }
      } catch (error) {
        int20RecordError(name, error);
      }
    });
  }

  /* ================================================================
     12. APP-WIDE ERROR CAPTURE
     ================================================================ */

  function int20InstallErrorCapture() {
    if (window.__PBL_INT20_ERRORS__) return;

    window.__PBL_INT20_ERRORS__ = true;

    window.addEventListener('error', function (event) {
      int20RecordError(
        'JavaScript',
        event.message || 'Erreur JavaScript'
      );
    });

    window.addEventListener(
      'unhandledrejection',
      function (event) {
        int20RecordError(
          'Promise',
          event.reason || 'Erreur asynchrone'
        );
      }
    );
  }

  /* ================================================================
     13. APPLICATION INITIALIZATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblInitializeIntegration = async function () {
    if (INT20.initialized) {
      return true;
    }

    try {
      int20EnsureMasterModal();

      int20InstallErrorCapture();

      int20ConnectKeyboard();

      int20ConnectNavigation();

      int20WrapNavigation();

      int20InitializePageModules();

      await int20LoadInitialData();

      int20CheckModules();

      int20RenderHealth();

      INT20.initialized = true;

      INT20.lastCheck = new Date();

      console.log(
        'PONT BASCULE LIVE — Frontend integration initialized.'
      );

      return true;

    } catch (error) {
      int20RecordError('Initialization', error);

      return false;
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     14. RETRY INITIALIZATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblRetryIntegration = async function () {
    INT20.initialized = false;

    const success = await pblInitializeIntegration();

    if (success) {
      int20Toast(
        'Initialisation des modules terminée.',
        'success'
      );
    } else {
      int20Toast(
        'Une erreur est survenue pendant l’initialisation.',
        'error'
      );
    }

    return success;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     15. START
     ================================================================ */

  function int20Start() {
    pblInitializeIntegration();

    window.setTimeout(function () {
      int20ConnectNavigation();
      int20WrapNavigation();
      int20CheckModules();
      int20RenderHealth();
    }, 1200);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      int20Start,
      { once: true }
    );
  } else {
    int20Start();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ================================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 21
   PRE-DEPLOYMENT TEST & API COMPATIBILITY CENTER
   ================================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  const PBL21 = window.PBL_APP.testing = {
    running: false,
    completed: false,
    startedAt: null,
    finishedAt: null,
    results: [],
    apiResults: [],
    summary: {
      passed: 0,
      failed: 0,
      warnings: 0,
      total: 0
    }
  };

  const p21 = {
    escape: function (value) {
      return String(value == null ? '' : value)
        .replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
          return {
            '&': '&amp;',
            '<': '&lt;',
            '>': '&gt;',
            '"': '&quot;',
            "'": '&#039;'
          }[c];
        });
    },

    addResult: function (name, status, detail) {
      PBL21.results.push({
        name: name,
        status: status,
        detail: detail || '',
        time: new Date().toISOString()
      });

      if (status === 'PASS') PBL21.summary.passed++;
      else if (status === 'FAIL') PBL21.summary.failed++;
      else PBL21.summary.warnings++;

      PBL21.summary.total++;

      p21.renderResults();
    },

    reset: function () {
      PBL21.results = [];
      PBL21.apiResults = [];

      PBL21.summary = {
        passed: 0,
        failed: 0,
        warnings: 0,
        total: 0
      };

      PBL21.completed = false;
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     1. TEST CENTER INTERFACE
     ================================================================ */

  function p21FindPage() {
    return document.getElementById('maintenancePage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="maintenance"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-maintenance') ||
      document.getElementById('dashboardPage') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-dashboard');
  }

  function p21CreateInterface() {
    if (document.getElementById('pbl21TestCenter')) {
      return;
    }

    const page = p21FindPage();

    if (!page) {
      console.warn('PBL21: No suitable page found.');
      return;
    }

    const section = document.createElement('section');

    section.id = 'pbl21TestCenter';

    section.style.cssText = `
      margin:20px 0;
      padding:20px;
      border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.25);
      border-radius:14px;
      background:rgba(15,23,42,.55);
      color:inherit;
    `;

    section.innerHTML = `
      <div style="
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        align-items:center;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
        gap:12px;
        margin-bottom:18px;
      ">
        <div>
          <h2 style="margin:0 0 5px">
            SYSTEM TEST CENTER
          </h2>
          <div style="opacity:.65;font-size:12px">
            PONT BASCULE LIVE — PRE-DEPLOYMENT VERIFICATION
          </div>
        </div>

        <span id="pbl21TestStatus"
          style="font-size:12px">
          READY
        </span>
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(120px,1fr));
        gap:10px;
        margin-bottom:18px;
      ">
        <div class="pbl21-kpi">
          <small>TESTS</small>
          <strong id="pbl21Total">0</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl21-kpi">
          <small>SUCCESS</small>
          <strong id="pbl21Passed">0</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl21-kpi">
          <small>ERRORS</small>
          <strong id="pbl21Failed">0</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl21-kpi">
          <small>WARNINGS</small>
          <strong id="pbl21Warnings">0</strong>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:flex;
        gap:9px;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
        margin-bottom:16px;
      ">
        <button type="button"
          id="pbl21RunTests"
          style="padding:10px 15px">
          RUN SYSTEM TESTS
        </button>

        <button type="button"
          id="pbl21RunApis"
          style="padding:10px 15px">
          TEST READ-ONLY APIs
        </button>

        <button type="button"
          id="pbl21Export"
          style="padding:10px 15px">
          EXPORT RESULTS
        </button>

        <button type="button"
          id="pbl21Clear"
          style="padding:10px 15px">
          CLEAR
        </button>
      </div>

      <div id="pbl21Progress"
        style="
          height:6px;
          background:rgba(148,163,184,.15);
          border-radius:10px;
          overflow:hidden;
          margin-bottom:18px;
        ">
        <div id="pbl21ProgressBar"
          style="
            height:100%;
            width:0%;
            background:#22c55e;
            transition:width .2s;
          ">
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="
        font-size:12px;
        font-weight:700;
        margin-bottom:9px;
      ">
        TEST RESULTS
      </div>

      <div id="pbl21Results"
        style="
          display:flex;
          flex-direction:column;
          gap:7px;
        ">
        <div style="opacity:.55;padding:12px">
          No tests have been executed.
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="
        margin-top:16px;
        padding:12px;
        border-radius:8px;
        background:rgba(245,158,11,.08);
        font-size:11px;
        line-height:1.6;
      ">
        IMPORTANT: These checks do not modify tickets,
        weights, objectives, or master records. Read-only API
        tests call only the explicitly listed retrieval functions.
        A passing frontend test does not certify the physical scale
        or the complete production security configuration.
      </div>
    `;

    const style = document.createElement('style');

    style.textContent = `
      .pbl21-kpi {
        padding:13px;
        border-radius:9px;
        background:rgba(148,163,184,.07);
      }

      .pbl21-kpi small {
        display:block;
        opacity:.6;
        font-size:10px;
        margin-bottom:7px;
      }

      .pbl21-kpi strong {
        font-size:22px;
      }

      .pbl21-result {
        display:flex;
        align-items:flex-start;
        justify-content:space-between;
        gap:12px;
        padding:10px 12px;
        border-radius:8px;
        background:rgba(148,163,184,.06);
        font-size:12px;
      }

      .pbl21-result small {
        display:block;
        opacity:.65;
        margin-top:4px;
      }
    `;

    document.head.appendChild(style);
    page.appendChild(section);

    document.getElementById('pbl21RunTests')
      .addEventListener('click', pbl21RunSystemTests);

    document.getElementById('pbl21RunApis')
      .addEventListener('click', pbl21RunReadOnlyApiTests);

    document.getElementById('pbl21Export')
      .addEventListener('click', pbl21ExportResults);

    document.getElementById('pbl21Clear')
      .addEventListener('click', function () {
        p21.reset();
        p21.renderResults();
        p21.setStatus('READY');
        p21.setProgress(0);
      });
  }

  p21.setStatus = function (value) {
    const el = document.getElementById('pbl21TestStatus');
    if (el) el.textContent = value;
  };

  p21.setProgress = function (value) {
    const el = document.getElementById('pbl21ProgressBar');
    if (el) {
      el.style.width = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, value)) + '%';
    }
  };

  p21.renderResults = function () {
    const ids = {
      pbl21Total: PBL21.summary.total,
      pbl21Passed: PBL21.summary.passed,
      pbl21Failed: PBL21.summary.failed,
      pbl21Warnings: PBL21.summary.warnings
    };

    Object.keys(ids).forEach(function (id) {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.textContent = ids[id];
    });

    const container = document.getElementById('pbl21Results');
    if (!container) return;

    if (!PBL21.results.length) {
      container.innerHTML =
        '<div style="opacity:.55;padding:12px">No results.</div>';
      return;
    }

    container.innerHTML = PBL21.results.map(function (item) {
      const color =
        item.status === 'PASS' ? '#4ade80' :
        item.status === 'FAIL' ? '#f87171' :
        '#fbbf24';

      return `
        <div class="pbl21-result">
          <div>
            <strong>${p21.escape(item.name)}</strong>
            <small>${p21.escape(item.detail)}</small>
          </div>

          <strong style="color:${color}">
            ${p21.escape(item.status)}
          </strong>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  };

  /* ================================================================
     2. FRONTEND STRUCTURE TESTS
     ================================================================ */

  function p21CheckElement(id, description) {
    const exists = Boolean(document.getElementById(id));

    p21.addResult(
      description,
      exists ? 'PASS' : 'WARN',
      exists ? 'Element detected.' : 'Element not found: ' + id
    );
  }

  function p21CheckFunction(name) {
    const exists = typeof window[name] === 'function';

    p21.addResult(
      'Function: ' + name,
      exists ? 'PASS' : 'WARN',
      exists ? 'Function available.' : 'Function unavailable.'
    );
  }

  function p21CheckData(name) {
    const data = PBL_APP.data;
    const exists = Boolean(
      data && Array.isArray(data[name])
    );

    p21.addResult(
      'Data collection: ' + name,
      exists ? 'PASS' : 'WARN',
      exists ? data[name].length + ' loaded records.' :
        'Collection unavailable or not an array.'
    );
  }

  window.pbl21RunSystemTests = async function () {
    if (PBL21.running) return;

    PBL21.running = true;
    PBL21.startedAt = new Date();
    P21_UNUSED();

    p21.reset();

    p21.setStatus('TESTING...');
    p21.setProgress(5);

    const tests = [
      function () {
        p21.addResult(
          'Application name',
          document.title.toLowerCase().includes('pont') ||
          document.body.innerText.includes('PONT BASCULE')
            ? 'PASS' : 'WARN',
          document.title || 'Document title not set.'
        );
      },

      function () {
        p21.addResult(
          'JavaScript runtime',
          typeof window === 'object' ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL',
          'Browser runtime detected.'
        );
      },

      function () {
        p21.addResult(
          'Google Apps Script bridge',
          typeof google !== 'undefined' &&
          Boolean(google.script && google.script.run)
            ? 'PASS' : 'WARN',
          'Checks google.script.run availability.'
        );
      },

      function () {
        p21.addResult(
          'PBL_APP state',
          Boolean(window.PBL_APP) ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL',
          'Application state object.'
        );
      },

      function () {
        p21.addResult(
          'Application data state',
          Boolean(PBL_APP.data) ? 'PASS' : 'WARN',
          'Data collections initialized.'
        );
      }
    ];

    const elements = [
      ['targetQuantity', 'Dashboard target'],
      ['cumulTonnes', 'Dashboard cumulative tonnes'],
      ['numberOfVoyages', 'Dashboard voyage count'],
      ['remainingTonnes', 'Dashboard remaining quantity'],
      ['scaleDisplay', 'Main scale display'],
      ['masterDataModal', 'Master data modal']
    ];

    const functions = [
      'showPage',
      'loadDashboard',
      'loadTickets',
      'loadProducts',
      'loadClients',
      'loadTrucks',
      'loadDrivers',
      'loadObjectives',
      'startWeighingSession',
      'captureFirstWeight',
      'captureSecondWeight',
      'finalizeWeighing',
      'apiUnlock',
      'apiLock',
      'pblIntegrationDiagnostics'
    ];

    const collections = [
      'tickets',
      'products',
      'clients',
      'trucks',
      'drivers',
      'objectives'
    ];

    let completed = 0;

    const total =
      tests.length + elements.length +
      functions.length + collections.length;

    for (const test of tests) {
      try {
        test();
      } catch (error) {
        p21.addResult('Runtime test', 'FAIL', error.message);
      }

      completed++;
      p21.setProgress(5 + completed / total * 90);
    }

    elements.forEach(function (item) {
      p21CheckElement(item[0], item[1]);
      completed++;
    });

    functions.forEach(function (name) {
      p21CheckFunction(name);
      completed++;
    });

    collections.forEach(function (name) {
      p21CheckData(name);
      completed++;
    });

    p21.setProgress(100);

    PBL21.finishedAt = new Date();
    PBL21.completed = true;
    PBL21.running = false;

    p21.setStatus(
      PBL21.summary.failed
        ? 'ERRORS DETECTED'
        : 'TESTS COMPLETED'
    );

    return PBL21.summary;
  };

  function P21_UNUSED() {
    // Reserved for future integration checks.
  }

  /* ================================================================
     3. READ-ONLY BACKEND API TESTS
     ================================================================ */

  const READ_ONLY_APIS = [
    {
      name: 'Initial application data',
      candidates: ['apiGetInitialData']
    },
    {
      name: 'Access status',
      candidates: ['apiAccessStatus']
    },
    {
      name: 'System health',
      candidates: ['apiSystemHealth']
    },
    {
      name: 'Products',
      candidates: ['apiGetProducts']
    },
    {
      name: 'Clients',
      candidates: ['apiGetClients']
    },
    {
      name: 'Trucks',
      candidates: ['apiGetTrucks']
    },
    {
      name: 'Drivers',
      candidates: ['apiGetDrivers']
    },
    {
      name: 'Tickets',
      candidates: ['apiGetTickets', 'apiGetAllTickets']
    },
    {
      name: 'Objectives',
      candidates: ['apiGetObjectives']
    },
    {
      name: 'Audit logs',
      candidates: ['apiAuditLogs']
    },
    {
      name: 'Live scale status',
      candidates: [
        'apiPbl16GetScaleStatus',
        'getScaleConnectionStatus'
      ]
    }
  ];

  function p21CallReadOnlyApi(name) {
    return new Promise(function (resolve) {
      if (
        typeof google === 'undefined' ||
        !google.script ||
        !google.script.run
      ) {
        resolve({
          success: false,
          detail: 'Google Apps Script bridge unavailable.'
        });

        return;
      }

      try {
        const runner = google.script.run
          .withSuccessHandler(function (result) {
            resolve({
              success: true,
              detail: 'Backend responded.',
              result: result
            });
          })
          .withFailureHandler(function (error) {
            resolve({
              success: false,
              detail: String(error && error.message || error)
            });
          });

        if (typeof runner[name] !== 'function') {
          resolve({
            success: false,
            detail: 'Backend function not exposed: ' + name
          });

          return;
        }

        runner[name]();

      } catch (error) {
        resolve({
          success: false,
          detail: error.message || String(error)
        });
      }
    });
  }

  window.pbl21RunReadOnlyApiTests = async function () {
    if (PBL21.running) return;

    PBL21.running = true;

    p21.setStatus('TESTING BACKEND...');

    for (let i = 0; i < READ_ONLY_APIS.length; i++) {
      const api = READ_ONLY_APIS[i];

      let chosen = null;
      let result = null;

      for (const candidate of api.candidates) {
        result = await p21CallReadOnlyApi(candidate);

        if (result.success) {
          chosen = candidate;
          break;
        }
      }

      const passed = Boolean(chosen);

      const record = {
        name: api.name,
        functionName: chosen || api.candidates.join(' / '),
        success: passed,
        detail: result ? result.detail : 'No response.',
        time: new Date().toISOString()
      };

      PBL21.apiResults.push(record);

      p21.addResult(
        'API: ' + api.name,
        passed ? 'PASS' : 'WARN',
        record.functionName + ' — ' + record.detail
      );

      p21.setProgress(
        (i + 1) / READ_ONLY_APIS.length * 100
      );
    }

    PBL21.running = false;
    PBL21.finishedAt = new Date();
    PBL21.completed = true;

    p21.setStatus('BACKEND TESTS COMPLETED');

    return PBL21.apiResults;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     4. EXPORT TEST REPORT
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl21ExportResults = function () {
    const report = {
      application: 'PONT BASCULE LIVE',
      reportVersion: '21.0',
      generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      startedAt: PBL21.startedAt,
      finishedAt: PBL21.finishedAt,
      summary: PBL21.summary,
      frontendResults: PBL21.results,
      backendApiResults: PBL21.apiResults,
      integrationDiagnostics:
        typeof pblIntegrationDiagnostics === 'function'
          ? pblIntegrationDiagnostics()
          : null
    };

    const blob = new Blob(
      [JSON.stringify(report, null, 2)],
      { type: 'application/json' }
    );

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

    const link = document.createElement('a');

    link.href = url;
    link.download =
      'PONT_BASCULE_LIVE_TEST_REPORT.json';

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  /* ================================================================
     5. PUBLIC TEST CENTER INITIALIZER
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblInitializeTestCenter = function () {
    p21CreateInterface();
    p21.renderResults();
    return true;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     6. START
     ================================================================ */

  function p21Start() {
    window.setTimeout(function () {
      pblInitializeTestCenter();
    }, 500);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      p21Start,
      { once: true }
    );
  } else {
    p21Start();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ================================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 22
   SECURITY CONTROL CENTER

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  const SEC22 = window.PBL_APP.securityCenter = {
    initialized: false,
    access: null,
    health: null,
    lastRefresh: null,
    events: [],
    refreshing: false
  };

  const S22 = {
    escape: function (value) {
      return String(value == null ? '' : value)
        .replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
          return {
            '&': '&amp;',
            '<': '&lt;',
            '>': '&gt;',
            '"': '&quot;',
            "'": '&#039;'
          }[c];
        });
    },

    element: function (id) {
      return document.getElementById(id);
    },

    log: function (type, message) {
      SEC22.events.unshift({
        type: type,
        message: message,
        time: new Date().toISOString()
      });

      SEC22.events = SEC22.events.slice(0, 50);

      S22.renderEvents();
    },

    setText: function (id, value) {
      const el = S22.element(id);
      if (el) el.textContent = value;
    },

    call: function (names, args) {
      if (typeof pblCallFirst === 'function') {
        return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
      }

      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Backend communication helper unavailable.')
      );
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     1. SECURITY CENTER INTERFACE
     ================================================================ */

  function s22FindPage() {
    return document.getElementById('settingsPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="settings"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-settings') ||
      document.getElementById('maintenancePage') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-maintenance');
  }

  function s22CreateInterface() {
    if (S22.element('pbl22SecurityCenter')) return true;

    const page = s22FindPage();

    if (!page) {
      console.warn('PBL22: Settings page not found.');
      return false;
    }

    const section = document.createElement('section');

    section.id = 'pbl22SecurityCenter';

    section.style.cssText = `
      margin:20px 0;
      padding:20px;
      border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.22);
      border-radius:14px;
      background:rgba(15,23,42,.58);
      color:inherit;
    `;

    section.innerHTML = `
      <div style="
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        align-items:center;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
        gap:12px;
        margin-bottom:20px;
      ">
        <div>
          <h2 style="margin:0 0 5px">
            SECURITY CONTROL CENTER
          </h2>

          <div style="font-size:12px;opacity:.65">
            Access protection · Session monitoring · System health
          </div>
        </div>

        <span id="pbl22OverallStatus">
          NOT VERIFIED
        </span>
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(170px,1fr));
        gap:12px;
        margin-bottom:18px;
      ">
        <div class="pbl22-card">
          <small>ACCESS MODE</small>
          <strong id="pbl22AccessMode">READ ONLY</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl22-card">
          <small>EDITING SESSION</small>
          <strong id="pbl22SessionStatus">LOCKED</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl22-card">
          <small>BACKEND HEALTH</small>
          <strong id="pbl22BackendStatus">UNKNOWN</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl22-card">
          <small>LAST CHECK</small>
          <strong id="pbl22LastCheck">--:--:--</strong>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:flex;
        gap:10px;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
        margin-bottom:20px;
      ">
        <button type="button" id="pbl22Refresh">
          REFRESH SECURITY STATUS
        </button>

        <button type="button" id="pbl22Lock">
          LOCK APPLICATION
        </button>

        <button type="button" id="pbl22Unlock">
          UNLOCK APPLICATION
        </button>

        <button type="button" id="pbl22ChangePin">
          CHANGE PIN
        </button>
      </div>

      <div style="
        padding:15px;
        border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.15);
        border-radius:10px;
        margin-bottom:18px;
      ">
        <h3 style="margin:0 0 12px;font-size:14px">
          ACCESS INFORMATION
        </h3>

        <div id="pbl22AccessDetails"
          style="font-size:12px;line-height:1.8;opacity:.85">
          Waiting for access information...
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="
        padding:15px;
        border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.15);
        border-radius:10px;
        margin-bottom:18px;
      ">
        <h3 style="margin:0 0 12px;font-size:14px">
          SECURITY RECOMMENDATIONS
        </h3>

        <div id="pbl22Recommendations"
          style="font-size:12px;line-height:1.8">
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="
        padding:15px;
        border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.15);
        border-radius:10px;
      ">
        <div style="
          display:flex;
          justify-content:space-between;
          align-items:center;
          gap:10px;
          margin-bottom:12px;
        ">
          <h3 style="margin:0;font-size:14px">
            LOCAL SECURITY EVENT HISTORY
          </h3>

          <button type="button" id="pbl22ClearEvents">
            CLEAR LOCAL HISTORY
          </button>
        </div>

        <div id="pbl22Events"
          style="font-size:11px;line-height:1.7">
          No events recorded.
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="
        margin-top:16px;

        font-size:11px;
        line-height:1.7;
      ">
        SECURITY NOTICE: This screen monitors application-level
        access. It does not replace Google account security,
        restricted Apps Script deployment permissions, or
        server-side authorization. Local event history is not
        an authoritative permanent audit log.
      </div>
    `;

    const style = document.createElement('style');

    style.textContent = `

    document.head.appendChild(style);
    page.appendChild(section);

    S22.element('pbl22Refresh')
      .addEventListener('click', pbl22RefreshSecurity);

    S22.element('pbl22Lock')
      .addEventListener('click', pbl22LockApplication);

    S22.element('pbl22Unlock')
      .addEventListener('click', pbl22UnlockApplication);

    S22.element('pbl22ChangePin')
      .addEventListener('click', pbl22ChangePin);

    S22.element('pbl22ClearEvents')
      .addEventListener('click', function () {
        SEC22.events = [];
        S22.renderEvents();
      });

    S22.renderRecommendations();

    return true;
