  /* ================================================================
     2. ACCESS INFORMATION NORMALIZATION
     ================================================================ */

  function s22FindValue(object, keys) {
    if (!object || typeof object !== 'object') return undefined;

    for (const key of keys) {
      if (object[key] !== undefined && object[key] !== null) {
        return object[key];
      }
    }

    return undefined;
  }

  function s22NormalizeAccess(result) {
    const data = result && result.data
      ? result.data
      : result;

    if (!data || typeof data !== 'object') {
      return null;
    }

    const unlocked = s22FindValue(data, [
      'unlocked',
      'isUnlocked',
      'authorized',
      'editAccess'
    ]);

    const expires = s22FindValue(data, [
      'expiresAt',
      'expiration',
      'sessionExpiresAt'
    ]);

    return {
      unlocked: unlocked === true,
      expiresAt: expires || null,
      raw: data
    };
  }

  /* ================================================================
     3. ACCESS STATUS REFRESH
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl22RefreshSecurity = async function () {
    if (SEC22.refreshing) return;

    SEC22.refreshing = true;

    S22.setText('pbl22OverallStatus', 'CHECKING...');

    try {
      const access = await S22.call([
        'apiAccessStatus',
        'getAccessStatus'
      ]);

      SEC22.access = s22NormalizeAccess(access);

      if (SEC22.access) {
        S22.setText(
          'pbl22SessionStatus',
          SEC22.access.unlocked ? 'UNLOCKED' : 'LOCKED'
        );

        S22.setText(
          'pbl22AccessMode',
          SEC22.access.unlocked
            ? 'EDIT AUTHORIZED'
            : 'READ ONLY'
        );

        S22.setText(
          'pbl22AccessDetails',
          'Editing permission: ' +
          (SEC22.access.unlocked ? 'ACTIVE' : 'INACTIVE') +
          '<br>Session expiration: ' +
          S22.escape(
            SEC22.access.expiresAt || 'Not supplied by backend'
          )
        );

        S22.log(
          'ACCESS',
          'Access status successfully refreshed.'
        );
      } else {
        S22.setText(
          'pbl22SessionStatus',
          'UNKNOWN'
        );

        S22.setText(
          'pbl22AccessMode',
          'READ ONLY'
        );

        S22.setText(
          'pbl22AccessDetails',
          'Backend returned an unrecognized access response.'
        );
      }

    } catch (error) {
      SEC22.access = null;

      S22.setText('pbl22SessionStatus', 'UNKNOWN');
      S22.setText('pbl22AccessMode', 'READ ONLY');

      S22.setText(
        'pbl22AccessDetails',
        'Access status unavailable: ' +
        S22.escape(error.message || error)
      );

      S22.log('ERROR', 'Access status could not be retrieved.');
    }

    try {
      const health = await S22.call([
        'apiSystemHealth',
        'getSystemHealth'
      ]);

      SEC22.health = health;

      S22.setText(
        'pbl22BackendStatus',
        health ? 'RESPONDING' : 'UNKNOWN'
      );

    } catch (error) {
      SEC22.health = null;

      S22.setText(
        'pbl22BackendStatus',
        'UNAVAILABLE'
      );
    }

    SEC22.lastRefresh = new Date();

    S22.setText(
      'pbl22LastCheck',
      SEC22.lastRefresh.toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR')
    );

    S22.setText(
      'pbl22OverallStatus',
      SEC22.access && SEC22.health
        ? 'STATUS VERIFIED'
        : 'CHECK REQUIRED'
    );

    SEC22.refreshing = false;

    return {
      access: SEC22.access,
      health: SEC22.health,
      lastRefresh: SEC22.lastRefresh
    };
  };

  /* ================================================================
     4. LOCK APPLICATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl22LockApplication = async function () {
    if (typeof apiLock !== 'function') {
      S22.log('ERROR', 'Lock function unavailable.');
      return false;
    }

    try {
      await apiLock();

      SEC22.access = {
        unlocked: false,
        expiresAt: null
      };

      S22.log('LOCK', 'Lock command submitted.');

      if (typeof updateAccessDisplay === 'function') {
        updateAccessDisplay();
      }

      await pbl22RefreshSecurity();

      return true;

    } catch (error) {
      S22.log('ERROR', 'Lock operation failed.');
      return false;
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     5. UNLOCK APPLICATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl22UnlockApplication = function () {
    if (typeof openUnlockModal === 'function') {
      openUnlockModal();
      S22.log('ACCESS', 'Unlock dialog opened.');
      return true;
    }

    if (typeof pblOpenUnlockModal === 'function') {
      pblOpenUnlockModal();
      return true;
    }

    S22.log('WARNING', 'Unlock dialog function unavailable.');

    return false;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     6. CHANGE PIN
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl22ChangePin = function () {
    if (typeof openChangePinModal === 'function') {
      openChangePinModal();
      S22.log('SECURITY', 'PIN change dialog opened.');
      return true;
    }

    if (typeof pblOpenChangePinModal === 'function') {
      pblOpenChangePinModal();
      return true;
    }

    S22.log(
      'WARNING',
      'PIN change dialog function unavailable.'
    );

    return false;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     7. SECURITY RECOMMENDATIONS
     ================================================================ */

  S22.renderRecommendations = function () {
    const el = S22.element('pbl22Recommendations');
    if (!el) return;

    const recommendations = [
      'Use a unique PIN that is not easy to guess.',
      'Lock the application whenever you leave the weighing station.',
      'Restrict the Google Apps Script deployment to authorized users.',
      'Keep server-side edit authorization active for every write operation.',
      'Use a separate protected backup of operational records.',
      'Test backup restoration before relying on it.',
      'Do not expose administrative tokens or PIN values in browser code.',
      'Review the permanent server-side audit history regularly.'
    ];

    el.innerHTML = recommendations.map(function (item, index) {
      return `
        <div style="padding:5px 0">
          <span style="opacity:.55">${index + 1}.</span>
          ${S22.escape(item)}
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  };

  /* ================================================================
     8. LOCAL EVENT HISTORY
     ================================================================ */

  S22.renderEvents = function () {
    const el = S22.element('pbl22Events');
    if (!el) return;

    if (!SEC22.events.length) {
      el.textContent = 'No local security events.';
      return;
    }

    el.innerHTML = SEC22.events.map(function (event) {
      return `
        <div style="
          padding:7px 0;
          border-bottom:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.12);
        ">
          <strong>${S22.escape(event.type)}</strong>
          — ${S22.escape(event.message)}
          <div style="opacity:.55">
            ${S22.escape(event.time)}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  };

  /* ================================================================
     9. PUBLIC DIAGNOSTICS
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl22GetSecurityStatus = function () {
    return {
      application: 'PONT BASCULE LIVE',
      access: SEC22.access,
      backendHealth: Boolean(SEC22.health),
      lastRefresh: SEC22.lastRefresh,
      localEvents: SEC22.events.length,
      initialized: SEC22.initialized
    };
  };

  /* ================================================================
     10. INITIALIZATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblInitializeSecurityCenter = function () {
    if (SEC22.initialized) return true;

    const created = s22CreateInterface();

    if (!created) return false;

    SEC22.initialized = true;

    pbl22RefreshSecurity();

    return true;
  };

  function s22Start() {
    window.setTimeout(function () {
      pblInitializeSecurityCenter();
    }, 700);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      s22Start,
      { once: true }
    );
  } else {
    s22Start();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ================================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 23
   ADVANCED WEIGHING HISTORY & TRANSACTION TIMELINE
   ================================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  const TL23 = window.PBL_APP.timeline = {
    initialized: false,
    records: [],
    filtered: [],
    selected: null,
    page: 1,
    pageSize: 20,
    filters: {
      search: '',
      status: 'ALL',
      startDate: '',
      endDate: '',
      product: 'ALL',
      client: 'ALL'
    },
    loading: false,
    lastRefresh: null
  };

  const T23 = {
    el: function (id) {
      return document.getElementById(id);
    },

    escape: function (value) {
      return String(value == null ? '' : value)
        .replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
          return {
            '&': '&amp;',
            '<': '&lt;',
            '>': '&gt;',
            '"': '&quot;',
            "'": '&#039;'
          }[c];
        });
    },

    number: function (value) {
      const n = Number(value);
      return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
    },

    kg: function (ticket) {
      const direct = ticket.netKg ??
        ticket.netWeightKg ??
        ticket.netWeight;

      if (direct !== undefined && direct !== null) {
        return T23.number(direct);
      }

      return T23.number(ticket.netTonnes) * 1000;
    },

    date: function (ticket) {
      return ticket.date ||
        ticket.createdAt ||
        ticket.timestamp ||
        '';
    },

    id: function (ticket) {
      return String(
        ticket.id ||
        ticket.ticketId ||
        ticket.ticketNumber ||
        ''
      );
    },

    status: function (ticket) {
      return String(ticket.status || 'UNKNOWN').toUpperCase();
    },

    call: async function (names, args) {
      if (typeof pblCallFirst === 'function') {
        return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
      }

      throw new Error('Backend communication unavailable.');
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     1. FIND TIMELINE PAGE
     ================================================================ */

  function t23FindPage() {
    return document.getElementById('ticketsPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="tickets"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-tickets');
  }

  /* ================================================================
     2. CREATE TIMELINE INTERFACE
     ================================================================ */

  function t23CreateInterface() {
    if (T23.el('pbl23Timeline')) return true;

    const page = t23FindPage();

    if (!page) {
      console.warn('PBL23: Tickets page not found.');
      return false;
    }

    const section = document.createElement('section');

    section.id = 'pbl23Timeline';

    section.style.cssText = `
      margin:20px 0;
      padding:20px;
      border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.22);
      border-radius:14px;
      background:rgba(15,23,42,.58);
      color:inherit;
    `;

    section.innerHTML = `
      <div style="
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        align-items:center;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
        gap:12px;
        margin-bottom:20px;
      ">
        <div>
          <h2 style="margin:0 0 5px">
            WEIGHING TRANSACTION TIMELINE
          </h2>
          <div style="font-size:12px;opacity:.65">
            Complete vehicle movement and ticket traceability
          </div>
        </div>

        <span id="pbl23LastRefresh"
          style="font-size:11px;opacity:.7">
          Not refreshed
        </span>
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(130px,1fr));
        gap:10px;
        margin-bottom:18px;
      ">
        <div class="pbl23-card">
          <small>TOTAL TRANSACTIONS</small>
          <strong id="pbl23Total">0</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl23-card">
          <small>COMPLETED</small>
          <strong id="pbl23Completed">0</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl23-card">
          <small>TONNES WEIGHED</small>
          <strong id="pbl23Tonnes">0.000</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl23-card">
          <small>AVERAGE NET KG</small>
          <strong id="pbl23Average">0</strong>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(150px,1fr));
        gap:10px;
        margin-bottom:14px;
      ">
        <input id="pbl23Search"
          placeholder="Search ticket, truck, client..."
          type="search">

        <select id="pbl23Status">
          <option value="ALL">All statuses</option>
          <option value="COMPLETED">Completed</option>
          <option value="ACTIVE">Active</option>
          <option value="CANCELLED">Cancelled</option>
          <option value="PENDING">Pending</option>
        </select>

        <select id="pbl23Product">
          <option value="ALL">All products</option>
        </select>

        <select id="pbl23Client">
          <option value="ALL">All clients</option>
        </select>

        <input id="pbl23Start"
          type="date"
          aria-label="Start date">

        <input id="pbl23End"
          type="date"
          aria-label="End date">
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:flex;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
        gap:9px;
        margin-bottom:18px;
      ">
        <button type="button" id="pbl23Refresh">
          REFRESH HISTORY
        </button>

        <button type="button" id="pbl23Export">
          EXPORT CSV
        </button>

        <button type="button" id="pbl23Reset">
          RESET FILTERS
        </button>
      </div>

      <div style="
        overflow-x:auto;
        border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.14);
        border-radius:9px;
      ">
        <table style="
          width:100%;
          border-collapse:collapse;
          min-width:900px;
          font-size:12px;
        ">
          <thead>
            <tr style="background:rgba(148,163,184,.09)">
              <th class="pbl23-th">DATE / TIME</th>
              <th class="pbl23-th">TICKET</th>
              <th class="pbl23-th">TRUCK</th>
              <th class="pbl23-th">PRODUCT</th>
              <th class="pbl23-th">CLIENT</th>
              <th class="pbl23-th">GROSS KG</th>
              <th class="pbl23-th">TARE KG</th>
              <th class="pbl23-th">NET KG</th>
              <th class="pbl23-th">STATUS</th>
              <th class="pbl23-th">DETAILS</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody id="pbl23TableBody">
            <tr>
              <td colspan="10" style="padding:20px;text-align:center">
                Loading transaction history...
              </td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        align-items:center;
        gap:10px;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
        margin-top:14px;
      ">
        <span id="pbl23PageInfo"
          style="font-size:11px;opacity:.7">
          Page 1
        </span>

        <div style="display:flex;gap:8px">
          <button type="button" id="pbl23Previous">
            PREVIOUS
          </button>

          <button type="button" id="pbl23Next">
            NEXT
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div id="pbl23Details"
        style="
          display:none;
          margin-top:20px;
          padding:17px;
          border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.2);
          border-radius:10px;
        ">
      </div>
    `;

    const style = document.createElement('style');

    style.textContent = `
      .pbl23-card {
        background:rgba(148,163,184,.07);
        padding:14px;
        border-radius:9px;
      }

      .pbl23-card small {
        display:block;
        font-size:10px;
        opacity:.6;
        margin-bottom:7px;
      }

      .pbl23-card strong {
        font-size:21px;
      }

      .pbl23-th {
        padding:11px 9px;
        text-align:left;
        white-space:nowrap;
      }

      #pbl23Timeline input,
      #pbl23Timeline select {
        min-width:0;
        width:100%;
        padding:10px;
        border-radius:7px;
        border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.25);
        background:rgba(15,23,42,.65);
        color:inherit;
      }

      #pbl23Timeline td {
        padding:10px 9px;
        border-top:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.12);
        white-space:nowrap;
      }

      #pbl23Timeline button {
        cursor:pointer;
      }

      .pbl23-detail-grid {
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(150px,1fr));
        gap:12px;
      }

      .pbl23-detail-item {
        padding:11px;
        background:rgba(148,163,184,.07);
        border-radius:8px;
      }

      .pbl23-detail-item small {
        display:block;
        opacity:.6;
        margin-bottom:5px;
      }
    `;

    document.head.appendChild(style);
    page.appendChild(section);

    t23ConnectEvents();

    return true;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     3. EVENT CONNECTIONS
     ================================================================ */

  function t23ConnectEvents() {
    const bindings = {
      pbl23Refresh: pbl23RefreshTimeline,
      pbl23Export: pbl23ExportCSV,
      pbl23Reset: pbl23ResetFilters,
      pbl23Previous: function () {
        TL23.page = Math.max(1, TL23.page - 1);
        t23Render();
      },
      pbl23Next: function () {
        TL23.page++;
        t23Render();
      }
    };

    Object.keys(bindings).forEach(function (id) {
      const el = T23.el(id);
      if (!el || el.dataset.pbl23Bound === '1') return;

      el.dataset.pbl23Bound = '1';
      el.addEventListener('click', bindings[id]);
    });

    const filters = [
      'pbl23Search',
      'pbl23Status',
      'pbl23Product',
      'pbl23Client',
      'pbl23Start',
      'pbl23End'
    ];

    filters.forEach(function (id) {
      const el = T23.el(id);
      if (!el || el.dataset.pbl23Bound === '1') return;

      el.dataset.pbl23Bound = '1';

      el.addEventListener('input', t23ReadFilters);
      el.addEventListener('change', t23ReadFilters);
    });
  }

  /* ================================================================
     4. LOAD TRANSACTION HISTORY
     ================================================================ */

  function t23Rows(result) {
    if (Array.isArray(result)) return result;

    if (!result || typeof result !== 'object') return [];

    const data = result.data || result;

    if (Array.isArray(data)) return data;

    const keys = [
      'tickets',
      'records',
      'rows',
      'results',
      'items'
    ];

    for (const key of keys) {
      if (Array.isArray(data[key])) {
        return data[key];
      }
    }

    return [];
  }

  window.pbl23RefreshTimeline = async function () {
    if (TL23.loading) return;

    TL23.loading = true;

    const body = T23.el('pbl23TableBody');

    if (body) {
      body.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="10" style="padding:20px;text-align:center">
            Loading...
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    }

    try {
      let result = null;

      try {
        result = await T23.call([
          'apiGetTickets',
          'apiGetAllTickets',
          'getAllTickets'
        ]);
      } catch (error) {
        console.warn('PBL23: Server ticket retrieval failed.', error);
      }

      let records = t23Rows(result);

      if (!records.length && PBL_APP.data &&
          Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data.tickets)) {
        records = PBL_APP.data.tickets;
      }

      TL23.records = records.slice();

      TL23.lastRefresh = new Date();

      t23PopulateFilters();
      t23ApplyFilters();
      t23Render();

      const refreshLabel = T23.el('pbl23LastRefresh');

      if (refreshLabel) {
        refreshLabel.textContent =
          'Updated: ' +
          TL23.lastRefresh.toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR');
      }

    } catch (error) {
      console.error('PBL23 timeline error:', error);

      if (body) {
        body.innerHTML = `
          <tr>
            <td colspan="10" style="padding:20px">
              Unable to load transaction history.
              Check the backend connection.
            </td>
          </tr>
        `;
      }

    } finally {
      TL23.loading = false;
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     5. FILTER OPTIONS
     ================================================================ */

  function t23Unique(field) {
    const values = TL23.records.map(function (ticket) {
      return String(ticket[field] || '').trim();
    }).filter(Boolean);

    return Array.from(new Set(values)).sort();
  }

  function t23SetOptions(id, values, label) {
    const el = T23.el(id);
    if (!el) return;

    const previous = el.value || 'ALL';

    el.innerHTML =
      '<option value="ALL">All ' + label + '</option>' +
      values.map(function (value) {
        return '<option value="' +
          T23.escape(value) + '">' +
          T23.escape(value) + '</option>';
      }).join('');

    if (values.includes(previous)) {
      el.value = previous;
    }
  }

  function t23PopulateFilters() {
    t23SetOptions(
      'pbl23Product',
      t23Unique('product'),
      'products'
    );

    t23SetOptions(
      'pbl23Client',
      t23Unique('client'),
      'clients'
    );
  }

  /* ================================================================
     6. FILTER ENGINE
     ================================================================ */

  function t23ReadFilters() {
    TL23.filters.search =
      T23.el('pbl23Search')?.value.trim().toLowerCase() || '';

    TL23.filters.status =
      T23.el('pbl23Status')?.value || 'ALL';

    TL23.filters.product =
      T23.el('pbl23Product')?.value || 'ALL';

    TL23.filters.client =
      T23.el('pbl23Client')?.value || 'ALL';

    TL23.filters.startDate =
      T23.el('pbl23Start')?.value || '';

    TL23.filters.endDate =
      T23.el('pbl23End')?.value || '';

    TL23.page = 1;

    t23ApplyFilters();
    t23Render();
  }

  function t23DateValue(ticket) {
    const raw = T23.date(ticket);

    if (!raw) return null;

    const parsed = new Date(raw);

    if (!Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {
      return parsed;
    }

    const match = String(raw).match(
      /^(\d{2})\/(\d{2})\/(\d{4})/
    );

    if (match) {
      return new Date(
        Number(match[3]),
        Number(match[2]) - 1,
        Number(match[1])
      );
    }

    return null;
  }

  function t23ApplyFilters() {
    const f = TL23.filters;

    TL23.filtered = TL23.records.filter(function (ticket) {
      const haystack = [
        ticket.ticketNumber,
        ticket.id,
        ticket.truckRegistration,
        ticket.truck,
        ticket.product,
        ticket.client,
        ticket.driver,
        ticket.driverName
      ].join(' ').toLowerCase();

      if (f.search && !haystack.includes(f.search)) {
        return false;
      }

      const status = T23.status(ticket);

      if (f.status !== 'ALL') {
        if (f.status === 'COMPLETED' &&
            !['COMPLETED', 'CLOSED', 'VALIDATED'].includes(status)) {
          return false;
        }

        if (f.status === 'ACTIVE' &&
            !['ACTIVE', 'IN_PROGRESS', 'OPEN'].includes(status)) {
          return false;
        }

        if (f.status === 'CANCELLED' &&
            !['CANCELLED', 'CANCELED', 'ANNULLED'].includes(status)) {
          return false;
        }

        if (f.status === 'PENDING' &&
            !['PENDING', 'WAITING', 'DRAFT'].includes(status)) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      if (f.product !== 'ALL' &&
          String(ticket.product || '') !== f.product) {
        return false;
      }

      if (f.client !== 'ALL' &&
          String(ticket.client || '') !== f.client) {
        return false;
      }

      const date = t23DateValue(ticket);

      if (f.startDate) {
        if (!date || date < new Date(f.startDate + 'T00:00:00')) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      if (f.endDate) {
        if (!date || date > new Date(f.endDate + 'T23:59:59')) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      return true;
    });

    TL23.page = Math.min(
      TL23.page,
      Math.max(1, Math.ceil(
        TL23.filtered.length / TL23.pageSize
      ))
    );
  }

  /* ================================================================
     7. KPI CALCULATIONS
     ================================================================ */

  function t23RenderKPIs() {
    const records = TL23.filtered;

    const completed = records.filter(function (ticket) {
      return [
        'COMPLETED',
        'CLOSED',
        'VALIDATED'
      ].includes(T23.status(ticket));
    });

    const totalKg = completed.reduce(function (sum, ticket) {
      return sum + Math.max(0, T23.kg(ticket));
    }, 0);

    const average = completed.length
      ? totalKg / completed.length
      : 0;

    T23.el('pbl23Total').textContent =
      records.length.toLocaleString('fr-FR');

    T23.el('pbl23Completed').textContent =
      completed.length.toLocaleString('fr-FR');

    T23.el('pbl23Tonnes').textContent =
      (totalKg / 1000).toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
        minimumFractionDigits: 3,
        maximumFractionDigits: 3
      });

    T23.el('pbl23Average').textContent =
      Math.round(average).toLocaleString('fr-FR');
  }

  /* ================================================================
     8. TRANSACTION TABLE
     ================================================================ */

  function t23Render() {
    t23RenderKPIs();

    const body = T23.el('pbl23TableBody');

    if (!body) return;

    const start = (TL23.page - 1) * TL23.pageSize;

    const rows = TL23.filtered.slice(
      start,
      start + TL23.pageSize
    );

    if (!rows.length) {
      body.innerHTML = `
        <tr>
          <td colspan="10"
            style="padding:22px;text-align:center;opacity:.65">
            No transactions found.
          </td>
        </tr>
      `;
    } else {
      body.innerHTML = rows.map(function (ticket) {
        const id = T23.id(ticket);

        const gross = T23.number(
          ticket.grossKg ?? ticket.grossWeight
        );

        const tare = T23.number(
          ticket.tareKg ?? ticket.tareWeight
        );

        const net = T23.kg(ticket);

        const status = T23.status(ticket);

        const date = T23.escape(T23.date(ticket));

        const number = T23.escape(
          ticket.ticketNumber || id || 'N/A'
        );

        return `
          <tr>
            <td>${date}</td>
            <td><strong>${number}</strong></td>
            <td>${T23.escape(ticket.truckRegistration || ticket.truck || '')}</td>
            <td>${T23.escape(ticket.product || '')}</td>
            <td>${T23.escape(ticket.client || '')}</td>
            <td>${gross.toLocaleString('fr-FR')}</td>
            <td>${tare.toLocaleString('fr-FR')}</td>
            <td><strong>${net.toLocaleString('fr-FR')}</strong></td>
            <td>${T23.escape(status)}</td>
            <td>
              <button type="button"
                data-pbl23-ticket="${T23.escape(id)}"
                class="pbl23-view">
                VIEW
              </button>
            </td>
          </tr>
        `;
      }).join('');
    }

    body.querySelectorAll('.pbl23-view').forEach(function (button) {
      button.addEventListener('click', function () {
        pbl23ViewTransaction(
          button.getAttribute('data-pbl23-ticket')
        );
      });
    });

    const pages = Math.max(
      1,
      Math.ceil(TL23.filtered.length / TL23.pageSize)
    );

    T23.el('pbl23PageInfo').textContent =
      'Page ' + TL23.page + ' / ' + pages +
      ' — ' + TL23.filtered.length + ' transactions';

    T23.el('pbl23Previous').disabled = TL23.page <= 1;
    T23.el('pbl23Next').disabled = TL23.page >= pages;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     9. TRANSACTION DETAILS & WEIGHING TIMELINE
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl23ViewTransaction = function (id) {
    const ticket = TL23.records.find(function (item) {
      return T23.id(item) === String(id);
    });

    if (!ticket) return false;

    TL23.selected = ticket;

    const container = T23.el('pbl23Details');

    if (!container) return false;

    const gross = T23.number(
      ticket.grossKg ?? ticket.grossWeight
    );

    const tare = T23.number(
      ticket.tareKg ?? ticket.tareWeight
    );

    const net = T23.kg(ticket);

    const events = [
      {
        title: 'VEHICLE ARRIVAL',
        detail: ticket.arrivalTime || ticket.createdAt || ticket.date
      },
      {
        title: 'FIRST WEIGHING',
        detail: ticket.firstWeightTime ||
          ticket.firstWeighingTime ||
          'Not recorded'
      },
      {
        title: 'SECOND WEIGHING',
        detail: ticket.secondWeightTime ||
          ticket.secondWeighingTime ||
          'Not recorded'
      },
      {
        title: 'TICKET COMPLETION',
        detail: ticket.completedAt ||
          ticket.updatedAt ||
          'Not recorded'
      }
    ];

    container.style.display = 'block';

    container.innerHTML = `
      <div style="
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        align-items:center;
        gap:10px;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
        margin-bottom:16px;
      ">
        <h3 style="margin:0">
          TRANSACTION DETAILS
        </h3>

        <button type="button"
          id="pbl23CloseDetails">
          CLOSE
        </button>
      </div>

      <div class="pbl23-detail-grid">
        <div class="pbl23-detail-item">
          <small>TICKET NUMBER</small>
          <strong>${T23.escape(ticket.ticketNumber || id)}</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl23-detail-item">
          <small>TRUCK</small>
          <strong>${T23.escape(ticket.truckRegistration || '')}</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl23-detail-item">
          <small>PRODUCT</small>
          <strong>${T23.escape(ticket.product || '')}</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl23-detail-item">
          <small>CLIENT</small>
          <strong>${T23.escape(ticket.client || '')}</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl23-detail-item">
          <small>GROSS WEIGHT</small>
          <strong>${gross.toLocaleString('fr-FR')} KG</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl23-detail-item">
          <small>TARE WEIGHT</small>
          <strong>${tare.toLocaleString('fr-FR')} KG</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl23-detail-item">
          <small>NET WEIGHT</small>
          <strong>${net.toLocaleString('fr-FR')} KG</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl23-detail-item">
          <small>DRIVER</small>
          <strong>${T23.escape(ticket.driver || ticket.driverName || '')}</strong>
        </div>
      </div>

      <h4 style="margin:20px 0 12px">
        TRANSACTION TIMELINE
      </h4>

      <div style="border-left:2px solid rgba(34,197,94,.5);padding-left:15px">
        ${events.map(function (event) {
          return `
            <div style="padding:0 0 15px">
              <strong>${T23.escape(event.title)}</strong>
              <div style="font-size:11px;opacity:.65">
                ${T23.escape(event.detail || 'Not recorded')}
              </div>
            </div>
          `;
        }).join('')}
      </div>

      <div style="
        font-size:11px;
        opacity:.65;
        margin-top:12px;
      ">
        Timeline events are displayed only when the corresponding
        timestamps exist in the ticket record. Missing timestamps
        are not reconstructed or invented.
      </div>
    `;

    T23.el('pbl23CloseDetails').addEventListener(
      'click',
      function () {
        container.style.display = 'none';
      }
    );

    container.scrollIntoView({
      behavior: 'smooth',
      block: 'nearest'
    });

    return true;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     10. CSV EXPORT
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl23ExportCSV = function () {
    const headers = [
      'Date',
      'Ticket',
      'Truck',
      'Product',
      'Client',
      'Driver',
      'Gross KG',
      'Tare KG',
      'Net KG',
      'Status'
    ];

    const rows = TL23.filtered.map(function (ticket) {
      return [
        T23.date(ticket),
        ticket.ticketNumber || T23.id(ticket),
        ticket.truckRegistration || ticket.truck || '',
        ticket.product || '',
        ticket.client || '',
        ticket.driver || ticket.driverName || '',
        T23.number(ticket.grossKg ?? ticket.grossWeight),
        T23.number(ticket.tareKg ?? ticket.tareWeight),
        T23.kg(ticket),
        T23.status(ticket)
      ];
    });

    const csv = [headers].concat(rows).map(function (row) {
      return row.map(function (value) {
        return '"' +
          String(value == null ? '' : value)
            .replace(/"/g, '""') +
          '"';
      }).join(';');
    }).join('\r\n');

    const blob = new Blob(
      ['\uFEFF' + csv],
      { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' }
    );

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

    const link = document.createElement('a');

    link.href = url;
    link.download = 'PBL_WEIGHING_TIMELINE.csv';

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  /* ================================================================
     11. RESET FILTERS
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl23ResetFilters = function () {
    [
      'pbl23Search',
      'pbl23Start',
      'pbl23End'
    ].forEach(function (id) {
      if (T23.el(id)) T23.el(id).value = '';
    });

    [
      'pbl23Status',
      'pbl23Product',
      'pbl23Client'
    ].forEach(function (id) {
      if (T23.el(id)) T23.el(id).value = 'ALL';
    });

    t23ReadFilters();
  };

  /* ================================================================
     12. INITIALIZATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblInitializeTimeline = function () {
    if (TL23.initialized) return true;

    const created = t23CreateInterface();

    if (!created) return false;

    TL23.initialized = true;

    pbl23RefreshTimeline();

    return true;
  };

  function t23Start() {
    window.setTimeout(function () {
      pblInitializeTimeline();
    }, 900);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      t23Start,
      { once: true }
    );
  } else {
    t23Start();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ================================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 24
   PROFESSIONAL PRINTABLE WEIGHBRIDGE TICKETS
   ================================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  const PT24 = window.PBL_APP.printCenter = {
    initialized: false,
    selectedTicket: null,
    company: {},
    history: [],
    printing: false
  };

  const P24 = {
    el: id => document.getElementById(id),

    escape: value => String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({
        '&': '&amp;',
        '<': '&lt;',
        '>': '&gt;',
        '"': '&quot;',
        "'": '&#039;'
      })[c]),

    num: value => {
      const n = Number(value);
      return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
    },

    kg: ticket => {
      const direct = ticket.netKg ??
        ticket.netWeightKg ??
        ticket.netWeight;

      return direct != null
        ? P24.num(direct)
        : P24.num(ticket.netTonnes) * 1000;
    },

    date: ticket =>
      ticket.date ||
      ticket.createdAt ||
      new Date().toISOString(),

    call: (names, args) => {
      if (typeof pblCallFirst === 'function') {
        return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
      }

      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Backend communication unavailable.')
      );
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     1. PRINT CENTER INTERFACE
     ================================================================ */

  function p24FindPage() {
    return document.getElementById('ticketsPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="tickets"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-tickets');
  }

  function p24CreateInterface() {
    if (P24.el('pbl24PrintCenter')) return true;

    const page = p24FindPage();

    if (!page) return false;

    const section = document.createElement('section');

    section.id = 'pbl24PrintCenter';

    section.style.cssText = `
      margin:20px 0;
      padding:20px;
      border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.22);
      border-radius:14px;
      background:rgba(15,23,42,.58);
      color:inherit;
    `;

    section.innerHTML = `
      <div style="margin-bottom:18px">
        <h2 style="margin:0 0 5px">
          PROFESSIONAL TICKET PRINTING
        </h2>
        <div style="font-size:12px;opacity:.65">
          Official weighbridge ticket preview and document control
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(200px,1fr));
        gap:12px;
        margin-bottom:15px;
      ">
        <input id="pbl24TicketSearch"
          placeholder="Ticket number or truck registration">

        <button type="button" id="pbl24Find">
          FIND TICKET
        </button>

        <button type="button" id="pbl24Print">
          PRINT TICKET
        </button>
      </div>

      <div id="pbl24Message"
        style="font-size:12px;opacity:.7;margin-bottom:12px">
        Search for a ticket to generate its print preview.
      </div>

      <div id="pbl24Preview"
        style="
          display:none;
          padding:18px;
          background:#fff;
          color:#111827;
          border-radius:8px;
        ">
      </div>

      <div style="
        margin-top:18px;
        padding:12px;
        background:rgba(245,158,11,.08);
        border-radius:8px;
        font-size:11px;
        line-height:1.6;
      ">
        The print preview uses existing ticket data and
        company settings. It does not generate a certified
        PDF or digital signature. Verify the ticket before printing.
      </div>
    `;

    const style = document.createElement('style');

    style.textContent = `
      #pbl24PrintCenter input {
        width:100%;
        min-width:0;
        padding:11px;
        border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.3);
        border-radius:7px;
        background:rgba(15,23,42,.65);
        color:inherit;
      }

      .pbl24-paper {
        width:100%;
        max-width:850px;

        font-family:Arial,sans-serif;
        color:#111827;
      }

        font-size:23px;
        margin:0;
        letter-spacing:1px;
      }

        width:100%;
        border-collapse:collapse;
        margin-top:15px;
      }

      .pbl24-paper th,
      .pbl24-paper td {
        border:1px solid #cbd5e1;
        padding:10px;
        text-align:left;
      }

      .pbl24-paper th {
        background:#f1f5f9;
      }

      .pbl24-net {
        font-size:23px;
        font-weight:bold;
        text-align:right;
      }

      .pbl24-signatures {
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;
        gap:25px;
        margin-top:55px;
      }

      .pbl24-signatures div {
        border-top:1px solid #64748b;
        padding-top:9px;
        min-height:45px;
      }

      @media print {
        body * {
          visibility:hidden !important;
        }

        #pbl24PrintDocument,
        #pbl24PrintDocument * {
          visibility:visible !important;
        }

        #pbl24PrintDocument {
          display:block !important;
          position:absolute;
          left:0;
          top:0;
          width:100%;
          background:white !important;
          color:#111827 !important;
          padding:12mm;
        }

        @page {
          size:A4;
          margin:10mm;
        }
      }
    `;

    document.head.appendChild(style);
    page.appendChild(section);

    P24.el('pbl24Find').addEventListener(
      'click',
      pbl24FindTicket
    );

    P24.el('pbl24Print').addEventListener(
      'click',
      pbl24PrintTicket
    );

    P24.el('pbl24TicketSearch').addEventListener(
      'keydown',
      function (event) {
        if (event.key === 'Enter') {
          pbl24FindTicket();
        }
      }
    );

    return true;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     2. SEARCH TICKET
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl24FindTicket = async function () {
    const query = P24.el('pbl24TicketSearch')
      .value.trim().toLowerCase();

    if (!query) {
      P24.el('pbl24Message').textContent =
        'Enter a ticket number or vehicle registration.';

      return false;
    }

    P24.el('pbl24Message').textContent =
      'Searching ticket archive...';

    let tickets = [];

    try {
      if (PBL_APP.data &&
          Array.isArray(PBL_APP.data.tickets)) {
        tickets = PBL_APP.data.tickets.slice();
      }

      if (!tickets.length) {
        const result = await P24.call([
          'apiGetTickets',
          'apiGetAllTickets',
          'getAllTickets'
        ]);

        if (Array.isArray(result)) {
          tickets = result;
        } else {
          const data = result && result.data
            ? result.data
            : result;

          tickets = data && (
            data.tickets ||
            data.records ||
            data.rows ||
            data.items
          ) || [];
        }
      }

    } catch (error) {
      P24.el('pbl24Message').textContent =
        'Unable to retrieve tickets from the backend.';

      return false;
    }

    const ticket = tickets.find(function (item) {
      const number = String(
        item.ticketNumber || item.id || ''
      ).toLowerCase();

      const truck = String(
        item.truckRegistration || item.truck || ''
      ).toLowerCase();

      return number === query || truck === query;
    });

    if (!ticket) {
      P24.el('pbl24Message').textContent =
        'No matching ticket found.';

      return false;
    }

    PT24.selectedTicket = ticket;

    pbl24RenderPreview(ticket);

    P24.el('pbl24Message').textContent =
      'Ticket loaded successfully. Verify all details before printing.';

    return true;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     3. COMPANY SETTINGS
     ================================================================ */

  function p24CompanyInformation() {
    const settings = PBL_APP.settings || {};

    return {
      name:
        settings.companyName ||
        settings.company ||
        'PONT BASCULE',

      address:
        settings.companyAddress ||
        settings.address ||
        '',

      phone:
        settings.companyPhone ||
        settings.phone ||
        '',

      email:
        settings.companyEmail ||
        settings.email ||
        '',

      identifier:
        settings.companyIdentifier ||
        settings.taxIdentifier ||
        '',

      station:
        settings.stationName ||
        'WEIGHBRIDGE STATION'
    };
  }

  /* ================================================================
     4. GENERATE PRINT PREVIEW
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl24RenderPreview = function (ticket) {
    if (!ticket) return false;

    const preview = P24.el('pbl24Preview');

    if (!preview) return false;

    PT24.selectedTicket = ticket;

    const company = p24CompanyInformation();

    const gross = P24.num(
      ticket.grossKg ?? ticket.grossWeight
    );

    const tare = P24.num(
      ticket.tareKg ?? ticket.tareWeight
    );

    const net = P24.kg(ticket);

    const number = ticket.ticketNumber ||
      ticket.id ||
      'N/A';

    const date = P24.date(ticket);

    const time = ticket.time ||
      ticket.createdTime ||
      '';

    const formatted = value =>
      value.toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' KG';

    preview.innerHTML = `
      <div id="pbl24PrintDocument"
        class="pbl24-paper">

        <div style="
          display:flex;
          justify-content:space-between;
          align-items:flex-start;
          gap:20px;
          border-bottom:3px solid #1e293b;
          padding-bottom:15px;
        ">
          <div>
            <h1>${P24.escape(company.name)}</h1>

            <div style="font-size:11px;margin-top:7px">
              ${P24.escape(company.address)}
            </div>

            <div style="font-size:11px">
              ${P24.escape(company.phone)}
            </div>

            <div style="font-size:11px">
              ${P24.escape(company.email)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style="text-align:right">
            <strong>${P24.escape(company.station)}</strong>

            <div style="
              font-size:11px;
              margin-top:8px;
              color:#64748b
            ">
              WEIGHBRIDGE DOCUMENT
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style="
          display:flex;
          justify-content:space-between;
          align-items:center;
          margin-top:20px;
          gap:15px;
        ">
          <div>
            <h2 style="margin:0;font-size:20px">
              BON DE PESÉE
            </h2>
            <div style="font-size:11px;color:#64748b">
              WEIGHING TICKET
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style="
            text-align:right;
            border:2px solid #1e293b;
            padding:10px 15px;
          ">
            <small>TICKET NUMBER</small>
            <h2 style="margin:5px 0 0">
              ${P24.escape(number)}
            </h2>
          </div>
        </div>

        <table>
          <tr>
            <th>DATE</th>
            <td>${P24.escape(date)}</td>
            <th>TIME</th>
            <td>${P24.escape(time)}</td>
          </tr>

          <tr>
            <th>CLIENT</th>
            <td colspan="3">
              ${P24.escape(ticket.client || '')}
            </td>
          </tr>

          <tr>
            <th>VEHICLE</th>
            <td>
              ${P24.escape(ticket.truckRegistration || ticket.truck || '')}
            </td>
            <th>DRIVER</th>
            <td>
              ${P24.escape(ticket.driver || ticket.driverName || '')}
            </td>
          </tr>

          <tr>
            <th>PRODUCT</th>
            <td colspan="3">
              ${P24.escape(ticket.product || '')}
            </td>
          </tr>
        </table>

        <table>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>WEIGHING OPERATION</th>
              <th>WEIGHT</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            <tr>
              <td>FIRST WEIGHT / GROSS</td>
              <td>${formatted(gross)}</td>
            </tr>

            <tr>
              <td>SECOND WEIGHT / TARE</td>
              <td>${formatted(tare)}</td>
            </tr>

              <td><strong>NET WEIGHT</strong></td>

              </td>
            </tr>
          </tbody>
        </table>

        <div style="

          display:flex;
          justify-content:space-between;
          align-items:center;
        ">
          <strong>NET TONNES</strong>

          <strong style="font-size:24px">
            ${(net / 1000).toLocaleString('fr-FR', {
              minimumFractionDigits:3,
              maximumFractionDigits:3
            })} T
          </strong>
        </div>

        <div style="
          margin-top:14px;
          font-size:10px;
          color:#64748b;
        ">
          Company identifier:
          ${P24.escape(company.identifier || 'Not configured')}
        </div>

          text-align:center;
          font-size:9px;
          color:#64748b;
        ">
          PONT BASCULE LIVE — ${P24.escape(company.station)}
        </div>
      </div>
    `;

    preview.style.display = 'block';

    return true;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     5. PRINT TICKET
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl24PrintTicket = function () {
    if (!PT24.selectedTicket) {
      P24.el('pbl24Message').textContent =
        'Find a ticket before printing.';

      return false;
    }

    if (PT24.printing) return false;

    const documentArea = P24.el('pbl24PrintDocument');

    if (!documentArea) return false;

    PT24.printing = true;

    try {
      window.print();

      PT24.history.push({
        ticketNumber:
          PT24.selectedTicket.ticketNumber ||
          PT24.selectedTicket.id ||
          '',
        attemptedAt: new Date().toISOString()
      });

      P24.el('pbl24Message').textContent =
        'Print dialog opened. Check your printer settings.';

      return true;

    } catch (error) {
      P24.el('pbl24Message').textContent =
        'Unable to open print dialog.';

      return false;

    } finally {
      PT24.printing = false;
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     6. PRINT HISTORY
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl24GetPrintHistory = function () {
    return PT24.history.slice();
  };

  /* ================================================================
     7. PUBLIC DOCUMENT PREVIEW
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl24PreviewTicket = function (ticket) {
    return pbl24RenderPreview(ticket);
  };

  /* ================================================================
     8. INITIALIZATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblInitializePrintCenter = function () {
    if (PT24.initialized) return true;

    const created = p24CreateInterface();

    if (!created) return false;

    PT24.initialized = true;

    return true;
  };

  function p24Start() {
    window.setTimeout(function () {
      pblInitializePrintCenter();
    }, 1000);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      p24Start,
      { once: true }
    );
  } else {
    p24Start();
  }

})();
</script>

<script>
/* ================================================================
   PONT BASCULE LIVE
   INDEX.HTML — PART 25
   FINAL APPLICATION CONTROL CENTER
   MODULE REGISTRY & SYSTEM DIAGNOSTICS
   ================================================================ */

(function () {
  'use strict';

  window.PBL_APP = window.PBL_APP || {};

  const FC25 = window.PBL_APP.finalControl = {
    initialized: false,
    modules: [],
    diagnostics: [],
    backend: 'UNKNOWN',
    lastCheck: null,
    checking: false
  };

  const F25 = {
    el: id => document.getElementById(id),

    escape: value => String(value == null ? '' : value)
      .replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({
        '&': '&amp;',
        '<': '&lt;',
        '>': '&gt;',
        '"': '&quot;',
        "'": '&#039;'
      })[c]),

    call: (names, args) => {
      if (typeof pblCallFirst === 'function') {
        return pblCallFirst(names, args || []);
      }

      return Promise.reject(
        new Error('Backend helper unavailable.')
      );
    }
  };

  /* ================================================================
     1. CONTROL CENTER INTERFACE
     ================================================================ */

  function f25FindPage() {
    return document.getElementById('dashboardPage') ||
      document.querySelector('[data-page="dashboard"]') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-dashboard') ||
      document.getElementById('maintenancePage') ||
      document.querySelector('#page-maintenance');
  }

  function f25CreateInterface() {
    if (F25.el('pbl25FinalControl')) return true;

    const page = f25FindPage();

    if (!page) {
      console.warn('PBL25: Main application page not found.');
      return false;
    }

    const section = document.createElement('section');

    section.id = 'pbl25FinalControl';

    section.style.cssText = `
      margin:20px 0;
      padding:20px;
      border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.22);
      border-radius:14px;
      background:rgba(15,23,42,.58);
      color:inherit;
    `;

    section.innerHTML = `
      <div style="
        display:flex;
        justify-content:space-between;
        align-items:center;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
        gap:12px;
        margin-bottom:20px;
      ">
        <div>
          <h2 style="margin:0 0 5px">
            PONT BASCULE LIVE — SYSTEM CONTROL
          </h2>

          <div style="font-size:12px;opacity:.65">
            Module registry · Backend verification · System diagnostics
          </div>
        </div>

        <span id="pbl25Overall"
          style="
            padding:7px 12px;
            border-radius:20px;
            background:rgba(148,163,184,.12);
            font-size:11px;
          ">
          NOT CHECKED
        </span>
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:grid;
        grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(145px,1fr));
        gap:11px;
        margin-bottom:18px;
      ">
        <div class="pbl25-card">
          <small>REGISTERED MODULES</small>
          <strong id="pbl25Modules">0</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl25-card">
          <small>AVAILABLE MODULES</small>
          <strong id="pbl25Available">0</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl25-card">
          <small>BACKEND STATUS</small>
          <strong id="pbl25Backend">UNKNOWN</strong>
        </div>

        <div class="pbl25-card">
          <small>LAST DIAGNOSTIC</small>
          <strong id="pbl25LastCheck">--:--:--</strong>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="
        display:flex;
        flex-wrap:wrap;
        gap:9px;
        margin-bottom:20px;
      ">
        <button type="button" id="pbl25RunDiagnostics">
          RUN FULL DIAGNOSTICS
        </button>

        <button type="button" id="pbl25Refresh">
          REFRESH MODULES
        </button>

        <button type="button" id="pbl25Export">
          EXPORT DIAGNOSTIC REPORT
        </button>
      </div>

      <h3 style="font-size:14px;margin-bottom:12px">
        APPLICATION MODULE REGISTRY
      </h3>

      <div id="pbl25ModuleList"
        style="
          display:grid;
          grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(220px,1fr));
          gap:10px;
        ">
      </div>

      <h3 style="font-size:14px;margin:22px 0 12px">
        SYSTEM DIAGNOSTICS
      </h3>

      <div id="pbl25DiagnosticList"
        style="
          padding:13px;
          border-radius:9px;
          background:rgba(148,163,184,.06);
          font-size:12px;
          line-height:1.8;
        ">
        Diagnostics have not been executed.
      </div>

      <h3 style="font-size:14px;margin:22px 0 12px">
        INTEGRATION READINESS
      </h3>

      <div id="pbl25Readiness"
        style="
          padding:14px;
          border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.15);
          border-radius:9px;
          font-size:12px;
          line-height:1.8;
        ">
      </div>

      <div style="
        margin-top:17px;
        padding:13px;
        border-radius:9px;
        background:rgba(245,158,11,.08);
        font-size:11px;
        line-height:1.7;
      ">
        IMPORTANT: A module appearing as available means its
        frontend function exists. It does not prove that every
        backend operation, hardware device, printer, backup or
        security configuration has been tested successfully.
      </div>
    `;

    const style = document.createElement('style');

    style.textContent = `
      .pbl25-card {
        padding:14px;
        border-radius:9px;
        background:rgba(148,163,184,.07);
      }

      .pbl25-card small {
        display:block;
        font-size:10px;
        opacity:.6;
        margin-bottom:7px;
      }

      .pbl25-card strong {
        font-size:19px;
      }

      .pbl25-module {
        padding:12px;
        border-radius:9px;
        background:rgba(148,163,184,.07);
        border:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.12);
      }

      .pbl25-module strong {
        display:block;
        font-size:12px;
        margin-bottom:6px;
      }

      .pbl25-module small {
        font-size:10px;
        opacity:.65;
      }

      #pbl25FinalControl button {
        cursor:pointer;
      }
    `;

    document.head.appendChild(style);
    page.appendChild(section);

    F25.el('pbl25RunDiagnostics').addEventListener(
      'click',
      pbl25RunDiagnostics
    );

    F25.el('pbl25Refresh').addEventListener(
      'click',
      pbl25RefreshModules
    );

    F25.el('pbl25Export').addEventListener(
      'click',
      pbl25ExportDiagnostics
    );

    f25RenderReadiness();

    return true;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     2. MODULE REGISTRY
     ================================================================ */

  function f25RegisterModules() {
    FC25.modules = [
      {
        name: 'Application Core',
        description: 'State, utilities and navigation',
        check: () =>
          typeof PBL_APP === 'object' &&
          typeof pblCallBackend === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Dashboard',
        description: 'Target, tonnes, voyages and remaining',
        check: () =>
          typeof loadDashboard === 'function' ||
          typeof pblRenderDeliveryDashboard === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Weighing Workflow',
        description: 'First weight, second weight and net',
        check: () =>
          typeof captureFirstWeight === 'function' &&
          typeof captureSecondWeight === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Ticket Management',
        description: 'Ticket search and management',
        check: () =>
          typeof openTicketDetails === 'function' ||
          typeof pbl23ViewTransaction === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Master Data',
        description: 'Products, clients, trucks and drivers',
        check: () =>
          typeof openProductForm === 'function' &&
          typeof openClientForm === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Objectives',
        description: 'Delivery objectives and progress',
        check: () =>
          typeof loadObjectives === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Reports',
        description: 'Reports, analysis and CSV exports',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblExportReportCSV === 'function' ||
          typeof rptExportCSV === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Advanced Search',
        description: 'Filters and ticket finder',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblSearchRun === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Maintenance',
        description: 'Diagnostics, backup and restore',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblMaintenanceRunDiagnostics === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Audit Center',
        description: 'Audit history and security events',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblLoadAuditLogs === 'function' ||
          typeof pblInitializeAuditCenter === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Fleet Management',
        description: 'Truck movement and fleet history',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblInitializeFleetCenter === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Driver Management',
        description: 'Driver activity and performance',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblInitializeDriverCenter === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Scale Monitor',
        description: 'Live weight and connection monitoring',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblInitializeScaleMonitor === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Operations Center',
        description: 'Live operational dashboard',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblInitializeOperationsCenter === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Security Center',
        description: 'Access and session monitoring',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblInitializeSecurityCenter === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Transaction Timeline',
        description: 'Weighing history and traceability',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblInitializeTimeline === 'function'
      },
      {
        name: 'Print Center',
        description: 'Professional weighing ticket printing',
        check: () =>
          typeof pblInitializePrintCenter === 'function'
      }
    ];
  }

  /* ================================================================
     3. MODULE STATUS RENDERING
     ================================================================ */

  function f25RenderModules() {
    const container = F25.el('pbl25ModuleList');

    if (!container) return;

    const available = FC25.modules.filter(function (module) {
      try {
        return Boolean(module.check());
      } catch (error) {
        return false;
      }
    });

    container.innerHTML = FC25.modules.map(function (module) {
      let ready = false;

      try {
        ready = Boolean(module.check());
      } catch (error) {
        ready = false;
      }

      return `
        <div class="pbl25-module">
          <strong>${F25.escape(module.name)}</strong>

          <small>${F25.escape(module.description)}</small>

          <div style="
            margin-top:9px;
            font-size:10px;
            font-weight:bold;
          ">
            ${ready ? 'FUNCTION AVAILABLE' : 'FUNCTION MISSING'}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');

    F25.el('pbl25Modules').textContent =
      FC25.modules.length;

    F25.el('pbl25Available').textContent =
      available.length;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     4. FRONTEND DIAGNOSTICS
     ================================================================ */

  function f25Check(name, condition, detail) {
    FC25.diagnostics.push({
      name: name,
      status: condition ? 'PASS' : 'WARNING',
      detail: detail,
      checkedAt: new Date().toISOString()
    });
  }

  async function f25CheckBackend() {
    try {
      const result = await F25.call([
        'apiSystemHealth',
        'getSystemHealth'
      ]);

      FC25.backend = result ? 'RESPONDING' : 'UNKNOWN';

      f25Check(
        'Backend communication',
        Boolean(result),
        result
          ? 'Backend returned a response.'
          : 'Backend returned an empty response.'
      );

    } catch (error) {
      FC25.backend = 'UNAVAILABLE';

      f25Check(
        'Backend communication',
        false,
        error.message || 'Backend unavailable.'
      );
    }
  }

  /* ================================================================
     5. FULL DIAGNOSTIC EXECUTION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl25RunDiagnostics = async function () {
    if (FC25.checking) return;

    FC25.checking = true;
    FC25.diagnostics = [];

    F25.el('pbl25DiagnosticList').textContent =
      'Running diagnostics...';

    f25RegisterModules();
    f25RenderModules();

    f25Check(
      'Application state',
      typeof PBL_APP === 'object',
      'PBL_APP global state is available.'
    );

    f25Check(
      'Backend communication helper',
      typeof pblCallBackend === 'function',
      'pblCallBackend is required for server communication.'
    );

    f25Check(
      'Google Apps Script environment',
      typeof google !== 'undefined' &&
      Boolean(google.script) &&
      Boolean(google.script.run),
      'Checks whether the page is running with Apps Script communication.'
    );

    f25Check(
      'Live clock',
      typeof updateLocalClock === 'function',
      'Live clock function availability.'
    );

    f25Check(
      'Manual entry hidden by default',
      !PBL_APP.weighing ||
      PBL_APP.weighing.manualEntryEnabled !== true,
      'Frontend setting check only; backend authorization must also enforce this.'
    );

    f25Check(
      'Scale integration',
      false,
      'Physical scale connection has not been verified. A real device and protocol test are required.'
    );

    f25Check(
      'PDF generation',
      false,
      'The current print module uses browser printing, not server-generated PDF.'
    );

    f25Check(
      'XLSX generation',
      false,
      'CSV export is available in some modules; native XLSX generation is not verified.'
    );

    f25Check(
      'QR generation',
      false,
      'A real QR image generation and scanning test is not verified.'
    );

    f25Check(
      'Permanent audit storage',
      false,
      'Permanent server-side audit behavior requires backend verification.'
    );

    await f25CheckBackend();

    FC25.lastCheck = new Date();

    FC25.checking = false;

    f25RenderDiagnostics();

    f25RenderModules();

    F25.el('pbl25LastCheck').textContent =
      FC25.lastCheck.toLocaleTimeString('fr-FR');

    const failures = FC25.diagnostics.filter(
      item => item.status !== 'PASS'
    ).length;

    F25.el('pbl25Overall').textContent =
      failures === 0
        ? 'CHECKS PASSED'
        : failures + ' ITEMS NEED ATTENTION';

    return FC25.diagnostics.slice();
  };

  /* ================================================================
     6. DIAGNOSTIC DISPLAY
     ================================================================ */

  function f25RenderDiagnostics() {
    const container = F25.el('pbl25DiagnosticList');

    if (!container) return;

    container.innerHTML = FC25.diagnostics.map(function (item) {
      return `
        <div style="
          padding:8px 0;
          border-bottom:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.12);
        ">
          <strong>${F25.escape(item.status)}</strong>
          — ${F25.escape(item.name)}

          <div style="opacity:.65;font-size:11px">
            ${F25.escape(item.detail)}
          </div>
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');

    F25.el('pbl25Backend').textContent =
      FC25.backend;
  }

  /* ================================================================
     7. INTEGRATION READINESS
     ================================================================ */

  function f25RenderReadiness() {
    const container = F25.el('pbl25Readiness');

    if (!container) return;

    const requirements = [
      'Confirm all Code.gs parts are combined without duplicate-function conflicts.',
      'Verify every frontend API name and argument order against the backend.',
      'Test PIN authorization on every server-side write operation.',
      'Connect and test the actual weighbridge indicator and communication protocol.',
      'Verify gross, tare and net calculations with real weighing tickets.',
      'Test ticket numbering and duplicate prevention.',
      'Test daily and monthly reporting against the original records.',
      'Configure protected backups and perform a restoration test.',
      'Restrict Google Apps Script deployment access to authorized users.',
      'Test printing on the actual weighing-station printer.',
      'Verify Morocco date, time and timezone behavior.',
      'Perform end-to-end testing before operational deployment.'
    ];

    container.innerHTML = requirements.map(function (item, index) {
      return `
        <div style="
          padding:6px 0;
          border-bottom:1px solid rgba(148,163,184,.1);
        ">
          <span style="opacity:.55">${index + 1}.</span>
          ${F25.escape(item)}
        </div>
      `;
    }).join('');
  }

  /* ================================================================
     8. MODULE REFRESH
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl25RefreshModules = function () {
    f25RegisterModules();
    f25RenderModules();

    return FC25.modules.length;
  };

  /* ================================================================
     9. EXPORT DIAGNOSTIC REPORT
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl25ExportDiagnostics = function () {
    const report = {
      application: 'PONT BASCULE LIVE',
      generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      backendStatus: FC25.backend,
      modules: FC25.modules.map(function (module) {
        let available = false;

        try {
          available = Boolean(module.check());
        } catch (error) {}

        return {
          name: module.name,
          description: module.description,
          functionAvailable: available
        };
      }),
      diagnostics: FC25.diagnostics,
      readinessRequirements: [
        'Backend integration verification',
        'PIN authorization testing',
        'Physical scale integration',
        'Ticket calculation testing',
        'Backup and restoration testing',
        'Printer testing',
        'Deployment access review'
      ]
    };

    const blob = new Blob(
      [JSON.stringify(report, null, 2)],
      { type: 'application/json' }
    );

    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

    const link = document.createElement('a');

    link.href = url;
    link.download = 'PONT_BASCULE_LIVE_DIAGNOSTICS.json';

    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    link.remove();

    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  };

  /* ================================================================
     10. PUBLIC STATUS
     ================================================================ */

  window.pbl25GetSystemStatus = function () {
    return {
      application: 'PONT BASCULE LIVE',
      initialized: FC25.initialized,
      backend: FC25.backend,
      moduleCount: FC25.modules.length,
      diagnostics: FC25.diagnostics.slice(),
      lastCheck: FC25.lastCheck
    };
  };

  /* ================================================================
     11. INITIALIZATION
     ================================================================ */

  window.pblInitializeFinalControl = function () {
    if (FC25.initialized) return true;

    const created = f25CreateInterface();

    if (!created) return false;

    FC25.initialized = true;

    f25RegisterModules();
    f25RenderModules();
    f25RenderReadiness();

    return true;
  };

  function f25Start() {
    window.setTimeout(function () {
      pblInitializeFinalControl();
    }, 1200);
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener(
      'DOMContentLoaded',
      f25Start,
      { once: true }
    );
  } else {
    f25Start();
  }

})();
</script>
